Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 801: Even if the Heavenly Killing Star Comes, We Still Have to Eat
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Gaye felt relieved.

Because Geom Mugeuk had said to the Lord of the Dark Palace what she herself had wanted to say.

If she wanted, he could curse him out even more?

If she followed her heart, she wanted to ask him to keep going until the Lord of the Dark Palace opened that door, came out, bowed his head, and apologized.

“Do not live your life holding back so much just because you are the Divine Maiden.”

The problem was that Geom Mugeuk’s intentions in saying such sweet words were far too obvious.

“No matter what you do, I won’t take your side.”

She added with a deliberate sigh.

“And you saw it, didn’t you? The Palace Lord did not open the door even when you tried to kill me. I have no value to you.”

Contrary to her words, however, she still believed in the Lord of the Dark Palace. The reason he had not opened the door even when Geom Mugeuk said he would kill her was probably because he knew Geom Mugeuk would not truly kill her.

“I know very well how good you are at bewitching people, so don’t waste your breath with pointless words.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and admitted it.

“That is true. With that bewitching art, I made the two finest people among yours into my people. Ah, I wonder what Brother Ak is doing right now. I hope he is not being bullied inside the cult because I am not there. Brother Ak, you must endure!”

He was saying something like that in this situation? Listening to him, Gaye truly felt that she could neither judge nor understand this young cult leader by her own common sense.

The reason Geom Mugeuk had mentioned them was to say this.

“If Brother Ak or Lady Cha were in the same situation, I would have opened the door and come out.”

Gaye bit her lip lightly.

“There’s no use trying to drive a wedge between us. That sort of provocation won’t open that door.”

But Geom Mugeuk’s attempt to sow discord was powerful.

“The Lord of the Dark Palace appeared before me twice earlier. Both times, he appeared as an illusion. And yet he sent you out in the flesh. Even though you could have been killed by me or by the Demon Venerables.”

Gaye’s chest grew tight. Even though she knew perfectly well that he was trying to drive a wedge between them, anger rose in her. It was all the worse because she had already gone through the experience of Heukno not protecting her.

“When we first met, you said it, didn’t you? That no prophecy would descend upon someone like me.”

“That was because you were acting far too shamelessly. I thought you would at least offer a word of apology to the Master of Cheonhwaru. ‘I had no choice but to summon you. I am sorry.’”

“Is one trivial phrase really so important?”

“Because that one phrase can change everything. Because missing the chance to say that one phrase may leave you living the rest of your life in regret.”

Gaye had never once thought of it that way.

“What meaning would an apology have after she had already been summoned?”

“That is not why you did not apologize, is it?”

Geom Mugeuk struck at the heart of her.

“Be honest. In your heart, did you not lack even the thought that you should apologize in the first place? Only now, after hearing my words, did you think, Ah, should I have done that? If so, then that is how the Lord of the Dark Palace raised you. You said I am good at bewitching people? Those are words you should say to the Lord of the Dark Palace first.”

She could not refute him. Geom Mugeuk pressed her mercilessly.

“You have not become the Divine Maiden of the Divine Maiden Palace, but a maidservant of the Dark Palace. That must be why prophecy descends upon the Master of Cheonhwaru, who left the palace.”

Gaye flared up and shouted.

“Shut up!”

Truth is always like a blade. It can protect you, and it can also wound you.

In Gaye’s case, it was the latter, and the wound was very deep.

“Fine, I still have no intention of apologizing. And even if I die, I will never take your side!”

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“I am not doing this to make you take my side.”

“Then why are you doing this to me? Why did you pretend to be friendly?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned back toward the door.

“I am merely trying to use you to open that door. You are the key that can open it.”

Since he said it so openly, Gaye was dumbfounded. At the same time, she was curious. How did he intend to open it?

“If I ask you just one question, that door will open.”

What sort of question could it possibly be? Geom Mugeuk’s question flew toward her.

“The six powers contained in that bead—how do you use them?”

In that instant, Gaye flinched. Judging from her expression and reaction, she knew the method.

“I could ask another question instead. Tell me what sort of person the master of the Heavenly Killing Star is, what story he has, and what abilities he possesses. Also where he is now and what he will do from here on. If you answer this question, I will spare you.”

Gaye gave no answer.

“The best way to take revenge on the Lord of the Dark Palace, who abandoned you, is to tell me the truth.”

It was at that very moment.

Srrrrrk.

The Dark Smoke guarding the door vanished, and the door opened on its own.

“Did I not tell you? You are the key.”

Gaye’s face hardened coldly. The Lord of the Dark Palace had opened the door out of fear that she might reveal the secret. Even though she had no intention of telling him, no matter what cruel torture she suffered.

It was not as if he had opened the door after even one of her fingers had been cut off. He judged my will to be worth only this much?

‘Just what does he think of me?’

Now she knew for certain. The Lord of the Dark Palace did not trust her.

Agitated, she shouted shrilly.

“The door is open now, so I have no value anymore, do I? Hurry up and kill me! Kill me! And you two high-and-mighty bastards had better fight each other and both die from your wounds! I’ll be watching to see who comes to hell first.”

The feeling of being abandoned made her miserable. Abandoned by the heavens, abandoned by the Lord of the Dark Palace, and now, to Geom Mugeuk, she no longer even had value as a tool.

Geom Mugeuk looked at her with calm eyes. He did not run toward that door he had opened with such difficulty. What would he do if it closed again?

If it were her, she would have cut the other person down at once and run.

But Geom Mugeuk was looking at her.

“Now that you have screamed, has your anger eased a little?”

The moment she heard those words, the strength left her. After making someone that furious, was that really something to say?

“Looking at your face makes me feel like my insides are going to burst and I’ll go mad.”

“Then stop looking and go.”

For a moment, Gaye was startled.

“You’re sparing me?”

“Is the life of a Divine Maiden not in the hands of people, but in the hands of those heavens?”

As if he truly intended to spare her, Geom Mugeuk turned around. From behind him, Gaye shouted.

“Tell me the real reason you’re sparing me.”

Geom Mugeuk thought of the Divine Maiden Palace Lord from three hundred years ago. The woman who had delivered the prophecy to him. Did you know your Divine Maiden Palace would become like this?

“Think of it as your ancestor helping you.”

Leaving behind those incomprehensible words, Geom Mugeuk walked toward the door. Watching him, Gaye did not leave that place. She ran over and walked side by side with Geom Mugeuk.

“My anger still hasn’t completely eased.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded and said in a low voice.

“The Divine Maiden Palace should have been like this long ago.”

And so the two of them entered the room where the Lord of the Dark Palace was.

* * *

Until the setting sun went down, they stood on the streets of Maga Village.

“We do not know when the Heavenly Killing Star will come, so until he does, why not wait comfortably inside the tavern? There is no need to waste your strength standing in the street before the fight even begins.”

At Cult Leader Pungcheon’s suggestion, Geom Ujin readily accepted his words.

As he had said, there was no knowing whether the Heavenly Killing Star would come today, tomorrow, a month from now, or never at all.

Even so, the reason Geom Ujin had set the Demonic Flame alight and brought them out was because of his son. He had felt that, with his son’s departure from the cult, the wheel of fate had begun to turn.

They entered the Pungryu Tavern.

Because Jo Chunbae had hurriedly emptied the tavern, the interior had not even been cleaned.

It was a place where the Heavenly Demon Cult Leader would stay; they could not have him sit in such a messy place.

“It seems we should clean this place up a bit.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon said playfully.

“Who is the youngest here? The youngest should clean it.”

At his joke of looking for the youngest among those present, everyone smiled faintly. Thanks to Cult Leader Pungcheon, at least the atmosphere was good.

Cult Leader Pungcheon glanced at the Poison King. Naturally, he had assumed the Poison King would be the youngest.

But an unexpected person stepped forward and revealed himself as the youngest.

“I am the youngest.”

It was none other than the Sword King.

“You are the youngest?”

“I am two years younger than the Poison King.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon shouted in surprise.

“I would believe it if someone said you were his son.”

“If you put it that way…”

The Sword King could not bring himself to say that, in that case, he would look like a grandson to him.

“But how do you know everyone’s ages?”

“I know the ages of everyone here.”

It was a remark that once again made them feel that he had once been their enemy.

“I will clean this place, so please go up to the second floor. Even if the Heavenly Killing Star comes, we still need to eat.”

They could not leave the cult leader standing, so for now, the Fist Demon and the Poison King escorted Geom Ujin up to the second floor.

Since, among them, the Sword King was the easiest for him to deal with and communicate with, Cult Leader Pungcheon remained on the first floor with him. He also felt somewhat sorry that, because he had needlessly brought up the matter of the youngest, the Sword King had ended up working.

Fortunately, the second floor was clean.

Geom Ujin sat in the seat where Geom Mugeuk always sat, and the Fist Demon sat across from him.

The Poison King sat separately at a nearby table. Because he knew the cult leader disliked poison arts, he deliberately sat somewhere inconspicuous.

The Fist Demon asked Geom Ujin,

“Are you uncomfortable? If you need anything, please tell me.”

“I am fine.”

The Fist Demon sensed that the cult leader was enjoying the tension on the eve of this decisive battle.

“It has been a long time since I felt an atmosphere like this.”

“Yes, it has.”

Others might not know, but the Fist Demon did. He knew how much this cult leader enjoyed fighting enemies.

If he could fight a powerful foe, he would gladly stay awake for days and nights, and he was someone who had once crossed the Central Plains from one end to the other without rest.

When freed from authority, the cult leader became a completely different person.

Everyone would probably be startled. The cult leader had this side to him? They would all think that.

At that moment, Cult Leader Pungcheon’s voice came from the first floor.

“You would have done well even as a waiter.”

Unable to hold back even for that short while, Cult Leader Pungcheon was evaluating the Sword King at work.

“Your wiping technique is extraordinary.”

In truth, with shabby clothes, a ponytail, and bare feet, if he had not been wearing a sword, one could believe he was an elderly waiter.

At a glance, it might have sounded offensive, but the Sword King did not mind at all.

“I have thought before that I might open an inn someday.”

“It suits you. It suits you very well. How about an inn where anyone who starts a fight inside must pay with their life? It would become known as the safest inn in the martial world. What should you name the inn?”

As Cult Leader Pungcheon excitedly chattered away, his stomach growled.

“Perhaps because I have talked so much, I am already hungry. Just how many meals a day does the young cult leader eat?”

“I will see if I can find something simple to eat.”

“Would you?”

Just then, the Fist Demon came down to the first floor and said,

“I will do it while preparing a meal for the cult leader.”

It might have seemed that he did not trust him enough to let him touch the food, but that was not the reason.

From the second floor, Geom Ujin said something unexpected.

“He cooked quite a bit in his younger days.”

When Geom Ujin and the Fist Demon had roamed the Central Plains in their youth, they had often cooked for themselves. Geom Ujin’s own skill at cooking had been learned back then as well.

The Sword King and Cult Leader Pungcheon looked at the Fist Demon with surprised expressions.

He cooked with those huge hands?

The large kitchen knife in the Fist Demon’s hand looked like a small fruit knife.

It was a precious scene.

While the Fist Demon chopped carrots in the kitchen, the Sword King held a rag and wiped the tables. And Cult Leader Pungcheon sat in a chair, watching them.

If someone were to paint this scene, the title of the painting would be this:

While Waiting for the Heavenly Killing Star.

Of course, it would become a painting no one could believe.

With those three having gone down to the first floor, only Geom Ujin and the Poison King remained on the second floor.

An awkward silence flowed between them, but even so, Geom Ujin had once gone to the Thousand Poison Forest and helped the Poison King with his poison combinations.

Geom Ujin spoke to the Poison King first.

“Your poison pouch is different from usual.”

The Poison King, who had been blankly staring out the window, lost in his own world, instantly returned to the real one.

“It is a pouch used for war.”

Geom Ujin spoke to the Poison King in a calm tone.

“This war is not your war.”

The Poison King lowered his head slightly. He thought the cult leader was saying it because he disliked poison arts, but that was not it.

“You must fight the war of protecting Mugeuk.”

“!”

The Poison King raised his head with a startled expression. The cult leader’s words were in preparation for the case in which he lost this battle.

The Fist Demon’s knife stopped, and the Sword King’s rag stopped moving.

Cult Leader Pungcheon’s head naturally turned toward the second floor.

“How will you protect Mugeuk from an enemy against whom my martial arts do not work?”

Their gazes intertwined in midair. The cult leader was asking the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s ultimate weapon: If I die, how will you protect Mugeuk?

After pondering for a moment, the Poison King answered calmly.

“I will protect him by making a threat only I can make.”

“What sort of threat? If he has enough skill to kill me, he may have reached immunity to all poisons.”

Then an unexpected answer flowed from the Poison King.

“It will not be a threat to poison him to death.”

“Then?”

“After hiding myself, I will send him this letter.”

The Poison King read out an imaginary threat.

“If you kill the young cult leader, I will poison every person in the world to death so that you can possess nothing. The martial world you seize will be a desolate one.”

It was the most vicious and terrifying threat in the world, and one that only the Poison King could make.

The destruction of the world.

Everyone listening fell silent. Because they knew the Poison King truly had the ability to do it.

The Poison King spoke calmly again. Considering how little he usually spoke with Geom Ujin, he was now having ten years’ worth of conversation all at once.

“But such a thing will not happen.”

Because the cult leader would absolutely never lose to the Heavenly Killing Star. They thought that would be his answer, but words they had never expected flowed out.

“I do not believe the young cult leader was born to balance the Heavenly Killing Star. For that to be true, the young cult leader is far too outstanding and admirable a person.”

The Poison King held a view different from what the Sword King had said earlier.

“Then what do you think Mugeuk was born for?”

At Geom Ujin’s question, the Poison King answered honestly.

“I believe the young cult leader was born to balance you, Cult Leader.”

“!”

Not only Geom Ujin, but also the three people on the first floor were shocked. They had not known the Poison King was thinking that.

“To me, you are more frightening than the Heavenly Killing Star, Cult Leader.”

Because he believed the young cult leader had been born to stop such a frightening cult leader.

“That is why the young cult leader frightens me most of all.”

Chapter 802: Don't Confuse It with Salt!
----------------------------------------
The Lord of the Dark Palace stood in the middle of the room.

Around him, the Five Great Scholars lay dead. Perhaps they had overexerted their internal energy; blood had poured from their noses and mouths.

Earlier, they had formed hand seals and created bizarre patterns and characters in the air, and each time, characters had been engraved into the floor where the Lord of the Dark Palace sat, glowing with light.

And those strange characters and symbols that had been carved into the floor had transferred onto the Lord of the Dark Palace. His body and hands, and even his face, were covered in all manner of incomprehensible writing.

The Lord of the Dark Palace exuded a presence far more intense than when they had seen him in the illusion. His eyes were filled with an unknowable rapture and confidence.

“At last we meet, Young Cult Leader.”

Gaye’s eyes trembled at the sight.

‘He succeeded in the Great Art using the Five Great Scholars.’

The Lord of the Dark Palace had not told even her what sort of Great Art it was. It must have been that dangerous and extraordinary.

Gaye looked at Geom Mugeuk, who stood beside her.

‘No matter how strong you are, can you handle that man?’

Whether he knew her thoughts or not, Geom Mugeuk remained at ease.

“You even locked the door. How did someone this cowardly ever think to take over the murim?”

Indeed, even in the world before his regression, the Lord of the Dark Palace had put the Twelve Branch Kings at the forefront while he himself never stepped into the world. No one had known of the Lord of the Dark Palace’s existence.

He was the sort of man who enjoyed manipulating the world from within the darkness like this.

“You drove me all the way into this corner. How could I not be afraid?”

The Lord of the Dark Palace was just as relaxed as Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the dead.

“Just how many people have to die for that old carcass of yours?”

“The path to a great cause is one paved with corpses.”

“I’ve never seen anyone who speaks of sacrifice for a great cause sacrifice themselves.”

“They left behind great sentences and gladly welcomed death with joyful hearts.”

Geom Mugeuk stared intently at the incomprehensible characters carved into his face.

“It says this right here, though. ‘How unjust! How infuriating! The Lord of the Dark Palace is trash who used us up and threw us away!’”

The dark energy swirling around the Lord of the Dark Palace writhed violently, revealing its master’s mood. Maintaining composure in front of Geom Mugeuk was never an easy thing.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Gaye.

“Now, vent the anger you never got to release.”

Until she entered, she had been full of vigor. She had intended to begin by cursing the Lord of the Dark Palace the moment she saw him.

‘You wretch who defied the will of Heaven!’

But when she actually saw him, those words would not come out.

The Lord of the Dark Palace spoke to her with gentle eyes.

“Do not misunderstand. I did not open the door because I was certain the Young Cult Leader would not kill you.”

“Then why did you open it now?”

“As you can see, the Great Art has just ended.”

The very fact that she was asking such a question was proof that she was wavering.

“Heungno did not try to save me.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace’s excuse came without hesitation.

“Is he not a man who cares only for himself?”

Because it was not wrong, Gaye could not readily refute him.

“Do not be bewitched by the Young Cult Leader. You know well what kind of tongue he possesses.”

Naturally, Gaye’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk. As if he had expected this situation, Geom Mugeuk asked calmly,

“Do you know why you want to believe those words?”

Geom Mugeuk laid bare a part of her heart even she had not known.

“Because right now, you want comfort more than the truth.”

“!”

Her heart sank because the Lord of the Dark Palace’s words had indeed been a comfort.

Perhaps he did not abandon me.

Instead of rebuking her, Geom Mugeuk consoled her.

“That is what happens when a person grows weary. Even walking your own path is tiring. Walking a path that isn’t yours is bound to exhaust you even more.”

Geom Mugeuk did not try to persuade her any further.

“If you believe his words, you may go over there.”

In truth, Gaye could not determine which side was telling the truth.

As she wavered, the Lord of the Dark Palace extended a hand, beckoning her to come to him.

The moment she saw that hand reaching toward her, she realized something anew.

“From the time I was young until now, you have never once reached out your hand to me.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace’s outstretched hand twitched.

Though she had watched him for many long years, she did not know the Lord of the Dark Palace well. He was never one to reveal himself.

But even if she did not know him well, she was familiar with that dark smoke coiling around him. It had always represented the Lord of the Dark Palace’s emotions, like some living creature.

The black smoke flowing around him now was unspeakably sinister and anxious.

Come quickly, hurry! I’ll swallow you whole!

It lurked like a beast waiting for prey.

Now Gaye knew for certain. Which of the two had been bewitching her. That she had been brainwashed for far too long.

“Do you understand now? Why the Divine Maiden Palace should have been the one to lead them?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, the first thing Gaye felt was a sense of injustice.

Even when she had been young, the Lord of the Dark Palace had been an old man and had held the position of Palace Lord. Even the previous Divine Maiden Palace Mistress, who had placed her in the position of Palace Mistress, had not dared think of resisting the Lord of the Dark Palace.

“I had no power.”

“You had the greatest power of all.”

“What do you mean?”

“Your backing is Heaven, is it not?”

Gaye could say nothing.

“You possessed the strongest power. You simply did not recognize it, and no one beside you told you.”

Gaye thought Geom Mugeuk was right.

Instead of using weakness as an excuse, she should have tried to change things somehow. If someone like Geom Mugeuk had been by her side, could she have changed them?

“The Heavenly Will Palace was annihilated three hundred years ago because the Heavenly Will Palace Lord did not listen to the Divine Maiden Palace Mistress.”

The Divine Maiden Palace Mistress had said that the Heavenly Will would arrive three hundred years later, but the Heavenly Will Palace Lord had denied her words.

“You speak as though you saw it with your own eyes.”

At the Lord of the Dark Palace’s words, Geom Mugeuk smiled with clear, deep eyes.

Because he truly seemed to possess the leisure only someone who had seen the past could have, the Lord of the Dark Palace’s heart sank coldly.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Gaye and said,

“Now, say what you wish to say. If you cannot say it now, you will regret it for the rest of your life. There will be no next time.”

Gaye did not rage at the Lord of the Dark Palace. Rather, she spoke calmly. They were words she should have spoken long ago.

“The way you have lived, the way I have watched all my life at your side, was wrong.”

It was the first time in her life she had spoken negatively to the Lord of the Dark Palace.

“You, and I, who failed to stop you—we will have to pay the price.”

Tears flowed from Gaye’s eyes. She had lived as though entranced. She had thought it only natural to live helping the Lord of the Dark Palace. Instinctively, there had been many times when she had thought, This isn’t right, but she had forced herself to ignore it.

Geom Mugeuk asked her,

“Has your anger eased a little?”

She lowered her head and gave no answer.

“Even so, you did well. When you look back on this later, you will think you were right to say it today.”

Later? Would there truly be a later for her? Her life felt as though it might not even have another two hours.

“Why are you being so considerate of me?”

“Because I used you to open this door, and because I want to look good in your eyes so I can hear the answer to the question I asked earlier.”

Even though he said it so openly, she did not feel repulsed.

The Lord of the Dark Palace cut into their conversation.

“Young Cult Leader, you truly are remarkable. To change a person so much in such a short time.”

“I’m not the remarkable one. The Divine Maiden here is.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Gaye.

“The ship called the Divine Maiden Palace has been anchored in harbor until now. You, its captain, have lived without knowing what the ship’s original purpose was. Even now, set out to sea.”

He was telling her to live as the true Palace Mistress of the Divine Maiden Palace.

“Do you think Heaven has abandoned me?”

“I think you have never once properly looked at Heaven. Only when you set out to sea will you be able to see the true sky. Lightning may strike, or a typhoon may descend, but first, go out. Do not die by my hand, and do not die by that man’s hand. If you must die, die on that sea.”

A surge of emotion swept through Gaye’s chest. Even if she lived only one more day, she wanted to live as the true Divine Maiden Palace Mistress.

And having experienced it for herself, she felt she understood. Why Ak Gunhak and Cha Iran had followed this Young Cult Leader.

At last, the answer to the question he had asked earlier flowed from her lips.

“Those six powers are energies called the Six Paths Origin Qi.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace did not stop her and merely watched in silence.

“Why did you seek them so desperately?”

“Because only that power can control that man.”

Geom Mugeuk flinched in surprise. Naturally, he had assumed those six energies were powers Hwa Mugi would use. But they were powers meant to control him?

Just what kind of martial art had Hwa Mugi learned that only the Six Paths Origin Qi could control him?

The Lord of the Dark Palace, who had remained silent until then, finally opened his mouth.

“The murim ruled by him will become a murim entirely different from everything until now. Evil, which has been treated as bad, will become good, and good will become evil. The weak will die and be mocked. The strong will be worshipped, and those who kill more people will be respected. Like the murim of the primordial age.”

An abyssal dark energy flowed from the Lord of the Dark Palace’s body.

“Young Cult Leader, if only you die, a new murim will unfold.”

* * *

At the Poison King’s words, silence flowed through the hall.

Geom Ujin silently gazed at the Poison King. Having said all he had to say, the Poison King bowed his head and said nothing more.

Looking down to the floor below, Geom Ujin said,

“Bring a bottle of liquor here.”

“Yes.”

The Fist Demon took the liquor and went up to the second floor.

Geom Ujin held out a fresh cup that had been turned upside down on the table to the Poison King. The Poison King respectfully accepted the cup.

Glug.

After filling the Poison King’s cup, Geom Ujin said,

“Pour me one as well.”

“Yes.”

This time, the Poison King filled Geom Ujin’s cup. He was a man who had declared he would kill everyone in the world, but at this moment, his tense hands were trembling.

“Let us drink.”

Geom Ujin emptied his cup first, and the Poison King emptied his.

After drinking, Geom Ujin sent the Poison King back to his seat. He said nothing else.

“That is enough. Go and be at ease.”

The Poison King bowed respectfully and returned to his seat. As if he had never spoken so well, he looked out the window and entered his own world.

The Fist Demon could understand, at least a little, what kind of person the Poison King was. Because he was someone who had a world completely his own, he could show such a side as he had earlier.

‘You said the Young Cult Leader was the most frightening? To me, you are the most frightening.’

Feeling anew the terror of the Poison King, the Fist Demon said to Geom Ujin,

“Please wait a moment. I will prepare the meal and bring it up shortly.”

Even apart from the minute change in the Cult Leader’s expression that only the Fist Demon could sense, anyone could see that Geom Ujin seemed to be in a good mood right now. A smile was on his lips.

The Poison King’s answer had passed. And not just passed—he had graduated at the top.

On the first floor, Cult Leader Pungcheon quietly said to the Sword King,

“You got out well.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon glanced up at the second floor and added,

“If you hadn’t, you would have had to fight those people. Could you have handled them?”

Geom Ujin sitting silently, the Poison King looking out the window, and the Fist Demon quietly descending the stairs.

There were only three of them, yet Cult Leader Pungcheon absolutely did not want to fight them. Even without that dreadful Geom Mugeuk, they were giving off this kind of pressure. And there were still more Demon Supremes besides them.

As if he had no interest, the Sword King resumed wiping with his rag and asked Cult Leader Pungcheon,

“Please just give a name to the inn you mentioned earlier.”

* * *

The Heavenly Killing Star did not come.

They continued staying at the Pungryu Tavern. They could have moved to an inn with beds, but they did not.

Geom Ujin had decided to remain here. It must have been because this place held meaning in many ways.

Geom Ujin, the two Demon Supremes, and Cult Leader Pungcheon replaced sleep with circulating their qi and regulating their breath. They were absolute masters, so there was no problem at all.

The Sword King pushed several chairs together on the first floor and slept lying across them. Cult Leader Pungcheon wanted to do the same, but his snoring was severe, so he had no choice.

No matter how brave and valiant he was, he was not brave enough to disturb the Heavenly Demon’s sleep.

They cooked meals, and laughed at Cult Leader Pungcheon’s jokes.

Was it all right to be this relaxed when the Heavenly Killing Star was coming?

Days that naturally made one think such thoughts continued.

And today, Geom Ujin rolled up his sleeves and personally entered the kitchen.

“I will prepare the meal.”

At those words, the four masters were startled.

They were going to eat a meal prepared by the Heavenly Demon?

“Would we even be able to digest such a burdensome meal? I would rather do it myself.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon tried to step forward, but the Fist Demon stopped him.

“Why not take this chance to see His Lordship’s skills?”

The Fist Demon knew that the Cult Leader’s cooking skills were excellent. The Cult Leader was about to show off those skills for the first time in a long while.

Just then, the Poison King entered the kitchen. He had been sitting by the second-floor window like a stone statue until now.

“I will help.”

More than Geom Ujin saying he would prepare the meal, the Poison King stepping forward to help was even more astonishing.

He had answered the earlier question, and liquor had been poured for him. Through that process, it was clear the relationship between the two had changed from before. At the very least, the Poison King had changed.

Geom Ujin nodded.

“Peel those onions over there.”

“Yes.”

The sight of Geom Ujin trimming meat while the Poison King peeled onions was even rarer than the sight from the first day, when the Fist Demon had chopped at the cutting board and the Sword King had wiped with a rag.

The Poison King, who handled deadly poisons freely, had tears gathering in his eyes because the onions stung.

Watching the scene, Cult Leader Pungcheon shouted to the Poison King,

“Don’t get distracted and mix up the salt with the powder in that pouch!”

At that worried joke, even Geom Ujin’s lips curved into a smile.

But in the next moment, Geom Ujin’s smile vanished.

His calm, sunken gaze turned toward the tavern entrance outside the kitchen.

Soon after, the other four naturally looked there as well.

Someone was coming here.

Everyone’s expressions hardened.

They felt the presence of someone walking down the streets of Maga Village toward this place. The other party was deliberately not hiding it.

A short while later, one person stepped inside.

A man dressed in light martial attire.

Astonishingly, it was the Evil Path Alliance Leader, Baek Jagang. He had arrived at the Pungryu Tavern before the Heavenly Killing Star.

A moment of silence passed.

The one who broke the silence born of that astonishment was Geom Ujin. Instead of the Heavenly Demon Sword, he held a kitchen knife as he poked his head out from the kitchen and asked,

“Have you eaten?”

Chapter 803: Deliciously Spicy
------------------------------
At the mention of the Tiansha Xing, Gaye felt fear.

Others might not know, but she did. Just how fearsome a being he was.

Because he had undone and emerged from the seal of the Seventy-Two Star Golden Soul Formation—a seal no human could possibly break. Because she herself had watched that process unfold in real time.

Before coming here, Gaye recalled that day.

In that place where starlight filled the ceiling, she had felt terror.

“You must leave now.”

Only one constellation remained unlit upon the ceiling. Once that final constellation lit up, he would break free from the seal.

But the Dark Palace Lord sat motionless in a meditative posture, like a man who had given up on everything. Even the darkness lingering around his body was listless today.

“There is a way to stop him from emerging.”

Gaye knew. That if the Seventy-Two Star Golden Soul Formation were completely destroyed, the one trapped inside could never come out. And the Dark Palace Lord knew this method as well.

“If we destroy the seal? What of this palace’s three-hundred-year wish?”

“But if he emerges before the Six Paths Origin Qi is obtained… he will kill the Palace Lord first.”

“Perhaps he will.”

The Dark Palace Lord looked as though he had already resigned himself to death.

“Destroy the seal and find another way.”

After a brief pause, the Dark Palace Lord asked.

“Do you think I have time left to start anew?”

Gaye could not answer that question. No matter how great a master the Dark Palace Lord was, he was still human in the end. The countless wrinkles etched into his face spoke of that limit.

“Go to Dongjeong Lake first and prepare what I told you.”

“What are you planning to do?”

The Dark Palace Lord said something astonishing.

“I shall meet him.”

As incomprehensible characters overlapped across the Dark Palace Lord’s face, Gaye returned to reality.

Afterward, the Dark Palace Lord did not die and came to Dongjeong Lake. Whether he had met the one who broke free from the seal, and what words they had exchanged, he did not say.

If he had met him, how had he survived? Had he begged? Had he negotiated? Or had he not met him at all?

The Dark Palace Lord glared at Geom Mugeuk and spoke.

“Now, every table in this world will be shattered.”

He knew precisely what kind of demonic path Geom Mugeuk dreamed of.

“Ridiculous.”

Geom Mugeuk sneered at that prophecy, as if it were a curse.

“A new Murim where evil becomes good and good becomes evil? That’s not the Murim you want, is it?”

The corners of the Dark Palace Lord’s eyes twitched.

“You dreamed of a Murim where everything operates normally, didn’t you? A Murim where your subordinates rule the world and you reign above them as the mastermind, isn’t that right?”

The Dark Palace Lord could not say no. How on earth did the Young Cult Leader know? That was truly the life he had dreamed of.

“If the world is completely shrouded in darkness, would your darkness hold any meaning?”

“!”

He must have known. That in such a world, the existence known as the Dark Palace Lord would no longer be special. That he would fall to become merely one among countless evils.

“Either I die or you die. Today, one of us will die. Before that, I want to hear it from you.”

Geom Mugeuk knew. That this Dark Palace Lord had deep ties to Hwamugi. He had to find out as much as possible about Hwamugi before fighting him.

If they were bound by such a deep fate, wouldn’t he feel the urge to tell someone, at least once, before the final battle?

“How did you come to know that person?”

* * *

When Baek Jagang had rushed here, he had never imagined the atmosphere would be like this.

He had expected a situation of grave solemnity.

How should he greet the Demonic Cult Leader in such a situation? Would he even believe that he had come to help?

Should he say he had come to help? Or make some small talk?

“I came to see what the Tiansha Xing looks like.”

He had arrived while thinking all sorts of thoughts, but—

Had he eaten?

“I have not yet eaten.”

At Baek Jagang’s answer, Geom Ujin, who had poked his head out from the kitchen, withdrew back inside.

“Then, please wait a moment.”

Through the opening in the kitchen wall, the sight of him slicing meat was visible.

The profile of the person with him was briefly visible. He looked so young that Baek Jagang mistook him for a chef or a waiter.

Then, a pouch hanging at the man’s waist caught Baek Jagang’s eye.

When checking the opponent’s weapons, something far more chilling than any treasured sword or blade had hung there. Twelve of them, no less.

Baek Jagang realized that the person peeling onions was the Poison King.

‘The Poison King is peeling onions?’

Even though the Lord of the Four Paths Alliance had arrived, the Poison King did not spare him a single glance, focusing solely on helping Geom Ujin.

Baek Jagang did not mind, as he already knew the Poison King was not one to be bound by ordinary courtesy.

The shocking fact right now was this:

‘The Demonic Cult Leader is cooking, and the Poison King is helping him?’

For a moment, even this thought crossed his mind.

‘Did he know I was coming and is making poisoned food with the Poison King?’

Wasn’t this a more reasonable suspicion than the Heavenly Demon and the Poison King personally cooking a meal for him?

Of course, Baek Jagang knew very well that it was not the case.

Geom Ujin had no reason to poison him. If he wanted to kill him, he would throw that kitchen knife; he was not one to resort to poison.

Moreover, the Poison King was shedding streams of tears as he peeled onions. It couldn’t be that he was crying because the Lord of the Four Paths Alliance was going to die of poisoning.

‘If he isn’t trying to poison me, then?’

Could it be that Tiansha Xing had already come, killed Geom Ujin, and was now wearing his skin?

How shocked must he be to have such outlandish thoughts?

Gwonma approached him and greeted him politely.

“You must have had a hard journey. Please, have a seat here.”

Baek Jagang returned the greeting with a fist salute.

“Thank you.”

He also exchanged greetings with the Wind Heaven Cult Leader. They were already acquainted.

“It has been a while.”

“Have you been well?”

Although he had stepped down as the cult leader of the Wind Heaven Cult, Baek Jagang still recognized him as the foremost master beyond the pass.

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader found Baek Jagang’s visit truly unexpected.

The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was in crisis, yet the Lord of the Four Paths Alliance had come to help? How was he to accept this fact?

“You came alone?”

“I came alone.”

He had even come alone? This was as surprising as the fact that he had come at all. What if he ended up being killed by the Demonic Cult Leader?

Being one who could not suppress his curiosity, the Wind Heaven Cult Leader subtly began to probe his intentions.

“When the Alliance Lord Baek entered through the gate, a thought crossed my mind for a moment. Could it be that Alliance Lord Baek is the Tiansha Xing?”

Knowing that the Wind Heaven Cult Leader was apt to make absurd jokes, Baek Jagang smiled and replied.

“When I fail to rein in my temper, I am sometimes misunderstood in that way.”

He returned the joke multiplied fivefold.

“By that logic, aren’t there already five Tiansha Xings inside this tavern alone?”

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader laughed aloud at the joke implying that he was not the only one who resembled the Tiansha Xing.

He was right. If any of these people went out into the rivers and lakes and caused trouble, it would become the worst incident in history.

“If you are not the Tiansha Xing, why have you come here?”

Gwonma, who was listening from the side, and the Sword King in the corner also waited curiously for Baek Jagang’s answer. As he listened to their conversation, Geom Ujin continued cooking in silence.

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader had asked with a smile, but it was not a question one could answer with a smile.

Though somewhat direct and rude, Baek Jagang was rather grateful to the Wind Heaven Cult Leader.

It was better to be asked outright than to be suspected in one’s heart. At least this way, he had a chance to answer.

Of course, Baek Jagang did not easily reveal his true feelings.

“I came because I was bored.”

Everyone was startled by those words. They wondered if it was a joke, but his voice held not a trace of laughter.

“If it weren’t for an incident of this magnitude, I would have no excuse to go out alone for fun. I saw it as an opportunity and rushed here.”

Baek Jagang could not speak the real reason aloud.

He had come because he was worried the Demonic Cult Leader might die.

It was a situation where he should have rejoiced at the weakening of the Demonic Cult, and he was wary of the cult leader’s ambition to unify the Murim, but even so, he wished for him to survive.

He could not say that when the lips are gone, the teeth grow cold. Nor could he say that if the father of his closest friend died, his disciple would be heartbroken.

He could not say that he wanted to grow old with this Demonic Cult Leader, bragging about their children.

Baek Jagang could not explain the reason.

Just then, Geom Ujin called out to Baek Jagang from the kitchen.

“Alliance Lord Baek.”

As Baek Jagang looked over, Geom Ujin poked his head out of the kitchen. A heavy tension hung in the air over what words might come out of Geom Ujin’s mouth.

“Do you eat spicy food well?”

In Geom Ujin’s gaze, Baek Jagang could sense that he knew why he had come.

Baek Jagang smiled and said.

“I quite enjoy spicy food.”

Geom Ujin nodded and disappeared back into the kitchen. And his voice was heard.

“Slice a few more chili peppers.”

The Poison King, who had yet to erase the tear tracks left by the onions, began slicing chili peppers this time.

Now the Wind Heaven Cult Leader no longer asked why he had come.

“I too enjoy spicy food very much. The best thing about coming to the Central Plains was the abundance of spicy dishes.”

“Remarkable. It is not a decision just anyone could make.”

It was said in regard to the Wind Heaven Cult Leader abandoning his position as cult leader and coming to the Central Plains.

“It seems I too was bored with life.”

Since the topic had come up, he introduced the Sword King.

“By the way, that man is in a similar situation as I.”

Wondering what that meant.

“He came all the way to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult because of the Young Cult Leader, you see.”

Of course, Baek Jagang knew of the Sword King.

Even without revealing his energy, his strength could be felt. It meant that such a force had been added to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, which was not good news for the Four Paths Alliance.

“Evil Martial Artist, if life ever becomes boring, look toward our alliance rather than the Martial Alliance.”

The Sword King silently bowed his head lightly, and the Wind Heaven Cult Leader answered on his behalf.

“Unfortunately for the Alliance Lord, it seems that man has already found his answer for what to do when life becomes boring.”

Just as they finished exchanging greetings with the Sword King, the sound of food being stir-fried came from the kitchen.

Sssss.

Geom Ujin was stir-frying meat and vegetables in a heated wok. The onions the Poison King had shed tears over were in there too.

Feeling sorry for just sitting idle, Baek Jagang got up and walked over to the kitchen.

“Is there nothing I can help with?”

“It’s fine. It’s almost done.”

Baek Jagang stood before the kitchen for a moment, watching Geom Ujin’s back as he cooked. He was not deliberately putting on this scene. He was truly serious about cooking right now.

‘Is this your own way of dispelling life’s emptiness?’

Finally, the cooking was finished.

Everyone sat around the table and tasted the dishes made by Geom Ujin.

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader watched for cues and did not pick up his chopsticks first. Only after seeing Geom Ujin and Gwonma eat first and deem it fine did he lift his chopsticks.

Eating a dish whose ingredients had been prepared by the Poison King was no small feat unless one had a strong heart.

He ate wondering if it could be that delicious, but the dish was unexpectedly tasty.

“Oh! It’s delicious.”

The Wind Heaven Cult Leader exclaimed. It was not words to ingratiate himself with the Heavenly Demon. The meat and vegetables were grilled perfectly, and the seasoning was exquisite.

Baek Jagang was also surprised by Geom Ujin’s cooking skills.

“I would believe it if you said a professional chef had made this. I would even like to learn, should the opportunity arise.”

“You exaggerate.”

Gwonma could sense it. Despite his stoic expression, Geom Ujin was immensely proud right now.

Geom Ujin said nonchalantly, chopsticks in hand.

“Thank you for coming.”

It sent no chill down his spine.

Baek Jagang picked up a mouthful of food with his chopsticks.

“It’s delightfully spicy.”

* * *

The next day, Jin Paecheon, the Martial Alliance Lord, entered Maga Village as darkness fell.

One could not know how many times he had contemplated turning back on his way here.

It was due to the worry that the Tiansha Xing might head to the Martial Alliance.

But in the end, he had come to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Despite the desperate dissuasion of Commander-in-Chief Jegal Hyeon, Jin Paecheon said this. Just as his grandson had once said to him.

“This is the first and last decision I have made according to my heart since becoming the Alliance Lord.”

It had been such a difficult journey to the Demonic Cult, yet Jin Paecheon still had many worries.

How would the Demonic Cult Leader regard his visit?

He would be suspicious, wouldn’t he?

He might think he had come to observe the fight against the Tiansha Xing and gather information on the Guhwa Demonic Art.

Or he might suspect that he intended to betray him at the decisive moment and kill him.

Jin Paecheon looked down at the Gentleman’s Sword at his waist. Since he had even come bearing the Foremost Sword of All Time, it was perhaps even more likely he would be suspected.

He had to persuade him well to avoid any unnecessary misunderstanding.

Moving with a heavy and cautious heart, he saw a single place with its lights on in the darkened street.

‘That place?’

It was precisely the tavern where the tripartite talks had been held long ago.

The energy felt from the tavern was unusual.

Jin Paecheon took a deep breath and carefully opened the tavern door and entered.

Four people sitting at the table were looking at him.

Gwonma and the Poison King sat facing him, and the Sword King and the Wind Heaven Cult Leader sat beside them. They were looking at him with surprised eyes.

‘A grave atmosphere! And the Demonic Cult Leader is not here!’

Could I be one step too late?

Just as Jin Paecheon’s heart sank, voices were heard from inside the kitchen.

“This is how you rotate it so the meat soaks up the seasoning evenly, yes?”

“There is no need to use internal energy for that.”

“If you’re going to do it, doesn’t it taste better when made splendidly?”

“You got it splattered everywhere showing off.”

Having confirmed whose voices they were, Jin Paecheon’s eyes went wide in shock. If he had not misheard!

‘Could it be! Have I fallen under sorcery?’

Just then, Baek Jagang poked his head out of the kitchen. As if he had just fought a bloody battle in the kitchen, his clothes and even his face were smeared with red seasoning.

With a bright face, he asked Jin Paecheon.

“Have you eaten?”

Chapter 804: Laughing, Chattering, Singing, and Dancing
-------------------------------------------------------
Jin Paecheon wondered whether or not to draw his sword.

Even the sauce stains on Baek Jagang’s face could not ease this taut tension. No, if anything, they made the sight even more absurd.

If this was not sorcery, then what on earth was?

It was so certain that it became suspicious instead.

Just then, Geom Ujin came out of the kitchen.

“You have come?”

Jin Paecheon stared piercingly at Geom Ujin and asked,

“Cult Leader Geom, is that really you?”

Geom Ujin nodded.

Baek Jagang was still sticking his head out through the hole in the kitchen.

“I was in the middle of learning to cook from Cult Leader Geom here.”

For a truly fleeting moment, this thought even crossed his mind.

‘Have the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Evil Dao Alliance conspired to kill me?’

What a perfect trap this was. Geom Ujin and Baek Jagang, and even the four masters sitting at the table.

Even if he had brought the Gentleman Sword with him, could he defeat them? Not a chance.

Of course, Jin Paecheon knew that was not the situation. How shocked must he have been for such a thought to occur to him?

The Fist Demon, the Poison King, the Pungcheon Cult Leader, and the Sword King, who had been watching, all understood Jin Paecheon’s reaction. It would have been strange not to be shocked upon seeing this.

The one who stepped forward to resolve this tense situation was, as expected, the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“If it were me, I’d have drawn my sword already.”

Jin Paecheon’s gaze turned to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

Skipping any greeting about how long it had been, the Pungcheon Cult Leader continued speaking with an expression that said he fully understood how Jin Paecheon felt.

“What Evil Dao Alliance Leader in the world would come running to help because the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was in crisis? He’d be praying, ‘Please let them perish.’”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader looked at Baek Jagang, who had his head poking out of the kitchen, then turned back to Geom Ujin beside him.

“What Demon Cult Leader in the world would teach cooking to an Evil Dao Alliance Leader who came to him like that? He’d send a sound transmission like this to the Poison King over there: ‘Season the Evil Dao Alliance Leader’s soup with Formless Ultimate Poison instead of salt.’”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader was in high spirits, teasing the cult leaders and alliance leaders. His gaze returned to Jin Paecheon.

“And is that all? What Martial Alliance Leader in the world would come alone all the way to the Demon Cult’s main altar to help the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult? This is all an absurd illusion and sorcery, isn’t it? If it were me, I’d have blown this whole place away by now.”

The moment he heard those words, the tension drained from Jin Paecheon.

Thinking about it, even the fact that he had come here was absurd.

Right now, Chief Strategist Jegal Hyeon was probably unable to tell anyone that he had gone alone to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s main altar. They would not believe it anyway, and even if they did, they would all leap up and down asking how he could have just let him go.

Jin Paecheon looked once more at each person present.

First, his eyes met Geom Ujin’s.

Yes. No sorcery could recreate that man’s bearing, that feeling, exactly as it was.

He also looked at Baek Jagang, who had his head poking out beside him. The Baek Jagang he knew was absolutely not the sort of person to do that.

Why are you sticking your head out from there? And what on earth is that on your face?

This time, Jin Paecheon’s gaze turned to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

He was spouting nonsense, but he was undeniably an absolute ruler beyond the borders. Yet here, he was not the protagonist, but merely one among several. What was astonishing was that he did not look displeased at all.

Next, Jin Paecheon looked at the two Demon Venerables.

Not just any Demon Venerables, but the Fist Demon and the Poison King.

‘The Cult Leader has truly resolved to go all the way.’

Lastly, he looked at the Sword King. Of course, Jin Paecheon knew of the Sword King as well. The addition of this powerhouse to the Divine Cult had been a major source of trouble for the Martial Alliance.

At first, Geom Mugeuk had been astonishing and remarkable.

And now, everyone was changing. Enough that he could not help but feel impatient, wondering if he alone was stagnant.

Only after looking at them all did Jin Paecheon finally cup his fist and greet them.

“My greetings are late.”

Everyone present also rose and greeted him politely.

After the greetings were over, Jin Paecheon added one more sentence.

“It seems I need not have come.”

With just the people gathered here, they seemed capable of destroying murim several times over.

The Fist Demon guided him to a seat.

“Please sit over here.”

“Thank you.”

Baek Jagang went back into the kitchen.

“I will finish the dish I was making. You two should talk.”

Geom Ujin sat down across from Jin Paecheon. Knowing Jin Paecheon’s personality, Geom Ujin understood well how difficult it must have been for him to leave the Alliance at a time like this and come here.

“Did you come alone?”

Jin Paecheon nodded and answered,

“I cannot remember the last time I went out into murim alone like this.”

Apart from his worries about the Alliance, Jin Paecheon felt a sense of freedom.

He had come as quickly as possible, but there had inevitably been a couple of moments when he had to stop by an inn to eat.

At those times, no one at the inn recognized him. No one could have imagined that the Martial Alliance Leader would be eating noodles alone in a corner seat.

It was a situation utterly unlike his usual official processions, where martial artists came out to bow their heads and welcome him, and that moment felt new and pleasant.

As he ate, he listened to the conversations people shared. Who was rising in fame these days, which village had produced a promising junior, which escort agency had gone under.

Just listening to those words was enjoyable. It was the fresh pleasure of a life he had never experienced.

If this had not been an urgent matter, he would have gone to a tea house to drink tea and walked through the marketplace alone.

Jin Paecheon wondered if Geom Ujin had ever had such an experience.

“Cult Leader, have you ever gone about on your own?”

“I once traveled quietly with my son.”

“That must have been nice.”

Geom Ujin nodded as though it truly had been.

“When you return to the Alliance, spend some time with your grandchildren.”

What would Jin Hagun or Jin Haryeong think if he asked them to travel with him? Would they feel burdened?

At that moment, Geom Ujin’s gaze turned to the sword at Jin Paecheon’s waist.

“Could that sword be the Gentleman Sword?”

“As expected, Cult Leader, you recognize it at once.”

At the words Gentleman Sword, the four masters present were startled.

The greatest Gentleman Sword of all time.

They had only heard of it and had never seen it in person. Since they were all martial artists, they showed interest.

“This sword belongs to my grandson.”

At least here, whose it was did not matter. What mattered was who had given it.

“Your son gave it to my grandson as a gift.”

At the words that it had been Geom Mugeuk’s gift, everyone was astonished. Then, soon after, they all nodded.

As expected! The generosity to give the Gentleman Sword to someone as a gift was something only Geom Mugeuk could possess.

A smile formed at the corners of Geom Ujin’s mouth.

The Fist Demon could understand what kind of joy was contained in the Cult Leader’s smile.

The sword Geom Mugeuk wore was the Black Demon Sword that the Cult Leader himself had bestowed upon him. He must be pleased that, even after obtaining a better sword, Geom Mugeuk had not coveted it. He must be pleased by the wisdom of gifting a sword symbolizing the orthodox faction to the next Alliance Leader.

Even he, as his master, felt proud hearing such words, so how much better must it feel as his father?

The Fist Demon glanced sideways at the Poison King. Even while sitting at the same table, the Poison King was staring blankly out the window.

But he was probably hearing everything that needed to be heard. That must have been why he could say the Young Cult Leader was the most frightening.

This incident, too, was an anecdote that allowed one to say the Young Cult Leader was the most frightening.

Of course, not everyone accepted this anecdote as beautiful.

“He gives so well and bestows so generously on others! Yet to me!”

The one who shouted was the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

And it was not only the Gentleman Sword.

The needless sparks flew to the Sword King.

“That White Flower Sword of yours was a gift from the Young Cult Leader too, wasn’t it? You said he gave you those shoes as well! He gives to everyone else, so why did he only take from me!”

Then Geom Ujin said to him in passing,

“Did you not say you were happy because of my son?”

Rather than comforting him, he was bringing that up now?

The Pungcheon Cult Leader narrowed his eyes and looked at Geom Ujin.

“I can tell very well who the Young Cult Leader takes after.”

Though he was saying that the son was as vicious as the father, Geom Ujin instead laughed in good spirits.

Just then, Baek Jagang came out carrying the dish.

“Here it is, the first dish of my life, completed under Cult Leader Geom’s instruction.”

Seeing Baek Jagang looking proud, Jin Paecheon finally realized anew what kind of situation this was.

It was the moment when the Martial Alliance Leader would eat a dish made by the Evil Dao Alliance Leader in the front yard of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Was this really all right?

This pounding in his heart was different from the tension he felt when facing an enemy. It was closer to the pounding one felt as a child after doing something bad.

One thing was certain.

Right now, he had become a part of the sorcery this gathering had created. If someone saw this sight, they would think it sorcery or an illusion because of him as well.

Geom Ujin was the first to lift his chopsticks and taste Baek Jagang’s dish.

Baek Jagang watched him nervously.

Geom Ujin nodded with a satisfied expression.

“I think you may descend the mountain now.”

Baek Jagang rejoiced and this time waited for Jin Paecheon’s evaluation.

Jin Paecheon lifted his chopsticks.

Surely it is not poisoned, is it?

Even though he tried not to think it, with the Poison King present, such a thought naturally came to mind.

Jin Paecheon slowly and carefully chewed the food and swallowed it. He was doing so in order to spit it out quickly if there was any poison, but to those watching from the side, it looked as though he was savoring the taste.

Jin Paecheon’s eyes widened.

“Why is this…”

After a brief pause, he spoke as if astonished.

“…delicious?”

Baek Jagang’s face brightened radiantly.

Once the two of them had tasted it, the others also lifted their chopsticks.

The Fist Demon, who had considerable knowledge of cooking, also nodded and acknowledged the taste. Of course, it was an evaluation born of loyalty. Who had taught him, after all? How could it possibly not be delicious?

The Pungcheon Cult Leader assessed it a little more coolly.

“It would be better if it were spicier, but this is excellent enough.”

“Next time, I will show you true spiciness.”

Listening, Jin Paecheon thought inwardly,

Will there truly be a next time to this meeting?

Then again, considering their first three-way meeting, who could have known that the three of them would meet again like this? And in the same place, no less.

When the meal had been roughly finished, the Pungcheon Cult Leader asked the question he had been holding back.

“Alliance Leader Baek here said he came because he was bored, but why did our Alliance Leader Jin come?”

Jin Paecheon looked at Baek Jagang. As if that man had come because he was bored.

“Do you perhaps have any wine?”

At Jin Paecheon’s request, the Sword King rose to fetch wine, but the Fist Demon stood first.

“I will bring it.”

To him, the other party was a guest who had come to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, and he believed that he himself ought to take care of that guest. Since the Poison King was not someone who did such things to begin with, the Fist Demon had taken charge of receiving the guests.

That was also why, when the alliance leaders had arrived earlier, he had personally stepped forward to guide them to their seats.

When the Fist Demon brought the wine, Geom Ujin poured a cup for Jin Paecheon.

“Thank you for coming.”

This time as well, goosebumps did not rise by Baek Jagang’s ears. The Cult Leader was sincerely grateful that they had come.

“Even while coming all the way here, I did not drink a drop. One cup here should be fine.”

It was not an atmosphere for drinking, so Jin Paecheon did not offer any to the others and drank alone.

For some reason, he wanted a drink.

“To be honest, I came because of Hagun.”

The moment he saw the Gentleman Sword lying beside the armchair, he had sensed that, in the end, he would go.

“Until now, I have held my grandson to a very strict standard.”

After entrusting him with the position of leader of the Demon Extermination Squad, he had emphasized the path of righteousness and chivalry. He had taught him that if he was a martial artist of the orthodox faction, he had to give his life for righteousness.

“I wanted my grandson to be without even a speck of blemish.”

Only then would he be able to inherit the position of Alliance Leader.

“Thinking back on it, that was not for my grandson’s sake, but for mine. ‘As expected, the Alliance Leader educated him well.’ I wanted to hear such an evaluation.”

Jin Paecheon calmly revealed his innermost thoughts, and everyone quietly listened to him.

“Since I have pushed him that way all his life, should I not grant my grandson’s request at least once?”

Jin Paecheon looked at Geom Ujin. Words that truly no one had expected flowed from him.

“At least here, you will not die before I do.”

He was determined to fulfill his grandson’s wish to help his friend’s father.

Geom Ujin and Jin Paecheon stared at each other. Jin Paecheon knew that he was not a man who lied, even if he chose not to speak.

Geom Ujin did not say thank you. He merely received Jin Paecheon’s gaze calmly.

No matter how the Pungcheon Cult Leader thought about it, he did not think the Heaven-Slaying Star would appear in a situation like this.

“If the Heaven-Slaying Star has any sense, would he come here? When all three of you have gathered as well?”

At the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s words, someone said,

“He will come.”

The one who spoke with such certainty was, astonishingly, Geom Ujin.

Everyone’s gaze turned to Geom Ujin. His calm eyes turned toward Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang.

“Until the two of you came, I was half in doubt. But seeing that you two have come, the Heaven-Slaying Star will definitely come.”

If the Pungcheon Cult Leader thought he would not come because everyone had gathered, Geom Ujin thought the opposite.

“Because fate has gathered us in one place.”

He meant that there had been a pull of destiny.

“Just as the two of you opened that door and came in, the Heaven-Slaying Star will open that door and come in.”

Geom Ujin could feel the wheel of that fate rolling toward them.

Unlike usual, Geom Ujin was revealing his inner thoughts. It was an expression of gratitude toward the two alliance leaders who had come all the way here.

“I know that the two of you are wary of me and do not trust me.”

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang’s eyes sank calmly.

They knew. Apart from this battle, Geom Ujin was still a man who dreamed of unifying murim.

They knew that perhaps an even harder and more frightening battle than the one against the Heaven-Slaying Star might be waiting for them.

As if acknowledging that, Geom Ujin said,

“However, let us consider this battle separately.”

The reason was not the pride of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, nor his pride as a martial artist. It was not for the peace of murim either.

“This battle is deeply connected to our children.”

Not only Geom Mugeuk, but also Jin Hagun and Bi Sain had fought life-and-death battles against them.

“As the years pass, those children will inevitably have to live busy and difficult lives too.”

Finding joy in a brief rest at an inn.

Smiling radiantly at the success of a first dish.

They would have to live while bearing the greatest responsibilities in murim on their shoulders.

“So for now, let the children gather among themselves, laugh and chatter, sing and dance.”

Geom Ujin looked at them all. After meeting the eyes of Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang, the Fist Demon and the Poison King, the Pungcheon Cult Leader and the Sword King in turn,

“Let us end this battle at our generation.”

Chapter 805: If You Hang Around a Drunkard You Become One
---------------------------------------------------------
“How did you come to know that man?”

When Geom Mugeuk asked about the Heavenly Killing Star, the Dark Palace Lord’s gaze darkened.

It was a look that seemed to have much to say, yet he said nothing.

Geom Mugeuk provoked him.

“You must be angry. It was the life you dreamed of your whole life, and now the Heavenly Killing Star has taken it.”

The Dark Palace Lord did not fall for Geom Mugeuk’s provocation. Instead, he answered calmly.

“Aren’t you the one who should be angry? If you became the Demon Cult Leader, the murim might have become yours.”

As they conversed, Geom Mugeuk did not neglect a single word the Dark Palace Lord spoke. When it came to Hwa Mugi, knowing even one more thing mattered.

“You truly believe in the Heavenly Killing Star. Just how strong is he, for you to believe in him this much?”

Geom Mugeuk knew that strength better than anyone, but he asked as if he did not.

The Dark Palace Lord’s gaze turned to Gaye, who stood behind Geom Mugeuk.

“If you’re curious, why not ask that Divine Maiden who came here fawning in every way just to save her own life?”

Though the words mocked and belittled her, Gaye did not grow angry. Somehow, she seemed calm. That change grated on the Dark Palace Lord’s nerves.

Geom Mugeuk took her side.

“If you used someone and threw her away, you shouldn’t treat her like a traitor. You should at least look a little sorry. Why is it so hard for all of you to say you’re sorry?”

Gaye recalled what Geom Mugeuk had said before.

When they first met, he had said the same thing to her. That he had thought she would at least be able to say a word of apology to the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord. That the Dark Palace Lord had raised her into someone who did not know remorse.

Seeing the Dark Palace Lord’s attitude now, she thought those words had been right. She had been raised that way, and she had become that kind of person.

Suddenly, the words Geom Mugeuk had spoken came back to her.

—Only when you go out to sea will you be able to see the true sky. You don’t know whether lightning will strike or a typhoon will descend, but go out first. Even if you die, die on that sea.

Her gaze turned toward the Dark Palace Lord.

“I am on no one’s side. I stand here as the Palace Lord of the Divine Maiden Palace.”

“Does the conviction of the Divine Maiden Palace Lord change so easily?”

The Dark Palace Lord was angry with her. He believed she had betrayed him.

“You’re right. I’m not someone with the right to do this. Ever since I became the Divine Maiden Palace Lord at a young age, I’ve lived helping you. It would be absurd for me to speak of Heaven’s Will now.”

She looked into the Dark Palace Lord’s eyes and added,

“My conviction may have changed easily, but this feeling I have now is sincere.”

She focused on her own heart. Now, she no longer resented the Dark Palace Lord. Her boat had left the pier and gone out to sea.

“Whether I die by your hand today, by the Young Cult Leader’s hand, or later by that man’s hand, I do not regret the decision I made today. Please give up now and tell the Young Cult Leader. Tell him how that man must be killed.”

She calmly conveyed her final sincerity.

“This is my last Heaven’s Will.”

But her Heaven’s Will did not reach him at all.

Shwaaaak.

Booom.

The palm force that flew toward her was blocked by Geom Mugeuk, who remained standing and simply extended his hand to the side. It was not an attack meant to kill her. It was a light strike launched with the knowledge that Geom Mugeuk would block it.

“This is my answer.”

The Dark Palace Lord wanted to appear magnificent. Like the ultimate leader at the very end. Stern, splendid, and mysterious. Perhaps he could have shown such a bearing before the Demon Venerables.

But before these two people, Geom Mugeuk and the Divine Maiden Palace Lord, it was impossible. They were the sort who stirred his most primal emotions simply by being seen.

“You should not have betrayed me. Not when you think of all I have done for you!”

The one who received that resentment was Geom Mugeuk.

“Do not be deceived.”

Geom Mugeuk said to Gaye.

“Whether I had taken you in, or my blunt father had taken you in, or the Blood Heaven Blade Demon of the past had taken you in, all of us would have done more for you than that man ever did. Do not be fooled by that selfish display of credit. Instead, demand an answer. Ask him why he makes such a show of having given you a dragon’s head when all he gave you was a rat’s tail.”

The Dark Palace Lord truly wanted to roar. You damned Young Cult Leader brat! He wanted to unleash a killing move at once.

But he endured it. No matter how much Geom Mugeuk enraged him, there was something he absolutely had to do.

Geom Mugeuk, on the other hand, continued to provoke the Dark Palace Lord.

“You are someone who thinks only of yourself to an appalling degree. If those wrinkles on your face were spread open, the spaces between them would surely be filled with selfishness.”

“Is there anyone who isn’t like that? Are you different? You claim to act for the people? The Demon Hero? Don’t make me laugh. That is nothing more than amusement you indulge in for your own satisfaction.”

Geom Mugeuk readily nodded.

“You’re right. I admit it. It is my amusement. But I live hoping that my amusement can become amusement for others as well. What about you? What is the result of your amusement, wanting to become the leader behind the shadows? Was killing everyone around you your amusement?”

“You were the one who killed them all.”

“It was to save everyone else.”

Their taut gazes tangled in the air.

“Where is the Heavenly Killing Star now?”

He would answer other things, but when it came to the Heavenly Killing Star, he gave no answer at all.

Gaye’s voice came from behind.

“The Heavenly Killing Star awakened on his own, and he undid the seal of the Seventy-Two Star Soul-Sealing Formation on his own.”

She believed it had something to do with Geom Mugeuk.

“I believe he awakened because of you, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk could not deny it. Because of his regression, the timing of that man’s appearance had changed.

“Has he already broken the seal and come out?”

Gaye nodded.

“I believe so.”

Geom Mugeuk asked the Dark Palace Lord,

“Where is he now?”

“Where do you think he went?”

There was only one place Geom Mugeuk could think of.

“Did he go to the main cult?”

The Dark Palace Lord spoke coldly, his expression saying, This is my revenge.

“Just as you killed everyone around me, everyone around you will die. This is my final amusement.”

Though those words were truly terrifying, Geom Mugeuk did not panic or waver.

“You are not surprised.”

“I am the sort who worries about all kinds of things.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke his honest thoughts.

“I’m just going to think of it this way. If Father can’t resolve it, then I can’t resolve it either.”

It was faith in his father. And within that faith was this: before his regression, his father had mastered the Guhwa Demonic Art to the tenth star, but now he had mastered it to the twelfth.

And above all, his father’s life had changed as well.

But Gaye feared the Heavenly Killing Star even more than his father.

“He is stronger than imagination can encompass. If the Six Paths Origin Qi cannot control him…”

Geom Mugeuk understood the words she had left unsaid: even the Demon Cult Leader could not defeat him.

Now he knew. He knew why Hwa Mugi had gone into seclusion in his life before regression.

It had not been seclusion.

After killing the Murim Alliance Leader, the Evil Dao Alliance Leader, and his father, the Dark Palace Lord must have controlled Hwa Mugi with the power of the Six Paths Origin Qi. Perhaps he had sealed him again, or perhaps even killed him.

“How do you use that Six Paths Origin Qi?”

As if he had been waiting for that question, the Dark Palace Lord answered.

“I will tell you that.”

“There is no way you would tell me properly.”

“In any case, we do not know the method. Only this can teach you.”

Srrrk.

The wall behind the Dark Palace Lord opened. When Geom Mugeuk saw what lay in the space beyond, he was shocked.

Astonishingly, it was a large Secret Coffer. For this final battle, the Dark Palace Lord had brought out the Secret Coffer.

“You know what this is, don’t you?”

At the Dark Palace Lord’s question, Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“I know it as your sacred object, handed down from long ago.”

The war three hundred years ago had begun from this Secret Coffer as well. When the Secret Coffer produced the small Secret Coffer, the Palace Lord of Heaven’s Will at the time had believed Heaven had bestowed its will upon him.

“To combine the Six Paths Origin Qi within your body into one, you must borrow the power of this Secret Coffer.”

Geom Mugeuk could tell that the Dark Palace Lord did not know he had already learned the formula through the Secret Coffer and combined the powers of the Six Paths Origin Qi into one.

In this place filled with the power of the Greed Great Annihilation Art, and after he had even succeeded in some unknown grand rite, the reason he had not immediately tried to kill him was this.

‘He still hasn’t given up! He means to steal the power of the Six Paths Origin Qi through this Secret Coffer.’

Yes, it was precisely because he possessed such tenacity that he had managed to come this far.

When the Secret Coffer met Geom Mugeuk, who had gathered all six powers, if something happened—

He intended to seize that moment and steal the power.

Geom Mugeuk thought the grand rite related to the characters carved all over his face might be connected to that.

Sorry, but you’re too late. Even if I approach the Secret Coffer, nothing will happen.

“May I go closer and look?”

The Dark Palace Lord stepped aside.

Geom Mugeuk took a few steps, then stopped.

“Why are you showing it to me so readily? Did you perhaps set up some trick?”

“You are the one who said you wanted to look at it up close. If you don’t want to, then step back.”

“I can’t do that, if only because I’m curious.”

Geom Mugeuk began walking again and approached the Secret Coffer. When they had parted for the last time, he had hoped there would be a fateful day when they met again. And now they had met like this.

‘Secret Coffer, good to see you!’

Seeing the Secret Coffer he thought he would never see again truly made him glad. Since the small Secret Coffer had vanished when he absorbed the final power, this was now the only Secret Coffer left.

Geom Mugeuk touched the Secret Coffer with his hand.

‘I never thought I’d see you again like this.’

At that very moment.

Srrrk.

As if sinking into a swamp, Geom Mugeuk’s body was drawn into the Secret Coffer. There had been no warning, no sign beforehand.

“Huh?”

Because he had experienced being sucked in once before, Geom Mugeuk knew what phenomenon this was.

‘Not now!’

The problem was the people left behind. The Divine Maiden Palace Lord was one thing, but the three Demon Venerables and the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord were still on the ship. They would certainly be coming here in search of him.

But more than half his body had already entered the Secret Coffer.

He could not escape by his own strength, and even if he could, this was a situation in which he must not leave.

In an instant, Geom Mugeuk vanished completely into the Secret Coffer.

When Geom Mugeuk disappeared, the Dark Palace Lord rushed to the Secret Coffer. When Geom Mugeuk had approached it, he had secretly hoped. Hoped he would gain his final chance.

But something utterly unexpected had occurred. He had never dreamed the Secret Coffer would absorb Geom Mugeuk.

The Dark Palace Lord carefully placed his hand on the part that had absorbed Geom Mugeuk.

He was cautious in case he too might be absorbed, but the wall of the Secret Coffer, which had swallowed a person like a swamp, was a hard, cold lump of metal.

He tried to inject qi and examine the inside, but he could not sense any energy within.

Placing his hand on the Secret Coffer, the Dark Palace Lord pleaded desperately.

“Noble sacred object of this palace, grant me the power the Young Cult Leader obtained!”

But the Secret Coffer did not budge.

“That power belongs to this palace, and it belongs to me. Grant me power!”

He cried out earnestly, but the Secret Coffer showed no response.

“If you abandon me like this, I will smash the Secret Coffer to pieces with the Young Cult Leader still inside.”

It was not a mere threat. If he did that, then for now, he would have won the battle against the Young Cult Leader. It was not the best situation, but it meant he had avoided the worst.

“Bestow Heaven’s Will upon me!”

As if he would smash the Secret Coffer apart, the Dark Palace Lord raised his hand high. Tremendous dark energy surged into his hand.

“Stop!”

The one who shouted to dissuade him was Gaye.

“The Secret Coffer is the absolute sacred object of Heaven’s Will Palace. You must not break it like that!”

With his hand still raised, the Dark Palace Lord asked Gaye,

“Are you worried about the Secret Coffer? Or are you worried about the Young Cult Leader?”

Then unexpected words flowed from Gaye’s mouth.

“I am worried about you.”

“!”

The Dark Palace Lord felt that she was speaking sincerely. He had watched her for a long time, so he could tell whether she was telling the truth or lying.

“I am worried about you right now.”

“Why are you worried about me?”

“Because I am the Divine Maiden Palace Lord, and you are the Dark Palace Lord.”

She was struggling to fulfill her final role as the Divine Maiden Palace Lord.

“It is unfortunate, but Heaven’s Will is not with you. It is with the Young Cult Leader.”
