Looking at the Dark Palace Lord, who twitched with rage, she finished what she had been saying.

“Please, do not defy the will of Heaven.”

Gaye knew those words would only provoke and anger him further, but she believed conveying them was her final calling.

I should have said this long ago. I am sorry I say it only now.

“A Divine Maiden who cannot even receive prophecies dares.”

Originally, those words would have inflicted the deepest wound on her.

Yet at that moment, Gaye felt that they no longer wounded her.

The moment he saw her peaceful face, rage surged up within the Dark Palace Lord.

It was an expression he had not seen even once over all the years that had passed. Thinking that Geom Mugeuk, whom she had met only recently, had created that serenity in her, a murderous intent beyond rage welled up in him.

The Dark Palace Lord said coldly to her,

“From the very beginning, I never believed in anything like Heaven’s Will.”

Shwaaaak.

The palm force launched by the Dark Palace Lord flew toward her.

Gaye closed her eyes.

In her heart, she was standing on the deck, looking at the sky. A massive storm was bearing down on her boat, but she looked up at the sky until the very end.

‘I am sorry I could not properly uphold your will.’

Bang.

Her body flew backward.

She naturally thought she would have died in a single strike. Yet she realized she had not died.

When she opened her eyes, someone behind her was supporting her body. She had not been struck by the palm force and flung back. Someone’s palm force had shattered the Dark Palace Lord’s, and she had been pushed back and sent flying by the aftermath of that collision.

Gaye turned in surprise and saw the Drunken Demon standing behind her. Beside him appeared the Demon Buddha, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable, and the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord. They had arrived just in time.

The boat had not capsized in the storm. She stood on the deck and looked up at the sky.

‘Is there still something I must do?’

Gaye looked at the Drunken Demon.

She found it strange that he had saved her. The Young Cult Leader had said he would spare her, but to the Demon Venerables, she was nothing but an enemy. Was it because of the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord?

“Why did you save me?”

Reeking of alcohol, the Drunken Demon answered,

“If you go around with a drunkard, you become a drunkard. If you go around with someone who resents the world, you become someone who resents the world. If you go around with someone who loves books, you become someone who loves books. And if you go around with someone like our Young Cult Leader, then for starters, when you see someone in danger, you save them first.”

With drunken eyes, the Drunken Demon looked around the scene and asked,

“Where is our Young Cult Leader?”

Chapter 806: Why Did You Bring Me Back?
---------------------------------------
Chwima turned Gaye around.

The reason he had sent out a palm strike to save her had not been for her sake.

It was because of the Lord of Cheonhwaru.

Earlier, in the fight with Heukno, the Lord of Cheonhwaru had tried to spare her. Chwima believed there must surely be a reason worth sparing her for.

And the fact that the Dark Palace Lord was trying to kill her meant that the relationship between the two of them had completely fallen apart. For now, he thought they should keep her alive and make use of her.

To the Demon Venerables, Gaye was the Dark Palace Lord’s hands and feet—nothing more, nothing less.

“Where is the Young Cult Leader?”

His words were polite, but Chwima’s gaze was intense.

Meanwhile, Demon Buddha and the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable were staring at the Dark Palace Lord. Even though they had appeared, he was looking at them with arrogant eyes.

Gaye answered obediently. He was the person who had saved her life, and this was a matter related to the Young Cult Leader. The boat she had boarded had not sunk in the typhoon and was still floating on the sea.

“The Young Cult Leader was sucked into that secret coffer.”

Everyone’s gaze turned toward the secret coffer.

Geom Mugeuk had been sucked into that iron box? It was incomprehensible.

“That secret coffer is the sacred object of the Heavenly Will Palace. The moment the Young Cult Leader touched it, he was absorbed inside.”

The Demon Venerables did not believe her, but the Lord of Cheonhwaru did.

“I don’t think the Divine Maiden Palace Lord is lying.”

At the Lord of Cheonhwaru’s trust, Demon Buddha asked Gaye again.

“Then did that man use an illusion art to drag him inside?”

“No.”

“No? What on earth do you mean?”

“I mean exactly what I said. That secret coffer absorbed the Young Cult Leader.”

Demon Buddha sensed that her words were not lies, but to confirm once more, he looked at the Lord of Cheonhwaru.

At his gaze, asking whether this truly seemed to be the truth, the Lord of Cheonhwaru silently nodded.

Watching this, Chwima laughed loudly.

“At any rate, he’s first-rate at startling people. Now he’s gone and gotten himself sucked into a place like that.”

Though he was speaking nonsense, Chwima’s gaze as he looked at the secret coffer was serious. In other words, it meant they could not know whether the Young Cult Leader was alive or dead.

“I don’t know who’s sitting inside there, but they’re going to regret calling him in for nothing.”

As he said that, Chwima exchanged glances with the two Demon Venerables. In a situation where the Young Cult Leader’s life or death was unclear, they had to kill this Dark Palace Lord themselves.

On top of that, they might have to fight while protecting not only the Lord of Cheonhwaru, but that secret coffer as well.

Considering the enemies they had encountered on the way here, dealing with this Dark Palace Lord would absolutely not be easy.

Indeed, even facing three Demon Venerables, the Dark Palace Lord was not intimidated in the slightest. He acted as if he were confident he could kill them all.

The aura of darkness flowing from his body began to seize the space.

It was clearly a darkness with a will of its own. The darkness flowed along the floor and walls, swallowing the light in that space little by little.

The Dark Palace Lord opened his mouth for the first time.

“Even if he comes out of there safely, the Young Cult Leader will see it. The deaths of everyone he likes.”

It was a declaration that he would kill everyone here.

Chwima was not the sort to let such words pass.

“If you’re going to kill everyone he likes…… you’ll have to kill a truly great many people. Our Young Cult Leader likes people so much that there are people he likes everywhere.”

The Dark Palace Lord looked at Chwima and spoke coldly.

“I’ll kill you first.”

At the Dark Palace Lord’s words, Chwima waved his hands.

“Not me! The Young Cult Leader likes everyone except me. He always comes to find me last.”

Demon Buddha also joined in Chwima’s stubborn sulking.

“That may not be much different for me.”

“Ah, that’s true. Our Demon Buddha only liked the Eldest Young Master. How is it that, even when we gather, it’s all cold rice like us?”

Chwima looked at the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable.

“The Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable is in trouble. Since you’re the youngest Demon Venerable, our Young Cult Leader cherishes you tremendously.”

Though he was telling her to die first, a faint smile formed at the corners of the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable’s mouth.

Their composure did not excite the Dark Palace Lord at all.

“Good. Then I’ll rephrase that. Not the people he likes, but the death of everyone the Young Cult Leader knows.”

As soon as those words ended, the surrounding space was instantly submerged in darkness.

To be precise, it was not darkness, but night.

Twinkle.

Far away, a single star brightened, giving off light.

Starting with that, stars began to shine in every direction. Like the countless stars of the night sky, stars began to fill the space around them as well. There were large stars, and there were small stars. Far in the distance, the Milky Way flowed.

The sight was truly enchanting and mysterious. They really were floating in a night sky full of stars.

It was the moment the Dark Palace Lord’s unique martial art, the Dark Heaven Star Soul Art, was unleashed.

The Dark Palace Lord stood before them, yet it created the illusion that he was very close, and, in another sense, that he was truly far away.

The three Demon Venerables instinctively sensed that this demonic art was anything but ordinary.

Looking around at the surrounding stars, Chwima said,

“I’ve drunk liquor in all sorts of places. On rooftops, in snowy mountains, floating on water, on the road. But there’s just one place I haven’t drunk—in the sky.”

Chwima looked at the stars floating around them and was moved.

“My lifelong dream was to drink liquor in the sky, and you’re making it come true!”

It was at that very moment.

Among the countless stars floating in every direction, one fell toward Chwima.

Shiiiiiiiiii.

The star flew like a comet. When it had been far away, it had been as small as a dot, but when it flew in front of Chwima, it was the size of an adult’s head.

Chwima drew up his internal energy and thrust out a palm force toward the incoming light.

Kwaaaang!

From far away it had been a star, but up close, it was a powerful dark qi force.

From the impact, Chwima was pushed several steps back. Only a single star had flown at him, yet the qi force, infused with the power of the Greedy Heart Demon-Extinguishing Art, was incomparably powerful.

The stars filling the darkness felt utterly threatening, so Demon Buddha and the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable also drew up their internal energy and prepared to fight in earnest.

Chwima finished the liquor he had saved to give Geom Mugeuk and said as if wronged,

“If I go around saying I got hit by a star, they’ll say I’m drunkenly talking nonsense, won’t they? Ah! Is this, too, the fate of a drunkard?”

Chwima was not overwhelmed in momentum at all.

And when it came to momentum on a battlefield, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable was no ordinary figure either.

She opened her mouth for the first time.

“You said that when the Young Cult Leader comes out, you’ll make him see everyone he knows dead?”

From her two eyes flowed a dizzying killing intent that made the world spin.

“Aren’t you included among those people he knows?”

* * *

When Geom Mugeuk came to his senses, he was standing in an unfamiliar place.

As he looked around, Geom Mugeuk flinched in surprise.

‘This place?’

In an empty space, a large secret coffer had been placed. Since he had come here before, Geom Mugeuk knew where this was.

This was the mountainside of Baegyang Peak, where the duel between the Heavenly Will Palace Lord and the Martial Emperor Divine Sword had taken place three hundred years ago.

The very place where the large secret coffer had been hidden within the Dark Palace’s maze formation!

Geom Mugeuk’s heart pounded.

‘I’ve returned to three hundred years ago again.’

The secret coffer had brought him back to that time and place once more.

Geom Mugeuk first examined the condition of his body.

As when he had been brought here before, all he had was a faint amount of internal energy. Just enough internal energy to use the Space-Time Transposition Art, as he had then.

Geom Mugeuk approached the secret coffer placed in the center of that place.

“Why did you bring me back again?”

Of course, the secret coffer gave no response.

To what point in time had it brought him? Was it after the fight between the Martial Emperor Divine Sword and the Heavenly Will Palace Lord had ended? In any case, the result of that fight had already been decided.

Just as he was about to go outside and assess the situation, he heard signs of someone entering the place.

Geom Mugeuk activated the Space-Time Transposition Art and hid himself.

Someone was hurriedly entering the place while carrying a person on his back.

“Please endure just a little longer!”

It was Baek Yanggi, the Guardian Palace Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace. The person he was carrying on his back was Wi Mucheon, the Heavenly Will Palace Lord. The duel at Baegyang Peak had already ended.

The Guardian Palace Lord carefully seated Wi Mucheon against the secret coffer.

“Are you all right?”

Wi Mucheon’s face was pale. As if feeling extreme cold, his entire body was trembling violently.

“Please remove the protective armor first.”

The Guardian Palace Lord took off Wi Mucheon’s outer robe. Beneath it, he was wearing the Ice Dragon Divine Armor.

The last time he had come here, Geom Mugeuk had seen it. The Dark Palace Lord had given the Ice Dragon Divine Armor and the Heavenly Yin Pill, which he had received from a masked man of the Demonic Cult, to the Heavenly Will Palace Lord as a gift.

It had been given because the Martial Emperor Divine Sword possessed martial arts of extreme yang.

But judging from Wi Mucheon’s current state, he had suffered an attack of extreme yin. The Heavenly Yin Pill and the Ice Dragon Divine Armor had instead worsened his injury even further.

“The Dark Palace Lord betrayed us!”

The Guardian Palace Lord regretted that he had failed to realize it in time.

On the day Wi Mucheon had received those gifts, he had been there too.

He had suspected something was strange, but because Wi Mucheon trusted the Dark Palace Lord, he had not been able to insist until the end. At the time, the Heavenly Will Palace Lord had accepted this fight as his destiny.

When the Guardian Palace Lord removed the Ice Dragon Divine Armor from his body, something rolled out from his breast.

What rolled across the floor was a small secret coffer.

Even with his severe injuries, Wi Mucheon reached out with difficulty, trying to take it.

“Give me that.”

“We must treat your wounds first.”

“Give it to me.”

The Guardian Palace Lord picked up the secret coffer that had fallen to the floor and gave it to Wi Mucheon.

Wi Mucheon gripped the secret coffer with both hands.

“……If it was going to be like this, why did you come into the world? If you were never going to grant me strength in the end, why?”

Woooong.

The secret coffer rang.

Wi Mucheon’s gaze grew intense.

“After calling me like this…… Kueeek.”

Wi Mucheon poured out blood.

“Palace Lord!”

Watching this, Geom Mugeuk could sense it. The secret coffer had reacted to him.

After seating him upright, the Guardian Palace Lord poured internal energy into his back.

Wooooong.

Though he carefully injected his internal energy, Wi Mucheon received a great shock.

Wi Mucheon vomited blood again. His current physical state was, quite literally, on the verge of collapse.

“I will try again.”

Wi Mucheon refused his treatment and turned to sit facing him.

“……The Divine Maiden was right. The Will of Heaven was not with me.”

The Guardian Palace Lord bowed his head. He knew that Wi Mucheon’s condition was already irreversible.

“Everything is my responsibility.”

He should have stopped him. From the beginning, he should have stopped him from starting the war. That had been the true duty of the Guardian Palace Lord.

If the Dark Palace Lord harbored other intentions, discovering that, too, had been his duty.

“Palace Lord Baek, listen carefully to what I say from now on.”

“Yes, Palace Lord.”

Wi Mucheon stared at the Guardian Palace Lord, who was filled with loyalty, then held out the bloodstained secret coffer in his hand.

“Take this secret coffer and escape this place.”

When the Guardian Palace Lord was about to say something, Wi Mucheon raised his hand and stopped him.

“Three hundred years from now, you must fulfill the Will of Heaven.”

The Guardian Palace Lord did not accept the secret coffer.

“I cannot do that. I am the Guardian Palace Lord. Protecting you, Palace Lord, is the calling and mission heaven bestowed upon me.”

Wi Mucheon shook his head.

“It is my final request.”

At that earnest gaze, the Guardian Palace Lord’s face trembled.

The Guardian Palace Lord knelt and respectfully accepted the secret coffer.

It was the moment he received the sacred object of the Heavenly Will Palace.

Even if he could leave with this, would he be able to inherit the Will of Heaven? And would that choice truly be right?

“Palace Lord Baek! There is no time.”

Since he could no longer refuse, the Guardian Palace Lord was just about to nod when a thunderous sound came from outside.

Bang!

It was the sound of the maze formation installed outside being destroyed. There was only one reason the Dark Palace’s formation could be destroyed so easily. Someone had told the enemies the method to break it.

Soon after, three people entered the place.

The person standing in the center was the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.

The two people who entered with him possessed presences no less formidable than the Martial Emperor Divine Sword. Though they entered side by side, they kept a certain distance from each other, as if wary of one another.

The Guardian Palace Lord stood in front of Wi Mucheon, blocking the way.

‘This is the Dark Palace Lord’s doing.’

That traitor had told them how to break the maze formation, allowing them to walk all the way in here. The Guardian Palace Lord wanted to kill the Dark Palace Lord even more than he wanted to kill them.

Wi Mucheon spoke from behind him.

“……Move aside.”

The Guardian Palace Lord slowly stepped aside.

The first place Wi Mucheon’s gaze turned was toward the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.

“……How could you do this?”

The attack of extreme yin he had suffered had not come from the Martial Emperor Divine Sword. It had come from one of the two people who entered with him.

The duel at Baegyang Peak had been a meticulously prepared trap.

As if he had no intention of making excuses, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword said nothing, his face sunken.

Wi Mucheon’s gaze turned toward the man to the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s left.

Wearing a robe as deep as blood, he radiated a presence more intense than anyone else in this place. The mere sight of him made it hard to breathe. His identity was none other than—

“……Demonic Cult Leader, to think you would interfere in this fight!”

Amazingly, one of the two was Hyeok Docheon, the cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult three hundred years ago.

The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult of three hundred years ago was different from the present. It was the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult that was, quite literally, terror itself.

The one who led that Heavenly Demon Divine Cult was this very Hyeok Docheon. He was more cruel and ruthless than any cult leader of the previous generations. Thus, the martial artists of the world did not dare speak his name aloud and trembled in fear.

Geom Mugeuk was shocked as he looked at the Heavenly Demon of three hundred years ago. He had known the Demonic Cult was involved, but he had never dreamed he would see him here.

‘That man is the Heavenly Demon of this era.’

His impression was intense. Though no wind blew, his hair rose in all directions as if it were whipping about, and from his two eyes flowed an intense demonic energy that seemed to contain hell itself.

This time, Wi Mucheon’s gaze turned toward the man on the opposite side.

He had neatly arranged pure white hair, and eyes in which loneliness and superiority could be felt at the same time. His presence was no ordinary thing either.

“……And as if that weren’t enough, you even dragged in the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path.”

Amazingly, he was none other than the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path.

Geom Mugeuk was as shocked by his identity as he had been when he saw the Demonic Cult Leader. No, he was even more shocked. Because he was someone Geom Mugeuk himself had met before.

The Alliance Leader of the Evil Path, Ju Gashin.

He was the owner of the coffin from which Geom Mugeuk had obtained the final bead, Jajeong, in the underground tomb—the man he had met as a white skeleton now stood before his eyes.

Geom Mugeuk had read Ju Gashin’s deeds on the pillar in that underground tomb.

—Together with the Martial Emperor Divine Sword, he destroyed the Heavenly Will Palace.

The Alliance Leader of the Evil Path, Ju Gashin, had truly killed the Heavenly Will Palace Lord together with the Martial Emperor Divine Sword. The Demonic Cult Leader had not been mentioned in that record, yet the three of them were together.

Within the Space-Time Transposition Art, Geom Mugeuk watched the three leaders of demonic, orthodox, and evil with a deep gaze.

Just as the three leaders would gather in one place three hundred years later, three hundred years ago, the three had gathered like this.

‘So all of you were gathered on this day.’

Chapter 807: I Will Take All the Secret Coffers
-----------------------------------------------
Cheonui Palace Lord Wi Mucheon glared at the Muhwang Divine Sword with eyes full of resentment. He could not understand it. For a man who received the respect of the orthodox sects to bring in the Demonic Cult and the Heretical Alliance.

“Why in the world did you do this?”

At last, the Muhwang Divine Sword spoke for the first time.

“Too many people have died because of you. Should we not end the war now?”

“Nonsense!”

Wi Mucheon shouted, then coughed up blood again. On the boundary between life and death, he clenched his teeth and cried out toward life.

“Are you not ashamed?”

His voice rang out as though he were perfectly unharmed.

The eyes of the Guardian Palace Lord beside him were filled with sorrow. The Cheonui Palace Lord was spending what little life force he had left on anger.

By contrast, the Muhwang Divine Sword was calm.

“In the end, the one who called these people here was not me, but you.”

The Muhwang Divine Sword had not known the war against them would drag on this long. Nor had he known that the mysterious organization called Cheonui Palace was this powerful.

“All of this happened because you dreamed a dream you should never have dreamed.”

“Even so, you brought in the Demonic Cult? At the very least, I thought you were a man who knew honor.”

Then astonishing words flowed from the Muhwang Divine Sword.

“I did not bring them in.”

“!”

The Muhwang Divine Sword’s gaze turned toward the Demonic Cult Leader, Hyeok Docheon.

“That man contacted me first.”

Wi Mucheon looked at Hyeok Docheon in shock. The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had contacted the Murim Alliance first? Why on earth?

At last, Hyeok Docheon opened his mouth. One might have expected it to be deep and low, but contrary to his appearance, his voice was sharp and ringing. From that voice alone, one could tell he had lived without hiding his emotions.

“They say a mysterious power capable of gaining the world is hidden within Cheonui Palace’s sacred relic.”

The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, having learned that information, had intervened in this matter first.

When Geom Mugeuk had come here before, he had already confirmed that the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult knew of the secret coffer’s existence.

‘They intervened because of that sacred relic.’

There was no way he had stepped in for the sake of the Murim Alliance.

But doubts arose as well.

‘There’s no way the Demonic Cult Leader believed some legendary tale.’

At that moment, one person came to mind.

‘The Dark Palace Lord!’

He must have packaged Cheonui Palace’s sacred relic beautifully as a legend and leaked it to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. He must have put in a great deal of care over a long time, eventually making Hyeok Docheon believe it.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Muhwang Divine Sword.

When he had come last time, they had eaten together, and he had spoken with him as well. He had been mentally exhausted, but he had not seemed like a man who would move according to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s wishes.

The final place Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned was toward Ju Gasin, Leader of the Heretical Alliance.

‘The answer must lie with that man.’

The Muhwang Divine Sword again emphasized that the one who had created today’s situation was Wi Mucheon.

“You called them all here. So stop blaming others.”

Originally, the Muhwang Divine Sword was not a man who shifted responsibility onto others, but he decided to make an exception this time. Otherwise, he would not be able to endure it. Each time he closed his eyes, he saw the figures of his subordinates who had died on the battlefield.

Of course, Wi Mucheon did not accept it meekly.

“You blame someone else while telling me not to?”

For the dying man, his disappointment in the Muhwang Divine Sword turned into fury. His eyes blazed, but all he could do now was glare like this.

Hyeok Docheon declared to everyone.

“We will be taking the secret coffer.”

Hyeok Docheon looked at the Guardian Palace Lord. The small secret coffer was in his hands.

“Bring it here.”

The Guardian Palace Lord knew. Whether he handed over the secret coffer or not, he would die either way. They would never allow what had happened here today to leak outside.

The Guardian Palace Lord looked at Wi Mucheon. With eyes losing their vitality, Wi Mucheon said nothing. Having served him for so long, he could understand the emotion contained in that gaze.

Do as you wish.

The Guardian Palace Lord gripped the secret coffer with both hands and held it forward.

“If you do not withdraw, I will destroy the secret coffer.”

A moment of silence flowed in an instant.

A cold smile formed on Hyeok Docheon’s lips.

“If it breaks so easily just because you destroy it, then it is not worth possessing.”

Hyeok Docheon said coldly.

“Break it.”

Threats did not work on him.

Of course, the Guardian Palace Lord was no easy man either. He lowered his eyes to the secret coffer.

Then he truly injected his internal energy into it, intending to destroy it. All of Cheonui Palace’s tragedies today had begun with this secret coffer.

‘I will bear this sin. Forgive me.’

He sincerely apologized to both the Cheonui Palace Lord and the secret coffer.

For an instant, Hyeok Docheon twitched, but he only watched. If it truly was something that could be smashed like that, then the information itself was wrong.

But no matter how much internal energy the Guardian Palace Lord poured into it, the secret coffer was not destroyed.

Changing methods, the Guardian Palace Lord raised his hand high, about to strike it down.

Shiiik.

Slice.

A streak of sword energy passed through the Guardian Palace Lord’s arm.

Thud.

His arm was severed and fell to the floor.

Papapapapap!

Blood sprayed from the cut. His blood poured down onto the secret coffer he had dropped to the ground.

Before anyone knew it, Hyeok Docheon’s sword had been drawn.

“That is enough.”

He had sent out sword energy in an instant and cut off the Guardian Palace Lord’s arm. Even if the Guardian Palace Lord had been focused on the secret coffer, he was not someone who should have been struck down so easily. That was how extraordinary Hyeok Docheon’s martial arts were.

The Guardian Palace Lord pressed his acupoints to stop the bleeding from his arm. Wi Mucheon’s face turned even paler as he watched.

Wi Mucheon stretched out his arm and picked up the secret coffer that had fallen to the floor. The secret coffer, already stained with his own blood, was now covered in the Guardian Palace Lord’s blood as well, making it look like a red box. Wi Mucheon embraced it.

Geom Mugeuk silently watched the scene.

First, he felt sorrow at the Guardian Palace Lord’s loyalty. It was the tragedy of one who served the wrong master. He was in no position to help, and even if he could, he could not carelessly change the history of the past.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the secret coffer, from which blood dripped steadily.

It was a secret coffer he had always treated like a friend, one on which he had even drawn a smiling face, so its current appearance felt far too unfamiliar.

Whenever he opened the beach within the Spacetime Transference Art, he had always placed the secret coffer beside him.

Thinking about it now, that smiling friend had been a secret coffer that had gone through something like this. It struck him that it was like a martial artist who had returned to his hometown from the battlefield.

Looking down at the secret coffer, Wi Mucheon asked.

“Did he want my position?”

At the calm question, Hyeok Docheon nodded and answered.

“He wanted your position. In exchange for offering up your lives, he demanded that we no longer pursue Cheonui Palace.”

The Guardian Palace Lord’s eyes, as he listened, turned red. They were bloodshot eyes, as though tears of blood might flow at any moment. The only thought in his mind was that he had to kill the Dark Palace Lord before he died.

Wi Mucheon, on the other hand, was tranquil. Rather, an inscrutable smile formed on his lips.

It looked like the hollow smile of a man facing death, but Geom Mugeuk felt a strange exhilaration dwelling in that smile.

And Geom Mugeuk could tell what that exhilaration was.

The Dark Palace Lord he had seen with his own eyes was an evil man, and a greatly ambitious one.

‘He wanted the position of Cheonui Palace Lord, but he would give up the secret coffer?’

Would he really try to seize a Cheonui Palace without its sacred relic? That would be the same as obtaining a Cheonui Palace with its core missing.

Wi Mucheon was now thinking the same thing he was. That this was not the end.

‘Dark Palace Lord, what on earth is the arrangement you have made?’

He had a feeling there was surely something more than a simple betrayal.

Hyeok Docheon said to the Muhwang Divine Sword,

“What you want must be that man, so take him and go.”

Ju Gasin, the Leader of the Heretical Alliance, who had silently watched the situation until then, spoke for the first time.

“I will take one of the secret coffers. Choose first.”

As if he had known it would come to this, a sneer formed on Hyeok Docheon’s lips.

“I knew why you brought in the Heretical Alliance. You brought them in to prevent me from monopolizing the secret coffers, didn’t you?”

The Muhwang Divine Sword had not told Hyeok Docheon in advance that Ju Gasin would come here today.

So when Hyeok Docheon saw Ju Gasin appear on Baegyang Peak, he knew the Muhwang Divine Sword had played a trick, but for the time being, he showed no reaction. Yes, the Muhwang Divine Sword was not a man who would simply be led around.

But the reason Ju Gasin had come was different from what he expected.

“You misunderstand. I never contacted the Heretical Alliance first.”

In other words, the Leader of the Heretical Alliance had also intervened on his own.

At that moment, Wi Mucheon let out a low laugh.

“……Now I know for certain.”

It was the moment his premonition turned into conviction.

“The Dark Palace Lord drew all of you into one place.”

He had leaked to both the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Heretical Alliance that the secret coffers were treasures of the ages.

Then Hyeok Docheon spoke loudly.

“So what if it is a trap? I need only smash everything apart.”

He was confident that no trap could contain him.

“I will take both the large and the small secret coffers.”

A chilling demonic energy whirled around his body.

But Ju Gasin was not afraid at all.

“You will have to concede one.”

There was another reason he could be so confident before the Demonic Cult Leader.

Ju Gasin took one step toward the Murim Alliance Leader. Hyeok Docheon immediately understood what that symbolic step meant.

“You two will join forces?”

Even in a situation where he should have erupted in rage, he remained calm. He had expected this from the moment the Leader of the Heretical Alliance appeared.

“What will you do if I come to take it later?”

It was a threat that he would strike the Heretical Alliance to obtain the small secret coffer, but it did not work on Ju Gasin.

“Better than letting you come after gaining the secret coffer’s power.”

He was saying that if Hyeok Docheon obtained power from the secret coffer, he would invade anyway. At the very least, Ju Gasin firmly believed that some kind of power was contained within the secret coffer.

When Hyeok Docheon looked at him and smiled, Ju Gasin smiled as well.

If Hyeok Docheon’s smile was one of intense will that said, I will definitely kill you, then Ju Gasin’s was a smile filled with madness that said, Then let’s die together, you bastard.

Hyeok Docheon wanted, if his heart had its way, to kill them all, but facing those two at the same time would be a battle in which even he would have to stake his life.

He was an incomparably cruel and terrifying figure, but he was not reckless or foolish enough to wage a life-and-death battle against the Murim Alliance Leader and the Heretical Alliance Leader in a place that might be a trap.

Between the two men, amid the taut tension passing back and forth, the Muhwang Divine Sword said nothing. One war had ended today, but he sensed that a new war had begun.

His gaze turned toward Wi Mucheon.

The Guardian Palace Lord had withdrawn to one side and was struggling to save Wi Mucheon, whose life was fading away, by pouring internal energy into him with his one remaining hand. Thanks to his efforts, Wi Mucheon was still alive.

‘Even after this, are you telling me not to blame you?’

Meanwhile, Hyeok Docheon decided he would take only one. The one he chose was the large secret coffer.

“If I am going to take one, the larger would be better, would it not?”

He walked toward the large secret coffer. His back was turned to the two alliance leaders, but he did not care at all. He was as relaxed as a tiger walking through its own territory.

How many steps had he taken?

Wooooong.

The large secret coffer, which had been quiet, began to vibrate. It was a heavy vibration that rang out from deep within the secret coffer.

In that instant, everyone there was shocked.

‘The secret coffer is reacting to the Demonic Cult Leader?’

Thinking that the secret coffer recognized him, Hyeok Docheon’s steps grew firmer.

Woooooooong.

The closer he drew, the louder the secret coffer vibrated. It was not simply vibrating; it felt as if it were speaking to him.

Saaaaaaa.

That was not all. A faint light flowed from the secret coffer, and a mysterious profound energy began to emerge. It was a wondrous aura that did not exist in the human world.

Joy flashed across Hyeok Docheon’s face.

If he obtained power capable of possessing the world? This murim would become his.

The dream of unifying the murim, hidden deep within his heart, began to stir.

But Geom Mugeuk instinctively knew.

‘This is the Dark Palace Lord’s arrangement!’

After all, in the history of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, there was no record of Hyeok Docheon having obtained great power. Nor had he left any special achievements in the history of murim.

‘But if that is so, how did the Dark Palace Lord know the secret coffer would react to the Demonic Cult Leader?’

The closer Hyeok Docheon drew, the louder the secret coffer’s resonance became, and the energy flowing from it grew stronger as well.

Standing before the secret coffer, Hyeok Docheon spoke boldly.

“From now on, I am your master.”

Then he slowly placed his palm on the secret coffer.

He had expected some greater reaction, but instead, nothing happened. The resonance vanished, and no profound energy flowed out.

Just as Hyeok Docheon, feeling somewhat deflated, was about to take his hand away from the secret coffer, he started in surprise.

His palm would not come off the coffer. The coffer had begun pulling him in with tremendous force.

“What the hell?”

It was not only his hand being pulled.

He braced himself with his other hand and resisted as his body tried to be dragged toward the coffer.

At the ominous premonition, a curse burst from his mouth on its own.

“Fuck!”

The secret coffer was pulling him in with incredible power. It was a kind of force he had never experienced before. A power he had never felt in the human world.

Hyeok Docheon lifted the hand he had been bracing himself with and struck the coffer with his fist.

Bang! Kwaang!

Even under the tremendous impact, the secret coffer did not budge, as if it had taken root in the floor.

Thud.

Rather, because he had taken one hand away and scattered his internal energy, Hyeok Docheon could not withstand the pulling force and slammed his face into the coffer.

It was a situation in which he, as the Heavenly Demon, ought to have felt humiliated.

All the more so because those watching were none other than the Murim Alliance Leader and the Sado Alliance Leader.

But he did not even have the leisure to feel shame.

It was not simply because the suction force was powerful. It was because he instinctively sensed that this power was different.

Like a waterfall falling from above to below, like water flowing from a river into the sea, it sucked him in as if it were an irresistible law of nature.

It was not a power a human could defy.

Even so, Hyeok Docheon drew up his internal energy to the extreme and endured.

“You goddamn bastard thing! Guaaaaaaaaaah!”

With his face crushed against the coffer, Hyeok Docheon forced his head to turn and looked back.

The Muhwang Divine Sword and Ju Gasin, startled by this sudden turn of events, were only staring this way.

Wi Mucheon and the Guardian Palace Lord were the same. They had revered the secret coffer as a divine object all their lives, but this was the first time they had ever seen it pull in a person.

At first, Geom Mugeuk’s blood had run cold. He feared it might send that Hyeok Docheon three hundred years into the future.

But that did not seem to be the case. When it had absorbed him, it had drawn him in as if sinking him into a swamp, but right now the surface of the coffer was hard.

At that very moment!

Geom Mugeuk saw it. A single existence that, for a fleeting instant, appeared superimposed over Hyeok Docheon’s body. Something was wavering as it slipped out of him.

And Geom Mugeuk knew better than anyone here what it was.

‘The Heavenly Demon Soul!’

Geom Mugeuk realized it. What the secret coffer was trying to suck in was not Hyeok Docheon.

‘The secret coffer is absorbing the Heavenly Demon Soul!’

Chapter 808: Do You Think Heaven Is on Your Side?
-------------------------------------------------
'It isn't pulling me in.'

Hyeok Docheon belatedly realized that the Bigwe was sucking in the Heavenly Demon Soul dwelling within his body.

When he resisted with all his might to keep it from being taken, the Bigwe pulled back with even greater force.

It would have been better if it had sucked him in instead. Forcibly tearing the Heavenly Demon Soul from his body and soul brought him horrific agony.

The blood qi that Hyeok Docheon had been forcibly holding back reversed its flow.

"Kuehk."

Blood spewed from his mouth. The gushing blood flowed along the Bigwe. Having absorbed the blood, the Bigwe vibrated even more intensely.

Geom Mugeuk could tell from the reactions of the others.

That the Heavenly Demon Soul superimposed over Hyeok Docheon's body was visible only to his eyes.

What he saw now was not the Heavenly Demon Soul made manifest through martial arts, but the spirit dwelling within Hyeok Docheon's soul.

That was why, to everyone else, it still looked like Hyeok Docheon was being sucked into the Bigwe.

The Muhwang Divine Sword shouted to Ju Ga-sin.

"The Demonic Cult Leader must not die!"

Honestly, the Muhwang Divine Sword had thought this:

'This is the perfect opportunity to kill the Demonic Cult Leader!'

But that was an emotional, momentary desire he must not harbor.

'He cannot die!'

If the Demonic Cult Leader died here, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would misunderstand that he had perished in a trap set by him. The result of that would be only one thing.

The Great Righteous-Demonic War.

Without the Demonic Cult Leader, how could they possibly defeat the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult with ease? Furthermore, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would gain the powerful justification of avenging their leader.

A war against demonic madmen rampaging with vengeance? It was something that must never come to pass. Even if they ultimately won, it would be a hollow victory bought with countless lives.

Too many had already died in this war. As the Alliance Leader, he could barely endure the guilt as it was. Another war must not happen.

There was another reason he had to save the Demonic Cult Leader.

Vice Alliance Leader Ju Ga-sin, standing right beside him!

Because he could never know how that inscrutable man would act in a situation where the Demonic Cult Leader was dead.

Having made up his mind, the Muhwang Divine Sword moved first.

"I will aid him."

The Muhwang Divine Sword threw himself toward Hyeok Docheon. To think that a day would come in his life when he would rescue the Demonic Cult Leader.

"I am going to help you, do not refuse!"

The Muhwang Divine Sword raised his internal energy and grabbed Hyeok Docheon's body.

Just as he grabbed him and tried to pull him away, the Muhwang Divine Sword saw it.

Hyeok Docheon, pinned beneath the Bigwe and looking this way—his pupils dilating in an instant.

Swhaaaaak!

Boom!

A palm strike flying from behind slammed into the Muhwang Divine Sword's back. Shockingly, it was an ambush carried out by Ju Ga-sin.

"Keuk."

The blood the Muhwang Divine Sword coughed up splattered onto Hyeok Docheon's back and the Bigwe.

Despite having sustained internal injuries, the Muhwang Divine Sword turned like lightning and swung his sword. He raised his body-protecting barrier at the last moment and avoided a fatal wound.

Chae-aeng!

The Muhwang Divine Sword barely knocked aside the sword flying toward his heart.

In truth, the Muhwang Divine Sword had suspected that Ju Ga-sin might perhaps attack him.

But he had not expected such a swift and decisive attack. To think that he would instinctively make a decision that would change the fate of so many others—not to mention himself?

Kwang!

Their two swords clashed in midair, erupting with explosive force.

"You!"

The enraged Muhwang Divine Sword glared at Ju Ga-sin beyond their crossed blades. Seeing the clear killing intent in his opponent's eyes, no further words were needed.

Ju Ga-sin judged that this moment was a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity to kill both the Demonic Cult Leader and the Martial Alliance Leader.

Now that he had drawn his sword, there was no turning back.

Their two blades flashed back and forth like rays of light.

Though internally injured by Ju Ga-sin's surprise attack, the Muhwang Divine Sword was not pushed back in the clash. It was a fight where yielding even a single step meant death. He swallowed the blood welling up in his throat.

Their swords were so fast that the next afterimage was left before the previous one vanished. It was as if the two were fighting with dozens of arms wielding dozens of swords.

The Muhwang Divine Sword's unique martial art was the Guangming Wuyang Sword Technique, based on extreme yang internal energy, while Ju Ga-sin's was the Tiandi Yinhan Sword Technique, based on extreme yin internal energy.

It was truly a battle of extreme yang and extreme yin, of heat and cold. If one side emitted light, the other devoured it.

A cold blizzard raged from Ju Ga-sin's sword, while a heat like the sun blazed from the Muhwang Divine Sword's blade. The world alternated between summer and winter.

Geom Mugeuk watched the battle, enthralled. He could not take his eyes off it.

Truly, one marvelous move after another continued. The swordsmanship from three hundred years ago was different from that of the present. There was learning and enlightenment to be gained from that difference.

While their bloody battle was raging, Hyeok Docheon was also fighting a desperate struggle against the Bigwe.

He tried somehow to protect the Heavenly Demon Soul, but it was a force that no human could stop.

In the end, unable to endure any longer, the Heavenly Demon Soul was torn from Hyeok Docheon's body.

"Uwaaaaaaaaah!"

The vivid agony of his soul being ripped away coursed through him.

Geom Mugeuk saw the Heavenly Demon Soul being absorbed into the Bigwe. The sight of such a powerful Heavenly Demon Soul being sucked in so helplessly was shocking. Would it have been sucked in even if it had been manifested on a massive scale? It was impossible to know.

Having absorbed the Heavenly Demon Soul, the Bigwe no longer pulled at Hyeok Docheon.

Watching the scene descend into chaos, the Cheonui Palace Lord laughed even as he vomited blood from his mouth.

'Yes, kill each other!'

Meanwhile, the Suho Palace Lord was embracing the Cheonui Palace Lord's body. This was to prevent the aftereffects of the battle from reaching him.

Boom! Kwaaang!

A stray attack from the fierce battle between the two leaders struck his body. Though he endured with his extreme body-protecting barrier, pain like his bones shattering surged through him.

But he tried to protect the Cheonui Palace Lord until his final breath. Though he knew the Cheonui Palace Lord was on the verge of death, he fulfilled his calling until the very end.

Then came a continuous string of explosions.

Kwang! Kwang! Kwaaang! Kwang!

The sounds were so loud and intense that the Muhwang Divine Sword and Ju Ga-sin stopped fighting and retreated from each other.

Hyeok Docheon, having had his Heavenly Demon Soul stolen, was pounding on the Bigwe with his fists like a madman.

To the eyes of the Muhwang Divine Sword, who could not see the Heavenly Demon Soul, it looked as though Hyeok Docheon had broken free from being pulled in.

"The Vice Alliance Leader has betrayed us. Let's take care of this side first—"

For a moment, the Muhwang Divine Sword could not continue.

Looking this way, blood-red energy was streaming from both of Hyeok Docheon's eyes.

'The Demonic Cult Leader has fallen into qi deviation!'

The Heavenly Demon Soul had been forcibly torn away, leaving him half out of his mind.

Hyeok Docheon stretched his hand toward Ju Ga-sin. He raised his internal energy to the extreme, attempting to execute a single technique.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk knew.

'No!'

What Hyeok Docheon was trying to use without warning was the Heavenly Demon World Destruction. If he used it in this deranged state, not only he himself but everything here would be annihilated.

Flash!

In the next instant, light burst forth.

And fortunately, that light had not come from Hyeok Docheon's hand.

The Bigwe emitted a light so blindingly bright in all directions that one could not open their eyes.

In that moment, everyone's movements stopped as if by a miracle. The same was true for Geom Mugeuk himself. The aftereffects of the light reached even him, who was inside the Spacetime Transference Technique.

Geom Mugeuk could not move his entire body. It was as if the light was saying this:

Just watch.

He could see the figures of people frozen within the light.

Hyeok Docheon with his hand outstretched. The Muhwang Divine Sword trying to say something to him. Ju Ga-sin with an unreadable expression. The smiling Cheonui Palace Lord. And the Suho Palace Lord protecting him with his body.

They were each letting out silent screams of their own.

In the next moment!

Something began to escape from Hyeok Docheon's frozen body.

It was not only him. Something escaped from the bodies of the Muhwang Divine Sword, Ju Ga-sin, and the dying Cheonui Palace Lord as well.

Golden radiance shone along the contours of their bodies, and then other versions of them separated out.

Amazingly, it was their souls.

The souls flailed their hands, trying not to leave their bodies, but like the dead departing from their flesh, they separated from their physical forms.

It was not only their souls. Innate true qi and internal energy radiated with brilliant light from their dantians and separated out together.

Geom Mugeuk was truly astonished. He had experienced all manner of things, but this was the first time he had witnessed such a sight. It was mysterious yet bizarre.

The escaping souls and innate true qi circled around the Bigwe once.

Then, he could see their expressions clearly.

He saw Hyeok Docheon roaring while lost to qi deviation. The astonished Muhwang Divine Sword. Ju Ga-sin, still not revealing his emotions even in this situation. And the Cheonui Palace Lord, smiling radiantly as if he believed they were all going to hell together.

They revealed their final expressions like that, and then...

Shhhuk.

Like being sucked into an abyss, they were instantly absorbed into the Bigwe.

Thud.

The remaining physical bodies all collapsed to the ground.

Geom Mugeuk understood. That they were all dead.

No, strictly speaking, they were not dead. The Bigwe had absorbed them all, leaving only empty shells behind. Their souls, innate true qi, and all the internal energy they possessed.

Now, the only ones left alive here were himself and the Suho Palace Lord.

The Suho Palace Lord looked at Wi Mucheon, whom he had been holding in one arm, with sorrowful eyes.

He had already breathed his last.

"...My Lord."

Fortunately, Wi Mucheon's final expression was one of a smile, but a stream of tears flowed from the Suho Palace Lord's eyes.

"I am sorry I could not protect you."

Even in death, he had wished to die first, yet it was the Cheonui Palace Lord who had died first.

Geom Mugeuk could feel it.

That the Bigwe had not absorbed the Suho Palace Lord was not because his martial arts were inferior to those of the other four.

He felt it was because the Suho Palace Lord was a different kind of person from them. Because he was someone who had lived his whole life protecting others, like a shield.

The Suho Palace Lord looked back. The corpses of the three leaders lay fallen on the ground.

While he stood in a daze with an expression of disbelief.

Clunk.

The Bigwe spat something out.

Rolling across the floor with a rattle, it was a perfectly transparent bead devoid of any color. It was so transparent that what lay behind it was clearly visible.

At the moment when the Suho Palace Lord was struggling to go pick it up.

Hwiiiiiiii.

Black energy rose from his legs and wrapped around his body.

"Ugh."

The dark energy pressed down on him with tremendous force and bound him.

With one arm already severed and internal injuries from protecting the Cheonui Palace Lord with his body-protecting barrier, he could not endure this pressure.

Crack.

As sounds of breaking bones rang out, the Suho Palace Lord vomited a gush of blood.

Sssss.

Along with black smoke, the one revealing himself before him was none other than Hwa Sogang, the Amheuk Palace Lord of this era.

Sreuk.

When the Amheuk Palace Lord stretched out his hand, the transparent bead that had fallen to the floor rose into the air. Joy flooded his face as he looked at it.

"Heaven's will was with me!"

He was so overjoyed that he felt like shouting. Even if he combined all the joy he had experienced since birth, it would not match this moment.

Watching that figure while trapped in darkness, the Suho Palace Lord let out a low groan and spoke.

"...Hwa Sogang."

The Amheuk Palace Lord was utterly absorbed in the transparent bead, even at his call.

A mysterious energy was flowing from the bead. An unprecedented power could be felt from it.

"...Traitor."

When the Suho Palace Lord glared at him with burning eyes, the Amheuk Palace Lord looked at him and asked.

"What about you? Are you a loser?"

The Suho Palace Lord hardened his expression and struggled to break free from the darkness. But the more he did so, the stronger the binding became.

The Amheuk Palace Lord's gaze turned back to the transparent bead.

"I had uncovered the secret of the Bigwe."

He had sought the secret of the Bigwe for a long time and finally uncovered it. He had not informed the Cheonui Palace Lord of that important fact.

"The Bigwe is originally an absolute divine object that absorbs evil spirits and converts them into primordial qi. They didn't even know that and merely enshrined it all these years."

Just by seeing what it had shown moments ago, one could tell those words were true.

The Amheuk Palace Lord's gaze turned to Hyeok Docheon, collapsed on the ground.

"The Heavenly Demon Soul is a great evil spirit among evil spirits, and furthermore, the one wielding that soul, Hyeok Docheon, is a wicked and cruel man by nature. I was certain the Bigwe would absorb it."

He had expected exactly that much. When the Demonic Cult Leader, having had his Heavenly Demon Soul stolen, went on a rampage, he had intended to secretly make off with this bead containing the Heavenly Demon Soul and escape.

That power alone would have been enough.

And yet the secret coffer had given him a gift far greater than that. Not even the Lord of the Dark Palace, who had orchestrated this affair, had known the secret coffer would absorb them all.

“With this power, I will rule the murim forever.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace stared at the bead as if bewitched. Within that transparent bead, he saw the heavens, and he saw the sea. He saw light, and he saw darkness. He saw life and death.

Even as his entire body was being crushed, the Lord of the Guardian Palace struggled not to lose consciousness.

“The heavens will not permit…… such mighty power to one like you.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace gazed at him and asked,

“Do you truly think the heavens are on your side?”

At least in this situation, no such words could come out.

“……The masters of the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions will not leave you be.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace laughed as he said,

“How, when they will not know I killed them? The three leaders of the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions fought over the sacred relic of Cheonui Palace and mutually destroyed one another! Truly a disgraceful affair. What happened today will never even become known to the world. It will become something that never existed in history.”

Craaaack.

The sound of bones being completely crushed came from the Lord of the Guardian Palace’s body.

Geom Mugeuk closed his eyes, unable to bear watching. He must not help, but even if he wished to, he could not.

After seeing the light earlier, Geom Mugeuk still could not move his body at all. The secret coffer was making absolutely certain he could not interfere in this matter.

The Lord of the Guardian Palace spoke with difficulty.

“The heavens may not have been on our side…… but they will not be on yours either.”

With those words, his head dropped limply.

Only then did the darkness surrounding him vanish, and his corpse fell to the floor.

“This affair will not remain in history, but…….”

The Lord of the Dark Palace’s body began to be shrouded in black smoke. He, too, left his final words to the corpse of the Lord of the Guardian Palace.

“I will remain as history.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace disappeared from that place along with the smoke.

A moment later, Geom Mugeuk released the Spacetime Transposition Art and revealed himself. Only after the Lord of the Dark Palace departed had his body begun to move again.

Geom Mugeuk’s expression was graver than ever.

Only now did he understand where Hwa Mugi’s strength had come from.

‘The Heavenly Demon, the Murim Alliance Leader, the Evil Dao Alliance Leader, and on top of that the Lord of Cheonui Palace and the Heavenly Demon Soul. Five souls and even their innate true qi are mingled within him. If I include the soul of Hwa Mugi, the owner of the body that accepted that energy, then a total of six souls dwell within him.’

Now he understood clearly why it had been said that without the special energy known as Six Paths Primordial Qi, Hwa Mugi could not be controlled.

He did not know why the Six Paths Primordial Qi had been scattered and hidden in that manner, but he could tell that the six energies he had gathered were deeply connected to those six souls.

Geom Mugeuk walked over to the corpse of the Lord of the Guardian Palace. He had died without even closing his eyes.

“That Lord of the Dark Palace did not fulfill Heaven’s Will. His name was not recorded in even a single line of history.”

Because that power had been far too strong, he must have failed to achieve his aim. Only in later generations would it come to be known that one needed the Six Paths Primordial Qi in order to handle that power.

“His descendants will not achieve that vain ambition either. So close your eyes and rest in peace.”

Geom Mugeuk closed the Lord of the Guardian Palace’s wide-open eyes.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the small secret coffer lying beside him, soaked in blood. Since it had vanished completely from his own world, this would be the last time he ever saw it.

‘Thank you, my friend.’

After bidding it farewell, Geom Mugeuk strode straight toward the large secret coffer.

“Send me back.”

Until now, he had always been sucked into the secret coffer at completely unexpected moments, but for the first time, he demanded it of his own will.

“To the place where my people are.”

Chapter 809: I’m a Bad Drunk
----------------------------
The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable answered the Lord of the Dark Palace, who had declared that he would kill everyone Geom Mugeuk knew.

“Doesn’t that include you, too?”

As the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable revealed her aura, the Drunken Demon and the Demon Buddha looked at each other.

The two of them could tell. The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable wanted to fight the Lord of the Dark Palace.

The Demon Buddha slowly walked over and stood before the Lady of Cheonhwaru. It meant that he would protect her, so she could fight to her heart’s content.

The Lady of Cheonhwaru moved to stand beside Gaye.

Gaye silently gazed at the Lady of Cheonhwaru. She could tell. Even here, the Lady of Cheonhwaru was trying to save her. Since the Demon Venerables would protect her anyway, if Gaye stayed close like this, she would survive as a bonus.

‘Why are you trying so hard to keep me alive? I lured you into a trap.’

She must have read the emotions in Gaye’s eyes, yet the Lady of Cheonhwaru only looked worriedly at the Lord of the Dark Palace and the Demon Venerables.

The Drunken Demon and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable stood side by side.

“Let’s have ourselves one more proper fight.”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was grateful to the two Demon Venerables. No matter how diligently she had trained, she was young, and compared to the other Demon Venerables, she was relatively lacking in real combat experience.

Even so, they acknowledged her as a Demon Venerable in her own right. Even when fighting the Lord of the Dark Palace.

She gave a slight bow to the Drunken Demon, and then to the Demon Buddha standing behind her as well. This journey was becoming the catalyst that allowed her to stand tall as a true Demon Venerable.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable slowly exhaled, as if blowing out a breath.

Whooo.

Her breath spread into the darkness.

From that breath came sounds. The sighs of people, screams of rage, cries of sorrow, peals of cackling laughter—the voices of all manner of people were contained within it.

As the silent space filled with the sounds of joy, anger, sorrow, and delight, the air around them changed.

The Demon Buddha emitted a bright radiance from his body, illuminating the space of darkness. As the darkness was lost, the stars, too, began to lose their light.

The Drunken Demon released a powerful aura of wine, making the night sky drunk.

As the three Demon Venerables simultaneously released their presences, the atmosphere of that place transformed.

But the Lord of the Dark Palace raised his index finger into the air, as if to say that he was the master of this place.

At that moment, the positions of the stars surrounding them changed.

Swish, swish, swish, swish, swish, swish, swish.

As if, since the positions of the stars had changed, it was only natural that the places where they stood should change as well, all the energy the Demon Venerables had painstakingly altered vanished without a trace. They were standing in a different location.

Just from that one move, they could feel how extraordinary the Lord of the Dark Palace was within this space.

Of course, the Demon Venerables were not the sort to be cowed by that.

“In the earlier fight, Soul-Capturing watched my back, so this time I will take the supporting role.”

When the Drunken Demon yielded the first strike, the Lord of the Dark Palace provoked the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

“He’s putting you in front because he’s afraid of dying.”

That provocation did not work on the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. On the contrary, it gave her an opening to counterattack.

“Can you really say that, after sacrificing countless subordinates and only now crawling out yourself?”

Her words were direct and rough. She liked the method of openly cursing at her opponent to provoke them.

“You ugly old bastard. It’s time you were punished for failing to age with grace.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace’s eyes sank coldly. Before the Demon Venerables, at least, he had intended to display authority and terror and fight with dignity. But he had forgotten one thing.

“You damned Demon Venerables all take after that bastard.”

“I consider that an honor.”

Flash.

One of the stars floating around them shone, then dropped toward her at a terrifying speed.

Shiiiiik.

It was an astral force with the same power that had forced the Drunken Demon several steps back in the first attack.

Just as the astral force was about to fall upon her.

Shrrrk.

A pattern was drawn in midair and swallowed the incoming force.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s Hunyuan Reversal Art, which redirected attacks elsewhere, had been unleashed.

At the same time, a pattern appeared beside the Lord of the Dark Palace, and the astral force burst out from there.

Boom!

The Lord of the Dark Palace thrust out his hand and blocked the attack he himself had launched. Though his body shook from the impact, he did not take a single step. It showed just how profound his inner force was.

Shoooooook!

This time, three stars flew toward her at once.

A pattern formed in midair once more. But though there were three incoming stars, there was only one pattern.

Kwaaang! Boom! Kwaang!

Three explosive sounds rang out in succession.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable redirected one, and the ones who blocked the other two stars were the Drunken Demon and the Demon Buddha. Each released palm force and stopped the other two attacks.

While unleashing the Hunyuan Reversal Art, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had not shouted to the two Demon Venerables to block the other astral forces. She had trusted that they naturally would, and the Drunken Demon and the Demon Buddha lived up to that trust.

And the Drunken Demon went one step further.

The instant he blocked the astral force, he rushed toward the Lord of the Dark Palace. When it came to charging, no one could match the Drunken Demon.

In a flash, the Drunken Demon was thrusting his dagger at the Lord of the Dark Palace.

Whoosh.

With a single sound of wind, the distance between the two of them instantly widened.

The Lord of the Dark Palace, who had been right before his eyes, was now far away, no more than a speck in the distance.

The Lord of the Dark Palace was freely shrinking and expanding this night-sky space he had unfolded.

The Drunken Demon cupped his hands around his mouth and shouted.

“Old sir! If you go too far, you’ll get lost!”

Though the Drunken Demon mocked him, his eyes were cautious. The move that had just been displayed was by no means a shallow trick. He truly seemed to be moving by folding this space.

“We have no chance of winning here. We have to tear our way out.”

Everyone agreed with the Demon Buddha’s words.

But that was by no means an easy task. The wall of darkness was overwhelmingly strong, and even if they could combine their strength and break through it, there was no way the Lord of the Dark Palace would simply stand by and watch.

Whirik.

The Lord of the Dark Palace once again crossed the space and approached to within a dozen or so paces.

“You can only leave by dying.”

At that very moment, the Lord of the Dark Palace felt that something was unnatural about his body.

Before he knew it, red threads were tied around his arms, shoulders, and legs. He looked like a doll being controlled by strings.

The Blood Thread Marionette Art (血絲人形術) unleashed by the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had taken effect.

His arms swung up. One arm went upward, the other downward.

The Lord of the Dark Palace, of all people under heaven, had become a ridiculous sight.

The reason the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had used the Blood Thread Marionette Art was not to insult him, nor because she was confident she could control him for long. With her insufficient inner force, there was no way she could.

The reason she had used it regardless was because she trusted the cleverness of the person fighting alongside her.

Sure enough, the Drunken Demon was already rushing toward him. Having gained great enlightenment in footwork during this outing from the cult, the Drunken Demon showed movement incomparable to before.

The Drunken Demon did not miss this opportunity. This fleeting instant when he could not move his body properly.

Shiiiiik.

The Drunken Demon’s dagger was already flying toward the Lord of the Dark Palace’s heart.

Puak.

Blood scattered through the air.

“Aaah!”

The one who screamed was the Lady of Cheonhwaru, who had been watching the fight.

Everyone froze with startled expressions.

The blood sprayed through the air belonged to the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

The Drunken Demon and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable were looking at each other.

What the dagger had cut was the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s shoulder. Just before the dagger pierced his heart, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable and the Lord of the Dark Palace had changed places.

The Lord of the Dark Palace stood where the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had been, looking at them and smiling.

He had unleashed one of his signature martial arts, the Dark Heaven Position-Exchange Art (暗天換位功).

If the Drunken Demon had not changed the direction of his dagger at the last moment, it would not have been her shoulder but her heart that was pierced. In that split second, he had seen not fear but exultation in the Lord of the Dark Palace’s eyes, and had instinctively altered its course.

The Drunken Demon’s heart sank. Truly, this was the first time he had ever seen such demonic arts.

If he could use this demonic art freely, how could they possibly kill him?

“Soul-Capturing, are you all right?”

“Yes.”

With an expression that said she truly was fine, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable nodded to the Drunken Demon and pressed an acupoint on her shoulder to stop the bleeding.

The Drunken Demon turned toward the Lord of the Dark Palace and laughed.

“So that’s where you were? Charging at one of our own because I was drunk! This is why drunkards are no good.”

Watching this, Gaye spoke in a low voice.

“As long as the Greedy Heart Great Annihilation Art continues, that man’s inner force is as good as infinite.”

Everyone heard her words.

“Someone outside has to get everyone aboard this ship to disembark and stop the Greedy Heart Great Annihilation Art. Then, maybe, there will be a chance of winning.”

Now the Lord of the Dark Palace did not even curse her as a traitor. As if this indifference was the greatest punishment he could give her.

Having said what she needed to say, Gaye also stood expressionless.

She was standing here as the enemy of the person she had followed her entire life. Could a person’s fate change like this in a single day? Was it allowed to?

While Gaye was speaking, another martial art had already been unleashed.

It was the martial art of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, who had nearly died just moments ago. They were such masters that, in the blink of an eye, martial arts were already unfolding.

Patterns filled the space around the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable and the Lord of the Dark Palace. Around her, around the Lord of the Dark Palace, and between them, they stretched out like a path.

The next moment.

Whoosh.

She stood before a single door.

Heart-Soul Direct Link (心魂直結).

She had unleashed a soul-capturing art that entered directly into the opponent’s mind. To attempt to directly seize the soul of a master like the Lord of the Dark Palace was an exceedingly dangerous undertaking.

But the strongest martial art she possessed was, in the end, the soul-capturing art that controlled another’s mind. If they were to win this fight, she had no choice.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable opened the door and stepped inside.

It was an empty room.

She entered again through the door in that room. The next room was empty as well.

These rooms were places where one could see the memories of the person subjected to the soul-capturing art. Not just any memories, but memories of their happiest moments. Those happiest moments were then used to control the opponent.

Each time she opened a door and entered, she moved backward through time.

The next room was empty as well.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had performed countless soul-capturing arts, but this was the first time she had ever encountered such empty rooms.

Passing from old age through middle age, she opened the door to his youth and entered.

But even the happy memories of his youth were empty.

How could this be?

When she opened the final room, one memory was there.

She saw the Lord of the Dark Palace as a child, together with someone. Looking up at the person beside him, the young Lord of the Dark Palace was smiling brightly. The person had his back turned, so she could not see who he was from where she stood.

In his entire life, this was the one and only pleasant memory in his heart.

Perhaps this had been the first and last time in his life that he had ever smiled so innocently.

Just as the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was about to approach and confirm the identity of the person standing with his back turned.

The eyes of the young Lord of the Dark Palace, who had been looking up at the man, shifted and looked at her.

“!”

This was the first time a figure in a memory had moved of its own accord like this.

The instant an ominous feeling surged up, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable quickly ran back the way she had come.

Each time she passed through a room she had already crossed, the door closed by itself.

Tak! Tak!

She instinctively knew that if she were trapped in a room, she would be unable to escape. That would mean qi deviation from the failure of the soul-capturing art.

Tak! Tak! Tak!

They closed, and closed again. She sprinted as fast as she could and, just as she narrowly emerged from the final room—

Her eyes flew open.

Clang.

The patterns connected between the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable and the Lord of the Dark Palace shattered and broke apart.

She swallowed down the blood surging up from within.

The soul-capturing art had failed.

“Who was that person?”

At the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s question, the Lord of the Dark Palace’s face twitched. Was it rage at having had the depths of his heart seen? A powerful aura of darkness surged from his body.

Seven of the stars in the night sky began to shine.

Northern Dipper Calamity Annihilation (北斗滅劫).

Those seven stars filled the space as they poured down upon the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

Swaaaaaaaaaak!

It was an attack of power incomparable to those that had flown at her before. It came too wide and too fast, leaving no space to evade.

She raised her protective astral energy to its limit, but it was a situation in which she had to be prepared for internal injuries.

Just then, the Demon Buddha’s voice came from behind her.

“It’s nice and spacious here.”

The Demon Buddha’s fingers were already forming a mudra.

Demonic Palm Annihilation Seal!

A gigantic blood-red Buddha rose in the middle of the night sky. The seven incoming streams of astral force struck the blood Buddha.

Boom! Kwaaang! Kwa-kwang! Kwaaang! Kwang! Kwang! Kwaaang!

The Blood Buddha blocked with his body every blade of qi that flew toward the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

A gigantic palm came crashing down toward the Dark Palace Lord.

Kuwaaaang.

Ordinarily, there would have been no way to evade the Blood Buddha’s palm within that space, but the Dark Palace Lord stretched the space and fled far into the distance.

The Drunken Demon judged that they could not afford to drag this out any longer.

They had to decide the battle before their own inner power was consumed further.

As the distance opened between them, the Drunken Demon drew Blood Tear, the unique weapon he wore at his waist. It was the first time he had drawn Blood Tear since boarding the ship.

“Careful. I’m a mean drunk.”

When he infused his unique inner power into the gourd-shaped Blood Tear, it trembled and emitted a pure white light.

The Drunken Demon’s signature martial art, the Wine God Art.

The final cup, the Wine God’s Utter Intoxication.

The Drunken Demon opened Blood Tear’s stopper and gulped down the liquor inside.

A black, drunken aura began to ripple from the Drunken Demon’s body, and from his two eyes flowed a pure white intoxication.

“Ah, I’m getting drunk!”

The Drunken Demon drew a dagger and swung it through the air, throwing a drunken fit.

“Who dares block my way! Move!”

Watching this, the Dark Palace Lord unleashed a new martial art.

“I’ll make it even more dizzying for you!”

Dark Heaven Revolving Star.

This time, the stars in every direction began to rotate around them. The stars spun as though the heavens themselves had gone mad.

Whiiiiiiiii.

It was so dizzying that the Lord of Cheonhwa Pavilion and Gaye collapsed where they stood.

The Drunken Demon, who had been about to charge at the Dark Palace Lord, staggered and ran off in another direction. It was not merely dizziness from their surroundings spinning. Currents of darkness that stole away the opponent’s mind were whirling together with it.

“Am I the one who’s drunk? Or is the world drunk? The whole world is spinning!”

Though he spoke with leisure, the Drunken Demon’s heart was urgent. He had to close in on the bastard before the effect of Blood Tear, his strongest move, disappeared.

The Demon Buddha pressed his palms together and steadied his mind, but that did not mean he could pierce through this dizziness and attack the Dark Palace Lord.

As the power of Dark Heaven Revolving Star grew even stronger, the stars spun faster still.

Amid it all, the Dark Palace Lord’s voice rang out thunderously.

“What will kill you is yourselves. Your own arrogance, knowing it was a trap and yet walking into it on your own feet.”

Black energy began to gather in the Dark Palace Lord’s hand.

“I will show the Young Cult Leader your deaths!”

At that moment, when everyone was too dizzy to come to their senses, there was one exception.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had been pretending to be dizzy, but her eyes were gleaming sharply.

‘You dare make the world spin when I am here?’

The whirlwind pattern was the symbol of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

This dizziness, if anything, made her even stronger. Within this mad, spinning vertigo, she felt tranquility and comfort.

She drew her inner force up to its limit. She even drew in the energy of Dark Heaven Revolving Star and made it her own power. After gathering that power—

Whoosh.

Her Soul-Capturing Art was unleashed.

The target of the Soul-Capturing Art was not the Dark Palace Lord.

The one upon whom she unleashed the Soul-Capturing Art was, astonishingly, the Drunken Demon.

—Do not reject me!

Chapter 810: Drunkards Drink Until Dawn
---------------------------------------
The Drunken Demon accepted her without resistance.

He could guess why she had used the Soul-Capturing Art on him. Because right now, in this place, she was probably the only one who could overcome this dizziness.

The Soul-Capturing Art that the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable used on the Drunken Demon was different from what she had attempted on the Dark Palace Lord earlier.

If the previous Soul-Capturing Art had been one that dominated and controlled the opponent, this one shared consciousness with the other and moved as one body.

Two Souls, One Body Art (二魂一體術).

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable settled into the Drunken Demon’s mind.

‘So this is Lord Drunken Demon’s mind.’

There, she saw it. The thing at the center of the Drunken Demon’s heart.

Emitting a sacred aura, it was the vessel containing the wine essence that lay deep within the forbidden grounds of the Great Drunken Forest.

The actual wine essence of the Great Drunken Forest had been tainted, but the wine essence within his heart had not. It was utterly pure. She could tell that the wish for the wine essence to remain untainted was the very core of the man known as the Drunken Demon.

When the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable closed her eyes and recited the incantation, unknown characters formed around her and the wine essence.

At the very moment the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable settled into his mind, the Drunken Demon experienced something astonishing.

The world, which had been so dizzying that it was hard even to stand, suddenly became calm. His head cleared, and his heart grew peaceful. It felt like lying in Chwimong Pavilion on some leisurely day when he had nothing at all to do, gazing up at drifting clouds.

With the power of the Wine God’s Total Intoxication unleashed through Blood Tears, and now the energy conveyed by the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable added on top of it, the Drunken Demon felt a mighty strength he had never felt before.

“You bastard, where are you! Where are you?”

The Drunken Demon staggered toward the Dark Palace Lord, then toppled over again. It was an act meant to bring him as close to the Dark Palace Lord as possible.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, who had become one with him, was impressed by the Drunken Demon’s response.

Even at this moment, when powerful dark astral energy was gathering in the Dark Palace Lord’s hand, the Drunken Demon did not hurry.

By now, the stars revolving around them were spinning so quickly they could barely be seen.

The Drunken Demon charged at the Dark Palace Lord again, only to fall forward.

“This crazy world must be drunker than I am.”

At those words, the Dark Palace Lord laughed and let his guard down—for that single fleeting instant.

Through the spinning world, the Drunken Demon became a streak of light and cut across it.

Shiiiiiiiii.

In the blink of an eye, he rushed right up before the Dark Palace Lord, and the dagger in his hand flashed.

The Dark Palace Lord had previously used a dark demonic art to switch places with the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, but the Drunken Demon did not hesitate. He was certain the man could never block such a swift attack, and on top of that, the Dark Palace Lord was in the midst of gathering dark energy in his hand.

Thud!

The Drunken Demon’s dagger drove into his chest, and blood burst out.

‘It missed the heart!’

As the Drunken Demon had expected, he had failed to use the Dark Heaven Transposition Art that switched positions with an opponent. Facing the Drunken Demon, who had rushed right up to his nose, the astral energy gathered in his hand only became a burden.

Shaaaaaak.

The astral energy he flung away as if discarding it missed.

Shiiiiik.

The Drunken Demon’s second attack lodged itself in the Dark Palace Lord’s body once more.

The Drunken Demon intended to end it with this strike.

Thuk.

Unfortunately, though this one was accurate, it was shallow.

Shuuuuuk.
