The Dark Palace Lord instantly stretched the space and vanished far into the distance.

Frowning, the Dark Palace Lord staunched the wound in his chest. Both attacks had been truly dangerous. Had he failed to respond even slightly, his heart would have been pierced.

With those attacks, the rapidly spinning stars stopped. The Dark Heaven Revolving Stars had been interrupted by the Dark Palace Lord’s injury.

The Dark Palace Lord looked at the Drunken Demon in astonishment.

“How on earth?”

Soon, the Dark Palace Lord’s gaze turned to the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, who still stood silently behind him. Seeing her, he realized that the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had entered the Drunken Demon’s body.

“So you used the Soul-Capturing Art.”

Meanwhile, the Drunken Demon did not chase after him as he fled. It was impossible to pursue someone who moved by freely folding and unfolding space with mere footwork.

Instead, he drew up his internal energy and rushed toward the nearest wall of darkness.

“Let’s see if you can afford not to come here.”

The Drunken Demon thrust his dagger into the wall.

The Demon Venerables believed they had no chance of winning within this dark space of the night sky. They had to break through this space somehow.

The Drunken Demon drew up all his internal energy and concentrated it into his dagger.

But the wall of darkness was not easily pierced.

At that moment, light flew toward the spot where he was stabbing with his dagger.

Hwaaaaak.

The Demon Buddha, freed from the dizziness, had emitted light from his hand. Under the Demon Buddha’s golden radiance, the energy of darkness weakened.

Just then, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable came over before anyone noticed, grasped the dagger together with him, and pushed.

She had stopped the Soul-Capturing Art and left the Drunken Demon’s body. Since they had escaped the dizzying Dark Heaven Revolving Stars, there was no longer any need for her to remain inside him.

Riiiip.

When the three Demon Venerables joined forces, the dagger began to pierce through the wall of darkness.

“Just a little, just a little more!”

It was right then.

Shiiiiik.

A star fell toward the Drunken Demon with light-like speed.

Boom!

The Demon Buddha, who had been shining light for him, struck out with his other hand and destroyed the astral energy attacking the Drunken Demon.

And just as the Drunken Demon’s dagger was about to break through the wall of darkness—

Shak, shak.

The constellations around them changed.

“Damn it.”

An exclamation slipped from the Drunken Demon’s mouth.

They had almost pierced through, but the place where they were standing had changed. The wall before them now was a new wall.

In the meantime, the Dark Palace Lord had approached nearby.

Ordinarily, no matter how profound the Dark Palace Lord’s internal energy was, he could not have used such tremendous dark demonic arts in succession. Each and every move he unleashed required truly enormous internal energy.

As Gaye had said, the Great Annihilation Art of Greed was granting him endless internal energy.

The Drunken Demon did not give up.

“Let’s see how long you can keep changing it!”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable sent a voice transmission to the Drunken Demon.

—I will enter once more.

The Drunken Demon said to her,

—Today, this body belongs to you, Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

This time, a Soul-Capturing Art that drew out the target’s potential to its extreme was unleashed.

Heart-Soul Awakening (心魂覺醒).

It was a Soul-Capturing Art that could strain both her body and the Drunken Demon’s, but there was no choice.

New patterns began to form around the Drunken Demon’s body, and an even stronger, mad drunkenness flowed from his eyes.

“Drunkards are the sort who break and tear everything apart. I’ll tear it open no matter what!”

At that tremendous momentum, the Dark Palace Lord tried to attack the Drunken Demon. If left alone like that, he would surely break through.

At that moment, the Demon Buddha rushed toward the Dark Palace Lord.

“Your opponent is me!”

Emitting a golden radiance, he charged at the Dark Palace Lord. In terms of martial arts compatibility, the Demon Buddha was someone strong against the Dark Palace Lord.

He had to protect the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion, but he realized that if he stayed out of this fight, they would have no chance of victory. He had to press the man so fiercely that he could touch neither the Drunken Demon nor the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

Shaaaaaak!

Boom!

The Dark Palace Lord’s and the Demon Buddha’s palm strikes collided.

The two were pushed back to either side by the shock of the impact. The one pushed back farther was the Demon Buddha, who was inferior in internal energy.

As he was forced backward, the Demon Buddha’s hands were forming mudras.

Golden Great Arhat Demonic Art!

Killing Dharma Seal!

Golden astral energy surged toward the Dark Palace Lord.

Shaaaaaaaaaak!

Before the Dark Palace Lord, the energy of darkness rose like a fortress wall.

Suuuuuuuk!

It was an extremely long wall that stretched all the way into the distance. Since it was far longer than the astral energy flying toward him, it had seemed pointless for him to raise such an unnecessarily long wall.

Kwaang!

The golden astral energy that struck the wall spread left and right along the fortress wall.

Ripple!

It was not a solid wall, but a fortress wall that absorbed impact. The shock rippled like waves and dispersed, flowing all the way to the far ends of the wall.

The golden astral energy that reached the very end vanished.

The Dark Palace Lord’s counterattack began. Judging that he must not drag things out, he unleashed a powerful move capable of killing the Demon Buddha in one blow.

The stars in the sky poured down upon the Demon Buddha.

Dark Heaven Meteor (暗天流星).

The meteors pouring toward him were not attacks that could be blocked by ordinary methods.

Golden Great Arhat Demonic Art.

Lotus in Full Bloom.

Golden radiance burst from the Demon Buddha’s body, and a lotus blossomed.

Boom! Kwaang! Kwaaang! Boom!

Stars fell one after another onto the lotus. The impact made it seem as if the night sky itself were overturning.

Ssssss.

When the attack ended, the lotus had also disappeared. Blood was flowing from the corner of the Demon Buddha’s mouth. Even though he had blocked it with Lotus in Full Bloom, he had suffered internal injuries. He was clearly being pushed back in terms of internal energy.

But the Demon Buddha did not stop attacking. He thrust out both palms in succession and clashed with him. It looked as though he was attacking wildly in excitement, but that was not the case. Those fierce palm techniques were feints meant to conceal this one move.

Hwaaaaak.

Golden radiance burst from the Demon Buddha’s hand, and astral energy in the shape of a palm began to grow.

Whoooooooaaaang!

Together with a golden wave, the palm flew straight forward.

It was the Demon Buddha’s newly created technique, the Demonic Palm of the Lesser Mudra.

As the golden palm flew toward him, the Dark Palace Lord retreated in an instant as if teleporting.

But the Demon Buddha’s enormous palm did not stop and continued flying toward him.

Suuuuuk.

The same fortress wall as before blocked his path.

But only against the attack of the Demonic Palm of the Lesser Mudra could it not disperse the impact. Before it could disperse it, the Demonic Palm of the Lesser Mudra shattered the wall and drove forward.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

The might of the Demonic Palm of the Lesser Mudra was tremendous.

The Dark Palace Lord fled endlessly into the distance, and the Demon Buddha’s palm continued to push forward.

But it could not press on until the very last moment.

The Demonic Palm of the Lesser Mudra, unable to reach with sufficient power, stopped at the end.

Standing right before it, the Dark Palace Lord watched as that golden palm disappeared, then instantly closed the distance and rushed at the Demon Buddha.

The Demon Buddha’s face had turned completely pale. He had forced himself to use the Demonic Palm of the Lesser Mudra while already suffering internal injuries.

The moment the Dark Palace Lord’s eyes flashed sharply, a pitch-black spear appeared in his hand.

Whoooong!

The Dark Palace Lord hurled that spear at the Demon Buddha.

It was the Dark Heaven Ghost Spear (暗天鬼槍), which had been waiting for this very moment.

Just as the Demon Buddha was about to throw himself aside to dodge, the energy of darkness seized him from beneath his feet. It was the dark energy that had been circling around the Dark Palace Lord. That energy even seized both of the Demon Buddha’s arms so he could not form mudras.

The technique was not merely the spear flying at him. It was an attack that momentarily immobilized the body before piercing it with the spear. The spear flew so quickly that he had no time to shake off the dark energy holding him.

The Demon Buddha raised his protective astral energy to its limit. He instinctively knew that even if he did not die instantly, he would suffer a fatal wound.

Just as the Dark Heaven Ghost Spear was about to pierce the Demon Buddha’s face—

Kwaaang!

The flying spear grazed past the Demon Buddha and vanished into the darkness behind him.

The Drunken Demon was standing before the Demon Buddha. He had given up on the wall he had nearly pierced through and thrown himself over to save the Demon Buddha.

The hand holding his dagger hung limply. The Demon Buddha could tell. In forcibly striking away the spear, the Drunken Demon’s arm had broken.

“Why did you save me? You should have kept breaking through that.”

Still with his back turned, the Drunken Demon answered.

“Didn’t you say you would buy me a drink? I intend to have that drink from you.”

For a moment, the Demon Buddha’s chest tightened.

But even if he died, the two of them should have broken through that place. Only then would the Drunken Demon and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable have had any chance of surviving.

“You should have gone out anyway!”

When the Demon Buddha shouted, the Drunken Demon answered in a cowed tone.

“Drunkards are known for poor judgment.”

The Dark Palace Lord’s gaze turned toward the Drunken Demon’s arm.

“Your arm is broken.”

The Drunken Demon took the dagger in his other hand.

“I’ll have to refrain from drinking until it heals. Now that I’m older, injuries don’t heal quickly anymore.”

Even as he remained relaxed, there was one thing the Drunken Demon regretted.

‘If only I could use Blood Tears just one more time!’

The energy of Blood Tears had already vanished. His chance to unleash his greatest power was gone. To use Blood Tears again, he needed time to recover.

That was not all. The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was also in poor condition. The great shock the Drunken Demon had received had been transmitted to her as well while she was maintaining the Soul-Capturing Art.

All three of them were still standing, but none of them were in a normal state.

Looking at them, a cold smile formed on the Dark Palace Lord’s face.

“Who, I wonder, would the Young Cult Leader grieve for most?”

The one who answered was the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

“Your death, no doubt. He would lament, asking why we killed you when he should have done it himself.”

The Drunken Demon took up her words.

“Only lament? He’ll nag endlessly too, so prepare earplugs in advance.”

The Drunken Demon and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable looked at each other and smiled faintly.

The more bravado they showed, the more the Demon Buddha behind them sensed death approaching. Their opponent was too strong to overcome with momentum alone.

The Demon Buddha looked back at the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

‘I am sorry I could not protect you.’

Apology filled the Demon Buddha’s eyes.

The Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion lowered her head with a calm expression. Her eyes held this emotion:

‘No. I will not forget this kindness today.’

The Demon Buddha was also sorry to the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon. The man had entrusted her to him, believing in the Young Cult Leader and in them. In his heart, he conveyed his apology to the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon.

‘I’m sorry.’

The Demon Buddha drew up all of his internal energy.

Even if he was going to die, he could not simply die obediently.

“The Blood Buddha tells me not to show mercy any longer.”

The Demon Buddha joined the momentum of the other two.

And just as they were about to begin their final battle—

Pat, pat, pat, paat.

The stars illuminating their surroundings began to lose their light one by one.

Gradually, the speed increased, and the countless shining stars almost all disappeared.

Now only several dozen stars remained there.

The Dark Palace Lord’s expression hardened, and the one who realized what had happened was Gaye.

“The Great Annihilation Art of Greed has been broken!”

That meant the gamblers aboard the boat had all gotten off.

It meant he could no longer wield unlimited internal energy, nor power greater than his original strength.

The three Demon Venerables could tell.

‘The Extreme Evil Lesser Demon moved!’

He must have taken care of the outside and entered the boat.

But it could not have been easy for him to break through the barrier outside and the dark haze surrounding the boat on his own.

The three Demon Venerables looked at one another and exchanged glances. Their hearts were one.

—Let’s give it a try.

Reading their thoughts, the Dark Palace Lord sneered coldly.

“That does not mean your fate will change!”

He had every reason to be confident. His internal energy still remained intact, while the three Demon Venerables were injured and had nearly exhausted their internal energy as well.

The Dark Palace Lord looked up at the dozens of stars floating around them.

“As you can see, there are still many stars.”

The Drunken Demon looked up at the stars with him. There was still no sign of surrender in his eyes.

“I’ll make all those stars fall. Drunkards drink until morning comes.”

Chapter 811: After a Lifetime of Nothing Happening
--------------------------------------------------
Now hope had come to the three Demon Venerables.

The Extreme Evil Soma had arrived outside, so they only had to hold out until he joined them!

In another sense, it also meant an even greater responsibility and duty had fallen upon them.

Even if they failed to endure and died, they had to inflict as much damage as possible on the Dark Palace Lord.

If the Dark Palace Lord walked out alive and unscathed, even if this space were shattered, the Extreme Evil Soma alone would not be able to face him.

The Drunken Demon sent a sound transmission to the Demon Buddha.

—If the moment comes when I seize him, do not worry about whether I live or die. Attack.

He had boasted that he would bring down all the remaining stars, but the Drunken Demon had already resolved himself for death. Even if the Greed-Heart Great Annihilation Art had vanished, the Dark Palace Lord was still a formidable opponent.

Decisively, all of them were suffering from internal and external injuries, and their internal energy was lacking as well, so defeating him would absolutely not be easy.

As he thought of death, two people came to the Drunken Demon’s mind.

The first was Yeobin, who would be waiting for him at the Drunken Dream Pavilion. If he died.

‘She’ll be drinking herself senseless every day.’

More than fear or regret over his own death, he was troubled by how much pain Yeobin would suffer.

Next came Geom Mugeuk, who had yet to emerge from the secret coffer, with no way of knowing whether he was alive or dead.

Yes, there was nothing to worry about. The Young Cult Leader would surely comfort Yeobin and look after her. For a martial artist, there could be no greater strength than knowing there was someone who would take responsibility for what came after.

‘Little brother, I leave the rest to you.’

The Drunken Demon looked at the Demon Buddha. He had never thought he would end up saving him twice. If they survived, he would gain a drinking companion.

—If we meet later in hell, buy me a drink.

The Demon Buddha stared at him, then sent a calm sound transmission.

—I will buy liquor and come to the Drunken Dream Pavilion. Invite me to your home.

It meant they should both survive and drink at the cult.

Sensing that the two of them were about to unleash their final move, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable also prepared her last strike.

She intended to use her soul-capturing art on the Dark Palace Lord one last time.

But it had not worked even when she had not yet been injured. If she entered the Dark Palace Lord’s body in her current state, suffering from severe internal wounds, she would surely fall into qi deviation.

‘Even so, there’s no other choice.’

If she could bind him for even a single instant! If luck favored them, the Drunken Demon and the Demon Buddha might somehow finish him off.

When she sensed death, Geom Mugeuk came to her mind. As during the previous crisis, this was still what she regretted most.

‘I am sorry I could not serve you to the end, Young Cult Leader.’

At that moment, the Drunken Demon’s sound transmission flew to her.

—I have a favor to ask of you, Soul-Capturing.

—Tell me.

The Drunken Demon’s request was something she had never imagined. When she heard it, she was startled.

—You will naturally know when that moment comes.

After entrusting one task to the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, the Drunken Demon stepped forward.

“Come now, old man! If you invited a drunkard, you should open the banquet again, shouldn’t you?”

The Demon Buddha and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable walked out with him on either side. Today, the two Demon Venerables were willingly becoming the Drunken Demon’s wings.

They no longer paid attention to the Tower Lord of Cheonhwa. The Dark Palace Lord was already certain of his victory, so the chances of him taking her hostage were slim.

Moreover, if they lost this fight, she would die with them, so protecting her in this situation was meaningless.

The Dark Palace Lord looked at the three Demon Venerables and spoke coldly.

“Enjoy yourselves. It will be your final banquet.”

Outwardly, he seemed confident, but like the dark energy coiling around his body, he too was tense.

‘Now that the Greed-Heart Great Annihilation Art is gone, I must be cautious.’

No matter how injured they were, Demon Venerables were still Demon Venerables.

From now on, he could not recklessly use the Dark Demonic Art, which consumed a great deal of internal energy. If his internal energy ran out and even one Demon Venerable remained, that would be the same as death.

“Bring out the liquor! And bring out the Young Cult Leader too!”

The Drunken Demon shouted and charged like a madman. Even though one of his arms was broken, he showed not the slightest fear.

The Demon Buddha and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable rushed forward together from behind his left and right. Though they had not devised a strategy, each of them knew what they had to do.

The pressure exerted by the combined attack of the three Demon Venerables was immense.

The Dark Palace Lord targeted the Drunken Demon first. He thrust both palms toward him.

Swaaaek, swaek!

Two streams of palm force flew toward the Drunken Demon from different directions.

The Drunken Demon used his footwork and evaded the palm forces. The mastery of footwork he had gained during this outing from the cult was shining in this final battle.

The Dark Palace Lord lightly launched himself backward and thrust both palms again. Two streams of palm force flew in with a time lag.

Swaaaek! Swaek!

This time too, the Drunken Demon seemed to be dodging well with his footwork.

Ripple.

The solid floor rippled, moving as though it were alive.

Because of that, the Drunken Demon’s footwork was thrown off.

Thud!

The second palm force struck the Drunken Demon’s body.

The Drunken Demon was sent sliding backward and collapsed onto the floor.

The one who neutralized the palm force that flew toward the fallen Drunken Demon was the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

A pattern formed in front of the Drunken Demon and swallowed the incoming qi force.

Her Primordial Reversal Art sent the qi force back toward the Dark Palace Lord.

In the meantime, the Demon Buddha stepped in front of the Drunken Demon.

“Are you all right?”

At the worried question, the Drunken Demon rose. Fortunately, he had been struck on the opposite side of his body, not on the side of his broken arm. Even though he had used protective qi, it hurt as though his bones had broken.

“I’m all right.”

The Drunken Demon got to his feet spiritedly.

“When you’re drinking, everyone falls over once in a while, don’t they?”

The Drunken Demon charged toward the Dark Palace Lord again.

Ripple, ripple.

When the floor rippled, one might have expected him to fly through the air and charge, but the Drunken Demon ran along that rippling floor. He had chosen to conserve even the smallest bit of internal energy.

The rippling floor hindered the Drunken Demon’s footwork.

But the Drunken Demon clearly showed the difference between being caught unaware and knowing what was coming.

He dodged attacks as though dancing atop the rippling floor, then slid forward and rushed at the Dark Palace Lord in an instant.

Until now, the Dark Palace Lord had only continued to flee, but this time he fought the Drunken Demon at close range. That old body moved with astonishing agility as he tried to strike the Drunken Demon’s throat and heart with his hands.

Thud!

Though he allowed the Dark Palace Lord to land a palm strike, the Drunken Demon did not retreat. He fought with the ferocity of a man who, if both his arms were broken, would fight with headbutts and tear with his teeth.

“Do you know why drunkards’ fights are so vicious? Once we’re drunk, we don’t even know pain.”

How could it not hurt? The Drunken Demon endured the agony and pressed him.

Because the two were engaged in close combat too swift to follow, the Demon Buddha and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable could only watch without intervening.

At the very moment their close-quarters battle reached its peak, the dark energy of the Dark Palace Lord, which had remained still until then, seized the Drunken Demon. It was energy that had been waiting solely for this moment.

“No!”

The Demon Buddha shouted, but the Dark Palace Lord’s blow was already smashing into the Drunken Demon’s chest.

Booooom!

The Drunken Demon vomited a mouthful of blood.

He looked once at the Demon Buddha with vacant eyes, then dropped his head and collapsed.

The Dark Palace Lord looked down at the Drunken Demon with a cold gaze.

“Your drinking party is over.”

A deep sigh escaped the Demon Buddha as he watched. He could not sense any life from the Drunken Demon’s body. That one move had ended his life.

The Drunken Demon had died this pointlessly?

A golden radiance filled with dense killing intent burst from the Demon Buddha’s eyes as he looked at the Dark Palace Lord.

By contrast, the Dark Palace Lord stepped forward with a much lighter heart.

The Drunken Demon, who had tried to close in during their combined attack, had been troublesome, but he believed he could handle the other two well enough.

The Dark Palace Lord sensed victory from the Demon Buddha’s appearance. He was agitated by the Drunken Demon’s death. Even if he fought calmly, he could not defeat him; if he was agitated as well, the outcome was obvious.

Golden radiance began to gather in the Demon Buddha’s hand. The Dark Palace Lord sensed that he was about to use a major demonic art.

That was exactly what the Dark Palace Lord wanted. If he unleashed a powerful martial art, his remaining internal energy would be depleted in an instant.

“I know well that your Blood Buddha is not merciful. But it seems that mercilessness applies to you as well.”

It was at that moment.

From behind, someone wrapped one arm around the Dark Palace Lord’s neck. It was the Drunken Demon, whom he had thought dead.

“Now!”

The Drunken Demon was not dead.

Before the battle began, what he had asked of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable through sound transmission was for her to make him appear dead. So that the Dark Palace Lord would believe in his death.

For this one and only chance.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable poured all her internal energy into unleashing her final soul-capturing art.

Soul-Capturing Turtle Breath.

She had perfectly disguised the Drunken Demon’s death. She had split the shock of the final blow equally between herself and him. It was a perfect move that had deceived even the Demon Buddha.

Whooooooooosh!

The Demon Palm Seal was released from the Demon Buddha’s hand.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwak!

A giant golden palm struck the Dark Palace Lord and the Drunken Demon, driving them back.

The two were swept away by the Demon Palm Seal and sent flying.

The Drunken Demon rolled across the floor. As he coughed up blood, his body was completely battered and torn.

A short distance away from him, the Dark Palace Lord stood with a discomfited expression. He had nearly died from that single move just now.

But he had not died. Blood flowed down his arm, and he had suffered a deep wound on his face, but he had not died.

A regretful sigh escaped the Drunken Demon’s lips. He turned his head and looked at the Demon Buddha.

“With a heart so weak, how can you call yourself one who serves the Blood Buddha?”

At the final moment, the Demon Buddha had not released his attack immediately and had hesitated for an instant. That hesitation had given the Dark Palace Lord time to escape. As he escaped, the Drunken Demon, who had been clinging to him, had survived as well.

The reason the Demon Buddha hesitated was because of the Drunken Demon. He could not bring himself to kill with his own hands the Drunken Demon who had saved him twice.

Even though that choice had been the only way to save himself and the others, at that final moment, the Demon Buddha had hesitated.

“I could not let you die together with a man like that, even in death.”

At those words, the Drunken Demon lay back and looked up at the night sky. Now true death remained.

“This is all because of the Young Cult Leader.”

The Demon Buddha had not originally been someone with such a weak heart. Though, by that logic, he himself would not have saved the Demon Buddha twice either.

The Demon Buddha sat down and silently rolled his prayer beads. Since he had poured out the last of his internal energy, he could not help the Drunken Demon.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was the same. By using Soul-Capturing Turtle Breath, she had exhausted all the internal energy she had left.

The Dark Palace Lord extended his palm toward the Drunken Demon to deliver a palm strike.

“You tiresome bastard!”

The Drunken Demon was truly an opponent he never wanted to fight again.

“Old man, move that hand. I can’t see the sky.”

The Dark Palace Lord laughed coldly and was about to blow off the Drunken Demon’s head with palm force.

“Stop.”

An unexpected person stepped forward. Astonishingly, it was the Tower Lord of Cheonhwa.

She walked forward fearlessly and stood in front of the Drunken Demon.

“If you kill this man, you will not hear the final prophecy from me.”

When she stepped forward, not only the Drunken Demon but also the Demon Buddha and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable were startled.

The deep wrinkles on the Dark Palace Lord’s face twitched.

“Do you think prophecy matters to me at this point?”

As though she had read his mind, the Tower Lord of Cheonhwa nodded calmly.

“I received a prophecy on this ship. Are you not curious what that prophecy was?”

At her words, the corners of the Dark Palace Lord’s eyes trembled slightly.

“What prophecy?”

“I will tell you if you spare the Demon Venerables.”

With an indifferent gaze, the Dark Palace Lord extended toward her the hand that had been aimed at the Drunken Demon. At the sight, as though he intended to kill her first, Gaye shouted.

“No!”

She could not stand by and watch the Tower Lord of Cheonhwa, who had saved her twice, die like this.

But the Dark Palace Lord had not extended his hand to kill her.

Using object manipulation from afar, the Dark Palace Lord moved the Tower Lord of Cheonhwa away from the remaining people.

“Even if I give up hearing the prophecy, I cannot spare them.”

Seeing that he moved only her aside, Gaye understood. He intended to spare the Tower Lord of Cheonhwa and kill her.

“The first person who should die is me.”

Gaye calmly accepted death.

“Because all of this is my responsibility, for failing to properly convey heaven’s will.”

The Dark Palace Lord spoke coldly.

“There is no need to decide the order of death. I will kill you all at once.”

When he raised his hand, the star floating in the sky began to descend.

It was different from before, when the stars had become ganggi and fallen.

All the remaining stars slowly descended. The pure-white masses of light were beautiful just to behold.

Those stars gathered around the three Demon Venerables and Gaye. Before their faces, and before their hearts.

They could all feel it. These stars would soon explode around them.

With this final technique using the stars, she meant to kill them all.

At the thought that death was now moments away, all the Demon Venerables closed their eyes.

The Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable said softly,

“It was an honor to fight alongside you two.”

It was his final expression of gratitude to the two senior Demon Venerables who had treated him, to the very end, as one of their own.

At the very instant the stars were about to explode—

Thunk!

The sound of something piercing through rang out there.

The gazes of the Demon Venerables, who had closed their eyes, turned toward the source of the sound.

A single sword had jutted out from the place the Drunken Demon had nearly torn apart earlier. Knowing that spot was weak, someone had thrust the sword in from the outside.

Was it because the Greed-Heart Great Annihilation Art had vanished? Or was it because the sword’s owner’s martial arts were simply that profound?

Riiiiip.

The protruding sword tore the dark energy downward.

At that moment, this space of darkness howled like a person.

Ssssssss.

Astonishingly, the stars floating before them, looking as though they would burst at any moment, lost their light and disappeared.

Because there were no stars in the night sky, the Lord of the Dark Palace could not alter the constellations to shift locations.

As the Lord of the Dark Palace hurriedly recited an incantation, the dark energy surrounding her surged toward the long tear below, trying to fill it.

It was right then.

Fwaaaaaak!

Through the spot where the sword had torn downward, something enormous broke in.

It was a great saber.

Above, a great saber. Below, a sword.

Two weapons pierced the wall of darkness at the same time.

The space let out an ear-splitting scream.

Pop-pop-pop-pop-pop-pop-pop!

With the sound of firecrackers bursting, the night sky began to vanish. It was the moment the Dark Heaven Star Soul Art unleashed by the Lord of the Dark Palace was broken.

The hellish night sky disappeared, and they returned to the space where they had originally been.

Seeing the people standing there, the Drunken Demon tried to laugh loudly, but clutched his chest at the pain that surged through him.

The Demon Buddha also let out a sigh of relief, and a bright smile spread across the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable’s face.

The Lord of Cheonhwa Pavilion cried out in a trembling voice.

“Lord Soma.”

The snow-white mask was smiling at her.

The one she had longed to see so desperately had come to save them.

And he was not the only one there. If he had been, the three Demon Venerables would not have been so relieved.

Two more people stood beside the Extreme Evil Soma. Two people whose presence explained how they had managed to break through the external barrier and the dark haze surrounding the ship.

“Why is it that nothing happens all our lives, and then the moment we go out to enjoy ourselves, this chaos breaks loose?”

The owner of the gentle voice was none other than the One-Flower Sword Venerable.

Beside him stood the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, his great saber resting on his shoulder.

“This is why I can’t go anywhere.”

Chapter 812: Extreme Evil Does Not Cross the Line
-------------------------------------------------
The Drunken Demon floated up into the air.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon, realizing his injuries were severe, had stretched out a hand and employed Void-Grasping. A gentle energy wrapped around him and carried him this way.

“Ah, this is nice. So comfortable!”

He said that, but the Drunken Demon was gritting his teeth against the pain. No matter how gently he was moved, there was not a single place on his body that did not hurt.

The Demon Buddha and the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable, whose injuries were relatively lighter, walked over toward the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

The Dark Palace Lord merely watched. Even if he wanted to act, he could not.

The Extreme Evil Young Demon and the One-Flower Sword Venerable were watching his movements with cold eyes.

It looked as though they were only staring at one another, but an invisible, taut current flowed between them. It was the sort of tension where, if even a finger twitched, sword qi and astral force would whirl forth.

Although they held the advantage in numbers, the Demon Venerables could not attack him rashly either. The wounded on their side were one matter, but the Dark Palace Lord still had room to spare, and his presence was no ordinary thing.

The Drunken Demon, having flown across that taut tension, landed before the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

“How did you know to come?”

After parting from Geom Mugeuk, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One-Flower Sword Venerable had gone to Taihu Lake in Jiangsu Province, as they had planned.

The two stood beneath a willow tree and gazed out over the lake. They had thought it would feel like their first time here, changed beyond recognition, but to their surprise, it looked just as it had in the past.

Memories of meeting the Cult Leader and the Fist Demon here came vividly back to them.

—Everything is the same, but only we have grown old.

At the One-Flower Sword Venerable’s wistful words, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon silently looked out over the lake. He thought that, in the past, he would have felt far more regret.

But now his heart had changed.

He had lived this far while bearing the weight of long years, and these days, that weight no longer felt only heavy.

—Isn’t that why there are good things, too?

To be honest, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon liked the present better than his youth. He liked these days of reading books beneath the sunlight more than those days when blood never dried on the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber. The same was true of his relationship with the One-Flower Sword Venerable.

The only regret was that this good present did not have much time left. But even that had been lengthened by Geom Mugeuk with the Stalactite Immortal Embryo and the Immortal Peach Spirit Fruit, so what was there to regret?

The One-Flower Sword Venerable knew very well what feelings lay behind the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s words. Her own feelings were not greatly different.

She pointed to a tavern standing on the lakeshore in the distance.

—Do you remember that tavern over there? The Cult Leader bought us drinks there.

—I remember. It was a day when the Cult Leader was furious.

—So you do remember.

—Now that I’m here, it all comes back to me.

—Shall we go have a look?

The two slowly walked along the lake to that place and drank while reminiscing. The owner had changed, and the taste of the food had changed as well, but the two passed a pleasant time lost in old memories.

Starting with Taihu Lake, they toured many places. Both of them traveled mainly to places they had never been. They sought out famous scenic views, and even stood in line at inns where skilled chefs worked. Now they knew how to enjoy the act of waiting in line itself.

While roaming the Central Plains that way, they heard through Tongcheon Pavilion that demonic flames had flared up. Even during their travels, the two had periodically stopped by Tongcheon Pavilion branches to check whether anything had happened in the cult. The organization behind everything had not yet been completely uprooted, after all.

“Why did you come here instead of going to headquarters?”

At the Drunken Demon’s question, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon showed his firm trust in one person.

“The Cult Leader will surely handle things well enough, will he not?”

He could have stopped there.

“And besides, the Poison King and the Fist Demon are there too.”

When the Drunken Demon looked as if to say, Then what about this side? the Blood Heaven Saber Demon prodded his broken arm with a finger.

“Aaaagh! That hurts!”

Of course, he had not pressed it to make it hurt. After examining the arm here and there, he pressed several acupoints and properly treated the injury.

“Fortunately, it’s a clean break. If you rest and recuperate for a while, the bone will set.”

“What about my backside, my ribs, my knees, my neck, and my elbows?”

“Seeing as you’ve found them all properly, your head must be fine.”

The Drunken Demon smiled and bowed his head in thanks.

“You did well to come here.”

If they had not come, all of them would have died. These three Demon Venerables had saved their lives.

The reason the Drunken Demon could converse so leisurely with the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was because the One-Flower Sword Venerable and the Extreme Evil Young Demon were facing off against the Dark Palace Lord in a taut standoff.

For whatever reason, the Dark Palace Lord simply watched the situation unfold in silence.

In the meantime, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable came over and handed the Drunken Demon internal injury medicine.

“It is far inferior to what you gave me, but please take this at least.”

Earlier, the Drunken Demon had given the internal injury medicine he treasured to her after she had risked her life to protect the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

“Thank you, Soul-Seizer.”

As the Drunken Demon drank the medicine, he added awkwardly,

“If someone saw this, they’d think I fought alone.”

At that, the Demon Buddha held out Golden Wound Salve and said,

“You did fight alone.”

Receiving the salve he offered, the Drunken Demon replied,

“If I had, I would already be a corpse.”

As the Drunken Demon applied the salve to his body, he said,

“You must buy me drinks.”

“When you’re fully healed, invite me to the Drunken Dream Pavilion.”

“Alcohol is medicine!”

At that moment, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable spoke cautiously.

“Please call me then as well.”

“Of course we should.”

During this trip outside the cult, the Drunken Demon had shared a deep bond with these two, the Demon Buddha and the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable. As expected, for martial artists, there was no occasion that brought people closer than fighting with their lives on the line.

The Extreme Evil Young Demon, who had been silently watching the Dark Palace Lord, asked the Drunken Demon,

“Where is the Young Cult Leader?”

The Drunken Demon’s gaze turned toward the secret coffer lying off to one side.

“It may be hard to believe, but they say he was absorbed into that coffer. I didn’t see it myself, so…”

Naturally, the gazes of the three Demon Venerables who had arrived later turned toward the Dark Palace Lord.

“Then did that man do it to him?”

They had naturally assumed he had been trapped there by the Dark Palace Lord’s sorcery.

“They said it wasn’t that, but that the coffer absorbed him on its own.”

The Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion nodded, confirming that those words were true.

That meant the Young Cult Leader’s life and death were still unclear.

Naturally, the eyes of the Demon Venerables toward the Dark Palace Lord could only be cold.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon slowly walked before the Dark Palace Lord and asked,

“And yet, even in this situation, you have no thought of fleeing.”

Someone like the Dark Palace Lord ought to have at least one method of escape. Though three of the Demon Venerables were injured, this was still a place where six Demon Venerables were gathered together.

It was likely almost the first time in the history of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult that six Demon Venerables had faced a single enemy like this.

Even in such a situation, the Dark Palace Lord was not intimidated in the slightest. Rather, with a meaningful smile that suggested he had something to rely on, he uttered words they could not understand.

“Do you think the situation changes just because more Demon Venerables have come?”

The Dark Palace Lord’s gaze turned toward one among them, the Extreme Evil Young Demon. Because there was some intent in that gaze, the Demon Venerables grew tense.

The moment the Dark Palace Lord’s eyes deepened, a dark demonic art unfolded.

Evil Heart Subjugation Art!

This demonic art, capable of controlling only wicked people, was the Dark Palace Lord’s ultimate martial art.

He had used the Evil Heart Subjugation Art, said to ensnare any wicked person without fail, on the Extreme Evil Young Demon, who was called the incarnation of evil.

‘The incarnation of evil, the Extreme Evil Young Demon, has come. Now you are mine.’

Of course, he could not completely dominate the other person. Depending on the target, he could control them for as short as fifteen minutes or as long as several days.

Naturally, it was a martial art that consumed a considerable amount of internal energy, and if it failed, he would suffer serious internal injuries.

‘I do not need even fifteen minutes. As long as I control him for half that, it will be enough!’

The three Demon Venerables from earlier had exhausted their internal energy, so there was no need to concern himself with them. He and the Extreme Evil Young Demon simply had to kill the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One-Flower Sword Venerable.

The moment the Evil Heart Subjugation Art was used, time did not flow in reality.

The instant it was deployed, one entered that person’s own cave. The deepest place within the heart, the space of instinct that the other person desired. In that space, one had only to subjugate the evil heart and bring him out.

Until now, the places he had seen had been spaces strewn with corpses, rooms filled with treasure, or places overflowing with gluttony and lust.

But this time was different. The Dark Palace Lord stood in a room that was pure white in every direction.

The Extreme Evil Young Demon, too, stood alone in the center of that room, looking at the wall.

‘As expected! He is different from ordinary wicked men!’

The longer he dragged out the time, the greater the consumption of internal energy would become, so he spoke to the Extreme Evil Young Demon at once.

“Your darkness has called me. As your master, I command you. Follow me!”

When the Evil Heart Subjugation Art unfolded, the Extreme Evil Young Demon turned toward him. It was a demonic art that took hold more easily the greater the other party’s evil heart was, and this was none other than the Extreme Evil Young Demon.

‘You can never refuse me.’

The Dark Palace Lord rejoiced as he stepped out of the room. Now all that remained was for the Extreme Evil Young Demon to come out.

But the Extreme Evil Young Demon stood before the door and did not come out.

“Come out at once!”

Despite his command, the Extreme Evil Young Demon did not come out.

This was the first time someone had defied his order after forming a contract of subjugation.

Then something came into the Dark Palace Lord’s eyes.

A single line had been drawn across the front of the door.

‘What is this?’

It was, quite literally, just a line.

The Dark Palace Lord could tell that this was the reason the Extreme Evil Young Demon was not leaving the room.

When he carefully touched it, he could feel the line. It was a unique sensation, one he had never experienced even once since birth.

The Dark Palace Lord tried to pull the line and snap it. But the line did not break. Even when he drew up his internal energy to the limit and tried to sever it, it would not break.

This time, he created a blade of astral force and cut at it.

But that thin line was not cut. No matter what methods he used, it would not be cut. The line was stronger than ten-thousand-year cold iron.

“What on earth is this?”

This line was precisely the line the Extreme Evil Young Demon had once drawn with his own hand on that white wall.

There had been a time when Geom Mugeuk and the Extreme Evil Young Demon had confirmed their relationship with each other by drawing lines. Asking how far their relationship had come.

And at the end, the Extreme Evil Young Demon had personally drawn a line on the wall of his own room, which he had seemed as though he would never damage, and asked Geom Mugeuk,

—Second Young Master, how far have we come?

That very line was blocking the Evil Heart Subjugation Art.

Extreme Evil no longer crossed the line.

The Dark Palace Lord looked at the Extreme Evil Young Demon standing beyond that line.

When his gaze met the two eyes within the mask, the Extreme Evil Young Demon’s eyes smiled coldly.

At that moment, the open door slammed shut.

At the same time, the Evil Heart Subjugation Art shattered.

Craaaack.

As the dark demonic art failed, the Dark Palace Lord’s qi and blood twisted violently. The stronger the demonic art, the greater the backlash when it failed.

He swallowed down the blood surging up within him. The Dark Palace Lord desperately kept himself from revealing that he had suffered internal injuries.

Even now, in reality, the eyes of the Extreme Evil Young Demon facing him were smiling coldly.

Even in this moment when his demonic art had failed, the Dark Palace Lord was curious about that line.

‘What on earth was it?’

Though it had been only an instant, the Demon Venerables intuitively sensed that something had occurred between the Extreme Evil Young Demon and the Dark Palace Lord.

The Dark Palace Lord’s gaze turned toward another Demon Venerable. Now that he had even suffered internal injuries, it was impossible to face the three newly arrived Demon Venerables.

His initial plan had gone awry, but he still had one chance left.

Fortunately, there was someone here who was once known to be even more fearsome and brutal than the Extreme Evil Young Demon.

The Dark Palace Lord’s gaze turned toward the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

This attempt was a great gamble. Not only would the consumption of internal energy be enormous, but if he failed again, the internal injuries added to the previous ones would leave him gravely wounded.

Even so, the Dark Palace Lord attempted the Evil Heart Subjugation Art on the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. He had to turn at least one person to his side.

This time as well, the Dark Palace Lord found himself standing in a room.

This time, it was a room filled with books. A room that should have been filled with corpses was filled with books instead.

‘Where on earth is this?’

Books were piled not only against every wall, but also all around where he stood.

‘Could they be martial arts manuals?’

He flipped through the book lying at the very top of the stack before him.

‘A collection of poems and paintings?’

The book beside it was about growing flowers, and the one beneath it was a book on how to officiate a wedding ceremony.

‘Don’t tell me I’ve fallen into qi deviation?’

For a moment, he wondered if he had fallen into qi deviation because he had forced himself to use martial arts while suffering internal injuries.

Just then, a sound came from the corner.

Rustle, rustle.

The Dark Palace Lord turned his head in that direction.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was sitting in the corner, reading a book. The sight was so unfamiliar and strange.

‘Why are you reading a book in a place like this?’

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was absorbed in his reading, and only the sound of pages turning could be heard.

The Dark Palace Lord spoke quietly to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

“Your darkness has called me. As your master, I command you—follow me!”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who had been reading, rose from his seat and followed him out.

Now all he had to do was leave this room. Fortunately, the line that had been in the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon’s room earlier was not there.

The Dark Palace Lord tried to lead him out, but this time he could not find the door.

When he toppled a stack of books, another pile of books appeared beyond it. Beyond that were more books, and more books still. It was truly a room where books were piled up endlessly.

The longer he delayed, the more his inner energy continued to be consumed, so the Dark Palace Lord, flustered, searched between the books even more urgently.

Then the stacked books came crashing down on him.

At that moment, the Evil Heart Enslavement Art was broken.

Craaaack!

This time, the Dark Palace Lord could not hide the fact that he had suffered internal injuries.

“Gueeek.”

As he violently spat blood, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon said,

“What are you doing, all by yourself?”

His face flushed with humiliation, the Dark Palace Lord raised his head and looked at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon. In the cold gaze with which the Blood Heaven Saber Demon stared at him, there seemed to be no mercy.

He was so different from the way he had looked reading his book earlier that it felt as if he were another person entirely.

‘What in the world are these people?’

He had truly never dreamed that his ultimate demonic arts would fail one after another.

The Dark Palace Lord stretched his hand out to the side. His palm was aimed at the secret coffer.

“If you don’t leave at once, the Young Cult Master dies.”

That threat took effect. If Geom Mugeuk had been absorbed into that thing, then at the very least, it could not be allowed to be destroyed.

“Get lost, now.”

Silence flowed through the hall.

As the Dark Palace Lord drew up all the inner energy he had left, the dark gangqi gathering in his hand grew even more powerful.

“I really will smash it to pieces.”

If he was going to die anyway, he had no intention of dying quietly.

“If I go together with the Young Cult Master, it won’t be such a bad bargain.”

At that moment, one of the Demon Venerables uttered something no one had expected.

“Break it.”

It was none other than the Demon Buddha.

“What bluffing nonsense!”

“Break it! Smash it to bits!”

There was a definite reason for the Demon Buddha’s bravado.

“At last, the Eldest Young Master will become the successor!”

At his expression of genuine delight, the Dark Palace Lord’s hand trembled. It looked as though the gangqi might fly out at any moment.

At that hair-trigger moment!

Something happened that shocked everyone. Someone popped out of the secret coffer.

“Whoops!”

The person the secret coffer spat out was Geom Mugeuk. As he emerged from within, he had heard every word the Demon Buddha had said.

“This is exactly why I can’t afford to leave my post!”

Chapter 813: Just as Those Here Have Done
-----------------------------------------
Needless to say, even the Dark Palace Lord was startled.

Even then, the Dark Palace Lord still had his hand extended toward the Secret Coffer.

Quick-witted Geom Mugeuk grasped the situation at once.

“You were going to break this? Not a chance. It will never break.”

Three hundred years ago, even when the Heavenly Demon Hyeok Docheon had hammered it with his fists like a madman, the Secret Coffer had not budged.

“And as you can see, hostage-taking doesn’t work on those cold-blooded, heartless Demon Venerables!”

Then he shouted at Demon Buddha.

“Break it? Smash it to pieces? I was inside that thing!”

The Drunken Demon turned to the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable and made a gesture as if to say that now was finally the time to put in earplugs. At the sight, the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable smiled.

With Geom Mugeuk’s appearance, the atmosphere changed at once.

“Have you still not given up on my brother?”

Demon Buddha’s deep relief could be felt. He had bluffed in front of the Dark Palace Lord, but how could he have meant it?

He had shouted because he was certain the Dark Palace Lord could never destroy the Secret Coffer. Since he had suffered internal injuries and even coughed up blood, that Secret Coffer had been his final hope.

Of course, he had been certain, but who knew what might happen in this world? What if that bastard really did smash the Secret Coffer? Demon Buddha’s heart had truly been pounding as if it would burst. At this moment, the one happiest to see Geom Mugeuk was him. Of course, he expressed it like this.

“The First Young Master might have become the successor. What a shame.”

Demon Buddha glared at the Dark Palace Lord and said,

“Why did you hesitate so much? You should have just smashed it.”

Geom Mugeuk turned to the Dark Palace Lord and said,

“See? I told you they were vicious people.”

Despair flashed across the Dark Palace Lord’s face. Three unharmed Demon Venerables, and now Geom Mugeuk as well.

On the other hand, his Greed Great Annihilation Art had vanished, even his ultimate demonic arts, the Dark Heaven Star Soul Art and the Evil Heart Subordination Technique, had failed, and he had suffered severe internal injuries.

Now there was only one move left to him. The one move he had saved for the final moment of his life.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable and feigned surprise.

“Wait, why are you two here?”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon shook his head with an incredulous expression. It really was just like Geom Mugeuk. To think he could startle people like this.

“We heard there was an uproar because someone had gone and crawled inside a lump of metal.”

“I didn’t go in. I was dragged in. Phew, if you knew what I saw, you’d be shocked.”

Geom Mugeuk glanced at the Dark Palace Lord. He was staring at Geom Mugeuk with eyes shadowed in darkness.

“Curious what I saw?”

Geom Mugeuk acted as if he was about to tell him.

“I’m not telling you.”

For an instant, the Dark Palace Lord’s face twitched. Truly, as soon as he returned, this young cult leader reminded him anew of just how infuriating he could be.

Watching that, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled.

“It seems the Young Cult Leader did not change inside the Secret Coffer.”

Geom Mugeuk asked her,

“Did you enjoy your journey?”

“It was the most enjoyable journey of my life.”

A pleasant smile appeared on the face of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who stood beside Jayeon.

“Don’t let this be the only time. Please travel often.”

“I intend to. Next time, I’m thinking of going to the southern region.”

Geom Mugeuk did not forget the words the Blood Heaven Saber Demon liked hearing most these days.

“You’ve somehow grown even younger while I wasn’t looking!”

He needlessly averted his gaze, but his smile only deepened.

After exchanging greetings with the two of them, Geom Mugeuk next attended to the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable.

“Soul-Subduer, are you all right?”

“Yes, Young Cult Leader.”

She, too, had suffered internal injuries, but they were not serious.

“By any chance, did those two bully or torment you for being the youngest? Did they dangerously make you take the lead?”

“If anything, I was overprotected.”

The Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable was overjoyed to see Geom Mugeuk again.

Since she had survived, her chance to become the first Demon Venerable of the next generation’s Demonic Cult Leader had survived with her.

Now she intended to add one more thing to that.

The future Demon Venerable whom the Cult Leader trusted most.

Just as the current Cult Leader had the Fist Demon, she wanted to stand in that place beside Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Drunken Demon, then made a belated fuss.

“No! Who on earth did this to our esteemed Drunken Demon?”

Geom Mugeuk glared at the Dark Palace Lord and said,

“Was it you? It was you, wasn’t it? You bastard!”

Just then, the Drunken Demon’s voice came from behind him.

“Why don’t you stop acting?”

“Acting?”

Unable to hold back, the Drunken Demon shouted,

“If you were that worried, you should have come to me first! You should have checked on me first! I was hurt the worst! And now you come to me last and pretend to be worried?”

Geom Mugeuk sidled over and lightly touched the Drunken Demon’s broken arm.

“What kind of Demon Venerable goes around breaking his arm?”

The three Demon Venerables who had arrived later could guess from that fact alone how difficult this fight had been. For someone at the level of a Demon Venerable, a broken arm was something unimaginable.

“I got drunk and fell on my face.”

“You should be more careful.”

The Drunken Demon looked at Geom Mugeuk’s hand holding his arm.

From that faintly trembling hand, he could feel Geom Mugeuk’s emotions.

Thank you for staying alive, brother.

At that moment, Demon Buddha said calmly,

“The Drunken Demon saved us.”

The Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable also nodded with an expression that said the same.

“That’s why I came late. The protagonist appears at the end, and you go to the protagonist last.”

Leaving behind the Drunken Demon, who looked needlessly proud, he turned toward the Extreme Evil Young Demon. From behind him, the Drunken Demon shouted,

“I’m not the last one!”

Pretending not to hear, Geom Mugeuk looked at the Extreme Evil Young Demon.

It was clear that he, who had been in charge of the outside, had brought in the two Demon Venerables who had joined them. His precise judgment must have saved them all.

The Extreme Evil Young Demon asked with a smile,

“Did you have a good trip?”

Because the Extreme Evil Young Demon had been there, he had been able to travel to the world three hundred years ago with peace of mind.

“Thanks to you, I did.”

The Dark Palace Lord watched with his eyes, but he could not understand their relationship. He had heard countless reports that Geom Mugeuk was close to the Demon Venerables.

But seeing it in person was shocking. He exchanged jokes like this with Demon Venerables? They exchanged gazes of such trust? It was a gaze he himself had never experienced even once. Not even from Hoe, who was his own hands and feet.

Once Geom Mugeuk finished greeting the Demon Venerables, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon asked with a curious expression,

“What on earth happened inside that lump of metal?”

He had said he would not tell the Dark Palace Lord, but to the Blood Heaven Saber Demon he answered honestly.

“The Secret Coffer showed me the time three hundred years ago, when Cheonui Palace started the war.”

He did not say that he had gone directly three hundred years into the past. They would simply think the Secret Coffer had shown him the past.

Amid everyone’s surprise, Geom Mugeuk asked the Dark Palace Lord. Now, he no longer concealed that name.

“What kind of person is Hwa Mugi?”

“!”

It was the first moment he had ever directly mentioned Hwa Mugi to anyone.

The Dark Palace Lord’s eyes flew wide open. He had never imagined that name would come from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth. Even the Divine Maiden Palace Lord Ga Ye had only known of his existence, not his name.

“The Secret Coffer told me that name.”

The Demon Venerables understood. The being Geom Mugeuk had vaguely feared was this person called Hwa Mugi. The Dark Palace Lord was not the final enemy.

The Dark Palace Lord’s gaze turned to the Secret Coffer.

“This Secret Coffer is our sacred relic. Why would it go to you?”

“To be precise, it’s the sacred relic of Cheonui Palace. You are a descendant of the Dark Palace that existed beneath Cheonui Palace.”

The Dark Palace Lord was curious. Why had the Secret Coffer absorbed Geom Mugeuk rather than him? In what way had the Secret Coffer revealed the truth? And what else had it told him?

“Do you have nothing to tell me?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, the Dark Palace Lord gave no answer.

Then Geom Mugeuk drew the Black Demon Sword without hesitation.

“Fine. I’ll respect your wishes.”

The three Demon Venerables standing behind him also raised their internal energy in unison, preparing for battle.

As they looked ready to charge at any moment, the Dark Palace Lord quickly said,

“He is not someone the likes of you can handle.”

Of course, Geom Mugeuk knew very well why he was so confident. With those five souls and their internal energy dwelling within him, how could he believe Hwa Mugi would lose to anyone?

“If you have heard of him, you should also know this is no time to be acting at ease here.”

The Dark Palace Lord was trying by any means to separate Geom Mugeuk from them and send him to the main headquarters. That, at least, was the thin thread of hope by which he might survive.

“You’re asking why I don’t run to my father right away? I’ll go. But even if I go, I have to kill you first. I’ll watch you die completely in front of me before I leave. That’s why I came out in the first place.”

Because as important as that place was, this place was important too.

But the Dark Palace Lord interpreted Geom Mugeuk’s composure differently.

“That is not it, is it? If your father dies, you can become the Cult Leader.”

At their conversation, the Demon Venerables’ gazes darkened. It was not because they suspected Geom Mugeuk of having such intentions, but because of the martial prowess of this man called Hwa Mugi.

Judging from the conversation, Hwa Mugi seemed to possess enough force to kill the Cult Leader. Geom Mugeuk, too, seemed to acknowledge it.

“As you can see, the people of our family bloodline aren’t the sort to meekly hand over their positions.”

The Dark Palace Lord laughed coldly. In his mind, Hwa Mugi’s defeat had never existed from the beginning.

“In any case, you will all die. Not just you—the Martial Alliance and the Evil Path Alliance will all be swept away. You have ruled this murim for far too long. Now a new era will open.”

Geom Mugeuk stared at him and said,

“Even if that happens, it will not be your success. Nor will it be your era. At best, you will become Hwa Mugi’s subordinate.”

Anger surged within the Dark Palace Lord. Because that was what he hated hearing most.

“Do you know why you failed?”

The words he hated hearing were only beginning now.

“You stayed in the darkness for far too many years. The darkness devoured you.”

“Of course it did. I am the Dark Palace Lord.”

“Because you are the Dark Palace Lord, you should have come out into the light. Only when you had the light at your back would you have been able to see exactly what your darkness was.”

Geom Mugeuk began to shake his heart.

“Since this is the end anyway, I’ll be honest. I was disappointed when I met you in person. You weren’t as impressive as I had imagined. Ah, of course, from the methods you chose up until now, I had expected you to be someone insignificant. But still, I thought you might have a different kind of style when I saw you directly. Yet you had none. And I understood why.”

The Dark Palace Lord felt humiliated and ashamed.

“You had hardened like a fossil in that darkness over far too many years. You had even forgotten what it meant to be impressive.”

“Shut up!”

At the torrent of blunt criticism, the Dark Palace Lord roared.

“I have lived my entire life sacrificing myself for the great cause of this palace. I have lived with only the thought of vengeance!”

But that great cause did not work on Geom Mugeuk.

“Then why did you not have a successor?”

“What?”

“If your true purpose was the great cause, you should have had a successor. If you could not achieve it yourself, you should have passed that will on to a successor and made them achieve it. Because even as time passed, that noble will would not change. But you did not have a successor. Shall I tell you why? Because you were not fighting for a great cause. You only had the greed to become the master behind the scenes and rule this murim.”

The Dark Palace Lord glared fiercely at Geom Mugeuk. Truly, no one could ever know just how infuriating this Geom Mugeuk was unless they experienced it themselves.

At last, the Dark Palace Lord began to speak of himself.

“This was my father’s dream.”

The moment a distant memory crossed the Dark Palace Lord’s face, the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable understood.

When she had entered the Dark Palace Lord’s mind during the earlier fight, the person he had been looking up at with a bright smile had been his father.

Had he come this far with only that one good memory?

“The power we obtained was not something an ordinary human could handle. Countless people died because of that power. After learning that only a human born under the aura of the Heavenly Slaughter Star could accept that power, I spent my entire life searching for that destiny. After that, I spent a long time trying to find the Six Paths Primordial Qi in order to control the power contained in the Heavenly Slaughter Star. And you, a mere man born as the Heavenly Demon’s son who has lived in comfort, dare mock that life of mine?”

The Dark Palace Lord’s rage burst forth. His internal injuries flared up, and blood trickled from his mouth.

Geom Mugeuk, on the other hand, was calm.

“You say you inherited your father’s dream and are trying to fulfill it? What a filial son. I am an unfilial son trying to stop my father’s dream.”

He meant it sincerely, but to the Dark Palace Lord, it sounded like mockery.

A deep aura of darkness flowed from the Lord of the Dark Palace’s eyes. One could sense that he had steeled himself for something.

“How do you think I designed my end?”

“You’re not the sort to end things in any grand fashion, so you’re probably thinking of dying together with us somehow. With some trifling self-destruction art.”

He had expected the man to have prepared a self-destruction art, and that expectation was correct.

Light began to run over the characters drawn on the Lord of the Dark Palace’s face. Geom Mugeuk had thought it was a great art meant to seize the Six Paths Origin Energy lodged within his body, but it was not.

Ghost Script Heaven-Explosion Art (鬼文爆天功).

It was by no means a trifling self-destruction art. It was the most powerful self-destruction art, one that even borrowed the power of ghosts.

As the characters on the Lord of the Dark Palace’s face and body shone, indecipherable writing began to be carved into the floor and the empty air. Those in the room were trapped within the characters that filled their surroundings.

“You will go with me into eternal darkness.”

The very moment he was about to unleash the Ghost Script Heaven-Explosion Art, the three Demon Venerables were already moving.

Shuk!

Thud!

A streak of finger wind flew toward his face. It was the Blood Grudge Finger of the Extreme Evil Little Demon. The dark aura that had always protected him blocked the Blood Grudge Finger.

“You cannot stop me!”

While the aura of darkness surrounded and shielded him, the light of the ghost scripts carved into his body and all around him shone even more intensely.

In this moment of self-destruction, the Dark Demon Art was, paradoxically, shining more brilliantly than any martial art.

Shuk! Shuk! Shuk! Shuk!

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

The Blood Grudge Fingers that flew as fast as light struck the exact same spot they had hit before.

Puk!

The finger winds, striking the same place again and again in succession, pierced through the aura of darkness.

Thud!

The instant the Blood Grudge Finger slammed into the Lord of the Dark Palace’s face, the dark aura weakened.

Puuuk!

As if she had been waiting for that very moment, the Ilhwa Sword pierced straight through the darkness and ran through his chest.

Slaaaash!

At the same time, the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber cleaved cleanly from his shoulder down to his chest.

Paaaak!

His body split open, and blood surged up like a fountain.

The Lord of the Dark Palace, injured internally and trying only to hide within the aura of darkness while unleashing his self-destruction art, could never have blocked the attacks of the three Demon Venerables.

Geom Mugeuk walked over to where he had collapsed on the floor.

With his consciousness fading, the Lord of the Dark Palace left his final words.

“……You will not be able to stop him.”

Geom Mugeuk readily admitted it.

“That’s right. I probably won’t.”

And he was not at all ashamed to say it.

“Instead, my father will stop him. The Alliance Leaders will stop him, and the Demon Venerables will stop him.”

Geom Mugeuk looked back at the six Demon Venerables standing behind him. After meeting the eyes of each of them as they looked at him, he said,

“Just as those here have done for me now.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze returned to the Lord of the Dark Palace.

“Your darkness was a darkness that hid itself while sacrificing all of its subordinates. A darkness that refuses to face the light is a darkness that must vanish from this world.”

The Lord of the Dark Palace opened his mouth as if to say something, but instead of words, only blood gushed out in choking spurts.

Geom Mugeuk’s final words were courteous.

“So harbor no more lingering attachment to this world, and return to the eternal darkness you love so much.”

Though great regret still remained in the Lord of the Dark Palace’s eyes, he could not defy death’s summons. His eyes slowly closed, and soon his breath ceased.

The aura of darkness that had faintly lingered around them also scattered into the air and disappeared forever.

At last, even the Lord of the Dark Palace, the final chief, had died.

Now only one person remained: Hwa Mugi.

Chapter 814: Who’s Doing the Dishes?
------------------------------------
Everyone who had been in the cabins came up onto the ship’s deck.

The dark smoke that had surrounded the ship, and the barrier that had lain over the lake around it, had all vanished.

The long night had passed, and dawn was breaking.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the boats in the distance ferrying the gamblers away. Countless small skiffs were carrying the gamblers of the Fortune Ship toward land.

Martial artists of the Ghost Shadow Unit were aboard the boats, and Geom Mugeuk waved toward the one Ian was riding.

“Ian!”

When Geom Mugeuk waved, Ian cried out in joy as well.

“Young Cult Leader!”

Seeing that Geom Mugeuk was safe, she jumped up and down in delight despite her subordinates watching. Her Young Cult Leader mattered more than dignity.

From the boats in every direction, cheers of joy and victory erupted all at once. Not only Geom Mugeuk, but all the Demon Venerables who had entered the ship had appeared on deck.

On the skiff were the four squad leaders of the Ghost Shadow Unit: Cheongmyeon, Chairan, Seojin, and Jihan. They, too, looked at Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Venerables with joy.

Cheongmyeon’s gaze, which had been fixed on Geom Mugeuk, shifted to the Extreme Evil Young Demon.

The words the Extreme Evil Young Demon had spoken to him came back to him.

—If you wish to come back, you may.

Whether he returned or not, simply hearing those words had made this expulsion from the cult deeply meaningful for Cheongmyeon.

I am glad you are safe.

Though they would not be able to see him from so far away, Cheongmyeon bowed politely to the two of them.

Chairan understood. The fact that they had emerged safely from this final battle meant—

‘The Dark Palace Lord is dead.’

It was the moment when the darkness that had followed her vanished forever.

‘You really did it.’

Had she not chosen Geom Mugeuk, her name would have been added to the list of victims as well.

Seojin and Jihan cheered, delighted that the Ghost Shadow Unit’s first mission had ended safely.

“That was a round I was winning! Because of you people!”

Jihan struck the back of the red-eyed gambler’s head.

“You lived because of us!”

He had survived for now, but if he could not escape gambling, he would ultimately meet ruin.

Yang Dong, the owner of the Fortune Ship who was aboard another skiff, was inwardly astonished.

When he learned that a terrifying conspiracy had unfolded on his own ship, he had truly thought he would die. Yet, astonishingly, the demonic practitioners had spared them.

“I will be sure to tell the Sado Alliance that you saved us.”

Ian, who was on the same boat, nodded.

Well, even if he did not say anything, there was someone who would boast until the young lord of the Sado Alliance’s ears ached, so everyone would find out anyway.

Ian’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk in the distance. Even standing among many people, Geom Mugeuk shone.

Geom Mugeuk’s shout came from far away.

“You all worked hard!”

The cheers of the Ghost Shadow Unit martial artists grew even louder. From farther away, the thunderous cheers of the demon troops waiting there could also be heard.

After raising his subordinates’ morale in that way, Geom Mugeuk bid farewell to the Demon Venerables.

“I will return to the main cult first.”

The Demon Venerables nodded. Since no one could match Geom Mugeuk’s lightness skill, he would arrive earliest if he ran alone.

“We will follow as quickly as we can.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, and the Extreme Evil Young Demon decided to follow after Geom Mugeuk with qinggong.

Because of their injuries, the Demon Buddha, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable, and the Drunken Demon had to return by carriage. They would go as quickly as possible, but it could not compare to the speed of lightness skill.

Gaye sat on the deck, staring blankly at the lake. The Dark Palace Lord’s death did not sadden her, nor did it give her satisfaction.

Her heart was indescribably complicated. What would become of her now?

‘Is the Young Cult Leader going to leave just like that?’

Before he left, she had to ask and confirm.

“Are you sparing me?”

“I have already spared you.”

Before entering the room where the Dark Palace Lord had been, Geom Mugeuk had given her a chance to leave.

“Without the Six Paths Primordial Qi, you cannot control him.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“I know.”

“Are you not worried? The Six Paths Primordial Qi is with you, Young Cult Leader.”

“Of course I’m worried. Worried enough to go mad.”

A look of puzzlement passed over Gaye’s face.

“And yet you have the leisure to have this conversation with me?”

An unexpected answer flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“Because matters concerning you are just as important.”

“!”

Gaye had this thought. Yes, because he is this kind of person, he has formed such deep bonds with the Demon Venerables.

She felt she now understood why the Dark Palace Lord had been unable to defeat this Young Cult Leader. To defeat someone like this… yes, he had become far too hardened, like a fossil.

“Even so, there is one thing I believe in. In the process of obtaining the Six Paths Primordial Qi, not only I, but my father and the Demon Venerables all became stronger. And everyone changed greatly. I believe in the power of that change as much as I believe in the power of the Six Paths Primordial Qi.”

Gaye felt as though it was fate that she would say these words.

“From now on, memorize the formula I recite. I don’t know exactly how this formula works, either. It is a formula that has been handed down since long ago in connection with the Six Paths Primordial Qi.”

Perhaps what the Dark Palace Lord had feared most was that she would pass this formula on to Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk memorized the formula Gaye recited with perfect accuracy. The formula was not very long, but he knew instinctively. This was the very formula essential to controlling Hwa Mugi.

“Thank you truly.”

The Heavenly Flower Pavilion Mistress, who had been watching from the side, spoke to her.

“Heaven has not abandoned you.”

A startling statement flowed from the Heavenly Flower Pavilion Mistress’s mouth.

“This form of yours was the prophecy I saw.”

“!”

“I saw you passing the formula on to the Young Cult Leader.”

She, who had once seen the prophecy of the Extreme Evil Young Demon stabbing Geom Mugeuk in the heart, had this time seen this scene as a prophecy.

Gaye stared at the Heavenly Flower Pavilion Mistress with wide, astonished eyes.

“So that is why you spared me.”

The Heavenly Flower Pavilion Mistress shook her head. It was true that she had told the Demon Venerables to spare her. It was also true that this was why she had survived.

“I do not think I spared you. I think you were destined to live.”

Feeling an indescribable surge in her chest, Gaye gazed at the sun rising in the distance.

Perhaps heaven had given her one final chance. To see whether she would pass the formula on to Geom Mugeuk.

Her shadow appeared in the sunlight.

Suddenly, she recalled what Geom Mugeuk had said to the Dark Palace Lord. When one turns one’s back to the light, only then can one see exactly what one’s darkness is.

This time, Gaye’s gaze turned toward the sky.

For the first time in a very long while, she looked up at the heavens. She had only resented heaven; it had been truly long since she had looked up at it like this.

As she watched the light pushing back the darkness, this feeling rose naturally within her.

‘For the rest of my life, I will live helping people with a heart of atonement.’

Through the conversation between the two women, Geom Mugeuk understood. The Six Paths Primordial Qi was important enough for a prophecy like this to descend. It was time to return.

Geom Mugeuk bade farewell to everyone.

“I will go to the main cult first. I’ll see you later.”

Leaping lightly into the air, Geom Mugeuk became a speck in an instant and disappeared.

“He’s become even faster.”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s admiration, everyone shook their heads with astonished expressions. It was truly a lightness skill so incredible that even a Demon Venerable’s jaw would drop.

With pure internal energy infused with the mysterious power of the Six Paths Primordial Qi, and with his body transformed as well, Geom Mugeuk’s Swift Step cut through the air at extreme speed.

‘Father, I am coming!’

* * *

The day after Murim Alliance Leader Jin Paecheon came to Pungryu Tavern, after finishing breakfast, Jin Paecheon suddenly asked,

“But who does the dishes?”

He had not thought of it yesterday because there had been no time, but now he suddenly became curious.

There was no one here besides themselves, so one of them had to do the dishes.

No matter how he looked at it, there was no one who seemed suited to washing dishes.

The Poison King did it? Judging by appearances alone, of course it looked like he should be the one washing dishes.

Or the Fist Demon? Could he even wash bowls with those huge hands? No, he would break them all.

At that moment, the Sword King spoke to Jin Paecheon.

“As the youngest, I am in charge of washing the dishes.”

“You are the youngest?”

“Yes, that is correct.”

Jin Paecheon once again thought that the Poison King truly did look young for his age.

“Today, I will help you.”

Everyone stared at Jin Paecheon in surprise. The Murim Alliance Leader was going to wash dishes?

At their astonished gazes, Jin Paecheon looked around and said,

“It is the way of things that the eldest and the youngest should not be troubled.”

Whatever he was thinking, Jin Paecheon helped the Sword King carry the dishes they had eaten from and went out to the rear building behind the tavern.

Baek Jagang saw through Jin Paecheon’s thoughts.

“Honestly, such greed for people. He is coveting Martial Artist Ak.”

He meant that Jin Paecheon was acting that way to curry favor with Ak Gunhak.
