He himself wanted to take Ak Gunhak to the Sado Alliance. How reassuring would it be to have such an absolute master at one’s side?

Cult Leader Pungcheon looked toward the rear building visible through the open door and said,

“Even if the Young Cult Leader gives up the Gentleman Sword, do you think he would give up that man? Not a chance.”

“If Alliance Leader Jin puts in the effort, you never know.”

At Baek Jagang’s words, Cult Leader Pungcheon nodded.

“True. That man does seem to have a disposition closer to the orthodox sects.”

At that moment, Heavenly Demon Geom Ujin spoke firmly.

“That will not happen.”

He had already made an agreement with Ak Gunhak to protect Geom Mugeuk.

Thinking that the Demonic Cult Leader had already made his move, Baek Jagang shook his head.

“The truly greedy one was right here.”

Their gazes turned again to Jin Paecheon, who was squatting beside the Sword King and washing dishes. In all likelihood, it was the first time in his life he had ever washed dishes.

“Even if you wash every dish in the world, it will be for nothing, Alliance Leader Jin.”

At Baek Jagang’s quiet words, Cult Leader Pungcheon laughed and said,

“Still, it is a sight that will never be seen again in the history of the martial world, so let us watch carefully.”

* * *

Several days had passed since Jin Paecheon joined them at Pungryu Tavern.

In that time, the Heavenly Slaughter Star still had not come.

When Jin Paecheon first came here, he had never imagined he would spend days like this in the same space with these people.

After becoming the Murim Alliance Leader, had he ever lived with anyone in such cramped quarters?

And the people in question were the Demonic Cult Leader and the Sado Alliance Leader?

What in the world could be more uncomfortable than that?

He could fight alongside them!

But eating together, washing together, sleeping in the same space—he had thought that would be a hundred times harder and more uncomfortable.

Unexpectedly, it was all right.

It truly was not bad. He had thought he would never adapt to it, but it was manageable.

A person one met briefly and a person one saw while living together for several days were certainly different.

To him, Geom Ujin had felt endlessly cold and arrogant.

“Sleep here. This spot has less of a draft.”

Geom Ujin gave up his own place. This man had such a side to him?

“It is all right.”

“Use this spot.”

How could a draft be a problem for someone of Jin Paecheon’s martial realm? Even so, it was Geom Ujin’s consideration for Jin Paecheon, the eldest among them.

“Thank you.”

And Geom Ujin spoke better than he had expected. He had thought the man was entirely taciturn, but he conversed quite well not only with him, but also with the Sado Alliance Leader, the two Demon Venerables, and the Sword King.

When he thought about why he had held the preconception that Geom Ujin was a man of few words, it was because Geom Ujin never said anything useless. Even when he spoke, he said only exactly what was necessary, so he had seemed like a man without words.

Next was Baek Jagang. This man was serious about cooking.

“Is there any food you like?”

“I eat anything well enough.”

“Even so, is there not something you particularly enjoy? I will learn it from Cult Leader Geom and make it for you.”

When he first heard those words, Jin Paecheon wondered if perhaps the man intended to poison the food at a decisive moment. Truly, if the Poison King had not been here, he would have continued eating with that suspicion in mind. To think he had to worry not about the Poison King’s poison, but the Sado Alliance Leader’s!

But after watching him for several days, he saw that Baek Jagang enjoyed cooking.

“Is cooking enjoyable?”

Today as well, Baek Jagang was hard at work making something in the kitchen. He was making a soup dish he had learned from Geom Ujin.

“There is joy in doing something I have never done in my life. Alliance Leader, you should learn once as well.”

“I have no talent for cooking. I would rather wash dishes.”

At that, Cult Leader Pungcheon, who was sitting at the counter, said,

“We might as well all open an inn together.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon began imagining a fictitious inn.

“Cult Leader Geom and Alliance Leader Baek will take charge of the kitchen. Martial Artist Ak and the Poison King over there will be the waiters. The Fist Demon will handle drunken customers who make a scene, and Alliance Leader Jin will do the dishes.”

When he left himself out, Jin Paecheon asked,

“Then what do you intend to do, Cult Leader?”

The man who had assigned the Heavenly Demon Cult Leader and the leader of the Evil Alliance to cooking, and the Murim Alliance Leader to washing dishes, replied shamelessly,

“Why, I should naturally sit at the counter. Judging by my bearing, don’t I look like the owner?”

Everyone listening smiled.

In this dangerous meeting, where war could have broken out several times over, no one could deny that a pleasant atmosphere was being maintained thanks to Cult Leader Pungcheon.

Of course, the two alliance leaders also kept well within the line. They did not bring up needlessly serious topics, and spoke instead of light and enjoyable things as much as possible.

For as long as they were here, everyone set down for a moment the heavy burden of bearing the weight of the world.

It was probably because of what Geom Ujin had said.

—Let us end this fight with our generation.

This fight was not a fight for their own honor, nor was it a fight for the peace of murim.

This fight was for their children.

That cause was stronger than any other, and so, at least here in this place, they considered themselves on the same side. They believed it so.

Cult Leader Pungcheon went one step further.

“Let’s leave murim to the clever young successors, and let us all live mingling like friends. How much longer can we live, anyway? Let’s stop glaring at one another and live.”

Jin Paecheon looked at Geom Ujin and Baek Jagang. In truth, if he were told to get along with Cult Leader Pungcheon like a friend, he could picture it somehow. The man was so cheerful and so good at lifting the mood.

But to mingle like friends with those two? Was that truly possible?

Then again, considering that he was even imagining something he would never have thought of in the past, their relationship had indeed come a very long way.

The Fist Demon brought tea to the table where Geom Ujin and Jin Paecheon sat facing each other.

Baek Jagang poked his head out from the kitchen and said,

“Have a cup of tea while you wait. The meal will be ready soon! Look forward to today’s cooking!”

The Fist Demon set teacups down before the two of them.

Geom Ujin’s hand, reaching for his cup, stopped in midair.

Ripples spread across the surface of the tea.

They were very faint ripples.

The next moment.

A larger ripple appeared.

A third, a fourth—the ripples gradually grew.

Geom Ujin knew what those ripples, like heartbeats, were.

Someone was walking toward this place. With each step, the ripples were forming.

They were not ripples born from heavy, thudding steps.

The person was walking without a sound, yet their very presence was creating them.

Jin Paecheon, seated before him, and the Fist Demon, who had brought the tea, were both looking down at those ripples with sunken gazes.

Had they ever, in their lives, felt such an overwhelming presence?

The Sword King, who had been wiping a table nearby, set down his rag, and the sound of the chopping board from the kitchen ceased.

The Poison King, drawn out of his own world, had eyes deeper and colder than ever before.

Without concealing their presence,

someone was walking toward this place.

Chapter 815: Let's Be Friends Just for Today
--------------------------------------------
The closer he came, the greater his presence grew.

It was as if his very existence was pushing the space aside as he walked.

Jin Paecheon, the Murim Alliance Leader who had been seated across from Geom Ujin, rose from his seat.

This was not a presence one could face while sitting at ease, and in any case, the host who would receive him here today was Geom Ujin. He and Baek Jagang had only come to assist Geom Ujin.

Jin Paecheon stood a short distance away from the table.

Srrk.

A kitchen knife emerged and lifted the bead curtain hanging over the kitchen. Behind it stood Baek Jagang, who spoke as if regretful.

“I simmered it so well, too.”

His tone was relaxed, but his eyes gleamed more sharply than ever. He put out the fire he had been tending, set down the kitchen knife, and walked out of the kitchen.

The Sword King slowly moved to stand by the rear wall. It was not far from the corner seat where the Poison King sat.

He did not know how the Poison King would fight, but he thought that when the enemy came charging in, someone would have to hold him back for the Poison King’s poison arts to shine.

Had he noticed that intention?

The Poison King glanced sidelong at the Sword King, then turned his gaze back toward the entrance.

In the meantime, carrying a tension that felt as though it might burst at any moment, the opponent arrived before the Pungryu Tavern.

A man entered the tavern.

He had long, flowing jet-black hair and a tall, well-proportioned frame. At a glance, his body seemed perfect for mastering martial arts. His gaze was intense, and a red aura lingered in his pupils.

It was neither killing intent nor a projected bearing. It was not an aura a person deliberately revealed, but an innate energy.

Everyone understood. That energy was the aura of the Heavenly Slaughter Star. It was the first time any of them had seen it in person.

He wore a single sword at his waist, and its sheath was black.

And what was most striking of all was that he was younger than they had imagined.

The moment the man entered the tavern, that overwhelming presence vanished. Since it meant he could reveal and conceal his presence at will, they found themselves unable to relax.

Without hesitation, the man strode over to the table where Geom Ujin sat and, without a trace of fear, took the seat Jin Paecheon had occupied.

To sit beside an enemy while that enemy was standing was an act of madness. Especially when the men standing there were the Murim Alliance Leader and the Fist Demon.

If not for the presence he had shown earlier, one might have thought some drunken passerby had wandered in by mistake.

The man picked up the teacup that had been poured for Jin Paecheon and drank it down.

Then, setting the cup down, he said politely,

“Not bad.”

It was unclear whether he meant the taste of the tea, or the people waiting for him here.

The man’s gaze turned to Geom Ujin.

“You are the current Demon Cult Leader.”

His voice was as composed as his eyes. He did not deliberately add weight to it, nor did he display a rough, savage momentum. Nor did he act light on purpose.

An opponent whose weight was difficult to measure. That was the man’s first impression.

Geom Ujin nodded and answered.

“So you are the Heavenly Slaughter Star.”

At that, the man smiled gently. His words flowed naturally, as if he had been conversing with them here all along.

“Is it not absurd? That a person is born bearing a destiny? No matter how kindly I try to live by my own will, in the end I am fated to cause a blood calamity. Cult Leader, do you also believe destiny cannot be defied?”

Geom Ujin said in a low voice,

“Why ask that? You proved it by coming here.”

Their gazes locked in midair.

“If you do not wish to be called the Heavenly Slaughter Star, state your name.”

“You are worthy of hearing my name.”

The Fist Demon, standing beside Geom Ujin, was ready to throw a punch at any moment. To him, that calmness was both the utmost danger and arrogance.

“Hwa Mugi.”

The man was Hwa Mugi. The very man who, in Geom Mugeuk’s life before regression, had killed the Cult Leaders and Alliance Leaders who had been here, now appeared at the Pungryu Tavern.

Hwa Mugi extended the empty teacup to the side, then opened the hand holding it.

Crash.

The teacup fell to the floor and shattered.

“Yes. Just as this cup fell and broke, I could not defy destiny.”

At that, Geom Ujin slowly drank his tea.

Then he dropped the empty cup to the floor in the exact same way as Hwa Mugi.

But this time, it did not break.

Just before the cup struck the floor, it rose through the air by the art of seizing objects through empty space and settled back into its original place.

“Destiny can be defied.”

Looking at Hwa Mugi, Geom Ujin added,

“There was a time when I, too, liked the sound of things shattering.”

Hwa Mugi’s expression brightened.

“I was right to come to you first. I feel better now.”

But the faint red light gleaming in his pupils grew even deeper.

Hwa Mugi met the eyes of each person there in turn.

His gaze met Jin Paecheon’s, then turned to Baek Jagang.

From the look in his eyes as he regarded them, they could tell. Hwa Mugi knew that Jin Paecheon was the Murim Alliance Leader, and that Baek Jagang was the leader of the Unorthodox Alliance.

Even so, he was not shaken in the slightest.

His gaze then met the eyes of the remaining people as well.

The Cult Leader Pungcheon and the Fist Demon, the Sword King and the Poison King.

Even if he did not know exactly who they were, he should have been able to estimate their strength. Yet Hwa Mugi’s expression did not change in the least.

That was why even these words sounded like false modesty.

“You overestimated me far too much.”

“It was not I who overestimated you.”

The person who came from Geom Ujin’s mouth was none other than Geom Mugeuk.

“My son overestimated you.”

From the very beginning, even the reason he had raised the Demon Flower was because of Geom Mugeuk. Bringing along the various Demon Supremes, leaving behind the Fist Demon and the Poison King—everything.

It was because he had believed all of it to be Geom Mugeuk’s arrangement. And his son’s prediction had been correct. Someone who could remain at ease even in this situation had truly come.

‘Son, was this the existence you feared?’

He was indeed an opponent worthy of fear. The incomprehensible potential he could sense from the other man was something he had never seen in his life. He could not predict at all what might spring out from within him.

For now, the mere fact that he remained calm in this situation proved that the opponent was truly extraordinary.

“In truth, the person I most wanted to meet was your son.”

“Then why did you seek me out?”

Unexpected words flowed from Hwa Mugi’s mouth.

“To wound your son.”

Hwa Mugi looked straight into Geom Ujin’s eyes and added,

“By killing you.”

Silence fell over the hall. It was the moment Hwa Mugi made his reason for coming unmistakably clear.

He had come to kill Geom Ujin. In Geom Mugeuk’s life before regression, he had first gone to the Murim Alliance, then to the Unorthodox Alliance, and finally to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Now, in reverse, he had come to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult first.

The Fist Demon silently clenched his fist. He would kill the Cult Leader to wound Geom Mugeuk?

At that moment, Cult Leader Pungcheon said to Geom Ujin,

“What a relief.”

The one who melted this suffocating atmosphere was, as expected, Cult Leader Pungcheon.

“Wouldn’t it be unpleasant if his reason for trying to kill you were something like the destiny of the heavens and so on?”

Geom Ujin responded to his words.

“Are you saying it is fine for my son to be wounded, then?”

“The Young Cult Leader is someone who turns wounds into armor, so it will be fine. He will become an even better Cult Leader.”

Hwa Mugi, listening to their conversation, burst into a broad smile. He truly smiled often.

Hwa Mugi looked around and changed the subject.

“I heard a three-party meeting was held here?”

Hwa Mugi sprang up from his seat and walked toward the second floor of the tavern. Ordinarily, this was a place filled with opponents among whom a single misstep, a single wrong breath, could cost him his life. The instant someone attacked, hell would unfold here.

Yet he moved naturally. His movements were neither grand nor extraordinary. He was so full of openings that it was actually difficult to find an opening to attack.

On his way to the second floor, his eyes met those of the Poison King and the Sword King by the rear wall.

Hwa Mugi passed them, then stopped before the stairs to the second floor and looked back at the Sword King.

“A horsetail hairstyle and bare feet. It’s you, isn’t it? The traitor.”

The Sword King showed no reaction and remained standing as he was.

“You’re brave.”

He meant that the Sword King was waiting for him like this despite having betrayed them.

The Sword King opened his mouth for the first time. Though he had once been part of that place, he was seeing the person before him for the first time.

“I have never seen anyone as brave as you.”

Hwa Mugi smiled brightly and said to the Sword King,

“I still won’t go easy on you.”

Hwa Mugi went up to the second floor. He looked at the words written on the wall.

“They really are here.”

It seemed he had come knowing that the writings were there. With his hands clasped behind his back, Hwa Mugi read them. After slowly reading every last one, he looked down from the second-floor railing and said,

“You people are different from the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions I know. They truly did nothing but fight, endlessly and disgustingly.”

A similar question arose in everyone’s mind. He was young, so what orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions was he talking about?

Could he perhaps be an old master who had returned to youth?

It was entirely possible. For now, they observed Hwa Mugi cautiously. At the moment, they allowed him to lead the situation. What they needed now was information about their opponent.

“When I leave, I should leave a line of writing here as well.”

One of the people downstairs could no longer bear to watch his leisure.

“Say what you want to leave behind in advance. I will leave it for you.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon had provoked him.

At that, Hwa Mugi leaned against the second-floor railing and asked Cult Leader Pungcheon,

“Who are you?”

It did not seem that he was deliberately trying to provoke him; he truly appeared not to know.

If so, then astonishingly, he must have come here without knowing exactly who was present. He had simply strapped on a single sword and charged alone into the main altar of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“I am not an important person, so pay me no mind. Remember me as the one who will leave behind your last words.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon’s pride had been needlessly wounded, but fortunately, Hwa Mugi recognized him belatedly.

“Ah, you are Cult Leader Pungcheon.”

Hwa Mugi recognized Cult Leader Pungcheon.

“I heard you were at the Divine Cult as well.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon’s gaze turned colder than ever. This was not the gentle demeanor he had shown until now.

“Yet they still sent you, so it seems they must have hoped you would die here.”

Instead of taking the bait, Hwa Mugi readily acknowledged it.

“That’s right. He did hope I would die. Together with all of you.”

Hwa Mugi had seen through the mind of the Dark Palace Lord.

“Do you know why I did not kill him even though I knew that? He knelt and begged me to spare him. He said he would offer me the Young Cult Leader’s head.”

They could tell he was not lying. The opponent before them was someone to whom the Dark Palace Lord, the mastermind behind this matter, had knelt and begged. The true leader was this man.

Upon realizing that clearly, Geom Ujin smiled.

‘Well done, son.’

It was praise for his son’s choice in leaving the true leader to him.

Cult Leader Pungcheon said with certainty,

“Even if you did not kill him, the Young Cult Leader will have cut off his head.”

“You people truly believe in the Young Cult Leader.”

He looked back at the second-floor wall again.

“Then again, this is not something just anyone can accomplish.”

Looking at the wall, Hwa Mugi said,

“Let us say your child is extraordinary. Even so, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult interacting with the orthodox faction? Is that not somewhat shameful?”

He asked without turning around, but everyone knew whom the question was for.

“Not at all.”

At Geom Ujin’s firm answer, Jin Paecheon was inwardly surprised.

‘The Cult Leader answered sincerely.’

He was not a man who lied. He would rather say nothing.

Then Hwa Mugi changed the target of his question.

“I suppose I should ask our Murim Alliance Leader this.”

Hwa Mugi looked back at Jin Paecheon and asked,

“Why are you here?”

Since he had not expected to be asked so suddenly why he was here, Jin Paecheon was inwardly flustered.

If he had instead been asked whether he was ashamed, he would have answered that he was not.

But explaining the reason he had come here would be a long story.

“Is it all right for the Murim Alliance Leader to associate with the Demon Cult Leader like this?”

“I am not here as the Murim Alliance Leader.”

“Then as what are you here?”

After a brief pause, Jin Paecheon answered calmly.

“I am here as that man’s friend.”

Even as he said it, Jin Paecheon wondered if this was right. A friend? Their relationship was still far too distant for the word friend, and even if they grew closer, they were people who could not become friends.

Yet when that bastard Hwa Mugi pressed him, the word friend slipped out before he knew it.

At that moment, Geom Ujin’s voice transmission came to him.

‘Let us be friends just for today.’

At the voice transmission that understood his heart all too well, Jin Paecheon looked at Geom Ujin and nodded.

It was just then. Suddenly, Hwa Mugi shouted.

“What nonsense is this? Alliance Leader Jin! You must not be here! You must never associate with that wicked Demon Cult and that cunning Unorthodox Alliance!”

Not only Jin Paecheon, but everyone watching was startled. Hwa Mugi spoke with a feeling completely different from what he had shown until now.

“If you associate with them, you will regret it for the rest of your life. Leave this place at once, even now.”

He seemed to be speaking out of genuine concern.

When Hwa Mugi suddenly acted like a different person, everyone there stared at him in bewilderment.

“I said leave at once! Hurry!”

In that moment, he looked like a man who had lost his mind.

Everyone thought Hwa Mugi was mocking the Murim Alliance Leader through an act.

But only Geom Ujin saw it. For the briefest instant, the red aura flowing in his eyes had vanished, and in its place, the bearing of the orthodox faction had flickered past.

‘That was definitely the aura of the orthodox faction.’

In other words, he was not acting. After a long wait, Geom Ujin had learned an important truth.

‘Something is inside that man’s body.’

Chapter 816: I Can Be the Demon Cult Leader, But Not the Murim Alliance Leader
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Geom Ujin sensed it instinctively.

Whatever dwelled within Hwa Mugi’s body would affect the outcome of today’s battle.

There was a reason he was trying to assess his opponent as calmly as possible.

Could that Heavenly Slaughter Star have come without knowing that he had achieved Great Completion of the Nine Flames Demonic Art? No, that couldn’t be. He had come knowing even that writing had been left on the second floor of the Pungryu Tavern.

And yet, for his opponent to be so relaxed meant he had prepared for it in some way.

‘What have you prepared against the Nine Flames Demonic Art?’

Before the fight began in earnest, he had to find the answer to that question.

Suddenly, Hwa Mugi laughed like a madman. When he abruptly stopped laughing, he had returned to normal.

“Friends? At your age, what are you doing babbling about friendship?”

Jin Paecheon stared at Hwa Mugi in disbelief. Until just a moment ago, it had truly sounded like advice a senior of the orthodox faction was giving him.

He had felt the desperate sincerity of someone who had been destroyed after associating with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Sado Alliance, telling him not to suffer the same fate.

But the Hwa Mugi before his eyes now felt completely different from before.

“Alliance Leader Jin, you’re lonely right now.”

Hwa Mugi calmly dug into Jin Paecheon.

“When you look down from a high mountain, beasts and people alike are all jostling together as they live. They all live on your mountain, but what about you yourself? There’s no one up there, is there? You’ve lived your whole life blazing brightly beneath the sun, so there isn’t even a single cloud to exchange a word with, is there?”

Hwa Mugi knew Jin Paecheon’s past life well.

“But no matter how lonely you are, should you really take a dark cloud laden with thunder as your friend? The downpour pouring from that cloud will sweep away everything living on your mountain.”

Despite his young age, he spoke like an old soul. To the point that one might think he really had rejuvenated after reversing his age.

In any case, since Hwa Mugi spoke so well, the things he had said earlier truly seemed like an act meant to mock Jin Paecheon.

That he was lonely.

Ordinarily, those words would have lodged deep in Jin Paecheon’s heart. After all, his relationship with Geom Ujin was one that never should have been formed in the first place.

Jin Paecheon looked at Geom Ujin. Geom Ujin was watching Hwa Mugi with calm eyes.

Seeing his face, Jin Paecheon suddenly recalled the sound transmission he had received a short while ago.

—Let us be friends just for today.

Those words became a shield for him. Hwa Mugi’s words, at least for today, could not pierce him.

He had come for his grandson’s sake, but yes, what of it if he became friends with the Demonic Cult Leader for a day?

“It seems one has to be at least this insane to become the Heavenly Slaughter Star.”

The one who stepped forward was the Pungcheon Cult Leader. He provoked Hwa Mugi in Jin Paecheon’s stead.

“What about you? Do you have any friends?”

For a moment, Hwa Mugi said nothing.

“Right, I figured you didn’t. You don’t have a single friend, so who are you to say anything to anyone? Me? I have one.”

Hwa Mugi’s target shifted from Jin Paecheon to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“I hear you even threw away your position as cult leader to make that one friend?”

“You must not have heard properly. I threw away not only my position as cult leader, but also my sacred relic and my pride.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader added cheerfully.

“In exchange, I’m living a far more interesting life. If I had stayed beyond the frontier, I wouldn’t have gotten to see this amusing spectacle.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader was not frightened at all. Usually, he was in charge of spouting nonsense, but once an enemy appeared, he stepped to the very front.

Hwa Mugi brought up what the Pungcheon Cult Leader had said earlier.

“Did you say that if the Young Cult Leader loses his father, he’ll turn that wound into armor and become an even better cult leader?”

It was what the Pungcheon Cult Leader had said to Geom Ujin earlier.

Hwa Mugi walked over and flung open the tavern window. Far in the distance, smoke from the demonic flames rising from the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s main headquarters could be seen.

“I’ll kill all of you here, and then I’ll kill every last one of those bastards over there too. Those who have learned martial arts, those who haven’t—every single one, without exception. I won’t leave so much as a dog alive.”

A faint killing intent spread from his body. His killing intent was unlike anything they had experienced until now. Unfamiliar, yet intense. Within that killing intent, they could feel a promise. I will keep this, without fail.

Hwa Mugi looked this way and grinned.

“If the entire cult disappears, he won’t be able to become a good cult leader, will he?”

It was an arrogant declaration that he would slaughter the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult all by himself.

Before the regression, he had killed only everyone in the Heavenly Demon Hall’s household, but now he intended to kill the entire Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Because of Geom Mugeuk’s regression, Hwa Mugi’s fate had also changed.

On the other hand, even at the words that he would massacre the main headquarters, Geom Ujin showed no reaction. He merely watched Hwa Mugi’s every movement in silence.

Beside him, the Fist Demon sensed that Geom Ujin was different from usual.

What he saw in Geom Ujin now was not soundless rage. He had even set rage aside.

‘Cult Leader, what are you looking at right now?’

Hwa Mugi’s arrogance did not stop with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

His gaze turned toward Jin Paecheon.

“Next, I’ll go to the Martial Alliance and kill every last orthodox bastard there.”

The faintly flowing killing intent finally turned toward Baek Jagang.

“You people will be last.”

It was the moment the most arrogant threat in the entire history of the murim was uttered: that he would annihilate the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions alike.

Hwa Mugi turned to the Pungcheon Cult Leader and said,

“Pungcheon Cult Leader, because you ran your mouth carelessly, even those people’s subordinates will all die.”

He shifted the responsibility for their deaths onto the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader wanted, in his heart, to run his mouth even more carelessly, but since the lives at stake were those of the Martial Alliance and Sado Alliance martial artists, he could not bring himself to say anything.

Truly, Hwa Mugi was an impossible man to grasp. He would smile brightly and seem utterly at ease, then sincerely threaten to massacre countless lives.

Then someone spoke from behind.

“He came here to kill everyone from the start, so don’t pay attention to what that bastard says.”

If the Pungcheon Cult Leader had stepped forward for Jin Paecheon, the one who stepped forward for him was Baek Jagang.

“The Martial Alliance Leader over there says he came because of a friend. Sado Alliance Leader, why did you come?”

At Hwa Mugi’s question, Baek Jagang answered leisurely.

“I came to see what the Heavenly Slaughter Star looks like.”

“And now that you’ve seen me?”

“You’re younger than I expected. More talkative than you look, too. Ah, perhaps you’re actually old. Seeing how much you talk.”

“Isn’t this an enjoyable occasion you’ll never experience again in your lifetime?”

Those words sounded as though he was saying he would toy with them to his heart’s content before killing them.

Just as he had done to Jin Paecheon, Hwa Mugi provoked Baek Jagang as well.

“What would the unorthodox martial artists think, seeing you caught between the orthodox faction and the Demonic Cult, watching one side and then the other?”

Baek Jagang’s reaction was different from Jin Paecheon’s.

“What would they think? ‘Ah, he’s reading the room well to survive! You’re doing great!’ That’s what they’d say. I only pick bastards like that.”

Baek Jagang was someone who, by nature, felt no inferiority complex whatsoever over words like these. That was why Bi Sain liked this side of him.

Baek Jagang did not wish for the sun, nor did he want to become darkness.

He planted his feet on the earth and lived like the wind.

That was the Sado that Baek Jagang dreamed of.

“Don’t expect anything so grand just because someone is a cult leader or an alliance leader. Just as you, the Heavenly Slaughter Star, are not grand at all.”

Geom Ujin and Jin Paecheon saw a new side of Baek Jagang. Since he had never said such things before, this was the first time they learned that Baek Jagang had this side to him.

Jin Paecheon in particular reflected on himself upon seeing that side of Baek Jagang. He could not speak quite like that, but he realized that, though they were both alliance leaders, he himself had lived too rigidly bound by forms.

Could he say such a thing as the Martial Alliance Leader? If he said such a thing, how would others see him? Could these words perhaps disgrace the honor of the orthodox murim? He realized he had bound himself with far too many restrictions.

Come to think of it, in his youth, he had truly been a free man.

At some point, he had stopped living as Jin Paecheon and had lived only as the Martial Alliance Leader.

In the vast leader’s hall of the Martial Alliance, he had never felt the emotions he had felt so many times over the past few days in this narrow tavern. He thought it was truly a good thing he had come.

Hwa Mugi, meanwhile, seemed pleased by Baek Jagang’s answer and smiled broadly in delight.

“The unorthodox faction I saw was gloomy and impossible to read. But you’re refreshingly straightforward!”

“You’re different from the Heavenly Slaughter Star I imagined, too.”

“What was the Heavenly Slaughter Star you imagined like?”

Curiosity flickered across Hwa Mugi’s face.

“Just because someone was born under the Heavenly Slaughter Star, they wouldn’t rampage about blindly declaring they’d kill everyone. The Heavenly Slaughter Star is one who cannot adapt to the world, who is pushed away from relationships with people, and who, with no other choice, brings about a bloodbath in the world. But you don’t seem to have any wounds at all. You’re just a murder maniac who has never once tried to live properly.”

Baek Jagang’s provocation was sharp. Even so, Hwa Mugi was even more delighted.

“Oh, you’re even clever! I like you.”

Hwa Mugi quietly stared at Baek Jagang, then abruptly said,

“Shall I spare you today?”

It was said with certainty, as though it was only natural that everyone else would die.

Baek Jagang nodded.

“Words like that are welcome any time.”

Then Hwa Mugi strode toward Baek Jagang.

A taut tension formed between the two of them in an instant, and Baek Jagang’s eyes narrowed even further.

Passing the distance at which either could kill the other by reaching out a hand, Hwa Mugi went so far as to stand right beside Baek Jagang.

As if they had become friends, he looked at Jin Paecheon together with him.

“What do you think of that Martial Alliance Leader? That hypocritical justice of his, wanting to become friends with the Demonic Cult Leader while claiming he’ll uphold the justice of the murim.”

Baek Jagang’s gaze turned toward Jin Paecheon. Jin Paecheon said nothing and merely listened silently to their conversation.

Unexpected words flowed from Baek Jagang’s mouth.

“He might be able to be the Demonic Cult Leader, but I don’t think he could be the Martial Alliance Leader.”

It was the first time Baek Jagang revealed his thoughts to Jin Paecheon.

“You can’t do evil deeds, you have to help even people you’ve never seen before, and even while knowing you’ll die, you have to sacrifice yourself for chivalry and goodwill. Isn’t it a path you can barely walk even if you act hypocritically? For me, even if I acted hypocritically a hundred times, a thousand times, it’s a path I couldn’t take.”

Baek Jagang slowly looked at Hwa Mugi standing beside him and asked,

“Honestly, you couldn’t take that path either, could you?”

Hwa Mugi’s bright smile changed into a strange one.

“How disappointing. I thought we saw eye to eye.”

Their gazes tangled in midair. Tension flowed. The two were so close that it seemed something might happen at any moment.

“Didn’t you say earlier? That the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions you know aren’t like this.”

Hwa Mugi, while reading the words on the wall on the second floor, had clearly said that.

“As you can see, this side is an exceptional case. Go pretend to be close over there.”

Naturally, no kind words came from Hwa Mugi’s mouth.

“Is that exceptional case the situation of being swept along in the young cult leader’s chaos?”

Baek Jagang struck straight into his heart.

“You’re afraid of the Young Cult Leader.”

In that instant, Hwa Mugi’s expression hardened.

“Isn’t your plan to kill that cult leader over there and wound the Young Cult Leader because you fear him?”

Baek Jagang asked, looking into his eyes. Baek Jagang’s eyes were small, but deep and dark as an abyss.

“Why do you fear the Young Cult Leader?”

It was at that very moment.

Swaaaaaaaaaaah.

A crimson aura burst fiercely from Hwa Mugi’s eyes.

It was tremendous killing intent. They had never felt killing intent this powerful before. His killing intent had surpassed the limits of what a human being could produce. It was such terrifying killing intent that any ordinary master would lose his life from it alone.

The protective qi of everyone there activated all at once.

“You verminous unorthodox mongrel!”

Hwa Mugi, who had always laughed things off whenever he was angered, transformed terrifyingly.

“Lower your eyes before I rip those tiny eyeballs out!”

This time, a rough and violent side completely different from what he had shown before burst forth.

Hwa Mugi poured out abuse.

“An unorthodox bastard who should be selling liquor in some back alley dares run his mouth in front of me? You things don’t even have the guts to become true evil. You’re more pathetic than those orthodox scraps!”

His momentum was such that he seemed ready to draw his sword and fight Baek Jagang at any moment.

The sense of crisis between the two was pulled taut as if it would snap at any second, but it did not break.

Once again, everyone thought Hwa Mugi was mocking Baek Jagang. After all, he had shown so many sides of himself like a lunatic.

But one person was the exception.

Geom Ujin had been watching only Hwa Mugi’s pupils the entire time.

In that fleeting instant, this time demonic qi brushed past. It was such a momentary reaction that anyone but him would have found it hard to notice. It was like sensing, for the briefest instant, that a soul had changed.

‘So there’s a demonic practitioner inside that body too!’

Geom Ujin could tell that Hwa Mugi’s body did not contain only the orthodox master he had seen earlier. And judging from what Hwa Mugi had said before—that the unorthodox faction member he had seen had not been like that—

‘All the souls of the demonic, orthodox, and unorthodox factions are inside that man’s body.’

It was truly unbelievable, but thinking it over again, he felt that because Hwa Mugi was such a Heavenly Slaughter Star, he might have been capable of attempting something so reckless.

‘Are there truly only three souls?’

It was the first time even Geom Ujin had encountered a case where multiple souls dwelled in a single person’s body.

If so, there was one thing he now had to confirm.

Geom Ujin sent a sound transmission to the Fist Demon.

—Land a blow on him.

The Fist Demon had truly been waiting for that order alone.

Whoooong!

The Fist Demon’s fist surged toward Hwa Mugi, flying straight at his face.

Hwa Mugi, who had been looking at Baek Jagang, turned his body slightly and extended a hand behind him, blocking the Fist Demon’s punch. The fist struck his palm.

Paaaaang!

In the aftermath of the collision, a tremendous wave of force spread in every direction. The chopstick holders and empty cups set on the other tables were all sent flying, rolling across the floor.

Hwa Mugi did not budge a single step from where he stood. The Fist Demon had not thrown the punch with all his might, but even so, it was not a fist that could be blocked so easily.

“The Demon Sovereign dares throw a fist at me?”

Just as Hwa Mugi turned around with a terrifying gaze—

Whoaaang!

Without hesitation, the Fist Demon’s second punch flew in first and struck Hwa Mugi squarely in the face.

Puuuuck!

Judging by his ability from before, he clearly could have avoided it. Yet Hwa Mugi had deliberately taken the blow.

His head had snapped back so violently it seemed as though it might have been torn off, but then it rose forward again.

Despite that powerful impact, not even a small scratch appeared on his face. It was an astonishing protective qi aura.

“That hurt less than the rumors claimed.”

At Hwa Mugi’s provocation, the Fist Demon’s fearsome face hardened coldly.

But the Fist Demon quietly stepped back. The Cult Leader’s order had been to land a blow on him, not to fight him to the death.

Geom Ujin was watching Hwa Mugi, who had already returned to his original state.

There were two things Geom Ujin had learned.

They might pop out abruptly, but they could not act entirely according to their own will. If that had not been the case, that demonic practitioner earlier would surely have gone berserk.

Geom Ujin recalled what the demonic practitioner had said.

—The Demon Sovereign dares throw a fist at me?

Dares? A demonic practitioner who could use the word “dares” toward the Demon Sovereign?

‘There’s a Heavenly Demon of a past generation inside him!’

Whether it was the previous generation, the one before that, or someone from long ago, he could not know, but there was no doubt that a Demonic Cult Leader was inside him.

If that was the case, then the answer to the question he had first asked had now emerged.

What had you prepared to counter the Guhwa Demonic Art?

‘This man uses the Guhwa Demonic Art as well!’

Chapter 817: If My Heart Had Been Warm, I Would Have Died with You
------------------------------------------------------------------
“Still, taking a hit really does clear the head.”

Hwa Mugi needlessly stroked his face, which did not have so much as a scratch on it. It was an exaggeration, and a provocation.

But the Fist Demon did not so much as blink at that provocation.

His gaze was fixed on Hwa Mugi, but his mind was turned toward the Cult Leader.

The Cult Leader was a man who never lost his composure under any circumstances. Yet now, the Fist Demon could sense that he was paying attention to something else.

‘What on earth is happening, my lord?’

There was no doubt that he had realized something by making him attack Hwa Mugi.

At that moment, Geom Ujin’s sound transmission flew to the Fist Demon.

—That man isn’t acting. Other souls have entered his body.

Geom Ujin told the Fist Demon first what he had discovered.

It had been that way from their youth until now. Whenever something important happened, he always told him first.

What shocked the Fist Demon even more was the sound transmission that followed.

—It seems the soul of one of the past Demon Cult Leaders is inside that man’s body.

—!

Only now did the Fist Demon understand why Geom Ujin was so grave. If it was not someone else, but a Demon Cult Leader who had entered him, then this was the worst possible situation.

—Do you know who it is?

—I have a rough idea.

Whenever his son went out from the cult and returned, he would tell him every little detail of what had happened outside.

This time I caught this bastard like this, and there seems to be something behind him, and from now on we need to be careful of this. Geom Ujin had listened to all of his son’s chatter.

Because that time had not been time spent receiving reports, but time spent together with his son. Because it had been conversation shared with his son.

That time with his son was finally proving its worth. If he had merely read the Tongcheon Pavilion reports and moved on, he would not have been able to recall it instantly at this moment.

‘It is them from three hundred years ago!’

He already knew that the origin of the Dark Palace, the force behind this conspiracy, had begun three hundred years ago in the battle between the Heavenly Will Palace and the Martial Alliance.

The orthodox master who had spoken to Jin Paecheon earlier had shown deep regret for having associated with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Sado Alliance. To be able to speak so strongly even though his opponent was the Martial Alliance Leader?

‘He was the Martial Emperor Divine Sword from three hundred years ago!’

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword was the most famous figure among all past Martial Alliance Leaders, known even to Geom Ujin.

If the two people he had seen were the Martial Alliance Leader and the Demon Cult Leader, then the soul of the unorthodox faction he had yet to see was surely the Sado Alliance Leader of that era.

Now Geom Ujin understood why Hwa Mugi was so strong.

Judging by the way he had suffered no impact at all from the Fist Demon’s punch earlier, it was not merely that souls dwelled within him.

‘All of their power has been combined inside that man!’

If that was the case, this would by no means be an easy fight.

No matter how many people they had on their side, their strength would not combine in proportion to their numbers.

The more skilled the masters, the easier and yet harder a combined assault became.

They knew better than anyone when and how to intervene, but conversely, it was never easy to intervene while another was unfolding martial arts of the highest level.

What if he tried to defeat them individually with overwhelming martial might? Aside from himself and the two Alliance Leaders, the rest might be taken down by him in an instant.

The best method was for their side to kill him first, all at once.

The first thing that came to mind was Heavenly Demon World-Extinction.

It could kill him cleanly and certainly, but there was a chance others would be caught up in it and die.

And an even greater problem was that Hwa Mugi might also be able to unleash Heavenly Demon World-Extinction.

The fleeting instant Heavenly Demon World-Extinction was unleashed.

Raise the Great Demon Wall.

And while the Great Demon Wall melted away, release Heavenly Demon World-Extinction.

If the opponent used Heavenly Demon World-Extinction, he would respond like that.

At the very least, it meant he would not simply die.

But what if that Hwa Mugi made the same choice?

No one here would survive.

The next method that came to mind was killing him with the Heavenly Demon Soul.

If the Heavenly Demon Soul manifested, Heavenly Demon World-Extinction could not be used. That was because the Heavenly Demon Soul was an absolute being born from the culmination of the Guhwa Demon Art and the ultimate principle of that martial learning, so the Guhwa Demon Art could not be used to oppose the Heavenly Demon Soul.

The problem was that, by their very nature, Heavenly Demon Souls were beings that refused to fight one another. If one manifested, there was a high possibility that the other would not appear, so there was no telling whose Heavenly Demon Soul would manifest.

Geom Ujin conveyed what he had discovered to the two Alliance Leaders through sound transmission.

If Hwa Mugi used the Guhwa Demon Art, there was a high chance he would use it against the two Alliance Leaders, so they had to know in advance as well.

Jin Paecheon was inwardly tense. It was a tension incomparable to anything thus far. The fact that his opponent could use the Guhwa Demon Art was terrifying, but what bothered him even more was this.

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword is inside that man’s body?

Among all the past Alliance Leaders, the one he respected most was none other than the Martial Emperor Divine Sword. The footprints he had left in the history of the orthodox murim were truly great.

If he truly was that man, as Geom Ujin said, then why was he dwelling inside the body of the Star of Heavenly Slaughter?

Did it mean there was some hidden story behind the noble history he had left in murim?

On the other hand, Baek Jagang had no interest in which Sado Alliance Leader was dwelling inside that body.

‘So that bastard can even use the Guhwa Demon Art, is that it?’

As much as he felt fear, his fighting spirit rose. Perhaps today, at long last, he might face the Guhwa Demon Art in person.

While those sound transmissions were being exchanged, Hwa Mugi was absorbed in provoking someone new.

“Our Poison King is awfully quiet.”

As far as they knew, Hwa Mugi was the first person ever to provoke the Poison King even after knowing his identity.

“You’re curious, aren’t you? Whether I’m impervious to all poisons or not.”

The Poison King sat quietly in a corner seat, gazing at him. His eyes had a depth that did not suit his youthful-looking face.

“You are absolutely not impervious to all poisons.”

Aside from his conversation with Geom Ujin, this was the first time the Poison King had spoken here in the Pungryu Tavern.

“Why are you so certain I’m not impervious to all poisons?”

The reason the Poison King thought so was simple.

“Because such a noble fortuitous encounter would never have been granted to you.”

To those who used poison, being impervious to all poisons was beyond a natural enemy; it could be called a natural disaster.

Even so, the Poison King described it as a noble fortuitous encounter. Because he truly loved poison, he did not even regard one impervious to all poisons as an enemy.

He also believed that Geom Mugeuk had received that precious fortune because his character was just as noble.

Though the words disregarded him, Hwa Mugi laughed instead of getting angry.

Unless another personality suddenly emerged, Hwa Mugi did not fall for most provocations. He was a man who even expressed anger with laughter.

“Right, I’m not impervious to all poisons. Instead, I suppose I’m impervious to about nine thousand nine hundred and ninety-nine poisons.”

It meant that while he was not perfect, he was prepared against most poisons.

“I wonder if you can accurately find the poison that works on me. The moment you administer any poison at all, I’ll kill you first.”

Hwa Mugi slowly stepped toward the Poison King. As he moved, the texture of the air flowing there changed. It was a different kind of tension from when he had approached Baek Jagang.

“Or should I just kill you now?”

The red energy in Hwa Mugi’s eyes deepened even further. He looked as if he would charge at the Poison King at any moment and cut off his head.

By contrast, even in that moment of crisis, the Poison King remained seated where he was.

“Why aren’t you poisoning me? Are you afraid of me?”

But the Poison King was not defenseless because he was afraid.

“Because the Cult Leader’s order has not been given.”

“!”

It was the Poison King’s will that he would administer poison only when Geom Ujin gave the order. In other words, if no order came, he would simply die.

“You asked how I would find a poison that works on you?”

Green light seeped from the Poison King’s eyes as though spreading.

“I won’t look for one.”

Then came the astonishing reason.

“Because if the order is given, I will administer all ten thousand poisons to you.”

“!”

It was truly a madly arrogant statement. How could one person carry ten thousand poisons? But when those words came from the Poison King’s mouth, their weight was different.

A thrill filled with madness flashed across Hwa Mugi’s face as he looked at the Poison King.

“I want to hurry up and cut off your head.”

Hwa Mugi turned his head toward Geom Ujin.

“And bring it to that man. I want to see how that Cult Leader accepts your death!”

It was clear he meant it sincerely. Though it did not show on the surface, Hwa Mugi’s killing intent had been rising higher and higher ever since he came here.

Just as Hwa Mugi began walking toward the Poison King again!

Shhk.

A single sword, drawn without a sound, blocked his path.

Astonishingly, that sword was the Sword King’s sword.

Since no one had expected him to step forward for the Poison King, everyone’s gaze focused on him.

The Poison King himself also looked at him with surprise in his eyes. In truth, from the very beginning, the Sword King had taken a place not far away in order to protect the Poison King.

“You dare draw a sword before me?”

Since those words were directed at the Sword King, they too were truly arrogant. But as they had come from the mouth of someone who had declared he would annihilate the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions alike, they were not especially surprising.

The Sword King answered calmly.

“Understand. This is all I have to put forward.”

It was far too powerful to be called “all.” When the White Flower Sword was held in the Sword King’s hand, its presence felt like a fortress wall hundreds of jang high blocking the way.

When the red energy flowing from Hwa Mugi’s body met the cold air streaming around the sword, it froze in midair. The White Flower Sword was blocking the waves of red energy like a breakwater.

“So you were a cold man? I heard you betrayed us and joined hands with the Young Cult Leader, so I thought you had a very hot heart.”

The Sword King replied calmly.

“If my heart had been hot, I would have died together with you.”

A new thrill rose across Hwa Mugi’s face. His expression truly said this: Now this is interesting.

“I’ll kill you second.”

Just as Hwa Mugi tried to pass him and approach the Poison King.

Kagak!

The Sword King’s sword split the air with lightning speed.

The tip of the sword, rising at a sharp angle, narrowly grazed Hwa Mugi’s jaw.

It was an attack so fast one had to wonder how anyone could possibly dodge it, yet Hwa Mugi had dodged.

Still with his head tilted back, Hwa Mugi looked at the Sword King with arrogant eyes.

“Do you want to be first?”

The Sword King was clearly showing his resolve that Hwa Mugi could not reach the Poison King before cutting him down.

With calm eyes, the Sword King recalled the past.

“The Lord of the Dark Palace always told me this. That what we were doing was following Heaven’s will.”

“Traitor, in the end, you did not follow Heaven’s will, did you?”

“Because Heaven’s will was not with you.”

In that instant, Hwa Mugi’s eyes shone intensely.

“Then where is that Heaven’s will? Is it here? No matter how much I look around, I don’t see it.”

After a brief pause, the Sword King said something astonishing.

“Heaven’s will lies with the Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Ujin, Jin Paecheon, Baek Jagang, Cult Leader Pungcheon, the Fist Demon, and the Poison King all listened to the Sword King’s words in silence.

At that very moment, Hwa Mugi’s eyes changed.

A new personality revealed itself from within Hwa Mugi.

“How dare a mere traitor speak of Heaven’s will!”

Geom Ujin could tell. This was a soul that had not appeared until now.

‘Is it the soul of the Sado Alliance Leader?’

No. Judging by how violently it reacted to the words Heaven’s will.

‘It is the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace!’

With this, Geom Ujin became certain.

The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and even the Sado Alliance had intervened in the war at the time, and something must have happened then. The souls imprisoned at that time had been passed down until now.

For a full three hundred years.

He wondered whether such a thing was truly possible, but he was witnessing it with his own eyes.

Hwa Mugi asked in a cold voice.

“Do you even know what the will of heaven is?”

Then came the answer of the Sword King, who had dreamed of freedom.

“I do not know the will of heaven, but I do know what the sky is. It is the place where birds live, where clouds live, where the wind lives, and where the sun lives.”

The Sword King mentioned the one person who always came to mind whenever he thought of the sky. The person he wanted to see most even at this very moment.

“When you people kept prattling on about Heaven’s will, Heaven’s will, Heaven’s will, the Young Cult Leader told me to look up at the sky. Not some grand meaning—he just told me to straighten my back and look up at the sky once.”

At those words, Geom Ujin thought of his son and smiled faintly.

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang were the same. If not for Geom Mugeuk, they would never have been here.

The Sword King honestly revealed the words he had kept in his heart.

“Watching the Young Cult Leader, I came to understand. Heaven’s will is not something granted with some grand meaning.”

It was also the first moment he revealed to everyone all of his thoughts on Heaven’s will.

“Heaven’s will is not something that descends; it is something one bears. It is to willingly shoulder the burden placed before one who can endure its weight.”

The Sword King looked into Hwa Mugi’s eyes and posed his final question.

“Do you see the burden laid before you? Or have you spent your whole life pretending not to, seeking only Heaven’s will?”

Until now, no matter how enraged the souls within Hwa Mugi’s body became, they had never crossed that line.

But this time was different. To be told there was no Heaven’s will by none other than the one who had betrayed the Palace—his fury crossed the line.

Shiiiiiik.

Hwa Mugi’s sword was drawn with the speed of light.

His sword was unusual: a black sword (黑劍), its blade dark as night.

Kaaang!

The instant he blocked the incoming blade, the Sword King was driven sliding backward.

Shiiik!

When the second attack came flying in, even faster than the first—

Puuuk!

There came the sound of flesh tearing.

But what the black sword had pierced was a soft, man-shaped doll. The doll seemed, somehow, to grin at Hwa Mugi.

Bang!

A moment later, it vanished like smoke.

Behind it stood the Pungcheon Cult Leader and the Sword King together. Hwa Mugi’s momentum had been so overwhelming that the Pungcheon Cult Leader had intervened to help him.

Since it was an attack that could have taken his life in an instant, the Pungcheon Cult Leader did not hesitate to step in.

“I still haven’t decided on the tavern’s name.”

At the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s joke, the Sword King rubbed his wrist as he replied. The tremendous internal energy contained in Hwa Mugi’s sword had nearly broken it.

“Just name it whatever you like.”

“I refuse. I’m going to give it a splendid name.”

Judging by his earlier momentum, Hwa Mugi should have charged in again and again, but instead he was looking at the two of them with a smile. He had returned to his usual self.

As though he found this situation immensely amusing, Hwa Mugi stretched broadly. The black sword he had drawn pointed toward the air along with the motion.

“Now then, shall we fight in earnest?”

Hwa Mugi’s gaze moved in turn from the Sword King to the Pungcheon Cult Leader, then to the Poison King.

“Who should I kill first? Or should I kill all three of you at once?”

Hwa Mugi paid no attention to the other four behind them. His attitude said they were free to interfere as much as they liked.

Then, from behind, a calm voice rang out.

“Come here.”

The one who spoke was Geom Ujin.

Hwa Mugi turned to look at Geom Ujin and grinned.

“Our Cult Leader is personally calling me—what should I do? I’m a bit busy here. Wait. I’ll reduce their numbers first, then come.”

Then came an astonishing sentence from Geom Ujin’s mouth.

“Not you.”

“!”

Cold demonic energy flowed from Geom Ujin’s eyes. Then, toward the souls inside Hwa Mugi’s body, he launched a provocation that truly no one had expected.

“Don’t hide in there like a bunch of cripples. All of you, come to me.”

Chapter 818: Because I'm Ashamed to Be a Fellow Heavenly Demon
--------------------------------------------------------------
The Fist Demon’s gaze turned toward Geom Ujin.

It had been a long time since he had heard the Cult Leader curse. In his youth, the Cult Leader had cursed quite often.

Before he became the Young Cult Leader, and even during his days as Young Cult Leader, the Cult Leader’s air had been quite different from now. If he had something to say, he said it, and he expressed his emotions readily. There had even been a time when he called the Blood Heaven Blade Demon “elder brother.”

In any case, one thing was certain. Over the course of their lives, the Cult Leader had cursed more than he had. Though no one would believe it.

The person most surprised by Geom Ujin’s words was Jin Paecheon.

‘To think that man knew how to curse like this?’

Jin Paecheon looked at Baek Jagang. At the look in his eyes that seemed to ask, Did you know? Baek Jagang shook his head.

But this much he knew. Cursing, too, was something only those who had done it before could do.

‘So he was a foul-mouthed Cult Leader? How has he held it in all this time?’

The more he saw, the more he thought the man was impossible to understand.

The others, who had not yet heard the sound transmission, could understand from Geom Ujin’s words. Hwa Mugi had not been acting; other souls had entered his body.

The Sword King took one step forward and looked at the Cult Leader of Pungcheon, who had blocked the attack for him.

At the gratitude in his gaze, the Cult Leader of Pungcheon nodded once with a reassuring expression.

If Hwa Mugi’s second and third attacks had continued to fly in, there was no telling what the result of that battle would have been.

He had blocked only a single attack, but the instant sword met sword, he knew.

‘He’s strong!’

It was truly the first time he had encountered internal energy so profound.

And that was not the only astonishing thing. Those clean, precise movements! The Sword King found himself wanting to keep fighting him. Having met a strong opponent, his blood was beginning to boil as well.

The Poison King silently watched Hwa Mugi, who had been approaching him, turn back around. He understood. The Cult Leader had diverted Hwa Mugi’s attention away from him.

The Poison King had known from the beginning. The Cult Leader did not want him to take an active role in this fight.

What the Cult Leader wanted from him was for him to protect the Young Cult Leader if he lost this battle.

When he had given that order earlier, the Poison King had said he would protect the Young Cult Leader by threatening to kill everyone in the world.

Even so, the reason he had seated him here today was surely to make him see clearly who the opponent was. After all, even a threat could only be made properly when one knew the other party.

Hwa Mugi slowly turned toward Geom Ujin. In an instant, the air around them grew heavy. His gaze was fixed solely on Geom Ujin.

The Poison King, whom he had first intended to kill, and the Sword King and the Cult Leader of Pungcheon who had interfered, were no longer in his interest.

It was only natural. For what was glaring at Geom Ujin now was not Hwa Mugi, but another soul.

“Do you know who I am, to dare!”

Geom Ujin could tell who had emerged. The Demonic Cult Leader from three hundred years ago!

“Where did you learn such manners of speech?”

It was a tense situation, as though they might clash at any moment, but Geom Ujin stared straight at him and said,

“And who might you be?”

None of them had yet revealed their identities. And, apart from the Palace Lord of Heaven’s Will, who had become enraged at the Sword King over their differing interpretations of Heaven’s Will, none of them had drawn a sword either.

Geom Ujin could tell. There was definitely some kind of restriction on those souls.

‘I have to break that restriction.’

And make them go berserk.

Because the more the souls inside the Heavenly Slaughter Star ran wild, the greater their chances of defeating the Heavenly Slaughter Star would become.

Ordinarily, the more confused and unpredictable an opponent was, the more disadvantageous it would be, but with the abilities of the seven experts gathered here, that confusion would work more in their favor.

“I asked who you are.”

“I am…”

The demon within Hwa Mugi could not reveal who he was. It was a control powerful enough to seal the mouth of a Demonic Cult Leader, but Geom Ujin provoked him further.

“You cowardly bastard, unable even to reveal who you are!”

The instant those words reached Hwa Mugi.

The next moment!

One of the restrictions was released.

Demonic energy began to dwell in the eyes where red energy had been wavering. Until now, only a faint aura had brushed past, weak enough that only Geom Ujin could sense it, but now he revealed his demonic energy in earnest.

The soul within Hwa Mugi revealed himself.

“I am Hyeok Docheon, who led the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult three hundred years ago.”

He might have endured it from anyone else, but he absolutely could not endure hearing such abuse from a later Demonic Cult Leader. His pride would not allow it.

Having revealed himself, Hyeok Docheon spoke sternly.

“Kneel at once and apologize!”

Tremendous demonic energy poured toward Geom Ujin.

Swaaaaaaah.

Everyone inside the tavern simultaneously raised their protective qi to shield their bodies. Though the demonic energy had been directed only at Geom Ujin, merely sharing the same space made it hard to breathe.

Then a single sentence was heard.

“No.”

Hyeok Docheon’s face stiffened. Rather than anger, his expression was closer to bewilderment.

He did not know. The Demonic Cult Leader he was facing now was the man who had left the words “you idiots” for the previous Cult Leaders on the wall of the Socheon Cave where Geom Mugeuk had undergone his trial.

Geom Ujin did not go easy on him just because he was a Demonic Cult Leader from three hundred years ago. Rather, he used the man’s fiery temperament against him.

“Because you are not the Heavenly Demon.”

When his very self was denied, Hyeok Docheon was enraged.

“You say you cannot believe me? I am Hyeok Docheon!”

Craaaaaack.

The floor split, and fissures began to spread.

It had not been broken by the exertion of internal energy, but solely by the force of demonic energy.

In truth, this demonic energy alone was more than enough to prove that he was Hyeok Docheon. He was not strong merely because his internal energy was profound. This was not ordinary demonic energy; it contained a majesty only a Demonic Cult Leader could display.

But far from being afraid, Geom Ujin poured oil onto that fury.

“If you were truly the Heavenly Demon, you would already have come out of there. You would not be trapped in another man’s body, defiling the honor of our cult.”

The fury of having been struck at his sore point flared up like fire.

Yes, go mad! Reveal your emotions! Let your demonic energy explode and stir everything up! Tear apart the other souls!

The black sword Hyeok Docheon extended pointed at Geom Ujin. His momentum was such that he seemed ready to charge at any moment.

“If by any chance you really are the Heavenly Demon.”

Everyone expected the words, Show me proof that you are the Heavenly Demon, to follow.

“Then I am ashamed that we are both called Heavenly Demon.”

But what flowed from Geom Ujin’s mouth was something no one had imagined.

“Kill yourself.”

“!”

A heavy silence fell.

Jin Paecheon swallowed hard. Judging from the situation, that soul was undoubtedly Hyeok Docheon, the Demonic Cult Leader from three hundred years ago, just as he had said.

And yet he told him to kill himself? Even though the other party was a soul?

Goosebumps did not rise around Baek Jagang’s ears. That meant those words had not been spoken lightly, but in all seriousness.

In this atmosphere, Baek Jagang nearly burst into loud laughter.

At that moment, there was someone who actually did laugh aloud.

It was Hwa Mugi.

Just as the demonic energy was about to explode, laughter burst out first. Hyeok Docheon, who had been on the verge of exploding, went back in, and Hwa Mugi came out again.

“They said the Young Cult Leader amused people by acting like a madman. It seems he takes after his father.”

In that moment, Geom Ujin understood.

Though who came out emerged at random each time, the one ultimately controlling them was that Hwa Mugi.

If he had not controlled him, Hyeok Docheon would already have charged at him.

Hwa Mugi walked into the kitchen and looked for liquor.

As if to soothe the enraged Hyeok Docheon, he poured liquor into a large cup and drank.

Hwa Mugi lifted the bottle toward Geom Ujin with an expression that seemed to ask, Would you like some too?

Geom Ujin shook his head.

“How utterly lacking in style. If you’re the Heavenly Demon, you should drink straight from the bottle!”

Hwa Mugi scooped up the stew Baek Jagang had made earlier and ate it as a side dish.

“It’s a little cold, but it’s tasty. Who made this?”

Hwa Mugi was still at ease.

From the ease he had shown ever since he first appeared, Geom Ujin had harbored one question.

‘Why doesn’t he attack us immediately?’

Because he had come out into the world after a long time and wanted to enjoy it? Because he wanted to toy with them and savor his own strength?

At first, that was what he had thought.

But from the killing intent that was gradually intensifying, Geom Ujin found another answer.

‘He is being cautious too.’

Geom Ujin could feel it. Though Hwa Mugi appeared calm on the outside, his murderous intent was steadily rising.

By using the emotions of the people within his body.

‘He is gathering Heavenly Slaughter.’

The time when this murderous star bestowed by heaven would display its greatest power was surely when the killing intent thus filled reached its peak.

Hwa Mugi would move at that moment.

And from this fact, Geom Ujin inferred one thing.

If this fight were too easy for him, there would be no need to gather killing intent like this.

Now Geom Ujin had two choices.

Fight before the killing intent was filled any further!

Or shake the souls dwelling in his body.

The method Geom Ujin chose was the second.

On this side were himself, the Martial Alliance Leader, and the Unorthodox Alliance Leader; on that side were the Demonic Cult Leader from three hundred years ago and the two Alliance Leaders.

If fate had brought them together like this, then surely those souls might play some role.

He could not know how many souls were inside, but the souls that had revealed their identities so far numbered three. The Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor, Hyeok Docheon, and the Palace Lord of Heaven’s Will.

There was one soul he suspected was there but had not yet revealed itself: the Unorthodox Alliance Leader.

The next target Geom Ujin aimed for after Hyeok Docheon was the most異질 among them.

Geom Ujin sent a sound transmission to Jin Paecheon.

—Do you not wish to see the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor?

Jin Paecheon understood. He did not know the exact intent, but it meant he was to draw the soul of the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor out of Hwa Mugi.

Even without his words, the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor was the one person he wanted to meet again properly.

Jin Paecheon said to Hwa Mugi,

“Give me a cup of wine.”

Hwa Mugi looked at Jin Paecheon and laughed heartily.

“Excellent! The Demonic Cult Leader, who looks like he would drink boldly, is guarding himself, while the Martial Alliance Leader, who looks like the primmest scholar, is truly magnanimous!”

Hwa Mugi lifted the wine bottle and beckoned Jin Paecheon closer.

“Come here! I’ll pour it for you myself.”

Srrrrk.

But Jin Paecheon did not approach. Using void-seizing force, he brought the wine bottle to himself.

At that, Hwa Mugi put on a deliberately disappointed expression.

“So you are a prim scholar after all.”

Jin Paecheon’s next action was unexpected.

Holding the wine bottle, he asked Hwa Mugi politely,

“Alliance Leader, will you accept a cup from me?”

Just as a strange smile formed at the corners of Hwa Mugi’s mouth.

In an instant, his eyes changed.

“Give me a cup.”

The calm voice made it clear that another soul had already appeared.

Jin Paecheon silently gazed at Hwa Mugi for a moment. He felt that he was not playing a prank, but had truly changed into another person.

Geom Ujin admired the way Jin Paecheon had summoned him. He had not provoked the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor to call him out, but had called him in a manner suited to his nature. With a single polite sentence.

Jin Paecheon carried the wine bottle and approached him.

Glug.

Jin Paecheon filled his cup to the brim.

After emptying the cup, Hwa Mugi asked,

“Do you know who I am?”

“Are you not the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor?”

“How did you know it was me?”

At the confirmation that he truly was the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor, fierce emotion coursed through Jin Paecheon’s heart.

‘Cult Leader Geom’s words were true.’

With calm eyes, Jin Paecheon spoke his honest feelings.

“The person I respect most is you, Alliance Leader. Whenever someone asked me whom I respected most, I always answered that it was you.”

For an instant, emotion flashed across the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor’s face. It had been a long time since he had conversed like this not with evil men, but with a member of the righteous sects—and now he was hearing that this man respected him most of all!

“How did you end up in that body?”

At Jin Paecheon’s question, the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor sighed and answered,

“There was a sacred object of the Palace of Heaven’s Will that absorbed evil spirits. As it absorbed the others, it sucked me in as well.”

“Then why was that Demonic Cult Leader there earlier?”

It was understandable that the Palace Lord of Heaven’s Will was there. But why was the Demonic Cult Leader there with him?

The Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor could not answer easily.

To the later-generation Martial Alliance Leader who said he respected him most, he could not bring himself to say that he had personally drawn the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Unorthodox Alliance into that war.

Jin Paecheon thought that unless it was for such a reason, there would be no reason for him to hesitate.

“By any chance, were you the one who called them?”

After a brief pause, instead of answering, the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor recalled another day.

“I remember the day I declared war against Cheonui Palace. I spoke boldly before all the righteous factions. I said we would root out evil and protect the murim. Back then, I was confident. I was certain I could end the war with my own hands in no time.”

Darkness settled in the eyes of the Divine Sword Martial Emperor.

“But the war did not end quickly. Countless people died in that war. It was all my fault.”

As someone who had also been an alliance leader, Jin Paecheon understood that feeling better than anyone.

But what he was curious about now was not his feelings. Jin Paecheon still had not heard the answer to the question he had asked earlier.

“Was it you, Alliance Leader, who brought in the Demon Cult Leader?”

In the end, the Divine Sword Martial Emperor answered honestly.

“Yes. It was me.”

All sorts of emotions rose in the Divine Sword Martial Emperor’s eyes. The greatest among them was resentment at the injustice of it all.

“I only wanted to minimize the damage to my subordinates. I wanted to end the war decisively so that no more of my men would lose their lives. Even if my honor was tarnished, I believed that was the path for the righteous murim. My choice was for the sake of the murim!”

Jin Paecheon said nothing, sensing that he was growing more and more agitated.

“And yet! The heavens treated me the same as the Demon Cult Leader and the leader of the Evil Path Alliance! They swallowed me up along with the Palace Lord of Cheonui Palace, the very one who started the war! For no reason other than that I had been there!”

Jin Paecheon suddenly forced down the words that rose to his throat.

‘Was that truly the only reason?’

When Jin Paecheon remained silent, the Divine Sword Martial Emperor thought he was condemning him.

“Would you have been any different? You, who are here together with the Demon Cult Leader and the leader of the Evil Path Alliance, even though no war has broken out?”

Still, Jin Paecheon gave no answer.

Then someone spoke.

“He would have been different.”

The one who spoke in Jin Paecheon’s stead was Geom Ujin.

Everyone’s gaze turned toward him.

“He may be the sort of man who comes running all the way here alone for his grandson’s sake, but he would not call me into his own war. Alliance Leader Jin is that kind of man.”

Geom Ujin’s and Jin Paecheon’s gazes tangled in midair. Jin Paecheon was surprised that Geom Ujin understood him so precisely.

He was right. If it had been him, he would not have drawn in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Even if that fight meant that he and everyone else would die.

On the other hand, rage surged across the Divine Sword Martial Emperor’s face. To hear such words from none other than the Demon Cult Leader was truly humiliating.

The reason Geom Ujin was pressing him so hard was that he hoped some variable would occur within that body.

Because the more murderous intent built up, the greater the chaos had to become.

“You say you did it out of concern for the murim?”

With deepened eyes, Geom Ujin stared straight into his.

“Then prove it.”

Geom Ujin did not say what he should prove or how. He knew full well that, at times like this, saying nothing had the strongest effect.

“If you cannot……”

Geom Ujin pushed him all the way to the end. He was not forcing him to do something; rather, it was the opposite.

“Stop with the stale whining and tell someone else to come out.”

Chapter 819: You Must Live
--------------------------
Saying to stop with the musty old grumbling and let someone else come out meant: shut up and get lost already.

In a situation where he should have been greatly enraged, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword had fallen into other thoughts.

The words that had flown in and lodged themselves in him earlier filled his heart.

—If you did it for the martial world, then prove it.

Had he truly brought in the Demonic Cult for the sake of the martial world?

It was a question he had asked himself for a long time, and after prolonged anguish, he had decided that he had.

But the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword knew. If it had truly been so, he would never have agonized over it for so long in the first place.

Bringing in the Demonic Cult had been a choice made entirely for himself.

He had wanted to end that war himself. If he were defeated without even ending the war that had taken so many lives? History would record it like this:

With the death of the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword, the war ended in defeat, leaving countless sacrifices in its wake.

He had not wanted the reputation he had built up over his entire life to end that way.

Death did not frighten him, but defeat did.

No matter what it took, he had wanted to win the war, to stand personally before the orthodox martial artists and say this: that he was sorry he had not ended the war sooner, but in the end, they had won.

Geom Ujin pressed him once more.

“Can you prove to Alliance Leader Jin that it truly was a choice made for the martial world?”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword gave no answer.

It was then that Jin Paecheon stepped forward.

“You need not prove it.”

Not only the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword, but everyone’s gaze turned to Jin Paecheon.

Jin Paecheon first looked at Geom Ujin. With his eyes, he conveyed, I am sorry, but please leave this matter to me. Then he spoke again to the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword.

“I know that you made efforts in your own way, Alliance Leader. I believe in you.”

For an instant, the eyes of the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword, borrowing Hwa Mugi’s body, trembled.

If the other man had said he could not trust him, he would have justified himself somehow.

But because he said he believed him, his heart instead grew heavier. He had nothing to say. At the very least, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword was not a villain who exploited another’s feelings.

How could Jin Paecheon not know?

The reason the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword could not answer with confidence. The feelings he could not speak aloud.

That was why he said he believed him. If the man had truly been evil, he would not have shown such a reaction. He would have shamelessly insisted that it truly had been a choice for the martial world. He would have loudly declared that he would prove it somehow.

People each had their own lives and their own reasons. Some would understand them, and others would criticize them.
