Even if others drove him into a corner, Jin Paecheon alone did not wish to do so. He did not want to see the person he had once respected collapse before everyone’s eyes.

“And I will protect the martial world of this era.”

This was Jin Paecheon’s sincerity.

“Even knowing that you are within that body, I will point my sword at the Heavenly Slaughter Star. I will swing my sword to kill him. For that, I apologize in advance.”

As a junior alliance leader, and as a descendant of the orthodox faction, Jin Paecheon observed every courtesy he could.

Watching him, Geom Ujin could tell.

More than his own pressure had done, Jin Paecheon’s courtesy, that uprightness, was provoking him even more deeply.

Whether intentional or not, Jin Paecheon was carrying out Geom Ujin’s intention in the finest possible way. He was shaking the soul of the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword without mercy.

In the end, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword returned to Hwa Mugi without saying a single word.

Hwa Mugi perched on the table and put on a deliberately sour expression.

“You acted like a meek scholar, but you turned out to be spicy ginger.”

“What does that mean?”

“You shook the old man’s heart properly.”

Hwa Mugi picked up the wine bottle on the table and drank from it.

“With a heart that weak, how can you be an alliance leader? If you’re going to be an alliance leader, you ought to be this vicious and cruel.”

Hwa Mugi criticized Jin Paecheon. He seemed intent on shaking Jin Paecheon just as Jin Paecheon had shaken the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword.

Jin Paecheon did not fall for the provocation and spoke calmly.

“I sincerely believe in the Alliance Leader.”

At that, Hwa Mugi looked not at Jin Paecheon but at Geom Ujin and asked,

“The Martial Alliance Leader of this era is this naive, and you still haven’t managed to smash the orthodox faction?”

It was a remark mocking both Geom Ujin and Jin Paecheon.

Of course, Geom Ujin was not shaken in the slightest. Rather, from Hwa Mugi’s reaction, he learned one thing.

‘The Heavenly Slaughter Star cares about the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword.’

And what did that mean?

‘The Heavenly Slaughter Star and the souls are connected.’

It meant those souls influenced the Heavenly Slaughter Star in some way. His choice to shake those souls had been correct.

Drinking wine, Hwa Mugi said to everyone,

“I can’t understand orthodox faction people. Why do you stake your lives on such useless things? Trust? What does it matter if you trust someone or not? You people live too conscious of others.”

Jin Paecheon asked Hwa Mugi,

“Then what is it that you believe in?”

“There is only one thing I believe: that what happens today is nothing but inevitability.”

“Do you consider yourself an inevitable existence?”

Hwa Mugi’s eyes gleamed with arrogance.

“That is for you people to consider. If today’s deaths were mere coincidence, wouldn’t that be far too tragic for you?”

Then someone spoke, cutting him off flatly.

“It is coincidence.”

The one who spoke was Baek Jagang.

“Whether we live today is coincidence, and whether we die is coincidence.”

He did not regard life and death as inevitabilities. He thought people died if they were unlucky.

“Countless people were born, and by chance you became the Heavenly Slaughter Star. We, too, became alliance leaders because many coincidences overlapped. So don’t dress yourself up as something grand.”

At that moment, Hwa Mugi’s gaze changed. It was an energy he had not revealed even once until now—evil qi.

“You knew I was here, didn’t you?”

He was Ju Gasin, the Evil Dao Alliance Leader from three hundred years ago. Hyeok Docheon had come out because Geom Ujin had provoked him, and the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword had come out because Jin Paecheon had courteously invited him.

But Ju Gasin came out on his own.

“If the Demonic Cult Leader was there, and the Martial Alliance Leader was there, then you would have been there too.”

Baek Jagang had expected the Evil Dao Alliance Leader from three hundred years ago to be inside Hwa Mugi’s body.

“Then why did you not seek me out?”

“I did not wish to see you.”

“Why?”

“We are opponents who will kill each other anyway, so what is the point of seeing you? It would only feel unpleasant.”

Ju Gasin did not get angry. Rather, he looked at Baek Jagang with an expression of interest.

“What is the current Evil Dao Alliance like?”

“It’s running well enough.”

Ju Gasin’s gaze turned toward Geom Ujin and Jin Paecheon.

“The orthodox faction regarded us as opportunists who could join hands with the Demonic Cult at any time, and the Demonic Cult always ignored and despised us.”

Ju Gasin’s gaze returned to Baek Jagang.

“Between the orthodox faction and the Demonic Cult, the ones always drenched in blood were us.”

Ju Gasin had dreamed of unifying the martial world.

He had regarded the war between the Cheonui Palace and the Martial Alliance as an opportunity bestowed upon him by the heavens.

But when he was sucked into the secret coffer, his dream was thwarted. Afterward, he had forgotten that dream for a long time, but upon seeing Baek Jagang, he felt it revive.

“It is not something to blame others for, is it? We acted that way.”

This attitude of Baek Jagang’s instead caught Ju Gasin’s interest. And so he was curious. What thoughts did this Evil Dao Alliance Leader, whom he had met three hundred years later, truly possess?

“What is your evil dao?”

At Ju Gasin’s question, Baek Jagang answered as if it were nothing.

“Evil dao? I have nothing that grand. If you come under me, I’ll make sure you eat well and live well. That is all.”

Ju Gasin stared quietly at Baek Jagang, then sent him a sound transmission.

—You should live.

A sound transmission he had truly never expected flew into him.

—And fulfill the dream of conquering the martial world that I could not complete.

It was not a sound transmission meant to hide the conversation from Hwa Mugi or the other souls. It was to keep Geom Ujin, Jin Paecheon, and the others here from hearing it.

—I will make sure that you alone survive today’s fight.

Baek Jagang sent him a sound transmission.

—How?

—By persuading the Heavenly Slaughter Star.

Hearing that, Baek Jagang could tell. These souls and that Heavenly Slaughter Star were able to converse.

—Even if I survive here, how am I supposed to conquer the martial world?

Everyone realized that Baek Jagang had begun conversing with Hwa Mugi through sound transmission, but they waited for their conversation to end.

—If the Demonic Cult Leader and the Martial Alliance Leader die here today, and afterward the main altar of the Demonic Cult and even the Martial Alliance are annihilated, you take that power vacuum for yourself.

Now that all of the Twelve Zodiac Kings were dead, someone would have to effectively rule the martial world.

—And that Heavenly Slaughter Star?

After a brief pause, Ju Gasin said,

—You will have to serve him. You will stand in a position beneath one man and above ten thousand.

Faced with an important decision, Baek Jagang continued questioning him.

—Has the Heavenly Slaughter Star learned all your martial arts?

—He has.

—And he has all your inner power as well?

—Not only our inner power, but even our innate qi. He took it all. He is an existence formed from all of us combined.

—Is that possible?

Since three hundred years ago, they had been planted into this body and that body, all manner of bodies. But no matter how innately gifted the vessel’s body and mental strength were, they could not withstand their souls and power. Most went mad before even a few years had passed.

But Hwa Mugi, who had been born under the Heavenly Slaughter Star, was different. He accepted their inner power and souls, and even absorbed all of their martial arts.

However, because no attempt to secure his loyalty had worked, the Dark Palace Lord had eventually gone so far as to seal him.

—What is his weakness?

—Why do you ask that?

—Later, he might try to kill even me, might he not?

—If you do not betray him, that will not happen.

—Does he have a weakness?

The answer “no” did not come immediately. Baek Jagang instinctively realized it. That Heavenly Slaughter Star definitely had some sort of weakness.

—If you tell me his weakness, I will accept your proposal.

Seeing him try to discover the weakness by any means, Ju Gasin realized Baek Jagang’s true thoughts.

—You have no intention of following my words.

Baek Jagang’s small eyes disappeared into a smile.

—Begging for my life and going under that Heavenly Slaughter Star? That would hurt my pride even more than watching my step while wedged between the Demonic Cult and the orthodox faction.

After a brief pause, Baek Jagang asked,

—If it were you three hundred years ago, would you have accepted?

—I would have accepted. And then I would have aimed for another opportunity.

It meant he would have become the second-in-command and sought a chance to kill Hwa Mugi.

—Is there anything more important than life?

—That is why I am refusing.

The reason Baek Jagang refused was something completely unexpected.

—I think this side is going to win.

—!

Silence flowed between the two men.

—You are a man I do not like, but at least if you survive today, the Evil Dao Alliance will not fall easily.

At the favorable assessment, Baek Jagang offered a favorable remark in return.

—You are a waste as well. If you had been born in the right era, I think you might have fulfilled your dream.

Hwa Mugi laughed. At first glance, it seemed as though Ju Gasin had changed back into Hwa Mugi and Hwa Mugi was laughing, but that final laugh was Ju Gasin’s.

As if he knew the entire contents of the sound transmission, Hwa Mugi stared at Baek Jagang and said,

“Though you say you watch the Demonic Cult’s and orthodox faction’s moods, the one with the greatest ambition is you.”

Geom Ujin and Jin Paecheon looked at Baek Jagang.

But Baek Jagang did not make any effort to explain Ju Gasin’s proposal. Nor did he worry that Geom Ujin or Jin Paecheon might misunderstand.

Rather, from that very attitude, Geom Ujin felt trust. No matter what Hwa Mugi said, he looked as though he had not the slightest thing to be ashamed of.

Instead, Baek Jagang informed everyone of what absolutely had to be known.

“He says the souls can converse with one another. That man has learned all the martial arts of theirs that we saw, and he also possesses their inner power and innate qi. So we should think of him as an opponent formed from all of them combined.”

It was something they had only vaguely guessed at, and this was the moment when the most important information for this fight was revealed.

“He was struggling to let you live, yet you are desperately trying to die first.”

Hwa Mugi was smiling, but his eyes were filled with intense killing intent.

Of course, Baek Jagang was not afraid in the slightest. Rather, he sowed discord in return.

“Evil faction bastards are good at stabbing people in the back by nature. So be careful of the man inside you. You never know when he’ll strike your soul from behind.”

As a cold, heavy atmosphere flowed, Geom Ujin stepped forward.

“Now that it seems we have seen everyone there is to see, shall we see your skill?”

With Ju Gasin as the last, they had seen all four people dwelling in Hwa Mugi’s body.

He did not know how these meetings would affect the fight against Hwa Mugi, but Geom Ujin believed this choice had been right.

Hwa Mugi drew his black sword and rested it on his shoulder as he answered,

“Now then, who will come first?”

Geom Ujin did not follow Hwa Mugi’s intentions.

“We will fight however is convenient for us. We may fight one-on-one and then launch a wheel battle, or all of us may attack together. Each person will fight the battle he wants.”

Those words were not meant for Hwa Mugi. He had said them to everyone here. Once the fight began, he was telling them to fight without setting restrictions. To throw away face and honor alike.

Focus only on winning!

Not only the two alliance leaders, but the Fist Demon, the Poison King, the Pungcheon Cult Leader, and the Sword King all realized what Geom Ujin intended by those words.

“Even without me, this martial world will soon fall into the hands of the Demonic Cult.”

It was praise for Geom Ujin’s excellent judgment, but Hwa Mugi’s words contained powerful discord.

But the attempt did not work. It was already something Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang worried about, and they had agreed to treat today’s fight separately.

Today was a fight for a son, a grandson, and a disciple.

The first person to step forward was Jin Paecheon.

“Give me the chance to strike first.”

Knowing that the one who had taken the most difficult step was Jin Paecheon, Geom Ujin willingly granted his request.

“Do so.”

“Thank you.”

Jin Paecheon stepped forward. Saying they should go outside to fight had little meaning. The issue was whether this entire Maga Village would remain at all.

The aura of Jin Paecheon as he stood before Hwa Mugi changed.

A clear spirit, like white jade, wrapped around his body, and his eyes shone coldly.

Jin Paecheon slowly drew the Gentleman Sword. With a perfect draw, the Gentleman Sword awakened.

Could the sound of a sword being drawn be this clear and beautiful?

The moment the Gentleman Sword fully emerged from its sheath, a cold radiance split the air. Invisible waves of force stretched out in every direction.

Zing.

The Gentleman Sword began to hum softly.

The greatest sword of all ages was humming in the hand of the orthodox faction’s greatest master.

Its dignified momentum seemed to say this:

There are two worlds.

The world before the Gentleman Sword is drawn, and the world after the Gentleman Sword has been drawn.

Jin Paecheon conveyed his heart to the Gentleman Sword.

‘Though you are held in my hand, this battle is for your master.’

So let us do well, Gentleman Sword.

Chapter 820: Those People Are All Here
--------------------------------------
Jin Paecheon had originally possessed an overbearing inclination in his martial arts.

When the grave yet incomparably clear energy of the Gentleman Sword was added to such a Jin Paecheon, he revealed a new bearing.

Hwa Mugi recognized what kind of sword Jin Paecheon was holding.

“The greatest of all ages—the Gentleman Sword!”

At the sight of the Gentleman Sword, Hwa Mugi grew excited. He did not feel fear; rather, he rejoiced.

“See? You weren’t some bookish gentleman after all. You came here fully intending to kill me.”

Hwa Mugi’s two eyes blazed with a single emotion. It was instinctive greed, drawn toward the Gentleman Sword.

Because he did not hide it, everyone could feel his greed.

“Do you want it?”

To Jin Paecheon’s question, Hwa Mugi answered that he did want it like this.

“Isn’t a gentleman and a slaughter star only a sheet of paper apart to begin with?”

The Cult Leader of Pungcheon, who had been listening, could not hold back and said a word.

“Just how thick is that sheet of paper supposed to be?”

At those words, everyone wore a faint smile.

“Look at yourself. In what way are you and Alliance Leader Jin only a hair’s breadth apart?”

Hwa Mugi laughed, but what lay within that laugh was unmistakable killing intent.

“I’ll pierce your heart with that sword.”

In other words, it was a declaration that he would kill both Jin Paecheon and the Cult Leader of Pungcheon.

As if telling him to try as much as he liked, the Cult Leader of Pungcheon thumped his own chest with his hand.

“I may not be a gentleman, but I’m no coward. Come at me all you want. No—since you’ll die by Alliance Leader Jin’s hand, I suppose you won’t be able to.”

In that way, the Cult Leader of Pungcheon raised Jin Paecheon’s morale. Whatever the situation, he was the best at enlivening the mood.

Jin Paecheon slowly aimed the Gentleman Sword at Hwa Mugi.

“Whose martial arts will you display?”

Hwa Mugi made a cruel choice.

“I’ll kill you with the martial arts of the man you say you trust!”

Hwa Mugi intended to face Jin Paecheon with the martial arts of the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s signature martial art was the Gwangmyeong Muyang Sword Art.

Jin Paecheon’s signature martial art was the Imperial Sword Form.

It was the moment when two supreme martial arts of the orthodox faction collided.

The two men charged toward each other at the same time and thrust out their swords.

Ting.

As if it were truly a lie, the tip of one sword struck precisely against the tip of the other. To clash sword tips while charging at each other so quickly? From the very first move, the two of them were displaying divine prowess.

Chiiiing.

The blades of the two swords scraped past each other and flew toward their targets.

With a sharp whistle of wind, the blades instantly brushed past each other’s ears. Had the two not turned their faces aside to evade, their faces would have been pierced through.

When the two swords crossed.

In that fleeting instant, Jin Paecheon’s face was reflected on the blade. Though it was a black sword, it was reflected clearly.

At some point, the face of his reflected on a sword blade during battle had become calm and composed.

It was a face so accustomed to battle that it no longer grew tense, and a weary face exhausted by life. Gradually, the face reflected in the blade had only grown older. And so he had stopped looking at it.

But today, Jin Paecheon’s face was different.

He was tense and excited. As if it were that day long ago, when he had fought his first real battle.

Jin Paecheon’s heart was pounding like the drums of a battlefield.

Kang! Kaang! Kang!

Sword clashed against sword, and sparks flew. Deadly attacks aimed at the opponent’s vital points continued one after another.

Since it was the Gentleman Sword, called the greatest sword of all ages, one would naturally think it could snap the opponent’s sword in a single blow, but Hwa Mugi’s sword remained intact.

First of all, the black sword he held was also no ordinary blade.

It was Amcheon Milgeom (暗天密劍), the Dark Heaven Secret Sword, the finest treasured sword of the Dark Palace.

And that treasured sword was being supported by Hwa Mugi’s powerful internal energy. If he had not protected it with such formidable internal energy, it would not have endured for long after clashing so violently.

Shwiik!

Hwa Mugi’s black sword flew in at terrifying speed.

Because the eye could not keep up with that speed, it had to be blocked by instinct.

No feints were used to gauge the opponent’s skill or test his response. Doing so would mean instant death.

Only when the greatest move one could make, the best move one could unfold, was brought out could one endure.

“What an incredible sword!”

Those watching the battle could tell. Hwa Mugi’s reaction was both a display of greed and a provocation toward Jin Paecheon.

What I am admiring right now is not your swordsmanship, but your sword.

This battle was a fight between those who had reached the pinnacle of swordsmanship.

It was a battle where victory and defeat would be decided by the difference of a single speck of dust in the heart.

If Jin Paecheon were affected by those words, it would certainly have an adverse effect on this fight.

But it was needless worry.

Jin Paecheon was a man who had experienced every hardship, weathered all manner of storms and bloodshed, and stood in this place.

The fact that he was being provoked meant there was a need to provoke him.

‘In other words, this is a fight worth having!’

Jin Paecheon had reached a realm where he interpreted any provocation from his opponent in a way that helped the battle.

The two men’s swords flew endlessly toward each other.

The trajectories of sword lines they created, moving between straight lines and curves.

Those lines were paths leading to death.

Jin Paecheon was walking that path like a gentleman.

The reason he could endure even while absolutely inferior in internal energy was thanks to the Gentleman Sword. Because the Gentleman Sword was so powerful, Hwa Mugi also had to pay attention to keeping his black sword from breaking.

Hwa Mugi’s sword stabbed in succession toward Jin Paecheon’s neck, shoulder, and heart.

Shwik! Shwiiik! Shwiiik!

Each time Jin Paecheon knocked those attacks away with his sword, an impact surged through him as if his wrist would break.

It was unbelievable that such swift attacks contained such profound internal energy. Each one of those rapid attacks felt like a single move into which an absolute master had poured his heart and blood.

And such attacks came flying in succession as if it were nothing.

Those watching felt it at the same time.

‘This is a battle difficult even to interfere in.’

The moment one lost focus, even for an instant, death would follow. If someone carelessly stepped in to help at the wrong moment, they might instead harm Jin Paecheon.

How could anyone intervene in a battle so perfect?

Rather, this was a battle no one could join because Jin Paecheon was fighting too well.

Geom Ujin was not watching each of their individual moves. If he tried to follow the techniques, the exchange of offense and defense was so fast that even he would be unable to intervene.

What Geom Ujin watched was the flow of the battle. He looked at the two men’s eyes, their expressions, and listened to their breathing.

Whether they were tired, afraid, seeking an opportunity, or thinking of a final move. He watched the overall flow of the fight.

The battle grew rougher and more savage by the moment.

The fight, which had been a calm lake, caught a rapid current and began to fall like a waterfall.

Jin Paecheon was completely immersed in the battle. He could neither see nor hear anything else. Only Hwa Mugi and his sword were visible.

He was fighting at the final realm he could unfold. Because he was utterly absorbed in the fight, the ones more tense than him were actually those watching.

At this moment, both Geom Ujin and Baek Jagang were sincerely worried for Jin Paecheon.

It was not because, if he died, they would be next. They were afraid that even though they had been there with him, they might fail to prevent his death—and that they would have to deliver that news to his son and disciple.

For that reason, the mental strain on those watching was also immense.

Chwaaaak.

Seogeok!

With the sound of something being cut, the two men separated.

A long strip of Jin Paecheon’s clothing was sliced away. Had it gone just a little deeper, it would have been a grievous wound.

“Haa, haa.”

Ragged breaths flowed from Jin Paecheon’s mouth. Hwa Mugi, on the other hand, was calm. Not a single breath of his had grown rough.

He was not strong merely because the martial arts and internal energy of several souls had combined within him.

He had been born with both the Heavenly Slaughter Star and the Heavenly Martial Body.

There was a joke that the Heavenly Martial Body performed straight punches even as fetal movement inside the mother’s womb. A body born from the beginning to learn martial arts.

That was why Hwa Mugi was not only powerful in internal energy, but was displaying martial arts even more outstandingly than the masters from three hundred years ago.

“Those people three hundred years ago, and you people now—you are all the same.”

Geom Ujin could sense it. During the fight, someone had spoken to him. That had interfered with his concentration, causing him to pause the battle for a moment. Who could it be? Geom Ujin thought of one person.

That rage turned toward the people gathered here.

“Do you consider me greedy merely because I tried to possess a single sword?”

Hwa Mugi’s gaze turned toward Geom Ujin.

“When you people are trying to possess this entire murim.”

This time, he looked at Baek Jagang as he spoke.

“You say my birth was a coincidence? I am an inevitable existence! One created by all of you!”

The final place his gaze turned back to was Jin Paecheon.

Hwa Mugi approached Jin Paecheon and asked.

“Is your justice not a justice built atop graves?”

Just as Baek Jagang stepped forward to take his place, Jin Paecheon looked at him.

It was a gaze asking for one more chance. Though he was exhausted, Jin Paecheon’s eyes were clear.

Baek Jagang looked at Geom Ujin. At that gaze asking what he thought, Geom Ujin looked at Jin Paecheon.

“He ought to answer that question, at least.”

It meant they should give him another chance, and so Baek Jagang stepped back.

Hwa Mugi asked Jin Paecheon.

“You defined yourselves, and decided the worth of your own existence.”

A red aura rippled in Hwa Mugi’s eyes. It was a powerful murderous intent unlike any they had seen until now.

“Are you truly righteous?”

Jin Paecheon let out a light sigh and answered.

“As you say, perhaps we truly are only a sheet of paper apart.”

In the past, Jin Paecheon had once left the alliance alone to kill every person involved in the death of his child. If he had committed that deed?

“That is why, even at this age, I am still searching for the answer to your question.”

Because Hwa Mugi knew he was speaking sincerely, he asked again.

“Alliance Leader, is the murim you are trying to protect a place worth giving your life for?”

“It is.”

The answer flowed out without hesitation.

“Does this murim truly have value enough to continue?”

Jin Paecheon’s answer was clear.

“It does.”

“Why?”

“Because in this murim, people greater than you or I will appear and preserve that value.”

Geom Mugeuk and his grandson Ha Gun would carry it on. He believed they would create a better murim than the one now. Before, he had only worried, but now he believed.

Jin Paecheon spoke proudly.

“You may be able to kill me, but you will not be able to destroy that value.”

At that proud answer, Hwa Mugi reacted coldly.

“It is because you are so stubborn that I want to destroy it.”

The two clashed again. Though they had worried because he was so exhausted, Jin Paecheon fought even better than before.

In an instant, dozens of moves passed.

Chang!

The two swords crossed in the shape of a cross. Beyond the swords, they could see each other’s expressions. Hwa Mugi was smiling. The moment he sensed the impure intent within that smile.

Hwa Mugi’s technique suddenly changed.

After displaying the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s martial arts, he had suddenly unfolded the martial arts of the Palace Lord of Cheonui.

“The Murim Alliance Leader I failed to kill back then—I shall kill you today!”

When the spirit of the swordsmanship suddenly changed, Jin Paecheon was flustered. His body had already been adjusted to the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s martial arts, but then a completely different swordsmanship suddenly emerged.

If it were an ordinary master, what would it matter what changes the opponent made? But his opponent was Hwa Mugi.

The changed swordsmanship flew toward Jin Paecheon at extreme speed.

Papapat!

Blood sprayed from Jin Paecheon’s shoulder and arm.

At the very moment Hwa Mugi rushed in to finish Jin Paecheon off!

Hwa Mugi turned around and swung his sword.

Kwaaang!

The sound of energy exploding in midair rang out. It was the formless sword energy the Sword King had fired, flying toward him.

After dispersing the formless sword energy, Hwa Mugi immediately flew toward the Sword King with the speed of light.

Changchangchangchangchang!

Under Hwa Mugi’s consecutive attacks, the Sword King was pushed back.

He was outmatched in speed and internal energy, but not in swordsmanship. If he had been, he would already have been stabbed five times in succession by those ferocious attacks.

Kakakakakang!

In an instant, Hwa Mugi drove the Sword King all the way to the wall.

Swaaaek!

The moment the Sword King’s back touched the wall, Hwa Mugi’s sword flew in at a bizarre angle.

It was an attack that anyone would have thought impossible to avoid.

Seogeok!

Cutting through the Sword King’s hair, it carved a long slash through the tavern wall. He had truly evaded the attack by a hair’s breadth.

Just as the second attack was about to fly in again.

Shwiiik!

The Gentleman Sword of Jin Paecheon, who had charged in from behind, stabbed toward Hwa Mugi’s back.

Hwa Mugi twisted his body, knocked aside the sword flying in from behind, and slipped away to the side.

“Is there such a thing as a gentleman who stabs someone in the back?”

At Hwa Mugi’s mockery, Jin Paecheon gave no answer. Blood was streaming down from his shoulder and arm.

“This is what happens when a gentleman keeps company with villains like them.”

Geom Ujin and Baek Jagang said nothing either. At the critical moment, they had seen the Sword King intervene a step ahead of them and so had not stepped in themselves.

In the earlier exchange between Jin Paecheon and Hwa Mugi, the place where the Sword King had been standing was the optimal position from which to help Jin Paecheon without harming him.

Despite his injury, Jin Paecheon remained composed.

“If I’d known wicked deeds were this interesting, I would have tried them long ago.”

The Sword King expressed his gratitude to Jin Paecheon.

“Thank you.”

Because Jin Paecheon had rushed in, a second attack had not come. If it had, he might not have died, but he would certainly have been cut badly.

“I’m the one who should be thanking you.”

If not for the Sword King’s formless sword qi just now, his wound would not have ended at this extent; he would have suffered a fatal injury.

It was then.

“There was no need for that.”

The one who spoke was Geom Ujin.

There was no need? Could he possibly mean there had been no need to help Jin Paecheon?

Everyone looked at Geom Ujin in puzzlement.

Then something astonishing happened.

Geom Ujin slowly raised his palm. The palm that seemed as though it would point toward Hwa Mugi was instead aimed at the second floor of the tavern.

Shwaaaaaak!

Kwaaaaang!

Under Geom Ujin’s palm force, the wall with the writing engraved on it was smashed apart and blown away. The wall Jo Chunbae treasured as dearly as his own life shattered into pieces and vanished.

No one had imagined Geom Ujin would destroy it, and they all stared at him in shock.

Hwa Mugi, too, asked with an expression of surprise at the act.

“Wasn’t that a work created by your son?”

At that, Geom Ujin said to the Sword King,

“You could have leaped upward just now to avoid it, but you didn’t, did you?”

Geom Ujin had seen it precisely. In the brief exchange with Hwa Mugi, the Sword King could have dodged a little more easily.

But his momentary instinct had chosen not to go up to the second floor. It was for Geom Mugeuk’s sake. Because he hoped that historic achievement left behind by him would not disappear.

Geom Ujin had read that feeling in his battle.

But in a fight this difficult, there could be no constraints of any kind.

That was why he had blown it away himself. So that from now on, everyone could fight with an easy mind.

And he had an alternative for Jo Chunbae as well. He would build him a new tavern, an even grander one.

“The writing can simply be written again. Everyone who wrote it is right here.”

Chapter 821: My Problems Are Always Because of the Young Cult Leader
--------------------------------------------------------------------
The Sword King was moved.

It was a completely unexpected feeling. Geom Ujin had blown away the wall for his sake.

To think he had noticed, while watching a battle that had passed in the blink of an eye, that he was worrying about the second-floor wall. It made him realize anew just how remarkable Geom Ujin was.

Of course, he had not removed the wall solely for him. It meant that from now on, they should fight without concern for anything.

The Sword King cupped his fist respectfully and paid his respects to Geom Ujin.

“Thank you for your consideration.”

Geom Ujin nodded as if it were nothing.

Hwa Mugi silently stared at the gaping hole in the second-floor wall.

His face was smiling, but it was a little different from his earlier smile.

The reason he had fought using only basic martial arts, without properly employing the Muhwang Divine Sword or the Cheonui Palace Lord’s signature martial art, had been to make use of that wall.

Because he had come knowing this place held meaning for them.

‘And yet he blows it away himself? As expected, he’s no easy opponent.’

In the meantime, Jin Paecheon staunched the bleeding from his injured shoulder and arm.

He had truly been cut in an instant.

He had keenly experienced the saying that life and death were decided in a single moment.

After checking his wounds, Jin Paecheon walked over to where Geom Ujin and Baek Jagang stood.

“I caused you needless worry.”

Jin Paecheon did not stubbornly insist on fighting further.

It was always harder to know when to retreat than when to advance, but Jin Paecheon knew. Now was the time to step back.

Geom Ujin was glad that he was safe.

“That was splendid martial arts.”

This battle had been fought only with sword against sword, with fundamentals alone.

Yet it had been fiercer and more remarkable than a battle in which countless sword qi and sword auras ran wild.

Baek Jagang spoke his mind more honestly.

“To be frank, I was worried.”

If Jin Paecheon had heard those words in the past, he would certainly have thought he was being looked down on.

But now, they did not sound unpleasant.

“Thank you.”

Jin Paecheon accepted them as they were. If someone had worried for him, he ought to thank them.

Hwa Mugi did not simply watch Jin Paecheon withdraw.

“You mean to end it like this?”

Since the Gentleman Sword was still in Jin Paecheon’s hand, he provoked him again.

“No matter what, a Murim Alliance Leader who only barely survives with another’s help? Aren’t you ashamed?”

Jin Paecheon calmly accepted the ridicule and returned the Gentleman Sword to its sheath.

“You’re right. I am deeply ashamed.”

He admitted it honestly, but there was no blow to his heart. Rather, he felt relieved. He had done all he could.

If he survived and, several days later, looked back on today, he would surely feel regret.

I should have used that technique then. I should have responded like this at that moment. And would that be all? When the Heavenly Killing Star said that, I should have answered this way. All manner of thoughts would keep him from sleep.

But for now, he had no lingering regret.

He might have been able to fight better than this, but even so, he did not think he could have fought well enough to kill Hwa Mugi.

This was his best. Since he had fought with everything he had, there was nothing to regret.

At that moment, the Pungcheon Cult Leader, who had been listening, cut in.

“What is there to be ashamed of? Shame is something that side should feel.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader spoke on Jin Paecheon’s behalf.

“Alliance Leader Jin is a gentlemanly, dignified man, so he’s letting it pass like this. But the martial arts of several people, and even the internal energy of several people—if you’re using all of that, you should be the one ashamed. If you use only one person’s martial arts, and only as much internal energy as one person, then I’ll acknowledge you.”

Hwa Mugi looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader and smiled. It contained murderous intent that said, I will make sure to kill you, of all people, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader paid it no mind at all.

“You should consider yourself lucky the Young Cult Leader isn’t here. If he were, a coward like you would already have collapsed from pent-up rage.”

Everyone listening agreed with the part about pent-up rage.

Then one person stepped forward.

“Please give me the opportunity this time.”

It was the Sword King. Since the Cult Leader had lifted his restriction, he wanted to fight to his heart’s content.

Geom Ujin gazed at him for a moment, then nodded in permission. There was a trust that the man named Ak Gunhak inspired. If he stepped forward like this, he must have his own thoughts—an inexplicable faith of that sort.

Indeed, the Sword King had not stepped forward out of a mere competitive spirit. He did not overestimate his own abilities. Knowing he could not face Hwa Mugi alone, he asked one person for help.

“Would you watch my back one more time?”

That person was none other than the Pungcheon Cult Leader. He was asking again of the man who had stepped forward earlier to save him.

“You use Formless Aura as well, and my martial arts also contain formless qi, so we should suit each other well.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader, too, had confirmed through Jin Paecheon’s battle that killing Hwa Mugi alone would by no means be easy.

Earlier, Geom Ujin had made his intention clear. In this fight, they were not to think of honor or face. Only victory mattered.

“That side has several souls, and I am only one man, but I should at least help.”

Taking another jab at Hwa Mugi, the Pungcheon Cult Leader stepped forward to assist.

Geom Ujin gave the Sword King one piece of advice.

“Do not forget your promise.”

The promise to stay by Geom Mugeuk’s side and help him from now on. In other words, he was telling him not to die, no matter what.

“Yes, I will not forget.”

The reason the Sword King stepped forward was partly because of his personal desire to fight him, but there was a more important reason: to reduce his internal energy as much as possible.

He intended to make him expend as much internal energy as he possibly could. Only then would the person who faced him at the end have a chance of victory.

The Sword King sent a voice transmission to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

—It would be best if we could kill him, but if that proves impossible, I think we must at least exhaust as much of his internal energy as we can.

—I think that is a wise choice.

Then Jin Paecheon, who had been watching, spoke to the Sword King.

“Be careful.”

The Sword King looked at Jin Paecheon and bowed his head slightly in thanks.

Watching Jin Paecheon pay special attention to the Sword King, Baek Jagang thought to himself.

‘Even if you give him the Gentleman Sword at your waist, he is already someone who has left.’

Regardless of the fact that Geom Ujin had spoken as though he had already taken hold of him.

Judging from his desire to protect the second-floor wall earlier, he was completely Geom Mugeuk’s man.

Well, perhaps that was exactly what made one want him even more.

With his horse-tail hair swaying, the Sword King walked forward, and the Pungcheon Cult Leader stood one step behind him.

Of course, just because the two fought together did not mean they would become twice as strong. If they had not trained in coordinated attack techniques together, they might instead get in each other’s way.

They had to fight by responding well and instinctively in each moment.

Hwa Mugi said haughtily, his arms folded.

“Who decided that? Do you think I’ll fight just because you say you will?”

Boom!

Hwa Mugi swung his sword and blocked the Formless Aura that had flown silently toward him.

Boom! Kwang! Kwang!

Hwa Mugi blocked the Formless Auras that came flying in succession while standing in place. He blocked those powerful attacks without taking even a single step back.

The one who had sent the Formless Auras at him one after another was the Sword King.

“Even now, do you not feel like fighting?”

It was impossible to tell from his expression whether he had been angered or not. But his murderous intent had clearly grown stronger.

“Every last one of you is unpleasant.”

Among those unpleasant people, the Pungcheon Cult Leader was someone who would resent being called second to anyone. This time, too, he could not hold back.

“If everyone around you is like that, could the problem perhaps be with you?”

Hwa Mugi smiled and nodded.

“From now on, it will be a problem for all of us.”

The moment his words ended.

Swaaaaaak.

Hwa Mugi charged not toward the Sword King, but toward the Pungcheon Cult Leader. Perhaps that mouth irritated him more than the aura that had flown at him.

The black sword cut through the air with speed truly like light.

Paaang!

Human-shaped figures appeared in an instant, blocking the space between the two men.

Shadow-Illusion Human Wall.

It was the moment the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s signature martial art, the Heavenly Outer Blood Demon Art, was unleashed.

Peng! Peong! Peong! Peng!

Hwa Mugi cut down the doll-shaped obstacles blocking his path and pressed forward. Though they were not originally things that should be sliced apart and vanish so easily, before Hwa Mugi’s black sword they disappeared with loud bursts, like smoke.

Behind those figures, the Sword King was already standing.

“Me first!”

The Sword King tried to take the lead in the battle. It was not because he thought himself stronger than the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

In terms of martial compatibility, it was not easy for the Pungcheon Cult Leader to face him.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader fought barehanded, while Hwa Mugi’s sword was far too fast and powerful. Since he would dig in and thrust his sword in an instant, facing him barehanded was truly difficult. It was hard enough even to block an ordinary opponent.

That was why he stepped forward first.

Thinking that it no longer mattered if the entire building was destroyed, the Sword King immediately displayed his signature martial art.

Formless Divine Sword, First Sword: Formless Return to Void.

Whiiiiiiing!

As the Sword King thrust out his sword, a whirlwind swept over Hwa Mugi’s body.

Papapapapapak!

A tremendous whirlwind of aura twisted Hwa Mugi’s body. Ordinarily, his bones and flesh should have been torn apart and vanished into the vortex.

But Hwa Mugi’s body did not twist. His protective aura turned that whirlwind into a spring breeze.

Swaaaaaak!

Peong!

In that state, the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s palm technique struck his body.

But Hwa Mugi endured the two men’s attacks as if to show them. In that moment, Hwa Mugi truly seemed like a monster.

Swish.

Then Hwa Mugi broke out of the whirlwind in an instant.

“Careful!”

By the time the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s shout burst out, Hwa Mugi was already charging in front of the Sword King and thrusting out his black sword.

Radiant Boundless Sword Art, Third Form: Extreme Yang Splitting Heaven.

The aura Hwa Mugi sent flying as it split space was no ordinary aura.

Fwoooooosh!

A tremendous flame aura, as if it would melt everything away, erupted toward the Sword King.

It was an attack that could never be blocked normally.

Formless Divine Sword, Third Form: Formless Reversal of Heaven.

A pitch-black space formed before the Sword King.

Whoosh.

It instantly swallowed the incoming flames.

Fwoooooosh!

Then the space burst open and spat the flames it had absorbed back at Hwa Mugi.

Fwooooooooosh!

Hwa Mugi took the scorching heat head-on, but—

Chwaaaaaak!

He split the blazing flames he had created to either side and pressed forward.

With terrifying momentum, Hwa Mugi thrust out his sword.

Kaaang!

Though the Sword King raised his internal energy to the limit to block it, a shock surged through him as if his wrist would be severed. Even though he had braced himself for it, he could not grow accustomed to the impact.

And attacks like that poured down like rain.

Kaang! Kaang! Kang!

As the two exchanged sword blows too quickly for the eye to follow, the Pungcheon Cult Leader could not intervene in the fight. In the blink of an eye, the two changed positions several times.

The Sword King did the very best he could.

But with each and every clash, he felt it.

He could not win.

Hwa Mugi was fast and strong, and there was not the slightest opening in his techniques.

It felt as if he were fighting while facing an enormous wall.

And what made this battle at the extreme limit even harder was the innate murderous intent Hwa Mugi exuded. That murderous intent, which had now far surpassed the limits of humanity, affected even the movement of his true qi.

In the end, the Sword King could not withstand Hwa Mugi’s fierce attacks.

Boom!

Unable to endure the attack, the Sword King was sent flying and slammed into the rear wall. He sprang up from the floor and spewed a mouthful of blood. After blocking those powerful attacks in succession, he had finally suffered internal injuries.

Was it because his murderous intent had grown stronger? Hwa Mugi was stronger than when he had faced Jin Paecheon earlier. He was becoming stronger and stronger.

Swaaaaaak.

As if to finish off the Sword King, Hwa Mugi charged straight ahead.

But a step ahead of him, the Pungcheon Cult Leader blocked his path.

Shashashashashashashashak!

Blue lines flew at Hwa Mugi from every direction. It was the Blue-Line Blood Net of the Heavenly Outer Blood Demon Art, which could cut a person’s body apart with even the slightest graze.

Dozens of blue lines sliced through the air.

Papapapapak.

Hwa Mugi broke through those lines with his body. The Blue-Line Blood Net was stopped by his protective aura and could not pierce his body.

Swaaaaaak!

Hwa Mugi continued to press forward.

Drop.

At that very moment, a tiny dot fell into the air, then suddenly swelled large.

Whirrrrrrrr!

A black hole pulled Hwa Mugi toward it.

But Hwa Mugi did not get sucked in, enduring it by using the Thousand-Catty Drop.

He was withstanding the Death-Extinction Vortex of the Heavenly Outer Blood Demon Art unleashed by the Pungcheon Cult Leader with pure strength.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader was truly astonished. One could fall into the Death-Extinction Vortex and then break free of it. But he had never imagined someone would endure it with strength.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader drew his inner energy up to its utmost limit and even unleashed a palm force.

Swaaaek! Thud!

But even under that impact, Hwa Mugi was not sucked in; instead, he walked forward.

Thump! Thoom!

As if to show that no matter what they tried, they could not stop him, he approached one step at a time, like a nightmare on a summer night.

Whirrrrrr.

Behind him, the Annihilation Vortex pulled at him even more fiercely, but he continued walking forward.

Hwa Mugi came right up to where the Pungcheon Cult Leader stood. Perhaps, because the Pungcheon Cult Leader had been the one spouting those hateful words all along, he was approaching in this manner all the more. Go on, run your mouth again!

Even when he had come close enough that a thrust of the sword would pierce his heart, the Pungcheon Cult Leader did not dodge.

The very moment Hwa Mugi thrust out the black sword.

Shiiiiiik.

Before the black sword, the White Flower Sword sliced through the air. The Sword King, despite his internal injuries, had pulled himself together in a dramatic instant.

Clang!

The moment Hwa Mugi split his inner energy to block the Sword King’s blade—

Whiririririk.

Hwa Mugi was sucked into the black hole created by the Annihilation Vortex.

Pop.

As though it had sent him off to another world, the black hole vanished in an instant.

But the Pungcheon Cult Leader could not feel relieved. Judging by Hwa Mugi’s skill, which he had faced firsthand—

“That won’t hold him for long either.”

No sooner had those words ended than—

Puuuk.

The black sword tore through the air and burst out.

Riiiiip.

Through the long-ripped space, Hwa Mugi poked his head out.

“I’m not fond of dark places.”

With his body immersed in a space that annihilated absolutely everything, Hwa Mugi was smiling.

As he looked upon that astonishing divine might, which went beyond the grotesque, the Sword King asked the Pungcheon Cult Leader,

“Why did you go this far to protect me?”

Just moments ago, the Pungcheon Cult Leader had risked his life to save him. Had he been even a little late, the man would have been stabbed by Hwa Mugi’s sword.

As if curious about the reason as well, Hwa Mugi listened with only his head sticking out of the darkness.

There are five other people here, so surely someone would save you.

The Sword King had naturally expected that kind of joke.

But the Pungcheon Cult Leader gave a reason no one had imagined.

“My problems always come back to the Young Cult Leader.”

Everyone’s eyes turned toward the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“If you die, then even if we win this fight, it will be a defeat for the Young Cult Leader.”

“!”

“A defeat he will grieve for a very long time. Perhaps for the rest of his life.”

The Sword King had not known that the Pungcheon Cult Leader was thinking such thoughts.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s gaze turned toward Geom Ujin. He thought Geom Ujin would understand his heart better than anyone.

Because he, more than anyone, would know with what feelings his son had entrusted this fight to them.

Because what he felt now was the same as what Geom Ujin had felt when he blew away the wall of the second floor. The same as his wish to save them all, so they could leave words upon a new wall.

“I want to give the Young Cult Leader a proper victory.”

A nightmare-like being was sticking his head out toward them, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader was not afraid in the least.

“So not one of us can die.”

Chapter 822: Judging by Your Looks, You Do Seem Like a Villain
--------------------------------------------------------------
A faint smile formed at the corner of Geom Ujin’s mouth.

Because the words of Pungcheon Sect Leader—that not a single person should die for the young sect leader—were his own will as well.

Gwon Ma had been seeing that kind of smile on the Sect Leader often these days, and he was truly glad. It was a smile he had often seen in his youth, but at some point, he had become unable to see it.

Gwon Ma knew. That the Sect Leader had grown weary while leading the sect.

A man who had dominated the Central Plains was now trapped in the Taesaui—how suffocating must it be? Because he was a man of such strong sense of responsibility, he could not simply kick away the Taesaui and rise up.

Perhaps the Sect Leader had felt depressed all along. That his relationship with the Demon Lords had been distant for a time was likely because of that.

The one who broke that was none other than the young sect leader. And it wasn’t just the Sect Leader. He had pulled even the Demon Lords, who had fallen into their own darkness, back out into the light.

Just as he had rescued him when he was trapped in a great hole that threatened to collapse the cliff.

“We must all survive and show off to the young sect leader, mustn’t we?”

Everyone agreed with Pungcheon Sect Leader’s words.

Yes, in this fight, no one must die. Because that would be a true victory for Geom Mugik. They had received much from that young, inexperienced young sect leader; now it was time to give back.

The Sword King asked Pungcheon Sect Leader.

“Did you not say you had suffered much at the hands of the young sect leader?”

At the tavern here as well, he had complained to him several times. He had spoken loudly on purpose so that Geom Ujin could hear.

“That is so.”

“Then why are you doing this?”

Pungcheon Sect Leader revealed his honest feelings.

“Because I have received even more.”

Pungcheon Sect Leader’s gaze turned toward the Sword King. Was it because he had promised to name the tavern and had spent a few days together here?

“And even if it weren’t for the young sect leader, surely I cannot afford to lose you to the likes of him?”

“You nearly came to danger, Sect Leader.”

Earlier, when Hwamugi had approached him, Pungcheon Sect Leader had felt death.

Had he ever felt death so vividly?

“There are five of us. Even if it weren’t you, someone would have saved me.”

Only now was Pungcheon Sect Leader making a joke, but the Sword King took that joke seriously.

“Which is precisely why you might not have been saved. Because there are five of you.”

“What do you mean?”

“Because everyone would assume someone else would save you.”

Pungcheon Sect Leader put on a deliberately startled expression.

“Could it be that I really almost died? No! I still have things I want to do piled up like Mount Tai!”

The Sword King’s gaze turned back to Hwamugi.

“If we do not kill him, you will not be able to do even a single one of them.”

Pungcheon Sect Leader could tell. That even though the Sword King had suffered internal injuries, he wanted to keep fighting.

“Will you be alright?”

“Yes.”

The Sword King had felt it while fighting Hwamugi. That this was an opponent he could not defeat.

But he had no intention of retreating like this.

As long as his internal energy remained, he had to make that Thousand Kill Star expend even a little more internal energy and pass the fight on to the next person.

Because the internal energy remaining in him would become attacks flying toward those who remained. He had to take even one blow in their place before passing it on.

And there was something more important than making him consume internal energy.

Those who had yet to fight this battle were watching.

Making them learn even a little more about Hwamugi—that was important.

“Will you be alright, Sect Leader?”

“I am a man born and raised among rough sandstorms. A fight like this is merely a scuffle in a greenhouse.”

He had brought up the greenhouse story to say this. Pungcheon Sect Leader spoke while looking at Hwamugi, who still had only his head poking out from the darkness.

“Is it warm in the greenhouse?”

Screeeeeech.

Hwamugi tore apart the darkness shrouding the lower half of his face with his hand and emerged.

“Your martial arts may be nothing to behold.”

His killing intent had grown even more intense. While facing seven masters, his primal killing intent was climbing to its limit as well.

“But at least you are no coward like someone.”

The Sword King parried the mocking words aimed at Jin Paecheon.

“He is being cautious and wise.”

If he were to overexert his injured body in battle and have the Gentleman Sword taken from him, nothing could be worse.

“Before we fight, there is something I wish to ask the person inside your body.”

Because this was the Sword King—a man who, unlike Pungcheon Sect Leader, did not seem the type to say such things.

Hwamugi nodded with an expression that said, “Do as you please.”

“But who is in charge? I have something to ask the leader.”

Hwamugi immediately grasped the Sword King’s intention.

“You think to cause internal division with such words? You have used your head, but it is useless. These people were never one to begin with.”

Hwamugi’s gaze turned to where Geom Ujin, Jin Paecheon, and Baek Jagang stood.

“Just as no matter how friendly they act, they are existences that can never become one.”

Geom Ujin and the two alliance leaders said nothing. He was not wrong.

Because once this fight ended, they were in a relationship where they would have to place each other’s names in the position of greatest enemy.

The Sword King spoke as if declaring a certainty.

“Because the young sect leader is here, this time will be different.”

Hwamugi made a surprised expression. First Pungcheon Sect Leader, and now even the Sword King.

“You truly favor the young sect leader. I am curious what sort of punk he is.”

“Then why did you not go to the young sect leader yourself?”

At that question, Hwamugi’s expression flinched before he soon formed a cold smile.

“I left the young sect leader to the Dark Palace Lord.”

“Not because you are afraid of the young sect leader?”

Hwamugi snorted at Pungcheon Sect Leader’s words.

“I am not afraid even with all of you gathered here; would I be afraid of the young sect leader?”

At that moment, goosebumps rose at Baek Jagang’s ears as he listened.

Which part was the lie? That he was not afraid even with all of them gathered? Or that he was not afraid of the young sect leader?

From the composure Hwamugi had shown until now, Baek Jagang could tell.

*The Thousand Kill Star fears the young sect leader.*

Earlier, when Baek Jagang had conversed via sound transmission with Ju Gasin, he had guessed that Hwamugi might have some weakness.

Now was the moment he realized that weakness was Geom Mugik.

Baek Jagang thought of Geom Mugik. To think even this Thousand Kill Star felt fear.

He felt that perhaps Geom Mugik was truly a fate bestowed by the heavens.

Hwamugi charged at the Sword King.

“I will kill all of you, and I will kill the young sect leader too!”

Once again, the two men’s swords clashed in the air.

With just a few exchanges, the Sword King’s insides were shaken.

It was speed and power he could not adapt to despite having exchanged many moves, not to mention the killing intent that had grown even stronger.

But the Sword King’s will was no less than that killing intent.

*Even if it is only a single move, I will do my utmost to exhaust you.*

Of course, Pungcheon Sect Leader was not merely watching the two fight.

Cheonoe Hyeolma Gong, Gwangsa Hyeolpung.

Swaaaaaaaash!

Wind whipped toward Hwamugi.

A martial art demonstrated in this situation—how could it be mere wind?

It was a wind of death that tore apart protective astral energy.

But Hwamugi was not the slightest bit disturbed. Even in that fierce wind, Hwamugi did not blink an eye and remained focused on his fight with the Sword King.

Pungcheon Sect Leader was not surprised.

His opponent had endured the Samyeol Wasang with the Cheongeunchu, and when trapped there, had torn his way out in the blink of an eye.

Still, thanks to Gwangsa Hyeolpung, the Sword King obtained an opening to unleash his sect’s unique martial arts in earnest.

Though he was absolutely inferior in internal energy, he began a fight of great internal energy consumption.

Muhyeong Shingeom, Second Form: Muhyeong Chamgyeok.

The moment the Baekhwageom trembled in the air.

A streak of sword energy as fast as lightning struck down at Hwamugi’s body.

Hwamugi dodged the unavoidable attack. What he unleashed this time was the footwork of Ju Gasin, the Demon Path Alliance Leader from three hundred years ago.

Gwangse Cheonwangbo.

He dodged the attack as fast as light with footwork as fast as light. It was a miraculous footwork that made one realize he had taken hits with his body until now simply to show off his skill.

The Sword King’s attacks continued.

Muhyeong Shingeom, Fourth Form: Muhyeong Pung’u.

Formless sword energy poured down like wind and rain from all directions.

“Excellent!”

Hwamugi swung his sword in all directions with an excited face. He showed that he could block even wind and rain.

Bang-bang-bang-bang-bang-bang!

The walls shook from the impact, and shattered debris and dust rained down from the ceiling.

Thud-thud-thud-thud.

The moment the shattered wreckage obscured their vision was the moment the Sword King had aimed for.

In order to unleash precisely this technique.

Shwiiiiik!

A sharp wind sound cut across the hall!

Hwamugi was extending his sword straight ahead.

Screeeeech.

A horizontal line began to form on the wall behind Hwamugi. The height at which that line was drawn was exactly where Hwamugi’s neck was.

The line drawn from the end of the wall continued to where Hwamugi stood. If it continued, it meant Hwamugi’s neck was cut.

The drawn line stopped beside Hwamugi’s neck. Now, a red line should have been drawn on Hwamugi’s neck.

Screeeeeeech.

From past Hwamugi’s neck, the line began to be drawn again.

Muhyeong Shingeom, Seventh Form: Muhyeong Ilsaeng.

The very technique that had severed the Dark King’s neck.

Hwamugi had blocked that lethal strike.

“This one surprised me a bit.”

Hwamugi laughed. The Hwamugi standing before them was an immortal and indestructible monster.

Hwamugi rushed at the Sword King again.

Clang! Clank! Claaang!

As if determined to kill him this time, Hwamugi pressed the Sword King even more fiercely.

The Sword King foresaw it. That he would now have difficulty enduring even fifty moves. In a fight where more than ten moves passed in an instant, fifty moves would come quickly.

Right then, the Sword King saw Pungcheon Sect Leader take something invisible from his body behind Hwamugi.

Pungcheon Sect Leader’s ultimate secret technique.

*Muhyeongchang!*

It was a Muhyeongchang infused with all the internal energy Pungcheon Sect Leader had saved.

In an instant, Pungcheon Sect Leader’s and the Sword King’s gazes met.

—*If only it can hit, I have confidence it will pierce the Thousand Kill Star’s body.*

It was right then.

Ting!

The Sword King’s Baekhwageom could no longer endure the attacks and escaped his grasp.

The next moment, the Sword King’s two hands grabbed Hwamugi’s wrist. At that moment, Hwamugi realized.

That the Sword King had intentionally dropped his sword.

Shwiiiiiik.

At the same time, a powerful energy was felt from behind.

The Sword King pulled up all his strength to keep him from moving. In that instant, Hwamugi read a will prepared for death in the Sword King’s eyes.

*You may pierce through me as well!*

Swaaaaaack!

In the next moment, this time the Sword King saw it. Hwamugi smiling cruelly.

Hwiik.

In an instant, the two men’s positions were reversed. Though he had pulled up all his internal energy to resist, he could not withstand Hwamugi’s strength.

As Hwamugi turned the Sword King around, the flying Muhyeongchang soared toward the Sword King’s back.

Swaaaaack.

Then Hwamugi saw again. The Sword King was not flustered, and his two eyes were calm.

*Could it be?*

The Sword King had not raised his protective astral energy.

The Sword King had expected that no matter how hard he held on, their positions would change like this.

If so? From the beginning, his intention had been to let the Muhyeongchang pierce his own body and, with that momentum, pierce Hwamugi as well.

The Sword King did not let go of his hands gripping Hwamugi until the end. A body that should have died by Geom Mugik’s hand—heaven must have kept him alive until this moment.

But the Muhyeongchang stopped right in front of the Sword King’s back.

Pungcheon Sect Leader had not thrown the Muhyeongchang; he had charged directly while holding the spear.

Throwing it would have been fastest and most effective, but Pungcheon Sect Leader had worried about their positions switching. He had not known the Sword King was trying to sacrifice himself.

Ssssssh.

The Muhyeongchang held in Pungcheon Sect Leader’s hand disappeared. Against another master, he would have tried to kill him with this Muhyeongchang.

But against Hwamugi, this Muhyeongchang would only get in the way. It was an opponent even the Spear King could not defeat.

Thud!

At Hwamugi’s palm strike, the Sword King and Pungcheon Sect Leader overlapped and were pushed back.

“Good! If you cannot do it, I will pierce through together with you!”

Hwamugi swung his black sword.

Shwiiiiik.

The Sword King did not dodge. If he dodged, Pungcheon Sect Leader behind him would be stabbed.

But having dropped his sword to grab Hwamugi’s arm moments ago, he had nothing but his body.

This time, the Sword King pulled his protective astral energy to the limit. Even if he was pierced, he hoped Pungcheon Sect Leader would not be stabbed as well.

Shwiik!

Stab!

The black sword pierced through the protective astral energy and embedded itself in the Sword King’s chest.

The moment Hwamugi pushed the sword, trying to run him completely through.

Tak.

The black sword stopped in midair.

Someone had grabbed his sword with a bare hand.

“You grab my sword with your hand?”

It was Gwonma, rushing in before anyone realized.

The hand gripping the black sword was not bare. Gwonma wore the Tusin gauntlets that Geommugeuk had given him as a gift.

Hwoooong!

Thwack!

The fist of Gwonma’s other hand slammed squarely into Hwamugi’s face.

Rumble, boom boom!

Simultaneously, a tremendous peal of thunder rang out.

As the black sword embedded in Geomwang’s chest was drawn out, Hwamugi was sent flying backward.

In that brief moment, Gwonma instantly pressed the acupoints on Geomwang’s chest to stanch the bleeding. Fortunately, the blade had missed his heart, and because it had been stopped before penetrating through, he had avoided a fatal wound.

“You should have dodged!”

Pungcheon Cult Leader shouted furiously at Geomwang, who had recklessly taken the blow with his body instead of dodging.

“…You should have thrown the Muhyeongchang.”

In the end, the two had saved each other.

“Thank you for saving me.”

To Geomwang’s thanks, Gwonma nodded once with a stoic expression.

Hwamugi was covering his ears rather than his struck face. The thunderous sound that had erupted when Gwonma’s fist struck his face had nearly torn his eardrums.

That fury was directed at Gwonma.

“An empty cart makes the most noise.”

But Pungcheon Cult Leader stood on this side.

“Why do you think that cart is empty? It’s empty because it must carry your corpse.”

Hwamugi’s killing intent intensified, but Pungcheon Cult Leader had already taken the injured Geomwang and gone to where Geom Woojin and the two alliance leaders were.

Geom Woojin did not ask Geomwang if he was alright. Geomwang would rather hear these words than *are you alright*.

“You fought well.”

Geomwang lowered his head, as if ashamed. He wished he could have fought a little better, but this was his best.

“And don’t ever do that again.”

Geom Woojin knew he had tried to sacrifice himself.

Geomwang lowered his head. It was a rebuke for having tried to die when he had promised to protect the Young Cult Leader. Yes, it was enough that someone understood.

*I will use the life remaining to me for the Young Cult Leader.*

Then someone walked toward where Gwonma stood. It was none other than Baek Jogang.

Hwamugi had taken a blow, but that didn’t mean Gwonma could face him alone. Baek Jogang judged that now was the time for him to step forward.

Baek Jogang stood beside Gwonma. Never in his life had he imagined a day would come when he would fight on the same side as this man.

As two new figures appeared, Hwamugi spoke loudly as if delivering an oration.

“The Cult Leader and Alliance Lords gather to wage a wheel battle! Who exactly is the villain and who is the hero?”

Pungcheon Cult Leader readily conceded the point, taking the wind out of his opponent’s sails.

“Frankly, judging by looks alone, you two are indeed the villains.”

Baek Jogang looked at Gwonma and shared his thoughts on the earlier fight.

“Everyone was too polite.”

Jin Pacheon needed no mention; by his standards, Geomwang and Pungcheon Cult Leader were likewise.

But with Gwonma?

“Since he wants to be the hero.”

Killing intent began to flow from Baek Jogang’s body. It was an unspeakably sinister and icy energy, unlike anything seen before.

“Let’s properly play the part of the villains.”

Chapter 823: What Can Kill Me
-----------------------------
The sinister qi Baek Jagang released seized control of the space.

The ultimate sinister qi.

The moment that qi was felt, every body grew stifled, as though trapped inside a narrow box; gooseflesh rose all over, and a chill set in. The surface grew cold, slowing movement, yet the heart burned hot.

Fear and pressure, rage and hatred—all negative desires began to run rampant.

It was a situation in which one should have been crushed by that energy alone, but Hwa Mugi drew in a deep breath and savored it.

“This is the first time I’ve felt such powerful sinister qi.”

Ju Gasin, who dwelled within his body, had never once revealed an energy like this. Hwa Mugi put it this way.

“The man inside me is far too insidious, you see.”

Baek Jagang recalled the Ju Gasin with whom he had spoken earlier. That insidious man had tried to save him. Thinking that he would be listening, Baek Jagang said to Hwa Mugi,

“Still, thank you for making the offer.”

It was a sudden remark, but everyone could guess what it meant. It was a continuation of the sound-transmission conversation from before. It must have been spoken to the leader of the Evil Dao Alliance from three hundred years ago.

The Fist Demon silently looked at Baek Jagang standing beside him.

He found it unexpected that Baek Jagang had stepped forward like this.

Since Jin Paecheon had fought alone, he had expected Baek Jagang to fight alone as well.

The Martial Alliance Leader fought alone, so how could I, the leader of the Evil Dao Alliance, join forces against him?

He had naturally expected Baek Jagang to act that way, but Baek Jagang chose practicality over face. And that was not all.

The Fist Demon read this resolve in Baek Jagang’s eyes.

Let the two of us end this fight!

In that instant, the Fist Demon clenched his fist tightly. It was exactly what he had hoped for. If this dangerous fight did not pass on to the Cult Leader, there was nothing more he could ask.

The Fist Demon began releasing his demonic qi in earnest. His demonic qi, too, was different from usual.

It was not demonic qi that spread and erupted outward. It was demonic qi like Mount Tai pressing down. Something vast and unbreakable began to crush Hwa Mugi.

Baek Jagang’s sinister qi and the Fist Demon’s demonic qi.

The two energies pressed down on Hwa Mugi at the same time.

It was an overwhelmingly powerful aura, enough to make even standing difficult, but Hwa Mugi was perfectly fine.

“These energies are not unfamiliar to me.”

Then, as if he intended to become a hero capturing true villains, Hwa Mugi said,

“Yes, you two are villains worth trying to catch!”

Hwa Mugi’s gaze turned to the Fist Demon.

“I wanted to fight you more than anyone else.”

Hwa Mugi slid his black sword back into its scabbard.

“With these fists.”

Hwa Mugi clenched both fists as if to show the Fist Demon. There could be no stronger provocation. He was proposing a fistfight with the Fist Demon, here and now.

“What do you say? Fight me with fist arts—”

Before Hwa Mugi could even finish speaking, Baek Jagang’s form cut through the air.

Shiiiiik!

Baek Jagang’s sword rushed in an instant, flying toward Hwa Mugi’s chest.

It was an unexpected attack. Who could have imagined Baek Jagang would charge in like this while Hwa Mugi was suggesting a fistfight with the Fist Demon?

Shiiii!

Baek Jagang’s sword grazed past Hwa Mugi’s body. When Hwa Mugi twisted aside and avoided the blade, the sword that had flown in changed its angle.

Kagak!

Baek Jagang’s sword again grazed past his face.

It was not an attack that could be avoided normally, so Hwa Mugi evaded it using Ju Gasin’s Gwangse Cheonwang Step, which he had displayed earlier.

As if he would not permit Hwa Mugi to draw his sword again, Baek Jagang continued attacking without pause.

Baek Jagang’s attacks were different again from Jin Paecheon’s or the Sword King’s.

Baek Jagang did not aim only for vital points.

After striking upward toward Hwa Mugi’s jaw, he drew his sword diagonally. This time he aimed for the shoulder, and next for the thigh.

An arm was fine. The waist was fine too. Anywhere he could cut his opponent was good enough. That made him even more threatening.

Hwiik.

In the end, Hwa Mugi employed the Gwangse Cheonwang Step and leaped backward. It was the first time he had retreated since the fight began.

“This is you disregarding the Fist Demon—”

Once again, Hwa Mugi could not finish speaking. This time, a fist wind released from the Fist Demon’s fist came flying in.

Huwaaaaaang!

While Hwa Mugi and Baek Jagang fought, the Fist Demon had drawn his internal energy into his fist to the limit.

Thus, it was no ordinary fist wind. It was a fist wind carrying enough power to turn boulders into powder.

Kwaaaaaang!

Swept up in the fist wind, Hwa Mugi smashed through the tavern wall and flew backward. He tried to withstand it with his protective astral qi, but from behind the fist wind, Baek Jagang and the Fist Demon flew in at the same time.

Hwa Mugi was forced outside Pungryu Tavern, and Baek Jagang and the Fist Demon also rushed after him. The two pressed him without giving him even a chance to catch his breath.

Sword qi and fist wind flew toward him at once.

Hwa Mugi shot upward into the air.

Kwaaaaaang!

The building across from Pungryu Tavern was swept up by the sword qi and fist wind and blown away in its entirety.

Now the stage of the fight was no longer Pungryu Tavern, but Maga Village.

Hwa Mugi landed on the roof of the neighboring building.

“Fist Demon, I wanted to have a splendid fight with you. Are you going to behave so disgracefully like that Evil Dao bastard?”

It seemed Hwa Mugi wanted to fight the Fist Demon no matter what.

“Why? Not confident?”

His provocation appeared to go beyond confidence and reach the height of arrogance.

But to Baek Jagang, it looked different.

—He is trying to avoid being attacked together.

If the Fist Demon engaged Hwa Mugi in close combat with fist arts, the two of them would become entangled in the fight. In the end, it would become difficult for Baek Jagang to join in against Hwa Mugi.

The Fist Demon immediately grasped what Baek Jagang meant.

—Let us not fight according to his wishes.

Jin Paecheon, who had been watching, also read Hwa Mugi’s intent. He interpreted the reason Hwa Mugi was avoiding a joint attack like this.

—It seems he has begun regulating his internal energy.

At Jin Paecheon’s sound transmission, Geom Ujin nodded in agreement.

The Heavenly Killing Star had displayed his strength by enduring attacks with protective astral qi, but now he had begun avoiding them.

Yes. No matter how much of a Heavenly Killing Star he was, as long as he was human, there was no way he possessed infinite internal energy.

Nor did he possess enough power to crush them all at once.

He had inflicted external injuries on Jin Paecheon and internal injuries on the Sword King. Jin Paecheon had the Gentleman’s Sword, and the Sword King had the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

The power Hwa Mugi had shown so far was only a little stronger than when two people joined forces against him. That meant that if a joint attack was properly executed, an even fight was possible.

On the other hand, the fight had become far more dangerous.

Because once he no longer had leisure with his internal energy, the Heavenly Killing Star might use a one-strike sure-kill technique.

As in the fights before, the situation could end before anyone could intervene to help.

In the end, Baek Jagang and the Fist Demon had to help each other.

Just how far would they go to help one another?

Because he could not know that, Geom Ujin was paying even closer attention to this fight than to any of the earlier ones.

The Fist Demon looked up at Hwa Mugi standing on the roof and said,

“You want to fight me with fist arts?”

The Fist Demon gestured for him to come down.

“Come down. A fistfight is fought with one’s feet planted on the ground.”

It was the very moment Hwa Mugi leaped down from the roof in front of the Fist Demon.

Baek Jagang’s sword, which had been waiting for that instant, cut through the air.

Hegemon’s Heaven-Shaking Sword Art.

First Form, White Heaven Form.

Pure white astral qi surged toward Hwa Mugi.

But as if he had already been waiting, Hwa Mugi’s black sword cut through the air.

Gwangmyeong Muyang Sword Art.

Second Form, Great Yang Gives Birth to Being.

Kwaaaaaang!

When two supreme martial arts that could not be judged superior or inferior collided, the surroundings shook with an explosion as though an earthquake had struck. Hwa Mugi had already anticipated the surprise attack.

Shiiiiik!

Hwa Mugi charged through the rising dust.

Kaaang! Kang! Kaaang!

The moment sword met sword, Baek Jagang felt the same impression as those who had fought before him.

‘He’s strong.’

Hwa Mugi’s black sword truly carried internal energy so powerful it felt as if it would sever his wrist.

‘It should be about time for his power to decrease.’

Yet Hwa Mugi was still powerful.

After they exchanged more than a dozen moves in an instant, Hwa Mugi’s final strike was properly imbued with internal energy.

He tried to break Baek Jagang’s sword with that single move, but the sword did not break.

Kwaaaang!

Baek Jagang slid backward. The attack had been too powerful, so he retreated to disperse and scatter his opponent’s force.

‘Yes, keep using your internal energy like that!’

In the meantime, Hwa Mugi stabbed toward the Fist Demon’s vital points and launched an attack.

Shik, shik, shik, shik!

Because the Fist Demon was so large, avoiding those attacks did not seem easy.

But astonishingly, the Fist Demon’s huge body avoided every attack that flew at him in succession. His footwork was dazzling.

In fist arts, what is more important than the fist is the step.

That was what the Fist Demon always emphasized to his subordinates.

In other words, avoiding was more important than striking.

Because bare hands had to face weapons, footwork and bodily techniques for evading and deflecting the opponent’s attacks were the most important.

How does he dodge like that with such a huge body?

The Fist Demon showed everyone the answer through his actions.

On top of that, Hwa Mugi could not attack only the Fist Demon. Before he knew it, Baek Jagang was attacking from his left.

Hwa Mugi avoided Baek Jagang’s sword and thrust out his own.

Shiiiik!

Dodging the incoming sword, Hwa Mugi thrust his blade. In an instant, the two crossing swords were aiming for each other’s hearts.

At the very moment the two blades missed.

Teong!

The Fist Demon’s fist slammed into his side.

The instant his fist struck directly, the Fist Demon could feel it.

It was protective astral qi unlike anything he had ever felt in his life.

It felt different from when he had first hit Hwa Mugi’s face. Back then, there had been a proper sensation of impact, but now it was different.

The sound was loud, but the moment his fist touched the body, it felt as if he were striking water. And he felt the internal force contained in his fist scatter into that water.

‘So this is the kind of protective astral qi that allowed him to block everything!’

Hwa Mugi thrust his sword toward the Fist Demon.

Shiiiik.

Chaeeng.

Baek Jagang intercepted and blocked the attack flying toward the Fist Demon.

At that very moment, the Fist Demon launched an attack no one had expected. An attack no one thought he would unleash from such close range.

The Fist Demon’s unique martial art, Thunderbolt Asura Fist.

First Fist, Black Cloud Asura.

Kwaaaang!

With an explosion, Hwa Mugi slid backward. Even after taking the Thunderbolt Asura Fist head-on, he did not collapse.

The two did not miss this chance.

This time, Baek Jagang’s unique martial art flew in again.

Hegemon’s Heaven-Shaking Sword Art, Fifth Form, Annihilating Illusion Form.

Sword qi split apart in every direction with Hwa Mugi at the center. There was nowhere to dodge.

Shuk, shuk, shuk, shuk, shuk, shuk, shuk, shuk, shuk!

The sword qi that flew in from all sides struck Hwa Mugi’s body directly.

The earth shook, and an explosion erupted.

Amid the rising cloud of dirt, Hwa Mugi still did not fall and stood in place.

Swaaaaaek!

The Fist Demon’s fist flew in again.

Puuuuk!

For the first time, Hwa Mugi was sent flying backward and rolled across the ground.

The Fist Demon’s heaviest fist, the Third Fist, Heavenly Thunder Asura, had struck him precisely.

Kwa-rururung!

The sound of thunder that shook heaven and earth followed.

As Hwa Mugi sprang to his feet, the astral qi released from Baek Jagang’s sword struck him in succession.

Kwaaaang! Kwaang! Kwang!

The Fist Demon was the same. Thinking that they could not miss this opportunity, he poured out fist astral force.

Hwa Mugi could not avoid those attacks and was taking them all.

It was a chance to kill him.

The Fist Demon was about to unleash the final move he possessed.

The final Sixth Fist, Flame Thunder Asura.
