It was the last technique he could use only once, because it poured all the internal energy in his body into a single strike.

Baek Jagang was the same. With the resolve to cut him down this time without fail, he was drawing up his internal energy to unleash the most powerful move of the Hegemon’s Heaven-Shaking Sword Art.

Just then, someone stopped the two of them.

“Wait!”

It was someone no one had expected. It was Geom Ujin, who had been silently watching the fight until now.

When the attack stopped at his restraint, the dirt cloud settled, revealing Hwa Mugi’s figure.

He was still standing without having fallen.

A fierce killing intent poured from Hwa Mugi’s body. If the killing intent until now had been one that gradually heightened as it was released, the killing intent now had torn its way out of him.

An uncontrollable, ungovernable killing intent.

At last, the Heavenly Killing Star had begun to crumble.

The Fist Demon could not understand.

‘It was the perfect chance. Why did the Cult Leader stop us?’

Geom Ujin’s gaze turned toward Hwa Mugi.

A crimson glow flowed from Hwa Mugi’s eyes. It was the first time he had seen killing intent tinged with such red energy.

Looking at him, Geom Ujin calmly said,

“Enough acting.”

At the word “acting,” not only the Fist Demon and Baek Jagang, but everyone else as well looked back and forth between Geom Ujin and Hwa Mugi in astonishment.

Geom Ujin said to Baek Jagang,

“It seems that man’s protective astral qi is no ordinary thing.”

Geom Ujin had been watching Hwa Mugi fight all this time.

Even in the earlier fights against others, he had blocked attacks he could have avoided with his protective astral qi. At first, Geom Ujin had thought that was to show off his own power.

But when he saw Hwa Mugi accept the terrifying attacks of the Fist Demon and Baek Jagang just now without resistance, Geom Ujin felt an inexplicable sense of wrongness.

Hwa Mugi could clearly have avoided them. The attacks that flew in were truly powerful, but considering the footwork he had shown earlier, there was no way he would allow attacks to land on him in succession like this.

Moreover, had he not been the one trying to fight the Fist Demon with fist arts in order to manage his internal energy?

Then why was he taking attacks like this now? The consumption of internal energy should have been extreme.

If he was deliberately inviting disadvantage, then?

An astonishing statement flowed from Geom Ujin’s mouth.

“It seems that man’s protective astral qi absorbs external attacks as internal energy.”

“!”

Because it was a shocking statement, everyone stared at Hwa Mugi with widened eyes.

Geom Ujin asked Hwa Mugi,

“Is that not so?”

Sssssss.

Then the killing intent that had been rising madly began to subside.

It had seemed like an uncontrollable killing intent, yet it sank in an instant. Astonishingly, he had been able to control it.

At the same time, ease appeared on Hwa Mugi’s rage-filled face.

“As expected! You noticed.”

Hwa Mugi had allowed the attacks not merely to flaunt his protective astral qi.

“Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art. It is a martial art I created.”

More precisely, it was a martial art he had created with the help of the souls inside his body.

“You all thought you were exhausting my internal energy until now, but I was exhausting yours.”

“!”

Two on their side had been injured, and everyone had depleted a great deal of internal energy.

“My internal energy has been fully replenished.”

If Geom Ujin had not noticed, Baek Jagang and the Fist Demon would also have had their internal energy completely drained.

He had let himself be hit from the very beginning in order to make everyone believe that he blindly trusted his protective astral qi. Only then would they not suspect anything even if he was struck like this.

Hwa Mugi was by no means an arrogant man. He had been pretending to be arrogant while conducting a sure and perfect fight.

The moment Hwa Mugi was truly arrogant was right now.

“The only one who can kill me is myself.”

Chapter 824: Because You Only Show Up When I Curse!
---------------------------------------------------
The atmosphere around Hwamugi changed.

Now that his killing intent had completely vanished, he felt far more terrifying than when his killing intent had run rampant.

The fact that he had withstood the combined assault of Gwonma and Baek Jagang, and had even recovered his internal energy in the process, was enough to crush the momentum of everyone present.

It was Gwonma who broke the heavy silence.

“I apologize. I should have noticed it first.”

When he had struck Hwamugi’s body with his fist earlier, Gwonma had experienced a protective astral qi unlike anything he had felt in his entire life. He should have realized it then.

Facing Gwonma’s self-reproach, Geom Ujin spoke firmly.

“It was something difficult even for those watching from the side to figure out. It would have been impossible to know while fighting such a difficult battle.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader nodded.

“None of us who fought earlier noticed either.”

Who could have imagined that such an incredible martial art existed?

That was why those words had naturally escaped the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“If one must be at this level to cause a Blood Calamity, then I will never cause one in my entire life.”

As if this, with one layer peeled away, was his true self, Hwamugi no longer smiled.

“Not just the righteous and demonic sects, but even the mundane world has gathered to wait for me. Are you lot truly sane?”

As he had said before, those words carried the meaning that they were the ones who had created him.

As Jin Paecheon watched Hwamugi’s cold gaze fly toward him, he thought he had been right to come here.

If he had not come to help the Demonic Cult Leader?

Could the Heavenly Devil Divine Cult have truly faced that monstrous man alone?

And if that Heavenly Slaughter Star who swept through the demonic cult came to the Murim Alliance? Could he himself have stopped him?

He had come for his grandson, but as a result…

‘Hagun, you sent these people to save this old man.’

And with the Gentleman Sword in tow, no less.

Geom Ujin asked Baek Jagang.

“Will you continue to fight?”

Though he himself had discovered the secret of the protective astral qi, this was still a fight between Baek Jagang and Gwonma. Whether to stop fighting was for the two of them to decide.

Before answering, Baek Jagang looked at Gwonma. Before making his own decision, he first asked Gwonma’s intentions. It was his courtesy toward Gwonma, with whom he had fought together.

Under normal circumstances, Gwonma would have naturally answered that he would keep fighting. Ending the fight after merely allowing his opponent to recover internal energy? That would have been an unbearable humiliation.

But seeing Baek Jagang’s choice to join forces with him had given him a realization.

Baek Jagang was someone who prioritized reasonable gain over pride or honor. Yet that did not make him seem like someone with low self-esteem or a dishonorable person.

His instincts told him there was no way to fight this opponent, so he would just fight blindly? Merely because he had been in the middle of fighting?

Thinking of gain rather than pride, Gwonma too took a step back.

“I will follow the Alliance Leader’s will.”

Baek Jagang did not immediately make a decision. He turned around and asked the others.

“Do any of you have a good idea?”

Since he had no intention of trying to look good to anyone, his demeanor was rather trustworthy.

Jin Paecheon spoke in a low voice.

“The more extreme the effects of a demonic art, the greater its side effects.”

Jin Paecheon was certain that since Hwamugi had absorbed internal energy, there must be something he had sacrificed in exchange.

“We must find that weakness.”

At that, Hwamugi began to reveal his killing intent once more. It was a killing intent completely different from what he had shown before.

It was his true killing intent, and also the killing intent of the souls dwelling within his body. That killing intent contained the power of the ages.

Making them feel the stride of death’s approach, killing intent accumulated over three hundred years drifted through the air.

That killing intent spoke thus.

I have no weakness!

The Pungcheon Cult Leader spoke as if sighing.

“Just where in the world is the Little Cult Leader? With that clever head of his, he should be saying this is this, and that is that! That this is how you fight that monstrous wretch! He should be telling us. He’s never around when you need him, but without fail he shows up when I’m taking my divine artifacts. When I took Gowol, he came flooding over with an entire crowd! He always appears whenever I’m cursing and complaining, doesn’t he? Hurry up and show yourself, Little Cult Leader!”

The Sword King looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader with an expression that said he couldn’t be stopped. Cracking such jokes at a time like this?

The Pungcheon Cult Leader even turned around.

He desperately hoped to see him walking from the end of that Magachon road, but such a thing did not happen.

‘What kind of absurd expectation is this?’ Though he thought this, the Sword King’s gaze also followed the Pungcheon Cult Leader into the distance. He too wanted to see Geom Mugik.

Was the Sword King the only one? Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang’s hearts were not much different. They harbored such expectations because of the amazing displays Geom Mugik had shown until now.

Surely, if it were the Little Cult Leader, he would find the answer to this battle.

At that moment, Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang’s gazes briefly met. The two realized they had just had the same thought.

The Murim Alliance Leader and the Deputy Alliance Leader were waiting for a young Little Cult Leader old enough to be their grandson or son?

That fearful heart—wondering who might find out—was their shared sentiment.

Then Geom Ujin opened his mouth.

“What do you think?”

Everyone’s gaze turned to Geom Ujin. Surely he knew Hwamugi would not answer if he asked him that.

“Do you think I would answer that?”

Hwamugi too had thought he was being asked.

“I wasn’t asking you.”

Geom Ujin’s gaze passed beyond Hwamugi to the roof of a distant building.

When everyone’s gaze turned there, someone was happily waving toward them from that far, distant roof. He was so far away that he looked like a dot.

“Come quickly.”

It was a soft utterance, yet the one waving seemed to have heard those words as he shot forward.

A flash of light.

He flew over in an instant as if traversing space. Truly, none of the masters present could display movement arts as fast as this.

And as far as they knew, there was only one person who could show such divine movement.

“I have returned, Father.”

The one who landed before Geom Ujin was none other than Geom Mugik.

Everyone looked at Geom Mugik with surprised faces. The Pungcheon Cult Leader in particular gaped wide. Surprise and joy filled his face. He wanted to shout out right then.

‘He’s here, he’s here! The Little Cult Leader really came!’

At long last, the long-awaited Geom Mugik had arrived.

“I rushed over so quickly that I couldn’t even buy Father a gift.”

The fact that he started with the story of not being able to buy a gift in this situation proved he was unmistakably a son.

“How long have you been watching?”

“I arrived when the fight broke out outside the tavern.”

Geom Mugik had arrived in Magachon when the Deputy Alliance Leader and Gwonma were fighting Hwamugi.

“Why were you hiding?”

When he arrived, his internal energy happened to be completely depleted.

No matter how urgent it was, he needed to restore his internal energy, so he recovered it through qi circulation at a distance so far that even Hwamugi or his father couldn’t sense him.

While watching the situation here with the Divine Eye Technique, he notified his father that he had arrived as soon as his circulation ended—using the technique he had learned when he first climbed the mountain with his father.

He had sent his father that same sensation with which he had detected wild boars from several dozen jang away.

“I wasn’t hiding. It’s been a while since you fought, so you should display your full strength. That way you can show off a little for your son too. So please fight later as well, Father. I’m yielding to you because I’m a dutiful son.”

A smirk formed on his father’s lips. Seeing that smirk for the first time in a while, Geom Mugik smiled together with him.

You’ve come at a good time.

Yes, Father.

Now they could tell each other’s hearts just by looking at their eyes.

Geom Mugik was relieved and happy.

‘I wasn’t late!’

Afraid someone might have died. Afraid someone might have suffered an irreversible wound. Afraid he would arrive only when everyone was already in shambles.

He had run without resting for a single moment, excluding the time taken to recover internal energy. He hadn’t slept or eaten.

The worrywart Geom Mugik had harbored all sorts of dark thoughts on his way here.

But there was one person who ripped through those distracting thoughts.

Because his father was here, everything would be fine.

And surprisingly, there were also unexpected people at this place.

“Alliance Leader!”

Geom Mugik continued greeting with his back to Hwamugi. Hwamugi watched such a sight silently.

Hwamugi was not angered by this thoroughly intentional slight. He stared at Geom Mugik with a cautious gaze he had never shown before.

Geom Mugik politely greeted Jin Paecheon.

“It has been a while!”

Geom Mugik’s gaze moved to Jin Paecheon’s wound, and then to the Gentleman Sword at his waist. Just from that, he could tell what frame of mind he had come with and what had transpired.

“Thank you for coming.”

Jin Paecheon looked down at his Gentleman Sword and spoke.

“Give your thanks to Hagun.”

“At this gathering, I will seat Hagun at the seat of honor. Though of course, as the price of my boasting, he must buy the drinks.”

This time, he politely greeted Baek Jagang before continuing the banter.

“What about Sain?”

“He told me not to go because I might get hurt.”

“Then I shall seat him at the lowest seat.”

Surprisingly, Geom Mugik was chatting as if at a gathering of friends with Hwamugi behind his back, even cracking idle jokes.

And then came a single promise.

“I will never forget the grace of you two coming today. If the day comes when I must go somewhere, I will be the first to arrive!”

There was no greater repayment for Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang. Because it was a promise that this Little Cult Leader would come to rescue them if Jin Hagun or Bi Sain ever fell into danger.

“Come to our side first.”

Jin Paecheon also smiled at Baek Jagang’s joke.

At the sight of the two Alliance Leaders’ tension easing, Hwamugi’s eyes gleamed sharply.

They who had been so serious.

‘Smiling?’

The moment Geom Mugik arrived, the atmosphere truly changed completely. The gloomy battlefield somehow felt surprisingly bright.

Though it would have been natural to shoot a remark back at Hwamugi, Geom Mugik continued his greetings without turning to look at him.

No one would know how many times Geom Mugik had thought of Hwamugi from his pre-regression life until now.

No one here would know how fateful and important this meeting was. No one would know how much he had feared and yet awaited this moment.

But Geom Mugik did not give him a single glance.

Because the people here were more important than Hwamugi.

One mindset he had maintained ever since his regression.

I did not regress to kill you. I regressed to live a happy life with these people.

Even at this final moment, Geom Mugik was upholding that principle.

The next person Geom Mugik greeted was Gwonma.

“Master, your disciple has returned.”

“Welcome back.”

He delivered the news Gwonma would like most first.

“Daeju has also completed the mission safely. She will arrive later with the subordinates.”

At the news that his daughter was safe, joy flashed through Gwonma’s eyes. And the fact that Geom Mugik’s expression was so bright meant that the Demon Lords and Demon Folk who had gone that way were all safe.

If it had been a fight with the Dark Palace Mistress, it wouldn’t have been easy.

Without a word, Gwonma patted Geom Mugik’s shoulder with his large hand.

“You’ve worked hard.”

Hwamugi, who had been watching, spoke in a low voice.

“Anyone watching would think the fight was already over.”

The truth was, he hadn’t expected his first meeting with Geom Mugik to be looking at the back of his head.

Without turning around, Geom Mugik spoke.

“I’m holding you in high esteem, aren’t I?”

“What?”

“Even if I die, I should pay my respects before I die.”

Because those words meant he might die at Hwamugi’s hands and wished to settle his farewells first, and because they showed exactly how seriously he took himself, Hwamugi could not argue.

Geom Mugik looked at the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

It was his turn now, wasn’t it?

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s face was full of anticipation.

Geom Mugik spoke as if complaining.

“Come now, Cult Leader, were you cursing me even during such an important battle?”

“I have to curse for you to show up!”

Their gazes intertwined in the air. The Pungcheon Cult Leader had never been so glad to see Geom Mugik. It seemed his divine artifacts were showing their efficacy—if he prayed like this, Geom Mugik appeared.

“As expected of the Cult Leader. In moments of crisis, you are always with our cult.”

“I came out against my will. If I want to keep mooching meals, I have to show up.”

In front of Geom Mugik, such jokes came naturally to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

Hwamugi watched with the thought, Surely he’s not going to greet everyone one by one? But Geom Mugik did just that, as if to show him.

The souls in Hwamugi’s heart began to clamor. Yes, how could anyone remain still after seeing this?

Now it was noisy outside, and noisy inside as well.

Next was the Poison King. The Poison King had been standing quietly behind the others.

“Why are you just standing back and watching? You should have melted them all with poison.”

What power do I have? With that look in his eyes, the Poison King glanced sidelong at Geom Woojin. The only time he could deliver such words, half joke and half sincerity, to the Cult Leader was when he was with Geom Mugeuk.

“Father, from now on, just send the Poison King. The rest of you can stay back at the cult and have a drink together.”

It was the highest praise he could give the Poison King. In truth, though he did not show it, the person happiest that Geom Mugeuk had come was the Poison King.

The moment the first casualty appeared in this battle, he had intended to slip quietly out of this place. To obey the Cult Leader’s absolute order to protect Geom Mugeuk.

He had been trapped in anxiety, thinking he might have to leave the battlefield without fighting, and even abandon the Cult Leader there. So Geom Mugeuk’s appearance was a joy like discovering rare poisonous insects one after another. About three of them.

Lastly, Geom Mugeuk looked back at the Sword King.

“Are you all right?”

No matter who looked, the Sword King was the one most badly injured.

“Brother Ak, why did you come out? You should have hidden away somewhere.”

“As you know, my looks stand out far too much. I couldn’t hide in time.”

Of course, joy at their reunion was one thing, but Geom Mugeuk did not miss this perfect chance to tease him.

“By the way, Squad Leader Cha is safe too.”

At the news that Cha Iran was safe, the Sword King grew flustered. He was teasing him with Cha Iran in this situation? And in front of the Cult Leader and the alliance leaders at that?

“……Why are you telling me that?”

Only after that noisy reunion did Geom Mugeuk finally turn toward Hwa Mugi.

Now he’s looking at me?

Hwa Mugi felt as if he had become some nameless nobody. Of course, that was precisely what the Young Cult Leader had intended.

Hwa Mugi did not know. In truth, Geom Mugeuk had wished he would become an existence even more insignificant than that.

Geom Mugeuk had dreamed of things turning out like this with Hwa Mugi.

Of saying, while playing Go with his father, I’ll step out for a moment, then going to kill him and returning. Of coming back as if nothing had happened and saying, It’s my turn, isn’t it?

But Hwa Mugi had emerged into the world sooner than he had expected, and so his fate and Geom Mugeuk’s had changed.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Hwa Mugi with a deepened gaze.

You do not know from where I came in order to meet you, in order to stop you. You do not know how far a road I have walked.

But Hwa Mugi, too, looked at Geom Mugeuk with eyes that seemed to have passed through an immeasurably long span of time.

“Strangely, you don’t feel unfamiliar.”

“That’s how it is when you see a handsome face.”

Then Geom Mugeuk smiled and made a request.

“I ran here without even eating, and I’m starving. I’m begging you.”

Now was the moment today’s battle moved on to its next stage.

“Let’s eat first, then fight.”

Chapter 825: They’re All Out of Their Minds
-------------------------------------------
Eat? In the middle of a life-or-death battle?

The moment he heard those words, the first thought that came to Hwa Mugi’s mind was this—the very thought that many who had experienced Geom Mugeuk had entertained at least once.

‘Is he insane?’

Of course, there was no way he was insane.

‘He’s aiming for something.’

He knew the Young Cult Leader was no ordinary man.

In truth, what was even more surprising was everyone else’s reaction. Shouldn’t at least one of the seven have looked dumbfounded, or reacted as if to ask what nonsense he was spouting in a situation like this?

Yet reactions he had never expected began to appear.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader rubbed his stomach.

“Come to think of it, we’re hungry too.”

Just as Baek Jagang had finished cooking, Hwa Mugi had barged in, so no one had been able to eat.

That Pungcheon Cult Leader had been saying all sorts of absurd things from the beginning, so perhaps that much could be excused.

Baek Jagang said to Geom Mugeuk, as if boasting,

“I learned how to cook from the Cult Leader.”

Why in the world was the leader of the Evil Path Alliance saying something like that? So what? Was he going to cook? A man who had been fighting like that only moments ago?

Even more, a look of pride crossed the Demon Cult Leader’s face when he heard it.

It felt as though some device had switched on the moment the Young Cult Leader arrived, and the whole group had become different people.

Those who had not taken their eyes off him for even an instant began looking elsewhere, and all their grave expressions brightened.

The Sword King looked up at the sky for the first time, and the Poison King, who had been staring at him with eyes as deep as an abyss, was now looking at a tree uprooted by the roadside.

You’re not seriously looking at that bug crawling out of the roots, are you?

The emotion they all shared was relief.

Even if the Heavenly Demon had arrived in the place where the Young Cult Leader stood, he would not have given them this sort of relief.

Hwa Mugi was dumbfounded, yet his curiosity was stirred.

‘What is this bastard?’

Even when he had heard from the Dark Palace Lord that the Young Cult Leader was influencing everyone, he had not imagined that influence would be this great.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to Hwa Mugi again. He did not make some pointless threat like, I’ll kill you after we eat.

“Even if you’re going to kill them, feed them first.”

“Is there any reason I should?”

“There is.”

Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“Because this moment will be the last of your life.”

“What?”

He thought he meant, Because you’ll die here today. But this too was the complete opposite.

“If you win and all of us die, then this atmosphere, this feeling, this tension—it will be the last of your life.”

Before he knew it, Geom Mugeuk’s expression had turned serious.

“Once we’re all dead, the only people left before you will be those who tremble in fear and swear loyalty. More precisely, people pretending to be loyal. You’ll be unable to endure your own boredom and temper, so you’ll kill this one, kill that one, and go on killing indiscriminately.”

After gazing into his eyes for a moment, Geom Mugeuk added,

“But no matter how many you kill, your heart will never beat the way it does today.”

“!”

Hwa Mugi’s gaze left Geom Mugeuk and moved to Geom Ujin standing behind him. He had been mindful of that man throughout the entire battle today. Though Geom Ujin had yet to draw his sword, it already felt as if he had fought an intense battle with him.

Beside him, the Fist Demon’s fists still revealed lingering regret.

Next to him stood Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang side by side, and beside them were the Pungcheon Cult Leader and the Sword King. And at the very back stood the Poison King.

Yes, those words were right. At least a moment like today would never come again.

Standing before them, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“So let’s eat.”

Hwa Mugi was strangely persuaded. From the moment that Young Cult Leader arrived, for some reason the tension kept fading.

“If you don’t feel like it, then let me eat at least! Don’t be so petty about food!”

Geom Mugeuk was stimulating his curiosity far too much for him to simply launch an attack.

In the end, Hwa Mugi nodded.

“Very well.”

With this deflated fighting spirit, he could not unfold a proper battle anyway.

Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly.

“Now, let’s go in.”

The moment Geom Mugeuk turned toward Pungryu Tavern, he screamed.

“Aaaah! What is this!”

One wall of Pungryu Tavern had collapsed, and the second-floor wall where the writing had been carved had been blown away entirely.

Geom Mugeuk whirled around toward Hwa Mugi.

“You bastard, you destroyed that? Do you know how precious that wall was? This won’t do.”

Geom Mugeuk looked ready to draw his sword at any moment.

“No matter how hungry I am, you need a beating first.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s abrupt change, Hwa Mugi also brought his hand to the black sword.

I welcome a fight, but you’re misunderstanding something right now.

Then Geom Ujin tossed out,

“I broke it.”

A silence flowed for an instant.

Geom Mugeuk lowered his voice and said seriously,

“Well done. It is best to remove any wall that might hinder a fight in advance. To tell the truth, I’m only saying this now, but since it was everyone’s first time leaving writing, it wasn’t that great. Now that they’ve practiced once, they’ll leave even finer words next time.”

No sooner had he finished speaking than Geom Mugeuk went inside.

Geom Ujin followed him in, and Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang followed after with smiles. Astonishingly, the three of them entered without even looking at Hwa Mugi.

Only the Fist Demon remained wary of Hwa Mugi moving until the end; the rest simply filed into Pungryu Tavern.

They’re all going in and leaving me behind? It was truly something he could not believe even after seeing it with his own eyes.

‘These bastards!’

Only moments ago, he had been fighting over whether he was a hero or a villain.

Yet now… he felt as if he were nothing at all.

He had been the main character, only to suddenly become a supporting role—no, not even a supporting role.

He was an extra left standing alone in the street.

Yes, there were stories where everyone died to an extra.

After quietly staring at the tavern, Hwa Mugi entered last.

Geom Mugeuk, who had entered first, looked around the interior.

“It’s more broken than I thought.”

The remaining walls were cracked in places, and broken tables and chairs lay scattered everywhere.

Looking up at the place where the second-floor wall had been, Geom Mugeuk said,

“I once spoke with the owner here about something. We said that if that wall were destroyed, then its destruction would have meaning in its own way.”

At that moment, Geom Ujin asked an unexpected question.

“Are you disappointed that I didn’t protect it?”

The broken tables and walls alike were part of the demonic path his son had sought to protect.

Geom Mugeuk looked at his father.

You have no idea how much it means to me that you would ask me a question like this, Father.

“You already protected it, didn’t you?”

When Geom Ujin looked puzzled, Geom Mugeuk looked far out the window toward the demonic fire rising from the main headquarters.

“What I was trying to protect wasn’t that wall or those tables, but the owner of this place and the people of Maga Village. You already protected them, even going so far as to raise the demonic fire.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze returned to his father.

“The people of Maga Village will never forget that grace for the rest of their lives. It was a truly magnificent choice, Father.”

Geom Ujin only looked at his son in silence.

The one who reacted instead was the Fist Demon, who had been listening. A faint smile formed at the corners of his mouth.

The Cult Leader was human too, so while sheltering the people of Maga Village within the cult, how could he not have thought of Geom Mugeuk? He must surely have hoped for the moment he would hear such words from his son.

“Simply by choosing this place as the battlefield where the fate of the murim is at stake, Father, you have given the owner a source of pride for a lifetime. It will carry a meaning far greater than the words left on the wall ever could.”

The Fist Demon knew just how pleased the Cult Leader must be right now. If he himself felt this gratified, how much more so would the Cult Leader?

“Still, luckily, there are some things here that are intact.”

Geom Mugeuk brought an unbroken table and chairs from the corner to the center.

“Please sit, Father.”

Hwa Mugi stood at the entrance, watching Geom Mugeuk’s words and actions. His eyes seemed to say he would see just how far he went.

This time, Baek Jagang stepped forward first.

“I learned a few dishes from your father. What do you think? Would you like to taste them?”

“Please make them! In the history of this murim, how many people have ever eaten food made by the leader of the Evil Path Alliance?”

Baek Jagang went straight into the kitchen and looked around here and there.

“You’re blessed when it comes to food. Fortunately, the kitchen isn’t badly damaged.”

“May I look forward to it?”

“Look forward to it!”

Even if it tasted bad, he had an escape route.

“After all, the skill was passed down by your father.”

The hand that had been unleashing the Tyrant’s Heaven-Shaking Sword Art only moments ago now held a kitchen knife again.

For some reason, he looked even more excited and thrilled than when he had been fighting.

“Is there anyone who can help me cook?”

At Baek Jagang’s request, Geom Ujin remained seated. He could not enter the kitchen while he was in the same space as Hwa Mugi.

The Poison King, who had shed streams of tears while peeling onions, turned his head far away toward the window. He clearly expressed that he had helped because it was the Cult Leader, but anyone else could forget it.

“I would like to help, but I have far too little talent.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader would obviously only cause trouble rather than help.

The Sword King, who might have been useful, was suffering from internal injuries.

Jin Paecheon had also been wounded externally, and even if he had not been, it was an awkward situation for him to step forward.

Then one person rose from his seat.

“I will help.”

The one who came forward was the Fist Demon.

He had already shown himself slicing carrots in the kitchen earlier. Though his appearance did not suit it, his cooking skills were far superior to Baek Jagang’s.

The Fist Demon entered the kitchen. The two men who had been about to fight Hwa Mugi continued a new battle in the kitchen.

The Fist Demon wanted to help him partly because there was no one suitable to do so, but more than that, it was because he simply wanted to help Baek Jagang.

He had learned something from him this time.

A choice made for practical benefit.

It had especially affected him, who had placed great importance on honor and pride.

Hwa Mugi watched the sight.

‘They’re all out of their minds.’

Baek Jagang and the Fist Demon, who had been trying to fight him only moments ago, disappeared into the kitchen. If they were sane, they should not have gone in to cook; and even if they did, at the very least—

We’ll postpone our fight with you until after the meal.

Shouldn’t they have said at least something like that?

Do you truly believe that Young Cult Leader can handle me? Or has the Poison King released some poison that makes all of you go mad?

Then his eyes met Geom Mugeuk’s.

“Come sit here.”

When Hwa Mugi still did not move, Geom Mugeuk added,

“We don’t kill people who are eating. So come quickly.”

Hwa Mugi thought Geom Mugeuk’s eyes were dangerous. Looking into those eyes, it seemed as if he was truly speaking the truth.

Hwa Mugi slowly moved his feet and sat down.

Geom Mugeuk went to the kitchen and brought out liquor and cups.

“I’ll pour you a drink for the first time in a while.”

Geom Mugeuk poured liquor for his father first.

“I should have brought the Drunken Demon’s delicious liquor, but it’s all gone.”

With those words, everyone understood. A battle had taken place where the Drunken Demon had needed to drink all his liquor.

Perhaps the fight on that side had been the truly fierce and terrible one.

Starting with Jin Paecheon, Geom Mugeuk poured liquor for the others one by one.

It was a drink that carried a more exceptional feeling than ever before.

Only moments ago, they had been frustrated as they faced the monstrous Hwa Mugi, and now they were receiving liquor poured by Geom Mugeuk.

Finally, Geom Mugeuk filled the cup placed before Hwa Mugi to the brim, then held the bottle out to him.

“Pour me one too.”

Geom Mugeuk wanted to receive liquor from him.

Glaring at Geom Mugeuk over the bottle, Hwa Mugi asked,

“What are you doing?”

“I’m having my first and last drink with the archenemy of my life.”

Hwa Mugi stared intently at Geom Mugeuk. But no matter how he looked, he could see only sincerity from Geom Mugeuk, not some ploy.

“In any case, before today is over, one of you and me will die. Even if you come to rule the murim later, live with at least this much composure.”

In the end, Hwa Mugi took the bottle and filled Geom Mugeuk’s cup. He prepared himself in case Geom Mugeuk tried a surprise attack after making him pour, but no such thing happened.

Geom Mugeuk raised his cup high.

“Now then, let us have a drink with the Heavenly Slaughter Star.”

Except for Hwa Mugi, who had not picked up his cup in the first place, everyone drank.

Until Geom Mugeuk arrived, Jin Paecheon had vaguely hoped that things would improve once he came.

But when he arrived, the entire board itself changed completely. Even when the opponent was the Heavenly Slaughter Star, there was no exception.

A short while later, Baek Jagang brought out a dish in a large bowl. The Fist Demon served portions into the small bowls he had brought.

Baek Jagang looked slightly tense with anticipation.

“Now, everyone, try it.”

When Geom Mugeuk smelled it, he exclaimed in admiration.

“It already smells delicious.”

Geom Ujin said to Baek Jagang,

“Let us eat together.”

Baek Jagang and the Fist Demon also joined them at the table.

Hwa Mugi looked down at the dish before him.

When Geom Mugeuk first said they should eat, he had never imagined such a situation would truly unfold.

And it was not only Hwa Mugi. Who could have imagined they would sit at the same table as the Heavenly Slaughter Star?

“It’s truly delicious!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s exclamation, Baek Jagang asked suspiciously,

“Are you only saying it’s delicious because you’re thinking of your father?”

“I have about nine tongues, and the tongue I’m using right now is the one for tasting food, not the one for flattery. It really is delicious.”

Only after the others each said that it was delicious did Baek Jagang’s expression brighten radiantly. He definitely had a talent for cooking.

“When I visit next time, please make it again!”

“Come anytime.”

A sneer formed at the corner of Hwa Mugi’s mouth as he watched. There is no next time for you.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Hwa Mugi.

“Why aren’t you eating?”

Hwa Mugi had not even picked up his chopsticks. Everyone else had lost their minds, but he could not allow himself to lose his as well.

As he quietly stared at Hwa Mugi, Geom Mugeuk said something astonishing. Hwa Mugi had been surprised by Geom Mugeuk several times today, but among them, these words surprised him the most.

“Actually, this is the second time I’ve eaten with you.”

Chapter 826: Because I Want to Talk About You
---------------------------------------------
‘This is the second time you’ve eaten with me?’

Hwa Mugi, of course, and everyone else at the table were startled.

Everyone’s gazes turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

“What nonsense are you spouting?”

At Hwa Mugi’s baffled reaction, Geom Mugeuk’s attitude toward him became respectful.

“It has been a long time.”

When Geom Mugeuk greeted him politely, Hwa Mugi could tell that the greeting was not meant for him.

“Alliance Leader, you lied to me.”

Everyone understood. Geom Mugeuk was speaking to one of the two Alliance Leaders dwelling inside Hwa Mugi’s body. Just who was he trying to converse with?

“Do you remember the question I asked that day?”

Of course, the person Geom Mugeuk was addressing was the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword.

When he had gone back three hundred years, Geom Mugeuk had asked the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword this:

“Is there a way for us to win?”

Still, he did not remember Geom Mugeuk.

“At the time, you answered like this: There is no special method. We have no choice but to fight and win.”

In that instant, Hwa Mugi’s gaze wavered. As if driving a wedge into that faint, doubtful memory, Geom Mugeuk continued.

“You promised to give a congratulatory speech at the squad leader’s wedding. I suppose you weren’t able to keep that promise either.”

Hwa Mugi sprang up from his seat. Before anyone knew it, his gaze had changed. The one who had risen was the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword.

“How could you?”

At last, he remembered that day.

Back then, just before the final battle, when he had finished his speech and stepped down, there had been a young martial artist who asked him a question.

In that solemn atmosphere, he had asked boldly:

—Is there a way for us to win?

Now that he saw his face, he remembered clearly.

Just as he said, he had, in a sense, lied to him. He had said there was no special method, yet he had brought in the Demon Cult and brought in the Evil Dao Alliance.

That martial artist from back then was the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult three hundred years later?

Faced with this unbelievable truth, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword was flustered and shocked.

Geom Mugeuk looked at him and said calmly,

“Please sit. Let us eat together today, just as we did then.”

Still wearing a shocked expression, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword slowly sat back down. Yes, they had eaten together as well.

Hwa Mugi could have forced the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s soul back inside, but perhaps he too was curious about this situation, for he simply left him be.

“How is it that you, whom I saw three hundred years ago, are here?”

Geom Ujin turned his head and looked at his son. It was the first time he had ever seen him look so surprised. He had not even been this shocked when the Heavenly Killing Star appeared.

The others were the same. Geom Mugeuk had a talent for surprising people, but this went beyond surprise and into shock.

Cult Leader Pungcheon barely held back the joke he wanted to make, that he had known this would happen, and that he had been beaten because Geom Mugeuk was three hundred years old.

Geom Mugeuk answered honestly.

“It may be hard to believe, but the Bigwe, the divine relic of Cheonui Palace, sent me three hundred years into the past.”

The only person who knew this fact was the Sword King. When Geom Mugeuk had returned through the Bigwe, he had told him honestly, since the Sword King had been the one guarding it.

At those words, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword let out an exclamation. “Ah!” Since it was the mysterious divine relic that had absorbed them like this, he could believe Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“I was there that day as well.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at his father.

Without needing any further explanation, Geom Ujin nodded once, his expression saying he understood what had happened. He had realized that Geom Mugeuk had obtained yet another astonishing fortuitous encounter.

The reason Hwa Mugi had not felt unfamiliar to Geom Mugeuk was likely due to the influence of the memories buried in the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s subconscious.

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword, who had been silently gazing at Geom Mugeuk, let out a long sigh that was almost a lament.

“I regret saying those words to you.”

He meant it. It would be a lie to say he had regretted it throughout all the years that had passed, but from time to time, he had thought of that young man.

He had been so memorable that he was difficult to forget, and above all, the fact that he had lied to him had weighed on his heart.

“I am sorry.”

“There is no need for you to apologize to me. I am a man from three hundred years in the future, and I am from the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

That fact was also what the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword found hardest to understand.

“Why did you come to me? Was it your choice?”

“No. The Bigwe sent me to you, Alliance Leader.”

If he was a descendant of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, should he not have gone to the Lord of Cheonui Palace? Was it because he had been the one to bring in the Demon Cult Leader?

“I do not know the reason either.”

It must have been related to his regression, so it was not something he could speak of here.

“The reason you called me out must be because you have a request to make of me.”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s gaze turned toward Jin Paecheon, who was sitting silently.

More than the Young Cult Leader, it was the Murim Alliance Leader of a later generation who had said he respected him most that weighed on his heart. Even now, he was silently listening to the conversation between the Young Cult Leader and himself.

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword felt that he ought to do something, if only for his sake.

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s gaze returned to Geom Mugeuk.

“What do you want from me?”

It was the moment Hwa Mugi could have cut into the conversation. Geom Mugeuk might have asked him to interfere with the fight, or asked him to reveal the secret of the Heavenly Killing Star.

But Hwa Mugi did not. As if he were confident no matter what they did.

Yet what Geom Mugeuk desired completely overturned their expectations.

“I did not call you out as the Heavenly Killing Star, but so that you could eat as the Murim Alliance Leader.”

“!”

“In the end, it may be filling the Heavenly Killing Star’s stomach, but please eat as the Alliance Leader.”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s eyes trembled.

“Is that all?”

“Yes, that is all.”

Geom Ujin, of course, and all those listening knew that those words were Geom Mugeuk’s sincerity. Their own hearts had been moved by that unbelievable sincerity as well.

But there was no way the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword could know that.

“Is it because you look down on me that you do not seek my help?”

“It is the opposite.”

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes and expression were more calm and serious than ever.

“At that time, I felt that you truly cared for your subordinates. I also felt your regret and anguish over not being able to end the war sooner.

Until then, I never imagined I would meet you again like this, but now that I have, I wanted to treat you to a meal.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Baek Jagang and added,

“This dish made by the Leader of the Evil Dao Alliance is truly delicious. Please try it.”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword silently looked down at the food placed before him.

Geom Mugeuk did not urge him any further and began eating the food in front of him as well.

Before long, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword brought out what he had kept buried in his heart for a very long time. His words were directed at Geom Mugeuk, and at the same time, at Jin Paecheon.

“It was not because I was unlucky that I was absorbed into the Bigwe.”

It was a hidden truth that had pained him for three hundred years.

“I was absorbed because I was the same kind as the Lord of Cheonui Palace, the Demon Cult Leader, and the Leader of the Evil Dao Alliance.”

Just thinking about it wounded his pride and enraged him, but in the end, he believed that was the truth.

“The moment I chose to bring them in, I became the same kind as them. I, too, am a wicked man.”

That was why he could not pick up his chopsticks.

“I am not qualified to eat the meal you offer.”

Geom Mugeuk swallowed the food he had been eating, drank some water, and then asked calmly,

“Why are you trying so hard to be perfect?”

At the unexpected question, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword flinched.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Paecheon.

“By the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s standards, Alliance Leader Jin over there must have made a great mistake as well. After all, he came all the way here alone to help the Demon Cult. By your standards, Divine Sword, it was probably an even greater mistake, was it not?”

That was why he had said it to Jin Paecheon at first. That if he associated with them, he would be destroyed.

This time, Geom Mugeuk asked Jin Paecheon,

“Do you think coming here was a mistake?”

Jin Paecheon could understand with what intention Geom Mugeuk was asking him this question. Yes, the matter of the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword was his matter as well.

“It was a mistake. A very great mistake.”

Unlike his words, Jin Paecheon’s expression was at ease. Looking at the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword, Jin Paecheon saw his former self. He felt that he too had once been like that. Becoming a perfect Alliance Leader had once been the entirety of his life as well.

But now, he was a little different from back then. Because he could think this, and even say it aloud.

“I am human too, so what of it if I make a mistake or two? I lived my whole life without making mistakes, so it is only natural to make one at least once.”

As though he had expected such an answer, Geom Mugeuk smiled and replied,

“Your subordinates will like you even more for it. They will think, So our Alliance Leader, who is perfect in everything, has a side like that too. He makes mistakes just like us.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze returned to the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword. Caring for him was also a reflection of his respect for Jin Paecheon.

“You say you are the same kind as them? No, I think you are completely different from them.”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s gaze shook.

“You lived your entire life for righteousness and chivalry, and sacrificed yourself to protect the peace of the murim. On the other hand, the Demon Cult Leader of that time was infamous for his cruelty. Just who are you comparing yourself to?”

Geom Mugeuk knew that Demon Cult Leader Hyeok Docheon was listening to this conversation right now, but he did not hesitate.

“If the Bigwe had not absorbed the Demon Cult Leader, I believe the history of our cult would have changed greatly as well. The history of our cult would certainly have flowed in a bad direction,

and as fate changed and changed again, Father might not have become the Cult Leader. I might not be here before your eyes.”

It was, in other words, saying that the event three hundred years ago and the events of the present age were bound together by one great karmic tie.

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword had never once thought of it in that way. He had never dreamed that he would meet the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult like this and have such a conversation.

“Even so, the sin of my having brought in the Demon Cult and the Evil Dao Alliance does not disappear. I said it was for the murim, but in the end, it was a choice for myself.”

“If you had not made that choice for yourself, the war might have gone on far longer. You might have lost the war. Far more people would have died. Your desire saved the murim.”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s eyes trembled.

Those words—he had said them to himself hundreds, thousands of times.

But he had wanted to hear them from someone else’s mouth.

That his choice had, in the end, been good. He had wanted to hear through someone else the thought he had repeated countless times in his own heart.

One time from another person was worth more than a thousand times within his own heart.

And finally, after three hundred years, he was hearing it from the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“If you truly were the same kind as them, you would not be having this conversation with me now. They would have long since forgotten everything. It is because you have carried that burden in your heart for three hundred years that we are able to have this conversation. You have truly suffered all this time.”

Geom Mugeuk picked up the chopsticks lying there and held them out to him.

“Now, please forgive yourself.”

An indescribable surge of emotion flowed through the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s chest. It felt as though something that had been lodged tightly somewhere in his heart for the past three hundred years was being drawn out.

With trembling hands, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword accepted the chopsticks.

Jin Paecheon looked at him and nodded. In his eyes, there was still respect directed toward him.

Just as his chopsticks were about to pick up the food—

Crash.

He suddenly grabbed the bowl in front of him and hurled it away. The bowl fell to the floor and shattered into pieces.

His gaze had changed. Before anyone knew it, he had returned to being Hwa Mugi.

Geom Mugeuk did not get angry at him. Though the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword had not been able to eat, he believed his feelings had been conveyed well enough.

“Since I have already met the Alliance Leader, tell the others to come out as well.”

Hwa Mugi did not hide his sneer.

“Why? So you can lure the others with some nonsense, the way you forced your way into the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s good graces? Do you think that will make them change their minds and harm me?”

Geom Mugeuk, on the other hand, remained composed.

“That is not why.”

“Then why?”

It was a reason Hwa Mugi could not have imagined.

“Because they must all want to talk.”

“What?”

“I heard you were sealed away for a long time. Inside you, you always conversed only with them, didn’t you? Then wouldn’t they also have something they want to say, not to the people they’re sick and tired of, but to others, to their descendants?”

Hwa Mugi did not readily grant the request.

“You mean to beguile their hearts and get what you want.”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk asked Hwa Mugi,

“Do you know why you obediently came in to eat instead of fighting outside?”

That was because Geom Mugeuk had stirred his curiosity.

“Because I can kill you all at any time.”

But Geom Mugeuk gave a different reason.

“It is because you too wanted to tell your story.”

“!”

“Not to those tiresome people inside your body, and not to the Dark Palace Lord who tried to use you, but to me, to my father, and to the people here. Whether that story is a boast, resentment, or whatever else it may be.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze was clear and deep.

“It cannot be that, just because you are the Heavenly Killing Star, all you think about is killing everyone.”

After a moment, Hwa Mugi asked in return,

“What if that is all there is in my heart?”

Geom Mugeuk walked to the kitchen, brought back a small bowl, served some food into it, and set it down in front of him.

“Eat, then search your heart again. When a person is hungry, they can’t see anything else. Eat first, then let’s talk. I haven’t finished eating either.”

Geom Mugeuk began eating again.

At that sight, Hwa Mugi slammed the table down without mercy.

Booooom!

It should have been completely smashed apart.

But the table was unharmed.

Geom Mugeuk’s palm was already resting on the table. He had released his internal energy and protected it.

“Stop throwing a tantrum. Honored guests are still eating.”

Hwa Mugi raised his internal energy even further and struck down again.

Booooooom!

But the table did not break. It was an astonishing amount of inner force, but Geom Mugeuk’s internal energy was no less formidable.

“Heaven has even stuck a grand name tag on you and told you to live by killing people. It must be your fate to keep killing until you are sick of it for the rest of your life. So why are you in such a hurry?

This is a fight that will end today anyway. So let them say everything they want to say. If you spent all those long years together, you should at least show that much courtesy.”

At least when it came to those final words, Hwa Mugi could not refute him.

“Not only their stories…”

Geom Mugeuk looked into Hwa Mugi’s eyes and added,

“I want to hear yours as well.”

Chapter 827: Let's Speak Korean!
--------------------------------
“You really are a madman.”

The words slipped naturally from Hwa Mugi’s mouth.

Geom Mugeuk grinned broadly, like a madman.

“That’s right. Even words like that should be said first. You, me, and those people inside your body, too. Say everything you want to say. Today, no one becomes a wronged ghost who died with words left unspoken.”

Geom Mugeuk looked around at the people seated with him.

“Father, if you have anything to say, please do.”

At that, Geom Ujin made a rare joke.

“Our family has more than enough with just you.”

Geom Mugeuk answered cheerfully.

“I’ll speak as the representative of the Geom family!”

Jin Paecheon’s gaze as he looked at Geom Mugeuk was endlessly gentle.

After seeing Geom Mugeuk converse with the Muhwang Divine Sword, his trust in him deepened even further.

He had spoken with the Muhwang Divine Sword first. He had tried his utmost, but he had not been able to move the man’s heart. Yet Geom Mugeuk had comforted him properly. He was a hundred times better than this old man.

Baek Jagang was admiring him from another angle. Not once, while Geom Mugeuk spoke to him, had the hairs on his ears stood on end. It was simply astonishing that all of those words were sincere.

“Alliance Leaders, please speak freely as well!”

Geom Mugeuk was about to turn his head again when the displeased face of the Pungcheon Cult Leader came into view.

“Why do you not tell me to speak freely?”

“You’d do it even if I told you not to.”

“Well, that is true.”

“First, take some credit.”

“Credit?”

Geom Mugeuk spoke as if he had seen with his own eyes what had happened before he arrived.

“When I wasn’t here, you must have been in charge of our side’s mouth, weren’t you? How much trouble must you have gone through, speaking on behalf of those blunt, taciturn people?”

“You truly miss nothing.”

“Even if I missed everything else, how could I possibly miss your performance, Cult Leader?”

After filling the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s face with deep emotion, Geom Mugeuk looked back at Hwa Mugi.

“I told you, if you have something to say, say it to your heart’s content.”

Of course, Hwa Mugi did not say a word. The ridicule in his eyes said, As if that could make me open my mouth.

“Then again, they say even a man in the middle of his antics can’t continue once you spread out a mat for him. If I say, ‘Let’s talk,’ even words that were about to come out would slip right back in.”

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was good at it whether there was a mat or not.

“You called me a madman, didn’t you? Then you’re an even greater madman.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Hwa Mugi was dumbfounded. To be called a madman by a madman! But when he heard what followed, he did not feel displeased.

“You charged in here alone, didn’t you? You may be the only person in the history of murim who tried to fight the entire martial world by himself. There was no one before you, and there will be no one after you. You set an unprecedented, unrepeatable record.”

Of course, Hwa Mugi was not someone who would let that slide so easily.

“Do not fawn over me. It will not make me tell you anything.”

“I’m not fawning. I’m saying that’s the truth.”

Hwa Mugi had intended not to engage in even a single word of conversation, but Geom Mugeuk’s words kept provoking him.

“There’s one thing I’m curious about.”

An entirely unexpected question flowed from Geom Mugeuk.

“Why did you charge in?”

Hwa Mugi and the others naturally interpreted the question this way: Why the Divine Cult first, instead of the Murim Alliance or the Sado Alliance? But that was not it.

“You could have lived a different life, couldn’t you? With those martial arts, you could have lived quite a splendid life.”

Hwa Mugi could not answer. Astonishingly, he had never once thought about living a different life.

Because he was the Heavenly Slaughter Star. Because he carried those souls. Because he had undone the seal.

He had thought it only natural that he had to charge in. He had thought it only natural that he had to kill them all and seize this murim.

Geom Mugeuk’s question continued.

“You could have simply said this, couldn’t you? ‘A fate bestowed by Heaven? Don’t make me laugh. I will never live according to your will!’”

“If Heaven granted me such great power, do you think it did so for me to live some ordinary life? If Heaven sent down someone like me, then that is all because of you.”

The words he had been repeating ever since arriving here came out again. But they did not work on Geom Mugeuk.

“Is that what you think? Or is that what the Dark Palace Lord or the souls inside you told you?”

Hwa Mugi could not answer that it was his own thought.

Was it because he had struck the mark? Hwa Mugi’s gaze sank coldly.

“You run your mouth as you please because it is someone else’s affair.”

“I apologize for that.”

“This must be your purpose. To confuse me with that glib tongue of yours.”

Geom Mugeuk drank the liquor set before him.

“I can’t help being selfish either. Do you think I’m asking for a conversation for your sake? It’s for me and for everyone here. I want to understand you better so I can kill you more certainly. Forgive me if my words turn sordid.”

Hwa Mugi felt as if he understood now. Why everyone had changed so abruptly when Geom Mugeuk arrived.

In this brief span of time, he had lifted a person’s mood, angered him, made him unable to understand what was happening, and caused him to feel several rises and falls of emotion.

He had said he would not converse, yet before he knew it, he was speaking with him, nerves drawn taut.

His curiosity toward Geom Mugeuk ended here. Just as he was about to rise from his seat and draw his sword—

“Ah, there is someone inside you I should thank.”

Naturally, he thought that person would be the Demon Cult Leader. But it was someone entirely unexpected.

“Please call out the Sado Alliance Leader.”

The person who found those words the most unexpected was Baek Jagang. As for the Muhwang Divine Sword, Geom Mugeuk had met him after going back three hundred years, but what connection could he have with Ju Gasin?

“For what reason?”

“You’ll know if you watch.”

If he had asked for the Demon Cult Leader, Hwa Mugi would never have done as he wished. Yet he, too, was curious as to why Geom Mugeuk had to thank the Sado Alliance Leader.

“We agreed there would be no ghosts dying with words left unspoken!”

Hwa Mugi granted Geom Mugeuk’s request.

His gaze settled calmly, and Ju Gasin appeared. He, too, did not know why Geom Mugeuk had sought him out.

“Why did you call for me?”

It was the question everyone was curious about.

“If we count today, this is the third time we have met.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s answer, Ju Gasin looked as if he could not understand.

“The third time? If we met three hundred years ago and now, that should be the second.”

“I met you at your tomb, Alliance Leader.”

In that instant, Ju Gasin’s eyes sharpened.

“You gave me a gift there.”

Geom Mugeuk had obtained the final orb from Ju Gasin’s corpse.

But Geom Mugeuk did not tell him what it was.

There was a reason Geom Mugeuk did not speak of the power of the Six Paths Origin Qi.

Hwa Mugi would not know about the Six Paths Origin Qi.

The Dark Palace Lord would never have told him about the one and only power capable of controlling him.

Before seeing what had happened that day, he had thought that the cult leaders and alliance leaders of the Orthodox, Unorthodox, and Demonic factions who had gathered then had divided the Six Paths Origin Qi among themselves. Some of it, due to various circumstances, had flowed into places like the Ice Palace and the Pungcheon Cult.

But that day, all of them had been absorbed into the secret coffer and trapped within transparent orbs, and at the time, they had fallen into the hands of the Dark Palace Lord.

Had the six orbs emerged from the secret coffer after that? Or had they been elsewhere from the beginning? Because he had returned to reality before seeing that sight, even Geom Mugeuk did not know.

At least one thing was certain. The Six Paths Origin Qi had been arranged in various places by someone other than those who had been present that day.

“So please, Sado Alliance Leader, eat the dish that the Muhwang Divine Sword could not.”

“Do you know what kind of person I am, telling me to eat this dish?”

He did not know much about him. But he could guess one thing.

“Are you not someone who still has not abandoned his dream?”

Ju Gasin’s eyes sharpened.

“Why do you think so?”

“Because the reason you involved yourself in what happened three hundred years ago must have been that you dreamed of unifying murim.”

Baek Jagang, who was listening, was inwardly impressed. He had heard Ju Gasin’s proposal, so he knew that Ju Gasin had not abandoned his dream. Yet Geom Mugeuk had read his heart accurately without hearing such words.

“Such a thing will not happen. So if there are words you are keeping in your heart, say them all. If not now, you will have no chance.”

It sounded as if he was saying he would kill Hwa Mugi, so Ju Gasin should leave no regrets.

After staring at Geom Mugeuk for a moment, Ju Gasin flatly refused.

“I have nothing to say to you.”

Just as Ju Gasin was about to disappear, Geom Mugeuk abruptly mentioned an unexpected being.

“At that tomb, I saw Jeok Rang, whom you used to bring with you, Alliance Leader.”

“!”

“Ah, of course, I did not see him in person. I saw him in a painting. He was truly magnificent and handsome.”

When Jeok Rang was mentioned, for the first time, an emotion passed through Ju Gasin’s eyes. It was longing.

“Even just seeing the painting of you together, I could tell you cherished him greatly.”

Ju Gasin nodded.

“The only one……”

Ju Gasin did not continue. Was he about to say, He was the only one who understood me?

“Have a drink for Jeok Rang. He must have grieved deeply over your death, Alliance Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk lifted the cup placed before him.

He seemed like a person who would never raise a cup for any other reason.

Ju Gasin lifted his cup. And he raised that cup toward Baek Jagang as well.

It meant they should drink together.

He still hoped Baek Jagang would accept his proposal. He would somehow let him live, so he should live a life beneath one man and above ten thousand.

Baek Jagang lifted his cup and quietly toasted him in the air.

Just as Ju Gasin was about to bring the cup to his lips—

Crash.

Once again, the cup fell to the floor and shattered.

Before anyone knew it, his eyes had changed. And the one who returned this time was not Hwa Mugi.

“Pathetic humans.”

It was the Demon Cult Leader, Hyeok Docheon.

“Orthodox or unorthodox, you are all the same. What is so significant about having drawn in a few outside forces that you would feel guilt over it for three hundred years? And in the current situation, you’re missing some mere dog?”

Geom Mugeuk spoke to him.

“Let me correct two things first. They were not some mere outside forces, but the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Sado Alliance. And he was not some mere dog, but a wolf. A very handsome wolf named Jeok Rang.”

Hyeok Docheon glared at Geom Mugeuk with blazing eyes.

“If I had not been absorbed into the secret coffer, are you saying our cult would have grown worse?”

Hyeok Docheon’s gaze turned toward Geom Ujin. He had not liked Geom Ujin at their first meeting either.

“An insolent whelp, just like his father.”

Of course, to Geom Mugeuk, that was the highest praise.

“Being told I resemble my father is what I love hearing most. Thank you for your kind words.”

Thinking he was being mocked, Hyeok Docheon twitched. Demonic energy and killing intent billowed forth together.

“You dare insult a former cult leader?”

Geom Mugeuk spoke to him calmly.

“The Heavenly Demon I know would not break a cup meant for a drink with his descendants after three hundred years. So I do not know who you are, but please go back in, and tell the real Demon Cult Leader to come out.”

Hyeok Docheon stared silently at Geom Mugeuk.

“You truly are no ordinary fellow!”

There was a reason he had pushed Ju Gasin aside and come out.

“Looking at you, a thought suddenly occurred to me.”

To him, it was far more important than guilt or a wolf.

“I will ask directly. Can you get me out of this body?”

At that question, everyone there was shocked. No one had expected him to suddenly appear and ask such a thing.

“Why are you asking me that?”

“If the secret coffer took you to that place three hundred years ago, then it must regard you as special.”

That was not the only reason. The Dark Palace Lord had said it: everything had fallen apart because of the Young Cult Leader. Just as he had said, even the sight of him seating the Heavenly Slaughter Star at a table and handling him proved he was no ordinary fellow.

Hyeok Docheon asked with blazing eyes.

“Do you know a way?”

Everyone’s gaze focused on Geom Mugeuk. They wondered if, no matter how much he was Geom Mugeuk, an answer saying he knew could possibly come out here.

An astonishing answer flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“I should be able to get you out of that body.”

Of course, it would not be in the way he wanted.

Hyeok Docheon’s eyes flew wide open. Because Geom Mugeuk had answered not “I can,” but “I should be able to,” it instead made him trust it more.

All the others listening were shocked as well. Hwa Mugi was probably shocked, and the other souls must have been shocked too. Baek Jagang, whose ears had not prickled, was the most shocked of all.

Soon, Hyeok Docheon laughed heartily. His laughter rang out thunderously.

Then, suddenly cutting off his laughter, he glared at Geom Mugeuk again.

“Is that true?”

In that one sentence, astonishment, doubt, expectation, and excitement were all mixed together.

“If it is a lie, I will tear you apart alive!”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the shattered dish on the floor and said,

“But you should have asked such an important question before breaking the wine cup.”

Hyeok Docheon was half in doubt, but in the hope that it might be true, he made an offer to Geom Mugeuk.

“If you just let me out, I’ll grant you anything you want!”

Certain that this was, of course, what he wanted, he added in an excited voice.

“I’ll let you bring about the unification of the murim.”

At that, this time Geom Mugeuk burst into loud laughter. Hyeok Docheon thought it was a laugh of joy.

“The unification of the murim I have now is already difficult enough for me. Honestly, it’s harder than dealing with you people!”

Hyeok Docheon could not understand what he meant.

On the other hand, everyone there wore a different expression. Some smiled faintly, while others looked slightly awkward. Only Geom Ujin showed no change in expression whatsoever.

“If I can just get out…”

Hyeok Docheon, who had been speaking with expectation, closed his mouth.

Before anyone knew it, he had returned to being Hwa Mugi. He could listen to anything else, but when it came to this matter, he cut off the conversation.

“As expected! You don’t deviate from my predictions by even an inch. You can remove them from my body? Are you trying to bewitch the Demonic Cult Leader with such nonsense?”

Geom Mugeuk, who had been quietly watching him, suddenly asked.

“Do you dislike it?”

“What?”

“I’m asking if you’re all right with those people being inside your body. Reading your thoughts, reading your emotions. Don’t you hate that?”

Hwa Mugi’s expression hardened. His relationship with these souls was not something that could be explained, or concluded, in a word or two.

Geom Mugeuk asked him.

“What are they to you?”

Hwa Mugi did not answer. He slowly rose from his seat, drew his black sword, and pointed it at Geom Mugeuk.

A powerful killing intent unlike anything he had ever experienced assailed him, but Geom Mugeuk remained composed.

“Fine, fighting is a kind of conversation too. Still, I prefer talking things out. There are still people who haven’t spoken yet.”

But Hwa Mugi’s killing intent grew even denser. He had no intention of being toyed with any longer by Geom Mugeuk’s glib tongue.

Geom Mugeuk accepted the fight.

“Fine, let’s fight. Once you’ve been beaten half to death, then you’ll say it: please, let’s talk this out!”

He spoke as if he would beat him up at any moment, but Geom Mugeuk was still seated.

There was a reason for that. Looking at one person, Geom Mugeuk spoke. It was something no one present had expected.

“Father, please beat that bastard half to death and sit him back down in front of me.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader thought Geom Mugeuk was joking like that because he had yet to see for himself just how strong his opponent was.

“Young Cult Leader, that bastard is no ordinary man. He even recovered all his internal energy with a martial art called the Reverse Heaven Absorbing Demon Art!”

But Geom Mugeuk had not said it as a joke.

“That is why I’m asking Father.”

In Geom Mugeuk’s eyes was absolute trust in his father.

Geom Ujin, who had been silently looking at his son, slowly rose from his seat.

“I’ve eaten, so perhaps I should work off the meal a little.”

Chapter 828: Because It Was in Front of His Son
-----------------------------------------------
“After all that showing off, all you amount to is hiding behind your father?”

Hwa Mugi openly mocked Geom Mugeuk.

But unless he stabbed him with the black sword in his hand, mere words could not wound Geom Mugeuk.

“Then of course I’ll hide behind my father. Who else would I hide behind? It’s the place where I feel most at ease.”

Geom Mugeuk was not the least bit ashamed of hiding behind his father.

“And Father should get a chance to show off his skills on an occasion like this, too.”

Geom Ujin slowly walked out from beside the table.

It was the first time he had stepped forward since Hwa Mugi arrived today.

Hwa Mugi also moved aside, and everyone who had been seated at the table rose and lined up behind Geom Ujin.

Was it because he was in front of his son?

Even with the Heavenly Slaughter Star before him, Geom Ujin showed no sign of tension. He was so calm and composed as to seem lofty.

The one who was tense, rather, was Cult Leader Pungcheon.

Although the Heavenly Slaughter Star had been swayed by Geom Mugeuk’s words, he himself had fought him directly.

Even without that special protective astral energy, the man’s skill was far from ordinary. He might be beaten in words, but he was not an opponent one could easily defeat in a fight.

If the Demon Cult Leader stepped forward on this side, that Heavenly Slaughter Star might use some method different from anything he had shown so far.

A son who asked his father to beat such a Heavenly Slaughter Star half to death and sit him back down, and a father who strode out so fearlessly just because of that.

Leaving those two aside, why was the Fist Demon so calm? Considering his relationship with the Cult Leader, he should have been the most worried, yet he was watching in silence without the slightest change in expression.

No, are you not anxious? Am I the only one who’s anxious!

Cult Leader Pungcheon’s gaze turned this time toward the Poison King.

Ever since Geom Mugeuk appeared, the Poison King had half drifted off into his own world. He, who had not taken his eyes off him for even a moment, was now constantly looking out the window.

It felt as though he were standing on the boundary between his own world and reality. In a way, Cult Leader Pungcheon thought the person who trusted Geom Mugeuk most might be the Poison King.

The Sword King was silently tending to his internal injuries, and he, too, showed no sign of worry at all. His faith in Geom Ujin was one thing, but Cult Leader Pungcheon could sense that his faith in Geom Mugeuk was even greater. After Geom Mugeuk appeared, his expression had become completely at ease.

Meanwhile, Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang were worried about something slightly different from Cult Leader Pungcheon. They had already shown their skills, and now the only one remaining was Geom Ujin.

Just how strong was the Demon Cult Leader?

It would be a problem if Geom Ujin lost to him, and it would be a problem if he overwhelmed him.

Hwa Mugi and Geom Ujin faced each other from about ten paces apart.

Though Hwa Mugi had aimed the black sword at him, Geom Ujin did not draw his own sword.

Hwa Mugi asked, half in disbelief.

“You intend to fight barehanded?”

He could tell why Geom Ujin was doing this. Geom Mugeuk had not asked him to kill the man, but to beat him half to death and sit him back down. Still, no matter how his child had asked it of him.

“You’re going to look down on me like this?”

Hwa Mugi’s eyes sank coldly.

If he had said, Let us fight fairly without swords, he would not have been this angry.

But coming at him as if to say, Fight however you like, I will beat you with my fists, made him feel thoroughly disdained.

Clack.

Hwa Mugi withdrew his sword.

“It will be humiliating to get beaten up in front of your son.”

Geom Mugeuk answered in his stead.

“They would be wounds of glory earned for his son. He’ll show off those scars for the rest of his life, saying they’re wounds he took in his son’s place. Thanks to this filial son, he gets to become an impressive father.”

Hwa Mugi confirmed once again that no words could contend with Geom Mugeuk.

Hwa Mugi resolved to smash that arrogant face to pieces. He would make him beg for his life in front of his son.

‘Let’s see what you say then.’

Geom Mugeuk called out loudly.

“Now then, the first one to draw his sword loses!”

As the two men were about to fight with their fists, Cult Leader Pungcheon said worriedly,

“His protective astral energy is absorbing external impacts as internal energy. A fistfight is absolutely disadvantageous.”

He could not tell exactly how the Heavenly Slaughter Star’s Heaven-Defying Absorbing Demon Art worked.

Whether that demonic art was continuously operating together with his protective astral energy, or whether external attacks were absorbed only when he activated the Heaven-Defying Absorbing Demon Art, he did not know.

Whichever it was, Hwa Mugi activated the Heaven-Defying Absorbing Demon Art in that brief instant when he was attacked.

The conclusion Cult Leader Pungcheon reached was this: to kill him, one had to tear through his protective astral energy and drive a sword into his body.

There was no way Geom Ujin, who had watched the fight with them, did not know that.

‘He is overexerting himself because of the Young Cult Leader.’

Geom Ujin crooked a finger, telling him to come first.

At that lofty sight, Hwa Mugi sneered.

“The weaker side should attack fir—”

Before he could finish speaking.

Boom!

Hwa Mugi was slammed into the ground.

Geom Ujin had rushed in like a flash, seized Hwa Mugi by the collar, and driven him straight down into the floor.

It was so fast that Hwa Mugi could not evade the attack. Hwa Mugi’s face, lying on the floor, was filled with astonishment.

“He’s this fast?”

Bang!

Geom Ujin’s fist struck the spot where Hwa Mugi’s face had been.

Craaaack!

The ground caved in around his fist, and cracks spread from that point in all directions like a spiderweb.

Hwa Mugi, who had been there a moment ago, was getting up some distance away. The instant the fist came flying in, he had moved his body as if rolling in a flash and slipped away.

Hwa Mugi was just as fast as Geom Ujin.

The faces of those watching were filled with astonishment.

Hwa Mugi was faster than when he had fought them earlier. They had wondered if, as the nature of his killing intent changed, he was displaying skill at the next level, and indeed he was.

Yet Geom Ujin had properly slammed that Hwa Mugi down.

Geom Ujin’s form flew toward Hwa Mugi again.

Hwa Mugi threw a punch toward Geom Ujin. If internal energy clashed against internal energy, he was confident he could defeat even the Demon Cult Leader.

Whoooong!

Shaaaang!

Just as the fists from both sides were about to collide.

Sssst.

Geom Ujin’s body seemed to shift sideways.

Whoooosh.

Geom Ujin’s kick struck Hwa Mugi squarely in the abdomen.

Thud!

Hwa Mugi slid back several steps. Hwa Mugi was shocked, and so were those watching.

From fist to kick, Geom Ujin’s instantaneous movement was truly like a flash of lightning with the intermediate process omitted.

Whoooong.

Geom Ujin’s fist flew in as if pursuing him and grazed Hwa Mugi’s face. Hwa Mugi dodged by bending his waist backward, but before anyone knew it, Geom Ujin had soared into the air and was stamping down with his foot from above.

Boom!

The ground split where Geom Ujin struck down with his stomp. Hwa Mugi narrowly avoided it and stood ahead.

Before anyone realized it, he was outside the gaping hole in one wall, standing on the road in front of Pungryu Tavern.

He could not hide the astonishment from his face. Geom Ujin was far faster and stronger than he had thought.

As Geom Ujin slowly walked toward him, everyone followed him out.

“This is the first time I’ve seen Father use his fists and feet.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, the Fist Demon, walking beside him, replied,

“In his younger days, he preferred using his fists.”

“Father did?”

“Many were smashed apart by those fists.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at his father’s back. Whenever he heard stories like this about his father’s younger days, he could not quite picture it. His father from a time when he expressed emotions and threw punches.

Bi Gwe, if you were going to take me back to the past, you should have taken me to Father’s youth.

The Fist Demon was inwardly relieved.

‘He has become much stronger.’

He knew that Geom Ujin had recently immersed himself in martial arts training, and that there had been results, but he had not known he had grown this strong.

If the Fist Demon could recognize it, how could the two Alliance Leaders fail to?

Jin Paecheon, watching the fight, was inwardly astonished by Geom Ujin’s skill. He had been cheering for him, but now that he saw that skill in action, other worries followed.

Jin Paecheon looked at Baek Jagang. From the look in his eyes, he could tell that Baek Jagang was feeling the same thing he was. It was not relief.

Astonishment and concern.

‘Cult Leader Geom’s skill is above ours.’
