He had not even drawn his sword yet, but that much was unmistakable. It meant that if he drew his sword and fought in earnest, they might feel the gap even more starkly.

Was that why? Though it was not a worry to have at this moment, one concern flashed through his mind.

And was it only the Demon Cult Leader? With the Demon Venerables stronger than ever, outstanding experts like Cult Leader Pungcheon and the Sword King were together with him.

And above all.

His gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

If even that Geom Mugeuk were to follow the Demon Cult Leader’s will?

Perhaps the unprecedented situation might arise in which the Murim Alliance would have to join hands with the Unorthodox Alliance. He had never once thought in his life that there would come a day when they would ally with the Unorthodox Alliance.

Geom Ujin and Hwa Mugi stood facing each other across the road. Though he had already allowed two attacks, Hwa Mugi was brimming with composure.

“Perhaps because it is the Demon Cult Leader’s internal energy, it tastes even better.”

It was precisely the situation Cult Leader Pungcheon had been worried about.

Even so, Geom Ujin’s expression showed that he did not care in the slightest.

Flash.

Geom Ujin’s fist flew in an instant, raining blows continuously toward Hwa Mugi. He was too fast—far too fast.

Hwa Mugi dodged the first punch, but he could not avoid the second.

Thud!

Geom Ujin’s fist struck Hwa Mugi’s shoulder.

He had felt it earlier when he kicked him as well, but it was like striking water. It was truly a protective astral energy with a texture he had never experienced before.

It felt as though the impact had not been transmitted at all, and therefore as though no pain would have been felt either.

Once again, fists passed between the two men.

Geom Mugeuk could feel it. To others, each of his father’s movements, imbued with profound martial intent, might appear as nothing more than fists being exchanged, but he could see why he attacked from that angle, and why he dodged in that way.

The martial insights gained in the process of reaching the grand completion of the Twelfth Star of the Guhwa Demonic Art were being displayed.

There was no wasted space between the two men. A truly perfect exchange of attack and defense passed back and forth. It was also a conversation carried out with their bodies. Attacks were questions, and defenses were answers.

And in that process, the one asking and answering more properly was Geom Ujin.

Thud!

Hwa Mugi was sent flying and smashed into the grain displayed in front of a grain merchant.

Shaaaaaak.

Rice poured like a waterfall from the torn sacks.

Though rice poured down over his head, Hwa Mugi laughed. He gestured for him to come, as if telling him to hit him as much as he wanted.

Cult Leader Pungcheon sent a voice transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

—Stop them before the fight drags on. Only the Cult Leader’s internal energy is being consumed.

Cult Leader Pungcheon believed Geom Ujin was overexerting himself because this fight was being fought to grant his son’s request.

—Because of you, the Cult Leader is pushing himself too hard.

Then Geom Mugeuk sent back an unexpected reply.

—You say that because you do not know my father well.

—What?

—My father is absolutely not the sort of man who would fight a losing battle for my sake. When it comes to fighting, he is a cold man.

He meant that there was no way he would be swayed by affection into fighting a disadvantageous battle. Especially not in such an important fight.

—Father watched the earlier fights as well, didn’t he?

—He did.

—And yet he says he will fight with his fists. That means he believes there is a sufficient chance of winning. Not because of me.

Even in this moment, Geom Mugeuk believed in his father.

—Even if everyone in the world worries, there is no need to worry about my father.

At that moment, Cult Leader Pungcheon envied Geom Ujin. He thought how wonderful it would be to have even one child who believed in him like this.

Hwa Mugi spread both arms wide and provoked Geom Ujin.

“You don’t want to admit it because you’re in front of your son, do you? That you were arrogant.”

Geom Ujin’s fist shot in like an arrow and once again struck the protective astral energy flowing like water.

Thud! Thud! Thud!

Hwa Mugi took every one of Geom Ujin’s consecutive punches head-on.

The two of them seemed almost to be engaged in a contest of will.

One side said, Go on, hit me. The other said, Let us see how long you can endure.

“At this rate, his internal energy will be exhausted.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon voiced his worry aloud. It was a concern meant for Geom Ujin to hear. He wondered whether, overcome by anger, Geom Ujin might not realize that his internal energy was currently being drained away.

Hwa Mugi was driven backward as blows struck his entire body. But his expression was bright.

“Your attacks are useless! They don’t hurt at all.”

Thud! Thud! Thuuud!

Amid the continuous attacks, Hwa Mugi shouted,

“Draw your sword! Admit defeat.”

But Geom Ujin’s fists flew in even faster.

Thudthudthudthudthudthudthud!

After driving Hwa Mugi against a wall, he unleashed punches without pause.

The two of them were like waves and a rock. No matter how the waves struck, the rock did not budge; Hwa Mugi endured every fist with his protective astral energy.

He was also like a lump of iron under a blacksmith’s hammer. The more it was struck, the stronger and more complete it seemed to become.

But Geom Ujin did not stop. The fists striking him grew faster and more powerful.

Everyone watched the sight in silence. Cult Leader Pungcheon looked at Geom Mugeuk again.

‘You’re the only one who can stop him!’

At the very next moment!

“Stop!”

A single shout burst out.

Cult Leader Pungcheon thought those words had been shouted by Geom Mugeuk.

But they were not.

A single sword cut across the air.

Hwa Mugi’s black sword had thrust through the air, and Geom Ujin had withdrawn to evade it.

Everyone watched this sudden situation with startled faces. He had endured so well until now.

Just when it seemed he was gleefully making him expend his internal energy, Hwa Mugi had suddenly drawn his sword. The one who had shouted for it to stop was none other than Hwa Mugi.

“...Enough.”

Hwa Mugi’s face was twisted in pain. The rock had shattered beneath the waves, and the sword had broken beneath the blacksmith’s hammer.

Geom Mugeuk knew that his father had attacked because he was certain this would happen.

“How did you know he was forcing himself to endure the pain?”

There were two reasons Geom Ujin had been so certain.

“If his martial art had been perfect, he would not have tried to drain our internal energy in the first place.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon nodded. He was right. If it had truly been such a perfect demonic art, given that man’s nature, he would have fought even more arrogantly.

Yet he had not revealed that fact from the beginning, instead making them exhaust their internal energy and suffer injuries.

And there was one more decisive reason.

“Why would such a meticulous man keep saying he was not in pain while fighting me? Instead of pretending to be hurt and making me deplete as much internal energy as possible.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded, guessing the reason. What other reason could there have been?

“Because it hurt too much.”

Chapter 829: You Had It Rough Dealing With Talkative Me
-------------------------------------------------------
“Father, you were truly magnificent!”

Geom Mugeuk cheered in delight, while the Fist Demon and the Poison King bowed to Geom Ujin in courtesy. For a Demon Venerable, watching the Cult Leader fight was a great honor.

“Thank you, Father.”

Geom Ujin’s expression did not change in the slightest, but the Fist Demon could feel it.

The Cult Leader was in better spirits than he had ever been.

He could tell that if someone so much as poked him, those emotions would come pouring out, and that was why he was acting even more blunt than usual.

He felt relieved. If the Cult Leader had lost to Hwa Mugi, Geom Mugeuk might have been perfectly fine, but the Cult Leader surely would have kept it in his heart.

‘You fought well, Cult Leader.’

Geom Ujin answered the look in the Fist Demon’s eyes.

“How was it? The skill I learned from you has yet to rust, has it?”

The two looked at each other and smiled.

Hwa Mugi’s face, on the other hand, was badly contorted.

‘The Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art has been broken.’

The Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art had a side effect.

As Jin Paecheon had said earlier, a demonic art that produced such astonishing effects was bound to have an equivalent side effect.

The side effect of the Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art was that when an external blow struck the protective astral qi and was absorbed as internal energy, a pain even greater than the pain one would originally have suffered was transmitted.

It looked as though the protective astral qi was being struck like water, making it seem as if it would not hurt at all, but in truth, every single blow inflicted excruciating pain.

But when that pain was endured, the opponent despaired, believing their attacks were of no use whatsoever.

That was the true terror of the Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art.

If one could not endure the pain and stopped midway, the art would be undone along with internal injuries, so Hwa Mugi had endured every attack that had flown at him before through extreme patience.

Considering whose attacks those had been, that alone made Hwa Mugi a truly remarkable martial artist.

At first, even Geom Ujin’s fists were bearable.

But after being hit again and again, the impact was anything but ordinary. He had withstood the combined attack of the Evil Alliance Leader and the Fist Demon earlier, yet he could not understand why these fists hurt so much.

After forcing himself to endure the pain, the Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art had finally shattered.

He did not know. As its name implied, the Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art was a martial art belonging to the demonic arts. When he had created it with the help of the souls within his body, Hyeok Docheon had given him the greatest assistance.

In the end, because the foundation of the Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art was based on demonic arts, he could not withstand the fists of Geom Ujin, who had mastered the Guhwa Demonic Art, the supreme demonic art above all demonic arts. And it was not merely the Guhwa Demonic Art.

They were the fists of Geom Ujin, who had achieved great completion of the twelfth stage of the Guhwa Demonic Art.

The fact that he had endured that much could instead be called astounding.

Hwa Mugi’s gaze turned toward Geom Ujin.

Now that the Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art had been broken, the method of killing his opponent by completely exhausting his internal energy had fallen through.

‘In the end, that is the only way to defeat this man!’

Hwa Mugi called to mind one martial art.

Geom Mugeuk boasted about his father to the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“Did you see that? That is what my father is like when he fights.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader nodded with a stunned expression. Just as Geom Mugeuk had said, Geom Ujin had not fought emotionally because of his son.

And was he the only one surprised? Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang were surprised as well.

Though it had been a fight in which he had not drawn his sword, Geom Ujin had overwhelmed Hwa Mugi.

Even more astonishing than that was that he had found his opponent’s weakness. If it had been them, they would have retreated to avoid consuming any more internal energy.

Until now, they had only thought Geom Mugeuk was special.

‘So the Young Cult Leader inherited his father’s blood.’

That thought came to them naturally.

Hwa Mugi looked at Geom Ujin and said coldly,

“Now let us fight with swords.”

There was no way Geom Mugeuk would simply let that pass.

“Wipe the blood from the corner of your mouth first.”

Blood was flowing from the corner of Hwa Mugi’s mouth. It was blood he had coughed up from internal injuries when the Heaven-Defying Demon-Absorbing Art was broken.

“And you should keep your promise. You said whoever drew his sword first would lose, didn’t you?”

Hwa Mugi stood there as if he refused to acknowledge it.

“Can a man who is going to leave a unique name in the history of murim go back on his promise?”

“If all of you die anyway, it will be history remembered only by me.”

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“There are people here besides us watching you, aren’t there?”

Who else is here? Just as Hwa Mugi, as well as the others, were thinking that, Geom Mugeuk mentioned people no one had expected.

“The souls dwelling in your heart. They are watching, aren’t they?”

“!”

“If you kill us, that will be the end of it, but they are watching what kind of person you are.”

Geom Mugeuk added with a sincere look in his eyes.

“Just as I do, you should think about your life after killing us.”

His life after killing them? Hwa Mugi had never once seriously thought about that.

Geom Mugeuk continued.

“I consider my life after killing you more important. I have lived that way until now as well.”

He would not think only of the destination and miss the scenery flowing past the carriage window. For a brief moment of joy at the summit, he would not neglect the time spent gasping for breath at the foot of the mountain.

“Once I kill you, I’m going to take Father and Master on a journey. I’ll go digging for poisonous herbs with the Poison King, and I’ll send the Murim Alliance Leader on a trip with Hagun and Haryeong, just the three of them.

I’ll visit the Evil Alliance Leader and eat the food he makes for me himself, and I’ll go traveling with the Pungcheon Cult Leader and Strategist Go, the three of us.

I’ll drink and have fun all night with Team Leader Cha, who likes my friend over there, and Ian. Those things are a thousand, ten thousand times more important than killing you.”

In truth, those words were not meant for Hwa Mugi. Geom Mugeuk was saying them to his father and to everyone here. Let us live that way from now on.

“Just as I have these people, you have those people, don’t you? You don’t want to show them the laughable sight of yourself not even keeping a promise, do you?”

Hwa Mugi’s face twitched. He did not like Geom Mugeuk speaking as if he knew him.

“Do you think those within me hold some great meaning to me?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader, who had been listening, nearly blurted out, If you look down on them, then don’t use their internal energy or martial arts! He naturally thought Geom Mugeuk would retort the same way.

“They do hold great meaning, don’t they?”

Hwa Mugi flinched, and Geom Mugeuk added with conviction.

“If they were people who meant nothing to you, you would have made sure they couldn’t come out freely and speak earlier.”

Even if not for that, how could his relationship with them be meaningless? They must have fought, reconciled, ignored one another, and gone through all kinds of things, forming bonds both hateful and fond.

Hwa Mugi, who had been quietly staring at Geom Mugeuk, began walking and said,

“Fine. I will keep the promise.”

Amazingly, he was the first to walk back inside the tavern.

When Hwa Mugi had first come here, he truly had never imagined this fight would proceed in such a manner. Was he going insane as well because he was keeping company with a madman?

When Hwa Mugi sat at the table where he had first been seated, Geom Mugeuk and the others all went back inside and sat in their original seats. In the end, it was as if Geom Ujin had beaten him back into his seat.

“I would like to hear your story as well.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, one corner of Hwa Mugi’s mouth seemed to rise slightly.

Then Hwa Mugi’s eyes changed in an instant.

“You said you were there three hundred years ago?”

Geom Mugeuk could tell. The Lord of Cheonui Palace, with whom he had not yet conversed, had appeared.

“That is correct.”

“Did you see me there?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded. At the time, Geom Mugeuk had infiltrated the Cheonui Palace camp and seen him.

“At the time, Palace Lord, you denied the Divine Maiden’s prophecy.”

The Lord of Cheonui Palace could tell for certain that Geom Mugeuk’s claim of having been there was true. That event was something only he knew.

“Just as the Divine Maiden prophesied, I failed to fulfill Heaven’s Will.”

Deep regret passed across the face of the Lord of Cheonui Palace. What would have happened if he had listened to her back then? He would probably already be dead, and a descendant of Cheonui Palace would be trying to fulfill Heaven’s Will.

“Do you know why I was able to endure until now?”

“I can guess.”

“What do you think it was?”

“Because you wanted to see whether Heaven’s Will would truly be fulfilled.”

As if to say he was correct, the Lord of Cheonui Palace nodded.

“Just as the Divine Maiden prophesied, only now, three hundred years later, has it become possible to fulfill Heaven’s Will.”

He spoke as though Heaven’s Will had already been completely fulfilled.

“Since you are inside that body, you think you are the one fulfilling Heaven’s Will.”

Perhaps those words displeased him, for his reply was sharp.

“All of you will die today. There was a prophecy.”

Geom Mugeuk firmly denied those words.

“That prophecy was wrong.”

“Prophecies are not wrong!”

“Do you mean the prophecy you denied three hundred years ago?”

The expression of the Lord of Cheonui Palace hardened, but Geom Mugeuk continued speaking.

“The Dark Palace Lord who inherited Cheonui Palace is dead. All of his subordinates are dead as well. Now all that remains are your soul and this Heavenly Slaughter Star.”

“As long as my soul lives, Heaven’s Will has been fulfilled.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk mentioned someone unexpected.

“The Guardian Palace Lord tried to protect you until the very last moment, even as his arms were cut off.”

The eyes of the Lord of Cheonui Palace trembled. The image of the Guardian Palace Lord trying to protect him came to mind.

Geom Mugeuk poured him a drink.

“Have a cup for him.”

Until now, he had continued to offer drinks and food to the souls, but every cup and dish had been flung to the floor and shattered.

Just when it seemed someone would finally drink a cup.

“No need.”

This time, the Lord of Cheonui Palace refused.

Geom Mugeuk guessed why he was not drinking.

“Do you know how he died?”

“I do not.”

Even if his voice had not trembled, one could tell those words were false. If he truly did not know, he would have asked how he died.

“The Dark Palace Lord killed him. After killing him, he obtained the orb where your souls resided.”

The eyes of the Lord of Cheonui Palace wavered. It was something he had tried so hard to deny. In his heart, he had imagined many times that the Guardian Palace Lord had left murim and lived happily until his death.

He had imagined him living as the master of a martial hall, having children, and dying after a good life; he had also imagined him becoming rich and dying after living in luxury.

But the Lord of Cheonui Palace had guessed it too. That he had died at the hands of the Dark Palace Lord. The reason he had struggled to deny that fact was because accepting that reality was too painful.

“The Guardian Palace Lord died with his eyes still open.”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk added,

“I closed his eyes for him.”

Geom Ujin, as well as everyone else, listened to Geom Mugeuk’s words with astonished expressions. It was already astonishing that he had gone three hundred years into the past, but to think he had been at the center of that incident and had even closed the Guardian Palace Lord’s eyes.

“And now you say you have fulfilled Heaven’s Will within the Heavenly Slaughter Star created by the descendants of those who killed that Guardian Palace Lord. Is your Heaven’s Will heavenly slaughter?”

The Lord of Cheonui Palace was swept up in agitation and rage.

“You think all of this happened because of me, don’t you? That is why you resent me, isn’t it?”

“I do not resent you. Thanks to you, I gained far too much. And you—whom do you resent?”

“I…”

The Lord of Cheonui Palace could not answer easily. Whom should he name?

The Dark Palace Lord who betrayed him? Or the Murim Alliance Leader who drew in the Demonic Cult? Bigwe, who dragged him into this? Or the heavens above? Or perhaps… himself, for dreaming a futile dream?

The eyes of the Lord of Cheonui Palace changed, and Hwa Mugi returned.

“Do you think provoking them like this will let you defeat me?”

Geom Mugeuk answered with calm eyes.

“That is not why.”

“Then why?”

“They were trapped inside you for three hundred years. They should know the truth before they die. They should leave behind their final words before they die.”

Because he felt that Geom Mugeuk was speaking sincerely, Hwa Mugi simply could not understand.

“What meaning is there in such nonsense?”

“It is important. Saying the words you have kept in your heart.”

Hwa Mugi still could not understand.

“Why go this far? They are enemies you will kill anyway, so why?”

“Yes, after today, I will never again think of you or those people. That is why I am doing everything I can today. If you have anything to say, say it all.”

Hwa Mugi, at least, had no intention of being swayed by Geom Mugeuk.

“I have nothing to say.”

“Then say it to those people, not to me. They may seem like your enemies, but were they not your friends and your masters? Do not take offense at my words, and say it while you have the chance. Since we are on the subject, I should do it too.”

Geom Mugeuk truly spoke to everyone.

“As you know, that man has achieved great completion of the Guhwa Demonic Art. Therefore, if things go wrong, we may lose. In case I die without being able to offer my farewells, I will say this in advance.”

Geom Mugeuk bowed his head politely.

“Thank you for looking upon me generously, even though I was a green young fool who went around stirring up trouble without knowing better.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to each person in turn.

The last place it went was to his father.

“You know my heart, don’t you?”

Geom Ujin said curtly,

“I do not.”

“No, that is too harsh!”

The Fist Demon beside him smiled faintly.

“Master, you know, don’t you?”

“I do not either.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader beside them, though no one had asked him, shook his head as if to say he knew least of all.

“You are all too much!”

After offering his greetings to everyone like that, Geom Mugeuk looked up at the sky through the gaping hole in the ceiling.

What was he thinking? The look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes as he gazed up at the sky was deeper than anything.

Since fate had let him arrive on time.

Geom Mugeuk said calmly,

“I will fight the final battle.”

With his father here, he would be able to devote himself entirely to the fight. No matter what unexpected situation arose, his father would handle it.

No one opposed him.

In truth, Geom Ujin had originally intended to finish off the Heavenly Slaughter Star himself. But after his son arrived and he saw the conversations he had with them, this fight was indeed his son’s fight.

Geom Ujin’s gaze as he looked at his son was deep, but he said only one thing.

“Be careful.”

“Yes, Father.”

When Geom Mugeuk rose from his seat, Hwa Mugi rose as well.

Before fighting, Geom Mugeuk poured liquor into the cup placed before Hwa Mugi.

“If you do not want to speak, then at least have a drink.”

Drip, drip.

He filled Hwa Mugi’s cup, then filled his own.

“You had a hard time dealing with a talkative man like me.”

Geom Mugeuk emptied his cup cleanly.

“Now let us end this.”

Chapter 830: Internal Energy Not Outmatched?
--------------------------------------------
Hwa Mugi picked up the cup of wine Geom Mugeuk had poured for him.

“Right now, I can hear an outcry within my heart, begging to be let out. I feel terrible rage and heartrending sorrow. They have never once revealed their emotions to this extent before.”

After speaking, Hwa Mugi drank. In the end, the one who emptied the cup today was not the souls, but Hwa Mugi, who had sworn he would never drink.

“I drank this wine not because of you, but for them.”

As Geom Mugeuk had said earlier, it was a moment that showed they held great meaning for Hwa Mugi in some way.

Setting down the cup, Hwa Mugi asked,

“You could have attacked me when I drank.”

Geom Mugeuk had expected an answer like, You were on guard, weren’t you? Or perhaps, That wouldn’t have been stylish, would it?

“Everyone is watching, aren’t they?”

Geom Mugeuk looked around at the people nearby. His father and the Alliance Leaders, the Demon Venerables and Cult Leader Pungcheon, and even the Sword King. Naturally, not one of them had taken their eyes off Geom Mugeuk and Hwa Mugi for even an instant.

“I may look like I do whatever I want, but I’m extremely conscious of how people see me. You have no idea how hard I try to look impressive to others.”

Hwa Mugi let out a hollow laugh. He had truly not expected that to be the reason. Even if that were the reason, to say it so honestly like this.

For the first time, Hwa Mugi revealed his thoughts.

“To be honest, before I met you, I didn’t think much of this murim. But all of a sudden, it became interesting.”

He meant it sincerely.

“If this is a murim where people like you exist.”

Geom Mugeuk said with a smile,

“Sorry for raising your expectations too high from the start. Still, after you kill me, look around carefully. There are plenty of interesting things here and there, and plenty of interesting people too.

Don’t take pleasure in killing people. Tormenting those weaker than you and feeling superior for it—that’s the lowest of the low.”

“You’re trying to lecture me again. You said we should fight, yet here you are rambling about useless things again.”

Of course, he had been the one to start this conversation.

But Geom Mugeuk readily admitted fault.

“Sorry. Thinking I won’t get another chance to say things like this made me talk too much. People who talk a lot tend to make mistakes easily too. Since you don’t say much, I suppose you don’t have to worry about that.”

For the briefest moment, Hwa Mugi thought that having someone like this as a subordinate would at least keep things from becoming boring. As the Young Cult Leader had said, he could already imagine how infuriating it would be to look at people terrified of him, offering false loyalty.

“Now, let’s really fight.”

Geom Mugeuk drew the Black Demon Sword first.

Was it because the sword knew his opponent was its master’s destined archenemy?

Jiiing.

The Black Demon Sword rang softly.

“You must truly be strong, seeing how the Black Demon Sword cries like this.”

Hwa Mugi also drew his black sword.

The two swords aimed at each other gleamed sharply.

Then Geom Mugeuk made a suggestion.

“It’s cramped in here. Let’s go out and fight.”

Hwa Mugi readily accepted.

“Let’s.”

The two of them went back outside the tavern.

They faced each other in the middle of the road in Maga Village, while the others stood before the Pungryu Tavern and watched their fight.

Geom Mugeuk began to fully reveal his presence.

The next moment, Hwa Mugi was standing in the sky. Originally, Geom Mugeuk’s presence had been a sea as deep as an abyss. The illusion of standing in the sky had come from the heavens reflected in clear water.

But now it was different.

It was a true sky.

As he underwent a complete rebirth and absorbed even the wondrous energy of the Six Paths Primordial Qi, Geom Mugeuk’s presence had changed from sea to sky.

Unlike before, it did not draw his opponent into the depths of an abyssal sea. Nor did it make him feel the fear of suffocation.

It simply showed him the sky.

Hwa Mugi looked around. It was a presence he had truly never imagined.

The feeling of standing in a real sky.

When a martial artist reveals his presence, it is to pressure and intimidate his opponent.

Yet this sky inflicted no harm on him at all.

And that was why he was overwhelmed. He felt as if he stood utterly alone before vast nature.

A transcendent presence of an absolute master who did not need to threaten his opponent at all.

That was Hwa Mugi’s impression of Geom Mugeuk’s presence.

After looking around, Hwa Mugi gazed at Geom Mugeuk standing tall in the midst of that sky. The sky and Geom Mugeuk were not separate.

The sky was Geom Mugeuk, and Geom Mugeuk was the sky.

‘So young, yet he shows the presence of such an absolute being?’

That was why he grew angry. This rage was anger that rose from the depths of his heart.

“A man who possesses such an extraordinary presence was merely going to travel, gather poisonous herbs, and drink all night?”

Geom Mugeuk answered that anger calmly.

“I guarantee you, finding people to enjoy doing that with will be far harder than ruling the murim.”

Hwa Mugi revealed his own presence.

Cold killing intent flowing from the black sword stretched in all directions like a spiderweb. The killing intent became a fierce northern wind, freezing the surrounding air.

Crack. Crack.

Powerful killing intent that froze the lungs.

The sky began to freeze. Ice flowers bloomed all around them.

His killing intent rejected the sky. It refused to acknowledge nature, and it also denied Geom Mugeuk’s way of life.

“Don’t make me laugh. If the heavens granted you strength, they must have granted it so you could use it in a place befitting that strength. You are deceiving the fate of a martial artist.”

Hwa Mugi struck the frozen part of the air with his fist.

Clang.

Astonishingly, the sky shattered, revealing what lay behind it.

Pitch-black darkness was there beyond.

Hwa Mugi looked into the darkness through the broken opening.

“So this is your true self.”

When Hwa Mugi tore at the edges of the crack with his hand, the sky fell away as if an ice wall were collapsing. Then the darkness beyond was revealed even more widely.

Geom Mugeuk simply watched as Hwa Mugi shattered his presence.

“This abyssal darkness is your true self, isn’t it? Why do you live hiding yourself?”

Geom Mugeuk answered as if it were nothing.

“What lies beyond is also me, and this side is also me. Just as there is a you who wants to kill me, and a you who wants to talk with me.”

Geom Mugeuk slowly walked toward Hwa Mugi.

He came close enough that if Geom Mugeuk reached out, he could touch him, but Hwa Mugi stood where he was. A suffocating tension flowed between them. It seemed that in the next flashing instant, one would thrust his sword at the other.

Geom Mugeuk ran his hand over the broken part as though repairing it. Then that place became blue sky once more.

“I simply like this side better than that one.”

Then, staring at Hwa Mugi standing before him, he asked,

“When I was filling that hole, why didn’t you attack?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Hwa Mugi flinched.

Because he did not want to fight this battle with something like a surprise attack.

He truly felt that way. He wanted to fight this Young Cult Leader properly. Suddenly, he recalled what Geom Mugeuk had said.

“It must be because I was thinking of what comes after killing all of you.”

He was saying that his own souls were watching him too.

At those words, a smile formed on Geom Mugeuk’s lips.

“You’re doing well.”

“I’ll be doing well once I kill you.”

Wooooong!

Internal energy began to pour fully into the black sword in Hwa Mugi’s hand.

Wooooong!

Internal energy also dwelled in Geom Mugeuk’s Black Demon Sword.

The two, having filled their swords with internal energy, swung them at each other as if by agreement.

Shiiiiik.

Kwaaaang!

With a tremendous explosion, sword and sword collided.

Neither of them was pushed back.

Beyond the crossed blades, Hwa Mugi’s eyes widened.

“You!”

It had been a strike in which he had drawn up as much internal energy as possible, yet Geom Mugeuk was not pushed back at all. It was power formed from the combined internal energy and innate true qi of Hyeok Docheon, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword, Ju Gashin, and the Palace Lord of Cheonui.

“My internal energy does not surpass yours?”

Aside from Geom Mugeuk’s skill, this was shocking.

“I’m the incarnation of greed, so I ate all sorts of things.”

Those watching were just as shocked.

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang looked at each other and exchanged glances. They had unconsciously looked to one another, as if asking whether what they had seen was real.

It was the moment they confirmed that Geom Mugeuk had more internal energy than anyone here.

The Fist Demon had expected Geom Mugeuk to be pushed back at least one or two steps, so he was inwardly surprised to see him not retreat even a single step.

‘As expected, the Cult Leader saw more accurately than I did.’

Standing beside him, Geom Ujin watched in silence, as if he had already anticipated this.

Cult Leader Pungcheon did not miss the chance to take credit. Since he could not say it to Geom Mugeuk, who was fighting, he whispered to the Sword King standing beside him.

“My divine object is melted into that internal energy!”

In truth, the Sword King also had every right to boast in this regard. In the past, during the process of excavating the secret vault with Geom Mugeuk, the two of them had eaten their fill of elixirs together.

‘I fed him well that day.’

Of course, the Sword King did not say it aloud. After giving Cult Leader Pungcheon a faint smile, he watched Geom Mugeuk fight.

Cult Leader Pungcheon could make such a remark only because Geom Mugeuk was fighting that well.

Sword and sword clashed, flashing with light.

Geom Mugeuk did not merely possess abundant internal energy. The speed with which he moved his body and thrust his sword was also beyond imagination.

Once they entered the fight, the two men shared the same impression.

‘He’s strong!’

Hwa Mugi found Geom Mugeuk more difficult to deal with than when he had faced the Alliance Leaders. This was true even though he was now displaying all the skill he had kept hidden earlier.

Geom Mugeuk also realized that Hwa Mugi was far stronger than he had thought.

Each time sword light flashed, their lives hung in the balance. Deadly moves that allowed not even a single moment’s mistake passed between them.

Clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang!

In a single breath, dozens of exchanges passed in an instant.

Their battle was not one of limbs against limbs, nor sword against sword, nor technique against technique.

It was will against will, each determined to kill the other.

Right now, Geom Mugeuk was displaying the greatest skill he was capable of. He did not hide his strength.

He would be the one to end it. He had no intention of handing this fight over to the people behind him.

He knew his father wanted to protect him by personally killing Hwa Mugi. He knew the Alliance Leaders, the Demon Venerables, Cult Leader Pungcheon, and the Sword King all felt the same.

But he would protect them. That was why he had walked such a long road. That was why he had endured that difficult life—to save those who must not die.

‘I will save them all.’

Just then, as their tense offense and defense continued—

Kaaang!

With one powerful strike, the two were pushed sliding in opposite directions.

And at that moment, as if by prior agreement, both unleashed their decisive moves at the same time.

Swish, swish, swish, swish!

Four beings blocked Geom Mugeuk in from every direction.

They were evil spirits. Not the familiar evil spirits he had always seen, but evil spirits with unfamiliar faces.

It was the First Form of the Ninefire Demonic Art that Hwa Mugi had unleashed, the Annihilation Form.

The moment Geom Mugeuk was trapped by the evil spirits, Hwa Mugi smiled.

‘You’re finished now!’

It was a decisive move aimed at Geom Mugeuk’s carelessness.

At the same time, the move Geom Mugeuk had unleashed also manifested.

Whoosh.

The moment darkness settled on all sides—

Shiiiiing.

A single streak of light cut across the darkness.

It was the Fourth Form of the Ninefire Demonic Art unleashed by Geom Mugeuk, the Dark One Flash.

Ninefire Demonic Art against Ninefire Demonic Art.

Those watching were startled. After they had only been competing sword against sword, no one had imagined they would suddenly use the Ninefire Demonic Art in such a surprise attack.

The Sword King was about to leap in at once.

If Geom Ujin had not remained standing there and watching, he would already have rushed forward.

But the situation was already too late.

The evil spirits on all sides were bringing their swords down toward Geom Mugeuk.

The walls on every side were walls of death.

It was at that moment.

Kuaaaang!

Something pure white seemed to overlap with the evil spirit attacking Geom Mugeuk from the front.

Swaeek! Swaeek! Swaeek!

Only three evil spirit swords came slashing down.

What made an evil spirit of the Annihilation Form—something that absolutely could not be pierced by ordinary means—disappear was the Great Demon Wall.

The instant Geom Mugeuk raised a Great Demon Wall, just large enough to block one person, overlapping it with the place where the evil spirit in front stood, that evil spirit vanished.

At the same time, Geom Mugeuk withdrew the Great Demon Wall and shot forward in an instant. It had all happened in the blink of an eye.

Geom Mugeuk had not let his guard down.

In particular, he had thought in advance about how to respond if the Ninefire Demonic Art were used.

Because he had already decided that if the Annihilation Form were used on him, he would escape this way, he had responded with the Great Demon Wall in the fleeting instant the Annihilation Form was unleashed.

Hwa Mugi, too, blocked the Dark One Flash by a method Geom Mugeuk had not expected.

Swish, swish, swish, swish.

Four evil spirits appeared around Hwa Mugi.

Kaaang!

The Dark One Flash cut off the neck of an evil spirit, but it did not cut Hwa Mugi. He had used the Annihilation Form on himself and made the evil spirit into a shield.

Both men looked at each other with startled expressions.

Hwa Mugi was truly astonished. He had not expected Geom Mugeuk to break through the Annihilation Form with the Great Demon Wall.

‘And that Great Demon Wall is as small as mine.’

Geom Mugeuk, too, could tell that Hwa Mugi had achieved great success in the Ninefire Demonic Art, and that his realm was comparable to that of Hyeok Docheon, the Demonic Cult Leader from three hundred years ago.

Geom Mugeuk’s surprise was even greater.

“How did you use two Annihilation Forms at the same time?”

He had attacked him with the Annihilation Form and, at the same time, defended himself with the Annihilation Form.

Geom Mugeuk absolutely could not do that.

‘Don’t tell me he achieved the twelfth-star grand completion of the Ninefire Demonic Art?’

Geom Mugeuk looked at his father. His father, too, shook his head slightly with a surprised expression. It meant that even achieving twelfth-star grand completion would not allow one to use two Annihilation Forms at the same time.

“How on earth did you do that?”

Instead of answering, Hwa Mugi charged toward Geom Mugeuk.

His movements became dramatically faster.

Swaaaaaeek.

In a flashing instant, Hwa Mugi’s black sword was cleaving the space before him.

Geom Mugeuk twisted his body and dodged, but—

Paat!

Blood sprayed from Geom Mugeuk’s shoulder. Fortunately, the cut was shallow, but it had been an attack that could easily have severed his arm.

Swaeek!

He retreated to avoid the second attack. If not for the Four Steps of the Wind God, he would have been cut again by that speed.

Those watching were shocked. It was such a threatening and swift attack that they could not help thinking Hwa Mugi must have been hiding even more of his strength.

Hwaaaaaaa.

Powerful demonic energy wavered around Hwa Mugi like a heat haze.

That was not all. Before anyone knew it, both his eyes had turned black. The whites had vanished, and they had become entirely black.

The moment Geom Mugeuk saw those eyes, the fine hairs across his entire body stood on end, and the Heavenly Demon Protection Art activated to its limit.

Hwa Mugi’s voice held a deep resonance, as if it were rising from a bottomless pit.

“Why did you not try to speak with me?”

Chapter 831: Your Heavenly Demon Souls Shall Not Enter the World.
-----------------------------------------------------------------
“Heavenly Demon Soul!”

Geom Mugeuk recognized the other’s existence at once.

‘The Heavenly Demon Soul that was absorbed that day was here together with these souls?’

Three hundred years ago, on that day, the Heavenly Demon Soul had been the first to be absorbed into the Secret Ark. Hyeok Docheon had struggled desperately not to have the Heavenly Demon Soul taken from him, but in the end, it was sucked into the Secret Ark.

The remaining people had been absorbed into the Secret Ark afterward.

Geom Mugeuk had naturally assumed that the Heavenly Demon Soul had vanished. And yet that Heavenly Demon Soul had been together with these souls!

Geom Mugeuk was someone who did not become startled or flustered under any circumstances, but in this moment, he was shaken.

The appearance of the Heavenly Demon Soul struck not only Geom Mugeuk but everyone watching with shock.

Who would have thought that the Heavenly Demon Soul they had only heard of in stories would reveal itself like this, by borrowing a human body?

Even the Cult Leader Pungcheon, who had been spouting nonsense only moments before, froze solid.

The Fist Demon’s expression hardened. Any other enemy might have been one thing, but the Heavenly Demon Soul would never be easy to face.

Moreover, the Heavenly Demon Soul before them now was one that thought and even spoke on its own.

‘An enemy more dangerous than the Heavenly Killing Star we faced before.’

The Fist Demon looked at Geom Ujin standing beside him. Tension could be felt in the cult leader’s stiff expression.

It could not be helped. No demonic art would be able to shock the Heavenly Demon Soul, and even the Nine Hells Demonic Art of the cult leader or the young cult leader might not work on it.

A being that no demonic practitioner should ever make an enemy of had appeared.

Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly to Hwa Mugi—no, to the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“If I had known you were here, I would have tried to talk to you first.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul, borrowing Hwa Mugi’s body, asked in a voice that shook the soul.

“Why?”

It was a sound coming from a human, but it was not a human voice.

“Because speaking with the Heavenly Demon Soul was my dream.”

Of course, that dream had been to speak with his own Heavenly Demon Soul.

He recalled something his father had once told him.

—There are records saying there was once a Heavenly Demon who conversed with the Heavenly Demon Soul. It was a very long time ago.

So when he had said he wanted to converse with the Heavenly Demon Soul, his father had also said this.

—The Heavenly Demon Soul is a dangerous existence. If it happens to run rampant, it cannot be stopped. Do you still want to chat with it?

He must have been speaking of a situation exactly like this.

Before his eyes stood the Heavenly Demon Soul, on the verge of running rampant—or perhaps already having begun to run rampant.

“You certainly are different.”

The other souls had not been able to control Hwa Mugi’s body. They had merely borrowed his mouth to speak.

But the Heavenly Demon Soul had completely taken over Hwa Mugi’s body.

“I saw you being absorbed that day, but I didn’t know you were here with these people like this.”

Naturally, he had thought the Heavenly Demon Soul had been destroyed. Since Hwa Mugi had learned the Nine Hells Demonic Art, perhaps it would newly emerge from the Nine Hells Demonic Art he used, or perhaps it had become a Nine Hells Demonic Art without the Heavenly Demon Soul to begin with.

“Since we’ve met now, let’s talk to our hearts’ content. Do you know how to drink? Let’s go inside and have a cup while—”

At that moment, the Heavenly Demon Soul’s low words rang out.

“Kneel.”

Silence fell over the place in an instant.

Geom Mugeuk showed not the slightest sign of displeasure.

“I can kneel as much as you like. You’re not just anyone, but the Heavenly Demon Soul from three hundred years ago. If we’re family, then we’re family, aren’t we? I could even kneel and call you Grandfather Heavenly Demon Soul.”

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was not someone who would meekly kneel.

“However, I’ll need permission.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s gaze turned toward Geom Ujin.

“No permission is needed. I intend to make the Demon Cult Leader kneel as well.”

Even at those rude words, Geom Ujin showed no reaction. This family quarrel was, to the very end, his son’s fight.

Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“I wasn’t saying I’d get my father’s permission.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul looked at Geom Mugeuk with puzzled eyes. Those watching also looked at Geom Mugeuk, wondering whose permission he meant to get, then.

The one Geom Mugeuk named was truly someone no one had expected.

“I have to get permission from my Heavenly Demon Soul.”

“!”

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s face creased slightly.

“As for me, if I had to kneel, I’m the kind of person who could even kneel to a tavern owner. But I still don’t know what kind of personality my Heavenly Demon Soul has.”

No one had expected Geom Mugeuk to mention his own Heavenly Demon Soul.

“You heard earlier, didn’t you? I care tremendously about how others see me. And this is the Heavenly Demon Soul dwelling in my body, so how much more would I care? Right now, my head is filled only with thoughts of looking impressive to my Heavenly Demon Soul.”

At that moment, an immense demonic energy poured from the Heavenly Demon Soul’s body.

At demonic energy that surpassed all limits, those who had not cultivated demonic arts all drew up their internal energy at once and resisted it.

Amid the raging demonic energy, the Heavenly Demon Soul’s deep voice flowed out.

“Now that I have descended, your Heavenly Demon Souls will never emerge into the world.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul was the soul of Hyeok Docheon, who had been cruel and arrogant three hundred years ago. It was certain that it could overwhelm not only Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul but even Geom Ujin’s.

Geom Mugeuk had experienced something similar before. His own Heavenly Demon Soul had not appeared in front of his father.

Was it because his father’s Heavenly Demon Soul was stronger than his? Or was it showing respect to the Heavenly Demon Soul of the previous generation?

Geom Mugeuk summoned his own Heavenly Demon Soul.

But just as the Heavenly Demon Soul had said, his Heavenly Demon Soul did not manifest.

“If I kneel, that means my Heavenly Demon Soul kneels as well. Are you truly so great an existence as that?”

It was not something he said to his opponent, but to his own Heavenly Demon Soul.

‘Hey, friend. Do we really have to kneel to a bastard like this?’

His Heavenly Demon Soul gave no response, and the Heavenly Demon Soul dwelling in Hwa Mugi’s body responded with rage.

Shhhhhhh!

The Heavenly Demon Soul rushed toward Geom Mugeuk. As it had shown before, its speed was explosively fast.

If its speed had become faster, would its attacks remain the same?

Every time the Heavenly Demon Soul’s sword flew in, a tearing sound rang out as if space itself were being ripped apart.

Kakakakang!

The attacks were so fast that there was no room for conscious thought such as, Block this way, counterattack that way.

Only instinct and the reactions of a body trained through cultivation could withstand those terrifying attacks.

After the initial impact of the swords clashing, a new shock surged in as a wave. Within that wave was a dark energy he had never experienced even once in his life.

If this energy could have such a negative effect even on him, who had mastered the Nine Hells Demonic Art, he did not need to look to know what it would be like for the others.

The depth of the fight was different from before. The Heavenly Demon Soul’s sword was not a sword meant to cut people. It was a sword that cut the heavens and cut the world.

Those watching held their breath as they observed the fight.

Everyone was shocked by Geom Mugeuk’s skill. In particular, Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang had to acknowledge one fact.

‘The young cult leader is above us.’

It was not simply that he had more internal energy. His skill was clearly a level higher as well. Because they would not be able to block that Heavenly Demon Soul’s attacks like that.

Swing.

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s missed sword energy passed by, cutting through the building behind.

Saaak!

It cut through the building behind that as well, and then cut down all the trees beyond it. Buildings and trees were sliced apart like paper.

Geom Mugeuk was gradually being pushed back by the Heavenly Demon Soul’s attacks. Unable to counterattack, he was fully occupied with defense.

All those watching looked at Geom Ujin. Their eyes were filled with worry, asking whether he would let the young cult leader continue fighting like this.

This was an opponent the young cult leader could hardly handle. An enemy no one here could easily handle.

Then, before it was too late, everyone should have rushed in.

But Geom Ujin silently watched the fight. As if he would not step forward until his son shouted for help.

Geom Mugeuk, having desperately put distance between himself and the Heavenly Demon Soul, unleashed the Nine Hells Demonic Art.

Before Geom Mugeuk, an evil spirit appeared. The most terrifying evil spirit among east, west, south, and north.

Charrarararak.

That evil spirit began to split apart.

How did he intend to block it? The Heavenly Demon Soul simply watched as the evil spirit divided.

Kwakakakakakakakakakwang!

Geom Mugeuk’s Great Annihilation Form was unleashed upon the Heavenly Demon Soul.

The evil spirits rushed toward the Heavenly Demon Soul.

Geom Mugeuk saw it.

The other evil spirits around them swept through everything and passed by, but the evil spirit attacking the Heavenly Demon Soul destroyed itself and vanished.

Even at that shocking sight, Geom Mugeuk was not surprised. He had merely confirmed what he had already expected.

The Nine Hells Demonic Art did not work on the Heavenly Demon Soul.

Because the Heavenly Demon Soul was a being born from the pinnacle of the Nine Hells Demonic Art.

“The Nine Hells Demonic Art is of no use against me.”

He felt joy from the Heavenly Demon Soul, and also a sense of superiority. Was the Heavenly Demon Soul originally such an existence? Or was that Heavenly Demon Soul special?

Even in this situation, Geom Mugeuk was not cowed in the slightest.

“I’ll win without using the Nine Hells Demonic Art.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s pitch-black eyes smiled.

“Then die by the Nine Hells Demonic Art!”

The instant the Heavenly Demon Soul thrust out its sword.

Sword energy poured down from the sky like lightning.

It was the Fifth Form of the Nine Hells Demonic Art, Soul-Severing Demonic Strike.

Shukshukshukshukshukshukshukshukshukshuk!

Each and every strand of sword energy was a powerful attack capable of piercing through the crown of an absolute master.

Geom Mugeuk used the Wind God Four Steps. It was the moment when Geom Mugeuk, reborn through the shedding of his mortal frame, displayed the Wind God Four Steps that had reached the ultimate realm.

The Shadow Step Geom Mugeuk unfolded was on a different level from what he had used before.

To the eyes of those watching, it seemed as if several forms of Geom Mugeuk appeared at the same time.

Geom Mugeuk’s figure flashed as he escaped through the rain of sword energy. Amazingly, not a single drop of rain struck him.

That was not the end. The moment he escaped the Soul-Severing Demonic Strike, Geom Mugeuk unleashed the Dark King Step and rushed toward the Heavenly Demon Soul.

In the Wind God Four Steps manual, this was written about the Dark King Step.

It can nullify any defense or evasion.

The person who had written those words in the secret manual would have cried out in admiration upon seeing the Dark King Step Geom Mugeuk was displaying now. Exactly like that!

Swaaaaaak!

Geom Mugeuk’s sword grazed the Heavenly Killing Star’s chest. The Heavenly Killing Star twisted his body sideways to evade it, and his sword flew toward Geom Mugeuk.

Shwik.

Geom Mugeuk also dodged the Heavenly Killing Star’s counterattack.

The two passed each other and exchanged places.

The Heavenly Killing Star looked at Geom Mugeuk with startled eyes.

“I did not think you would avoid the Soul-Severing Demonic Strike with a mere footwork technique.”

Geom Mugeuk was just as surprised. His opponent had dodged the decisive blow he had attacked with using the Dark King Step. Even though it had been faster and more precise than any Dark King Step he had ever executed.

The one who wrote the Wind God Four Steps manual would have to add one sentence.

It can nullify any ‘human’ defense or evasion.

As their positions changed, Geom Mugeuk, while watching, met his father’s eyes.

—In this fight, please trust me to the end.

At his son’s sound transmission, Geom Ujin understood. His son was not running wild out of a desire to win, so he must have something in mind. But it was a fight too perilous to watch.

Geom Ujin silently sent back one sentence.

—Be careful.

Geom Mugeuk and the Heavenly Demon Soul clashed again.

Geom Mugeuk fought as if he were truly in a state of selflessness. It was a battle that surpassed human limits.

It was a fight in which he had to block attacks that stabbed seven times at the vital acupoints of his chest in the blink of an eye. It was also a fight in which he blocked all those attacks and counterattacked on the final, eighth strike.

Those watching could tell.

Through this fight, the young cult leader’s skill is increasing! No, it has already increased.’

Even though the Heavenly Demon Soul’s attacks had grown faster than at first, Geom Mugeuk was blocking them all.

Perhaps he himself was not aware that his realm of martial learning had risen. He did not even have the leisure for that right now.

No matter what achievement Geom Mugeuk had gained, the hearts of those watching were filled with anxiety. Geom Mugeuk was barely blocking the attacks. It would not be strange at all if he died at any moment.

Paak!

Blood sprayed from Geom Mugeuk’s shoulder. This time, the stab was fairly deep, scattering blood into the air.

Geom Mugeuk’s breathing was rough, and his heart felt as if it might burst at any moment.

This time, blood sprayed from his side. He had truly dodged by the barest margin.

Thinking that someone had to step in, Jin Paecheon, Baek Jagang, and everyone else looked at Geom Ujin.

The Fist Demon sent the sound transmission he had been holding back to Geom Ujin. He absolutely did not want to say such a thing, but—

—We must help Mugeuk.

He thought that because he was his son, he might be unable to make an even colder judgment. Because he was his son, he might rush in sooner, but because he was his son, he might also miss the timing.

—Wait.

Geom Ujin waited. Because the Fist Demon knew better than anyone what state of mind the cult leader was in when he said those words, he could say no more.

‘…Cult Leader.’

Bang!

In the meantime, Geom Mugeuk was sent flying again and slammed into a wall.

Wiping away the blood flowing from his mouth, Geom Mugeuk instead provoked the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“If you had been my father’s Heavenly Demon Soul, I would never have been able to win. But you are not.”

At the displeased gaze asking why, Geom Mugeuk looked straight at him and answered.

“Because you are one who has lost the honor and pride of the Heavenly Demon Soul.”

Geom Mugeuk scraped at the Heavenly Demon Soul’s temper.

“A being that ought to protect the Divine Cult dares to point its sword at the young cult leader of the Divine Cult!”

Geom Mugeuk shouted in a stern voice.

“The one who should kneel is you!”

From the Heavenly Demon Soul’s black eyes, intense demonic energy erupted. The demonic energy now was not something a human emitted.

Hwaaaaaaang!

Geom Mugeuk’s clothes flapped violently, and not only those who had not cultivated demonic arts, but everyone other than Geom Ujin also retreated.

The Fist Demon, watching the battle, could not understand. If the opponent had been some half-baked fool, perhaps, but in a fight this unfavorable—

—Why on earth is Mugeuk provoking the Heavenly Demon Soul like that?

He had asked because he wondered whether the cult leader knew something he did not.

And indeed, the cult leader knew.

—He is not provoking that Heavenly Demon Soul.

At first, the Fist Demon did not understand what he meant. As he chewed over those words again, a thought occurred to him.

‘No way?’

At the Heavenly Demon Soul’s strike, Geom Mugeuk rolled across the ground.

Geom Mugeuk sprang to his feet and vomited blood. He had suffered internal injuries from that blow.

At the sight of Geom Mugeuk covered entirely in blood, Geom Ujin could endure no longer and was about to step forward when his eyes met his son’s.

‘Father! Please trust me to the end!’

While he hesitated, the Heavenly Demon Soul’s sword flew toward Geom Mugeuk.

“This is the end!”

Swaaaaaak.

Everyone moved toward Geom Mugeuk, but only Geom Ujin stood there watching.

One step before those who were trying to help could reach him, just as the Heavenly Demon Soul’s sword was about to pierce Geom Mugeuk’s chest—

Chaaaang!

Someone struck away the Heavenly Demon Soul’s sword.

At that powerful restraint, the Heavenly Demon Soul was forced back, surprise blooming across his face.

Those who had thrown themselves forward to help Geom Mugeuk also all looked at one place with startled expressions.

A being stood before Geom Mugeuk.

This was the reason he had endured with his life on the line until now. It was also the true target Geom Mugeuk had been provoking.

“I believed.”

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes trembled as he looked at that back.

“That you would come out for me without fail.”

Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul had descended in human size.

Chapter 832: Do You Know Why I Won’t Fight?
-------------------------------------------
“You’re exactly as you were!”

Geom Mugeuk, who had been standing behind the Heavenly Demon Soul, walked forward and looked at it.

The great Heavenly Demon Soul had shrunk in size, but its appearance was unchanged.

It still possessed a face and gaze terrifying to meet, and at its heart, that hellfire in the shape of the Heavenly Demon Soul’s face remained as it had been.

Now that it was as small as a person, it somehow felt even more frightening; and yet, with its reduced size, there was something more refined and impressive about it.

With his heart swelling, Geom Mugeuk greeted him.

“It’s been a long time, my friend.”

Geom Mugeuk called him a friend.

When he had first met the Heavenly Demon Soul, he had said to it: I am your master, and your friend.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was someone who wanted to be a friend more than a master.

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s eyes seemed to flick briefly toward Geom Mugeuk, but soon fixed on the Heavenly Demon Soul lodged in Hwa Mugi’s body, standing before it.

“Thank you for saving me.”

But if he had simply said that and moved on, would he be Geom Mugeuk?

“Everyone expects you to be good at speaking since you’re my Heavenly Demon Soul. Say something.”

Was it just his imagination? He felt that the Heavenly Demon Soul was slightly troubled.

The joy of a talkative master continued. It was hard to believe this was a man who had been driven to the brink of death only moments ago.

“I once told Father that I had two wishes regarding you. The first was to talk with you.”

But the Heavenly Demon Soul did not say a single word.

“The second was to make you small and travel with you like a friend. One of my two wishes has already come true.”

All those who had thrown themselves forward to help Geom Mugeuk were staring at the Heavenly Demon Soul in astonishment.

Near Geom Mugeuk were Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang. This was the first time they had learned that the Heavenly Demon Soul could descend in such a small form.

Looking at them, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“Where in the world would you find a Martial Alliance Leader and an Unorthodox Alliance Leader throwing themselves forward for the young cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult?”

Jin Paecheon was truly relieved that Geom Mugeuk was unharmed.

“Right now, you are not the young cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. You are Ha-gun’s friend.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and bowed respectfully.

“Thank you.”

Because there was such a bond between them, Jin Paecheon’s regret ran deep.

“You have saved us several times, yet I have not managed to save you even once.”

“Even without the Heavenly Demon Soul, Alliance Leader, you would have saved me.”

Would he truly have been able to? When he had thrown himself forward earlier to protect Geom Mugeuk, it had been perilously close.

If the Heavenly Demon Soul had not descended, he would have tried to stop it even by sending out sword energy, but there was no guarantee that Geom Mugeuk would have been safe.

He had certainly regretted being one step too late.

Regardless of the Demonic Cult Leader’s will, he should have thrown himself forward first.

“I will brag about this for the rest of my life.”

Jin Paecheon could only feel grateful that Geom Mugeuk said that.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to Baek Jagang.

“You came with him?”

Baek Jagang answered impassively.

“We have a habit of instinctively moving together whenever the orthodox faction moves.”

Geom Mugeuk laughed at the joke. Because he did not put on the slightest air of taking credit, Geom Mugeuk felt all the more grateful.

Geom Mugeuk bowed his head respectfully to him as well.

“I will not forget the grace of you throwing your precious body forward for me today.”

It was a promise from Geom Mugeuk that did not ring hollow.

Beside them was the Fist Demon.

“As expected! Master is the best, far better than my heartless father!”

The Fist Demon could confirm that the Cult Leader had seen correctly.

To risk his life in order to make his own Heavenly Demon Soul descend.

Truly, it was something no one but Geom Mugeuk could ever attempt.

On the other hand, he also thought that Geom Mugeuk had made a wise judgment.

If neither Geom Mugeuk’s nor the Cult Leader’s Heavenly Demon Soul descended, the fight against that Heavenly Demon Soul would never have been easy.

Geom Mugeuk had found a breakthrough in an unfavorable fight.

Naturally, the Sword King was beside them as well. It truly was a historic scene, with all these extraordinary masters throwing themselves forward at the same time.

“To be honest, I wasn’t that worried.”

“Why not?”

“I thought, since you must have something in mind, you were holding out like that.”

Though for someone saying that, the Sword King had thrown himself forward far too urgently.

Cult Leader Pungcheon burst out scolding Geom Mugeuk.

“Are you trying to shock people like this? What were you planning to do if the Heavenly Demon Soul didn’t appear?”

“I had prepared in my own way.”

When Geom Mugeuk pulled open his robes, the finest Heavenly Silkworm Thread was wrapped tightly around his chest.

“On top of this, I was using the Heavenly Demon Body Protection Art, the Heavenly Steel Body, and even the Vajra Asura. Even if it was the Heavenly Demon Soul’s sword, it would not have pierced through easily. And the moment I was stabbed, if I felt this wasn’t right, I would have used the technique I learned from you, Cult Leader.”

Only then did a smile form on Cult Leader Pungcheon’s lips.

“As expected! You had thought it all through. Yes, you must have had a plan from the moment you took my sacred relic.”

Seeing the terrifyingly persistent talk of his sacred relic resurface, it seemed Cult Leader Pungcheon had finally been able to relax.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward his father, who still stood in place.

“Come on, even the Poison King over there came a few steps forward.”

At those words, the Poison King quietly stepped back.

“That is too much, Father!”

His father merely looked at his son in silence. How could that brat know? How hard it had been just now to hold himself back and watch until the end without stepping forward.

Recently—no, perhaps in his entire life—it might have been the hardest moment he had ever endured.

And how could Geom Mugeuk not know that? The relief he sensed now was an expression of relief he had never once seen on his father’s face in his entire life.

Geom Mugeuk dropped to the ground and performed a full bow on the spot. A mere greeting could not possibly convey the overwhelming gratitude in his heart.

“Thank you, Father.”

His father had believed in him until the end. Because his father had believed in him, the Heavenly Demon Soul had been able to descend.

After exchanging greetings with those who had tried to protect him, Geom Mugeuk walked back to the Heavenly Demon Soul’s side.

Even while those conversations were taking place, the two Heavenly Demon Souls had been staring at each other.

“You’ll have to get used to it from now on. Your friend is a person who talks this much.”

Unable to hold back, Cult Leader Pungcheon said a word.

“It’s your Heavenly Demon Soul, so it may talk even more than you. Right now, it might simply be hiding its thoughts deviously, just like its master.”

Geom Mugeuk found himself hoping that Cult Leader Pungcheon’s joke was true.

Someday, I want to talk with you about all sorts of things. I want to share with you the words I cannot say to anyone else.

So before this fight ends, even if it is just one word, please say something to me.

That was Geom Mugeuk’s wish.

Meanwhile, the Heavenly Demon Soul lodged in Hwa Mugi’s body still seemed unable to believe this fact.

“It truly descended?”

Earlier, it had boasted that as long as it existed, their Heavenly Demon Souls would never appear. And yet, shattering that boast, Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul had descended.

And there was one more thing that astonished Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

“How did it manifest in a small body?”

Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul gave no answer and merely gazed silently at its opponent.

Looking at his own Heavenly Demon Soul, Geom Mugeuk said,

“I do not know much about you. From Father, I heard only that Heavenly Demon Souls extremely avoid clashing with one another. So I am sorry for giving you this kind of command as my first order.”

In a stern voice, Geom Mugeuk gave the Heavenly Demon Soul his command.

“Destroy him.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul that had received the command walked forward without the slightest hesitation.

At that moment, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul sneered at Geom Mugeuk.

“Do you know what you have just done?”

For some reason, it did not sound like simple irritation, so Geom Mugeuk called his Heavenly Demon Soul to a halt.

“Wait.”

When his Heavenly Demon Soul stopped, Geom Mugeuk asked the creature,

“What do you mean?”

Instead of an answer, a question came flying back.

“Do you know why Heavenly Demon Souls do not wish to fight one another?”

That was a question Geom Mugeuk had also wondered about.

And today, that astonishing secret was revealed.

“Because if a Heavenly Demon Soul fights another and loses, it is extinguished forever.”

“!”

Geom Mugeuk was shocked. He was so shocked that he wondered whether he had been this shocked in recent memory. This was something neither he nor his father had known.

“That is why, if there is a Heavenly Demon Soul it believes to be stronger than itself, it will never manifest. That is the innate nature of a Heavenly Demon Soul.”

It was a secret that could only be heard from a Heavenly Demon Soul.

“The victor absorbs the opponent’s power and becomes even stronger. The defeated one’s existence vanishes, and all the power it possessed is absorbed.”

Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul revealed an emotion it had not shown until now. That emotion was greed.

“Your Heavenly Demon Soul is special.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to his own Heavenly Demon Soul. Yes, it was special. It was the Heavenly Demon Soul that had saved him from the very first time it had appeared.

“It manifested in such a small body.”

This was precisely the part of its descent that had shocked Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

“It descended even knowing it was weaker.”

Geom Mugeuk silently looked at his Heavenly Demon Soul. At the sight of it simply staring at its opponent with that blunt expression.

‘Did you descend for me, even though you might lose the fight and be extinguished?’

Because of that, the creature’s ambition did not reach Geom Mugeuk’s ears.

“I will absorb your power and become the strongest Heavenly Demon Soul in history.”

Geom Mugeuk asked in a calm tone,

“Is that true?”

The Heavenly Demon Soul gave no answer. It had expressed itself before by moving its eyes ever so slightly, but now it only stared straight ahead.

“I asked if it was true. I know you do not speak, but answer this.”

There was still no reaction, and because of that, he knew. The words about being extinguished forever were true.

“Go back in.”

Geom Mugeuk ordered the Heavenly Demon Soul to disappear again. But the Heavenly Demon Soul did not vanish.

“It is an order. Go back in at once.”

Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul did not disappear.

At that moment, Geom Ujin said quietly,

“The Heavenly Demon Soul is an existence that must protect its master. Right now, the Heavenly Demon Soul has judged that your life is in danger.”

It meant that, above its master’s command, its sense of duty to absolutely protect its master took precedence.

Of course it would have made such a judgment. After all, he had summoned it while barely holding on.

‘You truly descended to protect me, even going against your own nature.’

Geom Mugeuk could not simply be moved. He had to break the Heavenly Demon Soul’s stubbornness quickly.

“Listen, my friend. We may not have spent a very long time together, but at least enough time has passed for you to have a rough idea of what kind of person I am, right? You know that I will definitely find a way, don’t you?

So go back in. Besides, I am not alone. Father is here, and so are the others. So do not worry about me and go back in.”

Even when he coaxed it gently, the Heavenly Demon Soul did not go in.

In that gaze, Geom Mugeuk read not only the will to protect him, but another emotion as well.

The pride of a Heavenly Demon Soul that would not lose to anyone.

That gaze seemed to ask him this:

If it were you, would you retreat in this situation?

Of course you would not abandon someone you had to protect.

The Heavenly Demon Soul was exactly like him.

And so Geom Mugeuk could feel that he could not stop it.

When Geom Mugeuk nodded, the two Heavenly Demon Souls walked toward each other.

They strode forward and swung their swords at one another.

Swaaaaaaaak.

Enormous demonic energy surged as their swords collided in midair.

Boom!

From the impact, demonic energy burst outward as if exploding. Only their size had shrunk; the battle between the two was still emitting the same demonic energy that would pour from a clash between two gigantic Heavenly Demon Souls.

Whoooooooong!

Everyone watching retreated. Only by raising their inner force to the limit could they block the demonic energy.

Because it was a fight between Heavenly Demon Souls, the nature of the battle was different.

It was not a fight using swift techniques and footwork like humans.

A battle of demonic energy against demonic energy.

A battle of presence against presence.

A battle of pride against pride.

Two demonic energies of different natures stood tautly opposed.

Beyond their crossed swords, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul spoke arrogantly.

“Do you think a Heavenly Demon Soul that has not even been born for long can defeat me, who has survived for three hundred years?”

It would be overwhelmingly superior in combat experience.

In that instant, Geom Mugeuk’s heart sank.

There was one thing he had forgotten.

He had not yet learned the ninth form of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art. From the seventh to the ninth forms of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art were martial arts used by the Heavenly Demon Soul, and he had only learned up to the eighth form.

Earlier, he had been so focused on the thought that he had to summon the Heavenly Demon Soul that he had forgotten that point.

Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul would certainly have mastered it up to the final form.

And if it was none other than the final ninth form?

‘At this rate, we lose.’

And if he lost the Heavenly Demon Soul forever because of that?

This was an entirely different matter from his martial arts growing weaker.

This was losing a friend—

What should I do? Think, Geom Mugeuk! Hurry!

At that moment, a thought flashed through Geom Mugeuk’s mind.

“Maybe?”

It was a mad idea, but there was no other way.

Geom Mugeuk sent a voice transmission to his father.

—Father, transmit the ninth form of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art to me now.

Geom Ujin understood why his son was making such a request. That was why, if anyone other than his son had said those words, he would have dismissed it as absurd madness.

Geom Ujin did not deliberate for long. If he was going to choose anyway, then it was a battle against time.

—I will transmit the ninth form of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art from now on.

The ninth form of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art: Demon Soul Supreme.

To think he would transmit this final technique here, in this place.

And for this absurd transmission to succeed,

—You must comprehend the mnemonic formula all at once!

He was attempting it because it was his son. Because the son he had watched until now might, perhaps, be able to do it.

—I will definitely understand it.

This attempt was not to make his Heavenly Demon Soul defeat the opponent. It was an attempt to make it endure at least enough to avoid being extinguished.

‘Please!’

Geom Mugeuk sent voice transmissions to the two alliance leaders and the others.

—Please protect Father and me from the aftermath of that battle for a moment.

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang understood. The father and son were trying to make some kind of move.

The two of them blocked the space in front of Geom Mugeuk, and the rest blocked the space in front of Geom Ujin.

Geom Mugeuk himself had never dreamed that he would end up learning the final technique of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art here, where this final battle was taking place.

Geom Mugeuk focused all his mind.

He thought that all the martial arts he had learned and all the effort he had made until now had been for this moment. May his rebirth prove useful! May the martial attainments he had gained in his earlier fight with Hwa Mugi prove useful!

‘I will definitely comprehend it before the moment comes when you must unleash the ninth form.’

Listening to the formula his father transmitted by voice, Geom Mugeuk closed his eyes.

‘Until then, hold on.’

Chapter 833: Your Heavenly Demon Soul Has Been Destroyed
--------------------------------------------------------
Geom Ujin transmitted the mnemonic formula for the final, ninth form to his son.

“Where the will of the demon soul goes, there is death, and none shall be able to stop it. The true qi begins at the Chonghui acupoint. The result precedes the cause, and death comes before life. Do not fear the true qi flowing in reverse.............”

Geom Ujin was doing as his son wished. He did not interfere in the slightest.

When his son overcame this fight entirely on his own, he would grow and become stronger.

This fight was a once-in-a-lifetime crisis, with lives and the fate of murim hanging in the balance, but at the same time, it was also a fortuitous opportunity that would never come again in a martial artist’s life.

Geom Ujin willingly gave that opportunity to his son. Of course, if he had not trusted his son, he would never have been able to entrust it to him so completely.

While the mnemonic formula was being transmitted, the two Heavenly Demon Souls entered into full-fledged battle.

It was a fight in which the loser would be extinguished forever.

Kwaang!

Amid the clash of sword against sword in midair, neither of the two Heavenly Demon Souls retreated even a single step.

“I will make your power mine, no matter what!”

The sword of Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul was filled with desire, while the sword of Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul was filled with the will to protect its master.

Every time sword met sword, powerful demonic qi raged. The two kinds of demonic qi were similar, yet different, and seemed almost alive as they howled.

When the Great Annihilation Form was unleashed, it was because of this demonic qi that the opponent could not escape. Absolute demonic qi that sucked the opponent in and made it impossible for them to leave that space.

From the Heavenly Demon Souls, demonic qi even stronger than when the Great Annihilation Form was unleashed raged forth.

As not one but two Heavenly Demon Souls spewed out demonic qi, not only Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang, but the others as well, could not approach the area where the Heavenly Demon Souls were fighting.

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang gave up on the thought of intervening in the fight and stood before Geom Mugeuk, blocking the aftermath of the impacts that came flying toward him.

Judging by the atmosphere, Geom Ujin was transmitting something to Geom Mugeuk. If that was the case, even the slightest breeze would be a disturbance to them.

The Fist Demon, the Sword King, and the Cult Leader Pungcheon were of the same mind, and raised barriers with their internal energy.

Under their protection, Geom Mugeuk was memorizing the mnemonic formula his father was passing on to him.

He had already mastered up to the eighth form of the Nine Hwa Demonic Art, and his martial arts had reached the pinnacle. The moment he heard the meaning contained in most of the formula, he could understand it.

But even for Geom Mugeuk, there were parts he could not understand.

For the moment, he skipped over the parts he did not know and focused on the next line.

And so, when the transmission of the mnemonic formula was complete from beginning to end.

—Father, please guard this place for a moment.

Geom Mugeuk immediately unfolded the Time-Space Transposition Art and vanished from that place.

Geom Ujin believed his son had entered the Time-Space Transposition Art in order to concentrate entirely on the formula.

But the Cult Leader Pungcheon knew Geom Mugeuk’s true intent.

‘He means to use the Sicheon Secret Art inside there.’

The Cult Leader Pungcheon felt pleased that the things he had taught were being put to precious use at the most crucial moment. He even thought that all of it might have been fate for the sake of this very moment.

Indeed, Geom Mugeuk unfolded the Sicheon Secret Art within the Time-Space Transposition Art.

From the beginning, he could have entered this place with his father and listened to the formula there. If he had done so, he could have saved the time it took to receive the transmission.

But there was a reason he had not.

It was to avoid creating a situation where both he and his father were absent from the outside.

Their opponent was Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul. Its nature was cruel and heartless. The moment it realized that he and his father had disappeared, there was no telling what scheme it might use against those who remained.
