At least one of the two had to remain.

Because he could not focus on the mnemonic formula for this final technique while worrying about the outside.

Having gained more time through the Sicheon Secret Art, Geom Mugeuk mulled over the formula. He reconfirmed the parts he understood and separately organized the parts he did not.

Those were the parts he would ask his father about once he went out. There were more of them than he had expected.

‘Will I be able to understand all of this before it’s too late?’

A sudden fear came over Geom Mugeuk. This was not just any martial art, but the final form of the Nine Hwa Demonic Art. Had he been arrogant, overestimating his own ability?

A single thought suddenly sprang into his mind.

‘Wouldn’t it have been better to spend this time thinking about how to use that formula instead?’

There was a mnemonic formula he had learned from Gaye, the Palace Lord of the Divine Maiden Palace.

While learning that formula, he had sensed that it was a formula used to control Hwa Mugi.

But she had only transmitted the formula to him, and had not taught him how to use it. She herself had not known. Yes, there was no way the Dark Palace Lord would have told someone else even the method.

‘Still, should I have found a way to use that formula somehow?’

The moment that thought came to him, Geom Mugeuk understood.

This was an inner demon born of fear that he might not properly master the ninth form.

Now that the Heavenly Demon Soul had already appeared, such thoughts were nothing more than belated regrets. Even if it worked on Hwa Mugi, it would not work on the Heavenly Demon Soul.

He did not know what help that formula might provide later, but for now, he had to focus on mastering the ninth form.

But once he lost his concentration, it was not easy to steady his mind again. The more he tried to shake the thought off, the more it came to mind.

Time within the Sicheon Secret Art seemed to flow even faster.

At that moment, a scene appeared in Geom Mugeuk’s mind.

It was the image of himself walking alone through a snowy field where a blizzard raged.

That moment when he had walked amid vague despair and desperate loneliness, wondering if he would truly be able to find all the materials for the great ritual.

‘It was all to reach this very place today.’

The mnemonic formula of the Nine Hwa Demonic Art was important, and the mnemonic formula of the Six Paths Primordial Qi was important, but what mattered most was himself, who had walked that long road and arrived here.

Because that was not a road he had reached through tricks.

‘Believe in yourself, Mugeuk.’

If I have tried this hard and still cannot do it, then there is nothing to be done. I will gladly die.

Had he not worked hard enough to be able to say such words?

Once that thought came to him, Geom Mugeuk’s anxious heart calmly settled. His distracting thoughts sank away, and he felt his mind grow still.

The time that had seemed to flow quickly began to flow slowly again.

‘Time, please flow slowly.’

After organizing the formula in his mind from beginning to end like that, Geom Mugeuk released the Time-Space Transposition Art and went back out.

Outside, an even fiercer fight was taking place.

Even after seeing just one scene, he could tell. His own Heavenly Demon Soul was being pushed back.

But he had no time to worry about that.

—There are several parts I do not understand. First, when moving from the Qihai acupoint to the Zhongji acupoint, it says that the season stopped at winter. I do not know whether that means to stop the true qi, or to move the true qi with cold energy.

Geom Mugeuk asked his father about the parts that remained unclear.

Geom Ujin explained earnestly to his son.

—It is both. You stop the true qi while maintaining as cold an energy as possible. But what matters in this part is not that.

When one thing was taught to Geom Mugeuk, he understood ten.

—What matters is when spring comes.

Geom Ujin nodded and added one more thing.

“When is important, but how is important as well. The key is how gently you release the cold true qi you had stopped.”

“I understand what you mean, Father.”

Geom Mugeuk asked about what he did not know, but also confirmed what he found uncertain.

—When moving from the Yanggang acupoint to the Huangmen acupoint, must I move the true qi quickly?

—You saw it correctly.

—Then when moving from the Taixi acupoint to the Taizong acupoint.............

Geom Mugeuk continued asking questions, and absorbed the parts his father explained into himself.

Geom Mugeuk truly displayed terrifying concentration.

What if the father answering him had not been at twelve stars, but at the time when he had only achieved great completion of ten stars? Or what if Geom Mugeuk himself had only been at that level? This exchange today would have been absolutely impossible.

Swaeeeeeek!

Kwaang!

Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul was pushed sliding back toward where they were.

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang raised their protective astral qi and released their internal energy.

With the gentlest internal energy possible, they stopped Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul from being injured.

Enduring the powerful demonic qi the Heavenly Demon Soul emitted, the two pushed him forward again.

The Heavenly Demon Soul charged forward once more. It did not fear, and it did not hesitate.

Geom Mugeuk knew the reason his Heavenly Demon Soul was being pushed back, even though he and Hwa Mugi had fought to a draw in their internal energy contest.

His father had once told him. The strength of a Heavenly Demon Soul depended on how deeply the one who summoned it understood the Nine Hwa Demonic Art. He was being pushed back because he had not yet mastered the ninth form.

Forcing his impatient heart to calm, Geom Mugeuk sent another voice transmission to his father.

—I will organize my thoughts one last time.

After saying that, Geom Mugeuk vanished once again into the Time-Space Transposition Art.

Geom Ujin was inwardly greatly surprised. Not only had his son perfectly memorized the formula in such a short time, but he had understood most of its contents, and he was accurately asking about the parts Geom Ujin had predicted.

But there were still parts he had not asked about. From Geom Ujin’s perspective, they were definitely parts that needed to be asked.

If he had understood and moved on, then that was fortunate. But if he had missed them? It would not be easy to fully comprehend the formula.

Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul saw Geom Mugeuk disappear and reappear in the midst of the fight.

It was certain Geom Mugeuk was plotting something.

‘I must not drag this out any longer.’

Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul unleashed the Nine Hwa Demonic Art in earnest.

Seventh Form of the Nine Hwa Demonic Art, Demon Soul Genesis.

Countless blades of astral qi appeared as if a net had been spread across the sky.

Those watching knew. If that countless number of blades flew at them, they would never be able to block them.

All that remained was whether they could withstand it with their protective astral qi without being injured.

At that very moment.

Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul also unleashed the same Demon Soul Genesis without backing down.

Their movements were identical. The motion of thrusting out the sword, even the angle of their arms.

Even the countless blades of astral qi forming in the air behind them were identical.

The Demon Soul Genesis unleashed by the two Heavenly Demon Souls looked as if, were it folded in half, the two sides would overlap perfectly.

Swaeeeeeeeek.

Swaeeeeeeeek.

The countless streams of astral qi created by the two Heavenly Demon Souls poured toward each other.

Kakakakakakakakakakakang!

Tremendous astral qi collided in midair, sparks bursting from the impact.

The aftermath of that collision spread in every direction.

Hwaaaaaak.

A tremendous shockwave spread everywhere. The earth shook and the ground split open. The nearby cloth shop and hardware store collapsed outright.

Just then, as dust rose thickly.

Kwakakwakakwakakwak!

Something burst through the smoke.

Lined up in a single file and pushing forward, they were evil spirits.

What Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul had aimed for with this move was not Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

While Demon Soul Genesis had thrown their vision into chaos, it had unleashed the Great Annihilation Form toward the place where Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang stood.

“Be careful!”

Jin Paecheon shouted, raising his protective astral qi to the limit and releasing his internal energy.

Baek Jagang likewise responded to the incoming Great Annihilation Form. Baek Jagang pressed close to Jin Paecheon’s side, intending to face a single evil spirit together.

“In a line!”

It was a wise decision. Because the evil spirits were larger than people, even if two people stood in the path one passed through, there was enough room.

If they did not divide their strength and blocked one evil spirit, while the others avoided behind them in a single line, they would be safe.

Kwakakwakakwakakwak!

The very moment the evil spirit was about to engulf them!

Kwakakwakwang! Kwang! Kwangkwang!

Jin Paecheon, Baek Jagang, and everyone else all saw it.

Before the evil spirit could engulf them, it collided with something.

A wall of astral qi had been raised in front of Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang.

Amazingly, it was Geom Ujin’s Great Demon Wall.

The evil spirits that collided with the Great Demon Wall began to vanish. Just as Geom Mugeuk had escaped the Human Annihilation Form that had been sent at him through the Great Demon Wall, the evil spirits of the Great Annihilation Form likewise could not break through it.

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang looked back. Geom Mugeuk was nowhere to be seen, and Geom Ujin was looking at them, lowering his head slightly. It was an expression of gratitude for their having tried to protect him instead of avoiding danger.

The two Alliance Leaders also expressed their gratitude to him.

For an instant, this thought occurred to them. What if that Great Demon Wall had been erected behind them?

The Great Annihilation Form in front, and the Great Demon Wall behind.

No, there was something even more horrifying than that.

What if Geom Ujin had unleashed the Great Annihilation Form from behind?

Could they have blocked that Great Annihilation Form rushing in from both front and back? It would have been impossible. They would have died between the evil spirits on both sides. If Geom Ujin had betrayed them, that is.

But Geom Ujin had raised the Great Demon Wall in front of them.

They were grateful for this situation, but they could not be purely happy about it. Not when they thought of what it would be like if such a man became their enemy.

Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul did not waste any time and immediately unleashed the ninth form, Demon Soul Supreme.

Even if Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul had mastered up to the eighth form, it would not have mastered the ninth.

“It ends now!”

The pitch-black eyes of Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul began to shine pure white.

Everyone could feel it. No matter what kind of attack emerged, if that attack was allowed to land, Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul would be extinguished.

Geom Ujin spoke in a low voice, but more urgently than ever before.

“Son, you must come out now!”

Flash.

Light burst forth from the pure-white eyes of Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

It was the final technique for the Heavenly Demon Soul, which annihilated anything it gazed upon—Demon Soul Supreme.

The next moment.

Geom Ujin looked around. In an empty space where there was nothing, only Geom Mugeuk stood alone.

“Father.”

Geom Ujin realized his son had brought him into the Time-Space Transposition Art.

“I have not yet perfectly comprehended the ninth form.”

Yes, no matter how exceptional his son was, it was impossible to master it within this amount of time.

What was regrettable was this.

“Unfortunately, your Heavenly Demon Soul has been extinguished.”

Geom Ujin felt more pained than anyone. He should have stopped him instead. This was not his son’s mistake, but his own.

It was the first time Geom Mugeuk had seen his father look so regretful. In this final battle, he kept seeing expressions from his father that he had never seen before.

Geom Mugeuk conveyed unexpected words.

“There is still a chance.”

When Geom Ujin looked at his son with an expression asking what he meant.

“Look over there.”

Snap.

When Geom Mugeuk flicked his finger, the scene outside the Time-Space Transposition Art became visible. At the sight, Geom Ujin was startled.

By all rights, his son’s Heavenly Demon Soul should already have melted away under Demon Soul Supreme.

But the light flying from the Heavenly Demon Soul’s eyes had stopped in midair. It had not stopped completely. It was moving ever so faintly.

The others as well stood as if time had stopped, but they too were moving ever so faintly.

Regrettably, Geom Mugeuk had not fully comprehended the mnemonic formula of the ninth form within the allotted time.

But perhaps because he had cherished time with such desperate earnestness, he had gained another enlightenment.

“I have just achieved great completion of the Sicheon Secret Art.”

Chapter 834: How Have You Endured It Until Now?
-----------------------------------------------
“The Sicheon Secret Art?”

Geom Ujin knew nothing about the Sicheon Secret Art.

Geom Mugeuk quickly explained it.

“It is a secret art that can only be used within the Space-Time Transposition Art. When the Sicheon Secret Art is activated, one can gain more time here than passes outside. I just achieved its Great Mastery.”

Geom Ujin looked outside with a startled expression. Time truly seemed to have stopped, flowing only by the faintest fraction.

“I learned this from Cult Leader Pungcheon as well.”

Geom Ujin could now understand clearly why Cult Leader Pungcheon had kept lamenting that his divine treasure had been stolen. If he had taught even such a wondrous secret art, he had every right to say such things.

“Can Cult Leader Pungcheon use this secret art too?”

Geom Mugeuk shook his head. Cult Leader Pungcheon had only known the incantation; he had not been able to unfold the secret art.

“I am probably the first to have reached this realm.”

Geom Mugeuk could feel it.

Time was flowing like that because he had only just achieved Great Mastery. If that mastery matured further, or if he achieved Twelve-Star Great Mastery, perhaps time might stop altogether.

The reason he thought so was because of this.

The moment he achieved Great Mastery, Geom Mugeuk knew. From now on, even his own time would no longer flow in this place.

Originally, if he gained, say, nine times more time, his body would age by that much as well. Of course, until now, he had been so young that it had been fine.

Catching Hwa Mugi was so important that he had willingly accepted the side effects of the Sicheon Secret Art.

But the moment he achieved Great Mastery, even the time within the Sicheon Secret Art stopped. His body had stopped aging.

Geom Mugeuk could now possess perfect time of his own, as a bonus. This was like a gift bestowed by the heavens.

He respected and loved his father beyond measure, but he had no intention of telling him about this Sicheon Secret Art.

It was because of a worry he had long held. He feared that his father would become so absorbed in martial arts that he would never come out of the Sicheon Secret Art.

That thought had not changed even now. His father truly was someone who might pour everything he had toward the very end of martial learning.

Moreover, he might waste an immense amount of time to achieve Great Mastery of the Sicheon Secret Art, and unlike his young self, his father was in a different position.

Even in Geom Mugeuk’s own case, he had no idea how he had achieved Great Mastery this time.

He could only say that tremendous luck had followed him. He believed it was the result brought about by his desperate wish for time to pass slowly so that he could learn the ninth form.

Father, forgive me. I can do anything else for you, but not this.

“Father, right now there is something more important than the Sicheon Secret Art.”

Geom Ujin nodded. The time outside had not completely stopped.

“Hurry and ask about the parts you did not understand.”

Geom Mugeuk once again posed questions to his father. He asked about the parts of the incantation of the ninth form that he had been unable to understand no matter how much he pondered them alone.

“When sending true qi from the Yangji acupoint to the Yanggu acupoint…”

***

“No!”

The one who shouted the moment light burst from the eyes of Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul was the Sword King.

In that instant, the Sword King thought of Geom Mugeuk. He had sensed that the way Geom Mugeuk looked at the Heavenly Demon Soul was not merely as a part of martial arts.

If he were to lose that Heavenly Demon Soul, which he regarded like a friend, forever?

Geom Mugeuk would probably live the rest of his life blaming himself. Of course, given Geom Mugeuk’s personality, he would make even more deliberate jokes about the Heavenly Demon Soul and try somehow to get over it.

But it would remain a wound he would carry until the day he died.

If he could, the Sword King wanted to block it in its place, but in the very instant it flashed, the light was already crashing down upon the Heavenly Demon Soul’s face.

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s face, struck by the light, shone brilliantly. It shone so brightly that its face could not be seen.

Its face must have been melting under an energy as swift as light and as powerful as lava.

All the others watching also let out sighs of lament. Geom Mugeuk would lose his Heavenly Demon Soul, and the opposing Heavenly Demon Soul would become far stronger.

Just as everyone watched the Heavenly Demon Soul being extinguished with sorrow in their hearts—

The brilliant light on its face slowly faded, and the Heavenly Demon Soul’s face began to appear.

‘It didn’t melt!’

What astonished those watching was not only that its face had not melted.

Light was also bursting from its eyes.

Amazingly, Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul had also unleashed the ninth form of the Guhwa Demonic Art, Demon Soul Supreme.

Ziiiiing.

The light emitted by Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul began to push back the incoming light. Though it was the same light, the light of Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul was tinged with blue. Because of that, one could clearly see the other light being driven back.

Demon Soul Supreme against Demon Soul Supreme.

With the final move that would melt whatever it touched, the two Heavenly Demon Souls were locked in a fierce contest of strength.

Amazingly, Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul was not being pushed back. Even though it had unleashed Demon Soul Supreme belatedly, at the very moment the light had nearly touched its face, it was driving back the incoming radiance.

Not only the Sword King, but everyone watching was astonished.

What on earth had happened?

Geom Mugeuk’s cheer answered that question in their place.

“Hold on!”

Geom Mugeuk and Geom Ujin, who had been inside the Space-Time Transposition Art, were already outside.

Just before the light emitted by Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul struck his own Heavenly Demon Soul, Geom Mugeuk had perfectly comprehended the ninth form.

Boom!

The light that had been locked in a tense standoff midway between the two Heavenly Demon Souls vanished with a thunderous roar.

It had been a Demon Soul Supreme unleashed late, yet it had blocked the attack without suffering any damage. Once Geom Mugeuk mastered even the final ninth form, his Heavenly Demon Soul became even stronger.

Geom Mugeuk looked at his Heavenly Demon Soul.

“What a relief.”

It had truly escaped the crisis of annihilation in dramatic fashion.

The Heavenly Demon Soul glanced briefly at Geom Mugeuk. In those pitch-black eyes, Geom Mugeuk sensed one emotion.

The Heavenly Demon Soul was looking at him with gratitude.

Yes, the Heavenly Demon Soul had possessed emotions from the beginning. One could tell just by looking at Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul. It was just that, in ordinary cases, it never revealed those emotions.

Geom Mugeuk sent a sound transmission to his father.

—You saved my Heavenly Demon Soul. Thank you, Father.

Yes, it was thanks to him. Who else could have transmitted the ninth form of the Guhwa Demonic Art in such a short time?

But Geom Ujin knew. The only person in this murim who could allow him to take credit for it was also his son. No matter how much one taught, one had to be capable of understanding it in order to learn.

Jin Paecheon, Baek Jagang, and everyone else watched the situation with startled expressions.

They could all guess. What Geom Ujin had transmitted to Geom Mugeuk was the technique that the Heavenly Demon Soul had just displayed.

‘He transmitted the Guhwa Demonic Art, and a technique used by the Heavenly Demon Soul at that, right here on the spot?’

It was truly something they could not believe even after seeing it. Even they, who had witnessed it directly, found it hard to believe, so it was something no one would accept if told.

Jin Paecheon felt unease once again. Throughout his time here, he had been as conscious of Geom Ujin as he was of the enemy. The Demonic Cult Leader, who had erected the Great Demonic Wall for them, had transmitted the Guhwa Demonic Art in the middle of this chaos.

No matter how much Geom Mugeuk lowered his anxiety, Geom Ujin returned it to its original place. He knew that Geom Ujin dreamed of unifying the murim.

Baek Jagang, on the other hand, was thinking differently from him. In any case, this unease was not something that would be resolved by worrying over it.

Right now, he felt a fighting spirit that made him want to clash properly once.

The target was none other than the Heavenly Demon Soul. If he were to fight that Heavenly Demon Soul, how should he do it? Could he truly avoid that gaze from moments ago?

The Guhwa Demonic Art and the Heavenly Demon Soul were provoking Baek Jagang not as the Alliance Leader, but as a martial artist.

Of course, the one most astonished by the current situation was Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

Now he could understand clearly why that Heavenly Demon Soul before him was so special.

Because it was a Heavenly Demon Soul created from such a special Young Cult Leader.

“The more I see you, the more remarkable you are!”

It was astonishing enough that it had descended before him, but for a Heavenly Demon Soul that had only recently descended into the world to be this strong?

If he absorbed the power of that Heavenly Demon Soul?

He thought he might become the strongest Heavenly Demon Soul in history.

With that power, he might even be able to absorb him as well. If that happened…

An unknowable desire, along with demonic energy, sliced through the air.

Boom!

Once again, the two Heavenly Demon Souls collided.

Demonic energy more powerful than before swept through the surroundings. Those watching retreated several more steps.

Because an urgent battle had begun again between the Heavenly Demon Souls, Geom Mugeuk had no leisure to verbally express his gratitude to Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang. He merely bowed his head slightly to show his thanks.

But even in this urgent situation, Cult Leader Pungcheon sent him a sound transmission to confirm one thing.

—Just now, you used the Sicheon Secret Art, didn’t you?

—How did you know?

Although he had received the Sicheon Secret Art from Cult Leader Pungcheon, he had never told him about his accomplishments in it.

Perhaps seeing him enter and exit the Space-Time Transposition Art had made him think of the Sicheon Secret Art.

—I thought you would be able to do it.

—Thank you for believing in me.

Geom Mugeuk recalled the time when he first drew Cult Leader Pungcheon over to his side.

‘Strategist Go, your judgment was right.’

After becoming an ally, Cult Leader Pungcheon had been of enormous help to him. Even in this final battle.

Kwaang! Bang! Kwakwakwakwang!

Every time the two Heavenly Demon Souls moved, space warped, and the ground shattered and flew upward. Each time they clashed, the shockwave spread in all directions, sweeping away nearby buildings.

As if it had been newly born along with the final incantation, Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul was not being pushed back by Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul. No, it was gradually overwhelming Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

In strength, in speed, in demonic energy, and in momentum as well.

Kwaaang!

After being struck by a powerful blow and sent flying backward, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul wore an expression of disbelief.

‘That Young Cult Leader’s understanding of the Guhwa Demonic Art is deeper than the Cult Leader’s.’

How could that be? It was unbelievable. No matter how outstanding his talent, he was still so terribly young, and on top of that, he had only just comprehended the final ninth form.

The next moment, Hwa Mugi’s pitch-black eyes returned to normal. He had returned to being Hwa Mugi again.

“Truly remarkable.”

Naturally, Hwa Mugi had been watching this battle. The Heavenly Demon Soul had been too preoccupied fighting the Heavenly Demon Soul, but Hwa Mugi had been paying attention to Geom Mugeuk.

“I thought this Heavenly Demon Soul would be enough.”

It was no ordinary Heavenly Demon Soul. It was a Heavenly Demon Soul that had existed for three hundred years, borrowing the body of the Heavenly Slaughter Star.

And it was even a Heavenly Demon Soul that used not only the techniques unique to a Heavenly Demon Soul, but all the other techniques of the Guhwa Demonic Art as well.

“And yet it is being pushed back?”

“It takes after me, so when it comes to fighting, it’s unmatched.”

Hwa Mugi’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

“It does seem to have inherited your stubbornness.”

“Then please persuade it to go back in.”

Hwa Mugi said coldly,

“There is no need. I will make it vanish forever.”

His voice was filled with confidence.

His eyes turned black again, changing into the Heavenly Demon Soul.

It had already been pushed back by his own Heavenly Demon Soul, yet he was calling out the Heavenly Demon Soul again?

Geom Mugeuk could not understand.

But the next moment, he understood the reason. The reason it had briefly returned to Hwa Mugi before going back.

Sssssk.

Behind Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul, something enormous rose to its feet.

That thing, appearing with powerful demonic energy and an overwhelming presence, was a Heavenly Demon Soul.

Not the small-bodied one, but a gigantic, true Heavenly Demon Soul.

It was not that Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul had grown larger. Amazingly, a new Heavenly Demon Soul had descended there.

“Don’t tell me?”

The moment he saw it, Geom Mugeuk understood.

From the beginning until now, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul had been trapped together with the other souls, and Hwa Mugi, who had inherited the Great Mastery of the Guhwa Demonic Art, had given rise to a new Heavenly Demon Soul.

The Heavenly Demon Soul that had just descended was Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

Everyone looked up at Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul in astonishment. It was completely different from the small-bodied Heavenly Demon Soul. Its overwhelming size was like that of a colossal stone statue, and the black sword at its waist was so huge it looked capable of smashing dozens of buildings to pieces with a single stroke.

The Heavenly Demon Soul looked down at everyone with arrogant eyes. Within its gaze was a unique aura where demonic energy and killing intent mingled. The presence and pressure emanating from it were immense, yet also singular.

The Heavenly Demon Soul of the Heavenly Slaughter Star.

The first Heavenly Demon Soul in the history of the murim had revealed itself.

The one who broke the silence was Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

“Your Heavenly Demon Soul was originally an existence that should never have been able to appear. It must have known that there was not only me, but another Heavenly Demon Soul inside this body.”

Geomugeuk looked at his own Heavenly Demon Soul.

“Did you know from the beginning? That there were two Heavenly Demon Souls over there?”

His own Heavenly Demon Soul still gave no answer. It merely glanced at Geomugeuk.

If that was so, it meant that from the very start, it had come out prepared to be annihilated no matter what.

That was why it had refused to go back in, no matter how many times he ordered it to hurry back since there were many other masters here.

It had not gone back because it knew there were two Heavenly Demon Souls on the other side.

“No matter how special your Heavenly Demon Soul may be, it cannot defeat the two of us.”

Swaaaaaaaaaang!

Hwa Mugi’s enormous Heavenly Demon Soul brought its sword down toward Geomugeuk.

At the same time, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul rushed in.

Even under the attacks of the two Heavenly Demon Souls, Geomugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul did not dodge.

Even if it had to sacrifice itself, it intended to protect Geomugeuk. It had overcome the first crisis, but now the danger of annihilation had come again.

“No! Go back!”

Geomugeuk shouted, but the Heavenly Demon Soul did not dodge.

Left with no choice, Geomugeuk tried to take the Heavenly Demon Soul with him into the Space-Time Transposition Art.

But that was not the best course. There were others left behind, and this fight could not be ended by merely avoiding it for a moment.

Right then!

Thuuung.

The enormous sword descending from midair was blocked halfway.

Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul, which had been charging in, stopped in place and stared upward with a startled expression.

Geomugeuk also looked up, and everyone watching looked up as well.

Another enormous sword was blocking the incoming blade.

It was a newly appeared Heavenly Demon Soul.

It was the moment a fourth Heavenly Demon Soul appeared here. Truly, it was also the moment when the greatest number of Heavenly Demon Souls in the history of the murim had appeared in one place.

The final Heavenly Demon Soul was special.

Just as Geomugeuk’s evil spirit had differed in appearance and aura between the time it was at the ninth star and when it achieved great completion, the Heavenly Demon Soul that had appeared this time differed in appearance from the existing Heavenly Demon Souls.

If anything, its body was a little smaller, but its presence was even greater.

The flame burning in its chest was larger and more vivid. And on the back of its right hand, the symbol of the Heavenly Demon Soul was engraved.

The color of its body was different, and each part of its body differed slightly. Somehow, its overall appearance was refined.

But its face and gaze were so fearsome and intense that they overwhelmed any Heavenly Demon Soul here.

Looking up at that figure, Geomugeuk said in admiration,

“How did you endure until now when you must have wanted so badly to show it off?”

It was the moment when Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul, having achieved the great completion of the twelve stars, descended.

Chapter 835: Too Many Heavenly Demon Souls
------------------------------------------
It was the first time he had seen his father’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

“Magnificent!”

That was Geom Mugeuk’s honest impression. He wanted to say the same to his own Heavenly Demon Soul. You have to grow to be that magnificent too!

Kiiiiing.

The swords of the two enormous Heavenly Demon Souls pushed against each other, letting out a metallic shriek like a thunderous roar.

Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul radiated an aura in which killing intent and demonic energy were mingled, while his father’s Heavenly Demon Soul emitted only pure demonic energy.

It was a situation that seemed ready to erupt into a bloody battle at any moment.

“Wait!”

Geom Mugeuk spoke to Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

“Just a moment before you fight! This is a moment that will never come again in the history of murim, so just a moment!”

Of course, those words were meant for Hwa Mugi inside its body. He would be the only one capable of giving orders to that Heavenly Demon Soul.

When he accepted Geom Mugeuk’s words, the two Heavenly Demon Souls remained standing as they were, swords locked against swords.

Geom Mugeuk looked again at his father’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

“So you’re the completed form!”

One thought naturally arose in his mind. He wondered how his own Heavenly Demon Soul’s appearance would change once he achieved Great Completion of the Twelfth Stage.

Jin Paecheon sent Baek Jagang a voice transmission.

—Cult Leader Geom has achieved Great Completion of the Twelfth Stage of the Guhwa Demon Art.

He could confirm it through the Heavenly Demon Soul’s different appearance.

Baek Jagang’s gaze, which had been fixed on the Heavenly Demon Soul, turned toward Jin Paecheon.

—We should go stand beside that Heavenly Killing Star.

To Jin Paecheon, Baek Jagang’s joke did not sound like mere joking.

Even so, he thought he had done well to come here this time.

Although there had been moments when his life had been in danger, and although he had been made to feel such helplessness, he had come to know exactly what level of skill Geom Ujin possessed.

Even to the point of forgetting that an even greater danger now stood before him, Jin Paecheon was still most concerned about Geom Ujin.

This time, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul. That thing, too, was no ordinary Heavenly Demon Soul.

The killing intent mingled with its demonic energy was, without question, incomparable to any killing intent he had ever experienced.

If an ordinary expert were exposed to that killing intent, they would try to kill whoever was beside them, and if there was no one to kill, it was powerful enough that they would harm themselves.

Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul asked with an expression of incomprehension.

“How has yet another Heavenly Demon Soul descended?”

Since he himself was present, it was entirely possible for Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul to descend.

He was borrowing the body of the Heavenly Killing Star, and the two Heavenly Demon Souls were manifesting from a single body.

He could even understand that Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul had descended.

The human called Geom Mugeuk was exceptional beyond measure.

Like master, like Heavenly Demon Soul; he could accept that there might be at least one Heavenly Demon Soul in this world prepared to emerge even at the risk of annihilation.

His gaze turned to the final Heavenly Demon Soul, the one he could not understand no matter what.

It was Geom Mugeuk who answered that question.

“No, I know the main character appears at the end, but if you could call it forth like this, you should have done so sooner.”

From his father came the answer Geom Mugeuk had expected.

“Is this not your fight?”

There was that reason as well, but in truth, there was another reason. And Geom Ujin revealed that true reason.

“I called it forth earlier too.”

“What?”

Not only Geom Mugeuk, but everyone present was shocked.

Then came the astonishing truth.

“The Heavenly Demon Soul refused to descend.”

If it had been earlier, then it had been when he fought Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

Geom Mugeuk could not understand it.

That Heavenly Demon Soul of his father’s did not seem like it would have been frightened by Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

Nor did it seem likely that it would have shown respect simply because the other was a Heavenly Demon Soul of a former Heavenly Demon from three hundred years ago—at least, not if it was his father’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

“It did not regard the opponent as a true Heavenly Demon Soul.”

Had it seen it as a half-formed Heavenly Demon Soul lodged within a human body? Certainly, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul was not a normal Heavenly Demon Soul.

Strictly speaking, Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul was the same. It had not been a proper succession; rather, the body of the Heavenly Killing Star had absorbed the Guhwa Demon Art.

But his father’s thoughts were different.

“I do not believe that was why it refused.”

“Then why was it?”

Geom Ujin’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

Geom Mugeuk flinched in surprise.

“Because of my Heavenly Demon Soul?”

It could not have been because his own Heavenly Demon Soul was stronger.

“Out of respect for your Heavenly Demon Soul.”

“!”

Because, by nature, a Heavenly Demon Soul was not a being that received help from another. It had done so to preserve that pride.

Or perhaps his own Heavenly Demon Soul had conveyed its will. Leave this fight to me. He did not know if such a thing was possible.

“Then why has it come out now?”

At that, an even more surprising statement followed.

“I asked it.”

His father had not issued an order to the Heavenly Demon Soul. He had asked it.

“It was the first request I made after becoming the Cult Leader.”

Perhaps it might have been the first request his father had made in his entire life. He was not the sort of man who asked anyone for anything.

The Heavenly Demon Soul had accepted his father’s first request.

He had said he had never spoken with the Heavenly Demon Soul, but surely it had watched over his father for many long years.

And so, more than anyone, it must have understood just how great a meaning his father’s request carried.

“I cannot quite imagine you asking the Heavenly Demon Soul for something, Father.”

In truth, what moved Geom Mugeuk even more was not simply that his father had made a request.

What mattered was that he had told him that fact.

If you do not say it, no one will know—this was the moment his father was putting into practice what Geom Mugeuk had said to him again and again. And in front of these people, no less.

“Thank you, Father.”

After bowing respectfully to his father, Geom Mugeuk turned his gaze toward the Heavenly Demon Souls facing one another.

“There are too many Heavenly Demon Souls here!”

At those words, the four Heavenly Demon Souls moved at once.

The one that charged forward fastest among them was Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

Kaaang! Kang!

As it had shown in the earlier battle, his Heavenly Demon Soul overwhelmed Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul from the very first exchange.

It pressed forward, overwhelming it in strength, demonic energy, and speed. And then—

As Geom Mugeuk watched it drive the other back, he felt a strange sense of incongruity.

“Wait! Fall back!”

Geom Mugeuk called his Heavenly Demon Soul back. Although it held the advantage, the Heavenly Demon Soul retreated according to Geom Mugeuk’s command.

“I know how badly you want to fight. But something is strange.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul, which had been pushed far back in the first exchange.

“It fought you earlier too and was clearly overpowered. So why is it trying to fight again?”

At this rate, that Heavenly Demon Soul would be annihilated, and its power would be absorbed by you.

Of course, it was possible that Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul was being stubborn out of pride.

But Hwa Mugi was inside that body. There was no way he would so meekly give up Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

‘He was definitely trying to scheme something.’

That was the conclusion Geom Mugeuk reached.

As always, carelessness came when fear had lessened. Just like now, when his father’s Heavenly Demon Soul had appeared.

He must not forget. The master of that body was Hwa Mugi. A man born with a fate of slaughter, one for whom it would not be strange to kill every person in the world.

Meanwhile, the other two Heavenly Demon Souls were also clashing.

As mountain pressed against mountain, the earth screamed.

Kwadududuk.

When the two Heavenly Demon Souls exerted their strength, the ground beneath their feet shattered and caved in.

And the two Heavenly Demon Souls showed that the notion that a larger body meant slower movements was a mistaken prejudice.

Swaaaaaak.

A sword thrust swiftly toward its opponent was avoided by twisting the body. Even as it dodged the incoming attack, it counterattacked at the same time.

The battle between these two was different from the fight between the previous two Heavenly Demon Souls.

If the clash between Geom Mugeuk’s and Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Souls had been a battle of momentum and demonic energy, the current battle was far more refined.

The Heavenly Demon Soul of the Heavenly Killing Star and the Heavenly Demon Soul of the Great Completion of the Twelfth Stage of the Guhwa Demon Art.

They fought like people. The swords they swung were swift and precise, aiming for the opponent’s vital points.

Because such colossal beings moved in such a manner, the amazement of those watching was even greater.

Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul, too, watched their fight without missing a single moment. Geom Mugeuk could tell. This battle was affecting his own Heavenly Demon Soul.

‘It is learning to fight.’

Boom! Kwaang!

The sounds and sparks created by the collision of the enormous swords were like fireworks exploding in the sky.

‘This fight has to be decided quickly.’

Calling forth a Heavenly Demon Soul could be done even without internal energy, but once the Heavenly Demon Soul began fighting, it consumed an enormous amount of internal energy. Even now, his father and Hwa Mugi were expending tremendous amounts of it.

Chaeaaang.

After meeting several attacks head-on with its sword, Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul this time deflected the attack at an angle.

A single small opening formed as a result.

Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul did not miss it.

Shiiiiik!

With its shoulder as massive as a castle wall, it struck the opponent’s chest.

Thuuung.

When the shoulder collided with the chest, it was as if a low, deep bell rang out.

Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul staggered, but it did not fall.

Not letting that opportunity slip by, Geom Ujin’s sword flew in and this time cut into its side.

Paaak!

From the wound cut by the sword, black demonic energy flowed instead of blood.

It was the first time Geom Mugeuk had seen it. When a Heavenly Demon Soul was cut, it bled demonic energy like blood.

Just as it was about to charge in without missing the opportunity—

Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul turned around with lightning speed.

Shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk!

Before anyone knew it, the Seventh Form, Demon Soul Genesis, unleashed by Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul was unfolding behind its back.

Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul did not strike down the dozens of blades of force pouring in from behind on the spot.

If it did, it would allow an attack from Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul behind it. It was an exceptionally clever move, using itself as a shield while targeting the rear.

Whoosh.

Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul used the momentum of its turn to rotate once more and move sideways. Since it had intended to move that way from the beginning, it was able to take position in time.

Boom boom boom boom boom boom boom!

Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul swung its sword and struck down the surging force.

There was no attack coming from behind. Because it had shifted to the side, Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul also had to block its own attack pouring down upon it.

The moment it struck down the incoming assault—

They again sent their swords flying toward one another. As demonic energy and killing intent coiled around each other, the two Heavenly Demon Souls clashed once more.

Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul had displayed one threatening move, but its opponent was his father’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

No matter how great Hwa Mugi was, how could he match the depth with which his father understood the Guhwa Demon Art?

There existed a gap that even that profound internal energy and tremendous killing intent could not overcome.

Paaak!

Once again, the thigh of Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul was cut. Demonic energy flowed from the wound.

Thud!

This time, a fist slammed into its face.

Hwa Mugi could not have imagined it. That his father had achieved Great Completion of the Twelfth Stage of the Guhwa Demon Art. Not only had the Heavenly Demon Cult Leader Hyeok Docheon of three hundred years ago failed to reach it, but Cult Leaders who had attained the Twelfth Stage could likely be counted on one hand in the entire history of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Of course, that did not mean Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul was simply taking the beating.

Thwack!

Just as it slid backward and distance opened between them—

The two eyes of Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul turned pure white.

The Ninth Form of the Guhwa Demon Art, Demon Soul Supreme.

The instant its eyes flashed, light burst forth from them.

It was not trying to kill Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul with this. Since the opponent would also block with the Ninth Form, it intended to lead this fight into a contest of internal energy.

But Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul responded in an unexpected way.

Shiiiiik.

Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul ran, avoiding the light of force.

No matter how fast it was, how could it possibly evade Demon Soul Supreme?

Jiiiiiiing.

Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul ran, avoiding that light as it chased after it, cutting through the world. Astonishingly, what it unleashed was—

The Eighth Form of the Guhwa Demon Art, Demon Soul Rush.

Normally, it would not have been able to shake off that gaze.

But Demon Soul Rush at the Twelfth Stage narrowly avoided the Demon Soul Supreme of Great Completion.

Shiiiiik.

It seemed to draw a circle as it made one lap around Hwa Mugi.

Then, as if declaring, Here it is! upon reaching a certain point, its huge body soared upward with a whoosh.

The light extending from those two eyes split the sky.

As if even that had been anticipated, Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul twisted its body in midair to avoid it, then dropped straight down and struck with its sword. It was truly a move perfectly designed from beginning to end.

Swaaaaaak!

Puaaaak!

The sword of Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul split the opponent’s shoulder and carved all the way down to its chest.

From the cleaved wound, demonic energy gushed forth like a waterfall, and a deep, desperate scream like something heard from hell burst out.

Chwaaaaak!

The sword that had cut down from the shoulder descended all the way to the Heavenly Demon Soul mark engraved over the heart.

At the very moment when severing even that would have ended everything,

the Heavenly Demon Soul’s sword stopped.

At first, he thought Hwa Mugi had used some kind of trick. But that was not it.

Geom Woojin’s Heavenly Demon Soul had stopped of its own accord.

Shuuuuuk.

Geom Woojin’s Heavenly Demon Soul pulled out the sword that had been lodged there.

Paaaaak.

Demonic energy surged like blood, but not enough to annihilate him.

Geom Woojin’s Heavenly Demon Soul returned the sword to its sheath.

Clack.

Those watching could tell. Geom Woojin’s Heavenly Demon Soul had no intention of completely annihilating its opponent and absorbing its power.

The moment Geom Mugeuk saw that, someone seemed to overlap atop the Heavenly Demon Soul. It was his father.

‘So the Heavenly Demon Soul resembles Father.’

Because he was someone who would never choose to grow stronger by sucking away an opponent’s power.

It was the moment he confirmed for certain that a Heavenly Demon Soul was born based on the nature of its master.

Then what about my Heavenly Demon Soul? It only needs to take the first bite!

A change occurred in Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

Sssssss.

The killing intent began to vanish from eyes where killing intent and demonic energy had been tangled together, until only pure demonic energy remained.

Astonishingly, what filled those eyes brimming with demonic energy was gratitude.

Geom Mugeuk learned something new about Heavenly Demon Souls. If Hwa Mugi died, his Heavenly Demon Soul would disappear as well, but that death would be of a different dimension from having its power stolen by another Heavenly Demon Soul and being annihilated. The difference between vanishing forever and not.

The two Heavenly Demon Souls looked at each other with indifferent eyes, as if they had never fought so fiercely. Though defeated, it showed neither shame nor anger.

After sending that blunt gaze for a while, Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul silently disappeared.

At least one thing was certain. From now on, it would never descend in front of Geom Woojin.

Once it disappeared, as if its own role had ended there as well, Geom Woojin’s Heavenly Demon Soul looked once at the master it served, then disappeared too.

Whatever it was thinking, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul merely watched the scene in silence.

Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul was the same.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk jabbed his Heavenly Demon Soul in the side with his elbow and said,

“Don’t get moved by that.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul slowly turned its head toward Geom Mugeuk.

“If we’re in the same situation, you must absorb the power no matter what. Coolness lasts only a moment, but strength is eternal! You know that, right? That your master is the incarnation of greed.”

At the look in its eyes, as if it could not tell whether he was joking or serious, Geom Mugeuk nodded and said,

“We’re still at the stage where we need to get stronger.”

Chapter 836: I Haven't Lived the Life of a Protagonist
------------------------------------------------------
Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul felt a tangle of emotions.

The original plan had been to absorb Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Soul, then use that strengthened power to absorb Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

If he succeeded in absorbing the power of two Heavenly Demon Souls?

Might he not be able to return to his original Heavenly Demon Soul?

He wanted a body. A body of his own.

He was supposed to offer eternal loyalty to Hyeok Docheon, who had given birth to him, but after three hundred years, even that loyalty had faded.

After being together with them, he was a Heavenly Demon Soul that had become half-human. That was why he did not mind becoming Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul. As long as he could obtain a body, it did not matter whose Heavenly Demon Soul he became.

That was what he had been thinking—until he saw Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul refrain from annihilating Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul.

If a Heavenly Demon Soul lost to another and was extinguished, it would vanish forever; but if it disappeared because its master died, it might one day meet another master.

Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul had not stolen that chance simply to make himself stronger.

At that moment, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul remembered. He remembered what sort of being he had once been.

Yes. He had been such a proud, aloof being, overflowing with self-respect.

An absolute existence before whom all demons looked up in awe and trembled in fear, without ever coveting another’s power.

By contrast, now he was filled with all manner of stray thoughts, reduced to a truly vulgar Heavenly Demon Soul—and the realization filled him with despair.

Then a voice resounded in his heart.

—So you are wavering too.

It was Hwa Mugi’s voice.

Whoosh.

The instant he heard those words, the soul of the Heavenly Demon Soul was sucked into the darkness in a flash, as though plunging off a cliff.

The black pupils vanished, and Hwa Mugi returned once more.

Even though his own Heavenly Demon Soul had been defeated, Hwa Mugi was not enraged, nor was he shaken.

“You people even shake the souls of Heavenly Demon Souls.”

Until he had seen the other souls inside him waver and collapse, he had thought such a thing might be possible. They had been trapped in his body for far too long; the fact that they had not gone mad was fortunate enough.

It was not because they had been masters that they had endured. They had endured that long span of years because they had been together.

It was understandable that they, being human, would waver, but he had never imagined that even a Heavenly Demon Soul would be shaken by these people. Not only his own Heavenly Demon Soul, but even Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul had been shaken.

The question truly came out of him on its own.

“What in the world are you people?”

At that, Geom Mugeuk looked back at the people lined up behind him. Though a long battle was unfolding, they had been silently watching it through to the end.

“People who gathered because of you. If not for you.”

If Hwa Mugi had never existed in the first place? If, because of that, he had never regressed? What sort of life would he be living now?

His elder brother would have become Young Cult Leader, and perhaps he himself might already be dead. Most likely, that was how it would have turned out.

Even if he had been lucky enough to survive, his relationships with his father and the Demon Venerables would have been a mess. As for those two Alliance Leaders, they would have been people he could scarcely meet face to face. Would he have even met the Sword King?

“Rather than because of you, I suppose I should say thanks to you.”

Because of you, my whole life was painful; because of you, my life now is so very happy.

That was what Hwa Mugi was to him.

Hwa Mugi sensed the deep, complex emotions contained in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes. Why was he looking at him with such eyes? That Young Cult Leader had been like that from the very beginning.

Hwa Mugi stared quietly at that gaze he could not understand in the slightest, then suddenly said,

“I regret it.”

Hwa Mugi was not speaking idly; he regretted it sincerely.

“I should have killed them all one by one, then killed you last.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at him and smiled an inscrutable smile.

Hwa Mugi, you already did something similar once.

You killed the Alliance Leaders, killed my father, and tried to kill me as well. You have already lived such a life once.

Then came Hwa Mugi’s honest self-reproach.

“I was arrogant.”

In truth, he had every reason to be arrogant. That tremendous martial prowess, that internal energy beyond imagination, and on top of that, protective gangqi that could replenish his internal energy, as well as two Heavenly Demon Souls.

How could he possibly have imagined that he would lose?

Hwa Mugi’s self-reproach continued. Even if he had come this far in arrogance, this was what he should have done.

“I should have driven them madly into a corner and finished them all off before you arrived.”

From the moment Geom Mugeuk arrived, the situation had gone completely awry. He had even been dragged into eating a meal. He did not want to admit it, but in the end, it had not only been the souls who were swayed by this Young Cult Leader.

Regret and self-reproach.

Hwa Mugi was acting like a man who had already lost.

But Geom Mugeuk did not trust that sight. Earlier, when his Heavenly Demon Soul had attacked Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul, Hwa Mugi had tried to set some sort of trap, though he did not know what.

‘He has not given up yet.’

If anything, he was in an even more dangerous state. He was inducing complacency on this side while plotting something.

Originally, he had intended to do this.

Father, let us fight together as father and son.

If not for an opportunity like this, when would he ever fight alongside his father? He had intended to eliminate Hwa Mugi for certain with his father.

But Geom Mugeuk’s instincts told him this.

—Fight alone.

Geom Mugeuk followed the will of the instincts that had protected him for so long.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Hwa Mugi and spoke with ease.

“Don’t blame yourself. It’s not because you were arrogant, but because I lived too diligently.”

Though outwardly he pretended to be off guard, Geom Mugeuk was never careless.

Geom Mugeuk drew the Black Demon Sword.

Before the fight, Geom Mugeuk looked at his Heavenly Demon Soul. It had not yet disappeared and remained there.

With pitch-black eyes, it was staring at Hwa Mugi. Geom Mugeuk could feel the Heavenly Demon Soul’s heart.

“I know you want to fight to the end.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul still only looked straight ahead. It likely wanted to eliminate that Hwa Mugi itself. Perhaps the Heavenly Demon Soul could feel that he was the fated mortal enemy of its master.

With everyone’s eyes focused on him, Geom Mugeuk calmly spoke to the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“I have not lived my life trying to become the protagonist.”

At those unexpected words, the Heavenly Demon Soul turned its head slightly and looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Standing behind him, Cult Leader Pungcheon finally could not hold back and said, “That’s absurd!” while the Sword King beside him nodded in agreement with Cult Leader Pungcheon’s words.

But when they thought about it, everyone knew there was no one who had lived for others rather than himself as much as Geom Mugeuk had.

Because whenever one met Geom Mugeuk, one felt as if one had become the protagonist. He made them realize what kind of person they were and what they loved.

That was why, even if they were not the protagonist but supporting characters, even mere extras, he made them willing to stand here.

“If you are to live on as my Heavenly Demon Soul from now on, you must grow used to a life that is not the protagonist’s. There will be many times when you must step back and watch, endure, and yield.

There will likely be many things that do not suit your nature. Even so, I will not live as the protagonist of this world. From now on, we will not be standing in places that decide the fate of the murim; we will be lying in a wagon, looking up at the sky, wandering the Central Plains.”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk conveyed his fate. It was not only for the Heavenly Demon Soul, but for everyone.

“But in this fight, at least, I must become the protagonist.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s gaze remained on Geom Mugeuk. Until now, it had only glanced at him now and then, but now it stared at Geom Mugeuk in silence.

And this was what Geom Mugeuk most wanted to say.

“Thank you, truly, for descending for my sake today. I know that it was a descent prepared for annihilation. Thank you, my friend.”

The Heavenly Demon Soul, which had been silently gazing at Geom Mugeuk, disappeared without a sound.

“I said so much, so how could you leave without saying a single word!”

After sending off the Heavenly Demon Soul, Geom Mugeuk walked forward.

To Hwa Mugi, that sight seemed truly pathetic. It was nothing more than an evil spirit derived from martial arts.

“You truly do talk a great deal, and you concern yourself with every little thing.”

At Hwa Mugi’s words, Geom Mugeuk answered with a smile.

“You’re no different, living with all sorts of things hanging off you, are you? In that sense, we’re alike.”

At the word “alike,” the thought This madman! naturally rose in Hwa Mugi’s mind.

To Hwa Mugi, Geom Mugeuk was a man utterly beyond comprehension. The more he looked at him, the more unpleasant he felt, so he wanted to kill him immediately—yet at the same time, he was curious what more nonsense he might say.

“How about it? I’m still willing to talk with you.”

He had naturally expected to be treated like a lunatic, but this answer alone was different.

“Once the fight is over, I’ll talk to you as much as you want.”

Swaaaaaak.

Hwa Mugi launched himself toward Geom Mugeuk first, and Geom Mugeuk likewise charged without avoiding him.

Because they had already fought once before, they both knew that their abilities were neck and neck. That was why, from the very beginning, they unleashed their strongest moves.

Chaeeng!

The instant they exchanged the first move, a thrill ran all the way to their hearts.

Now began the final battle, in which everything had to be revealed without hiding anything.

Shiiik!

Geom Mugeuk’s sword flew in, aiming for Hwa Mugi’s waist. The sword was faster than the thought, I should aim for his waist.

Of course, the movement to dodge was faster than the thought, I must block that sword.

Hwa Mugi twisted aside to avoid it, and his sword stabbed toward Geom Mugeuk’s throat.

Shiiik!

Geom Mugeuk narrowly dodged and thrust at his heart, while Hwa Mugi aimed for his eyes.

To the point that it was hard to believe these were the same two people who had just exchanged a frivolous conversation, both men’s attacks were aimed solely at killing the other.

Both Geom Mugeuk and Hwa Mugi could feel it. Perhaps after killing each other, neither would ever again experience a fight like this until the day they died.

Sword-light flashed without rest.

Because their swords moved back and forth so quickly, there was no room for techniques that required preparation or consumed a great deal of internal energy. This was practical swordsmanship solely for slaughter.

Beauty and elegance could not intrude.

Only straight lines.

The shortest lines leading to death.

Only if the opponent were ordinary could one display the aesthetics of curves within the monotony of these straight lines.

Attacks that would pierce vital points if they hesitated even for an instant flew toward each other.

Those watching could not take their eyes away. They could not join in, nor could they help. Their positions changed far too quickly; it was a fight in which one could not even launch sword qi.

Especially those who used swords watched as though they themselves were fighting. Their hearts pounded. They wanted to fight in Geom Mugeuk’s place, not to help him, but because they wanted to experience such a battle.

Amid those swift attacks, sword qi began to be released. Short, rapid bursts of sword qi. The sight created the illusion that the swords were lengthening and shortening.

“Ah!”

Exclamations escaped the mouths of those watching. To move that quickly while releasing sword qi as well was something difficult to believe even after seeing it.

Chwaak! Chwaak! Chwak-chwak-chwak-chwak!

Dozens of lines had already been drawn around the two. On the ground, on the broken walls, the lines they left behind were everywhere. There were so many that it looked as if they were fighting atop a go board.

Had they grown frustrated at being trapped on that go board?

Without either one going first, the two raced along the main road of Maga Village as they fought.

The two exchanged sword blows as they ran.

The sword-lights they created formed long tails. Like the tail of a kite, the sword-lines continued on, running through the streets of Maga Village together with them.

They ran far away, then returned to their original place.

Even while running like mad, the two exchanged sword blows, and not for a single moment did they take their eyes off each other’s gaze. The instant they lost sight of the other, it would be death.

The two were clearly enjoying this fight.

But because such deadly attacks were exchanged, that enjoyment simply did not show.

Because they had no leisure to speak, nor even the leisure to smile once, it did not reveal itself.

This time, they soared into the air.

As though there were an invisible floor, they stepped through the air and fought.

Rising and fighting in flight was something a master could do, but stepping on empty air as though it were the ground while fighting was a realm not just anyone could reach.

They were showing everything that swordsmanship at the extremity of martial arts could display.

Their swords became light, then heavy. When light, they were slow; when heavy, they were fast. It was a battle that ran counter to the fundamental principles of swordsmanship.

And then, at some moment!

Geom Mugeuk experienced something new.

Normally, when one fell into a state of selflessness, one could no longer tell whether one was fighting or not. When one came to one’s senses, the battle should already be over.

But this time, it was the opposite.

Now, he felt as though his soul had left his body and become a third party, watching his own battle. It kept trying to slip out of his body and observe the fight from the outside.

Was this a realm beyond selflessness? If not, then?

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze deepened.

‘Could it be?’

In an instant, dozens of moves were exchanged, and just as that feeling grew even stronger—

Kaaang!

Hwa Mugi knocked away Geom Mugeuk’s sword and shouted. It was the first thing he had said since the two began fighting.

“You lost!”

At the same time, a dark energy erupted from Hwa Mugi’s body as though exploding outward.

Swaaaaaaaaak!

Geom Mugeuk instinctively knew. From the start of the fight, Hwa Mugi had been preparing and waiting solely for this one moment.

This was Hwa Mugi’s winning move.

From the tremendous energy that coiled around and pressed in on his body, he could tell that Hwa Mugi had poured almost all of his remaining internal energy into this single move. What did he plan to do afterward?

Hwaaaak!

At the very moment something unseen swallowed him whole—

Pat!

The world went dark, then brightened again.

Geom Mugeuk stood in a new space.

Thick fog was flowing around him. It was an unpleasant fog that felt as though it flowed alive.

From somewhere came the sound of water running, and he could also hear the sound of flames burning.

There was a fragrant scent, a smell of blood, and even the stench of rotting corpses.

Just then, someone abruptly emerged from the fog.

Astonishingly, the one who appeared was none other than the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor.

“Young Cult Leader.”

The Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor was not surprised to meet Geom Mugeuk here. It was as though he had known Geom Mugeuk had entered and had deliberately come to find him.

“He has used the Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art and imprisoned your soul. It is the dark demonic art that placed us inside his body. This place is within his mind.”

Even at those shocking words, Geom Mugeuk did not seem all that surprised.

“I know you are strong, but……”

With a worried expression, the Divine Sword of the Martial Emperor hurriedly added. The reason Hwa Mugi had said he would talk to him to his heart’s content after the fight was also revealed.

“If you cannot escape within one shichen, you will be trapped here forever.”

Chapter 837: The Demonic Cult Leader Loved by Murim
---------------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk stared intently at the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.

He looked vivid, like a truly living person.

“Pardon me, but may I touch you just once?”

Such composure, even after hearing he would be trapped here forever? From the moment they had first met, this Young Cult Leader had been a truly inscrutable man.

“This is no time for that. The time given to you is only one shichen.”

Unfortunately, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword did not know the way out. Could the Young Cult Leader truly escape this hellish place?

“Truly. I never imagined I would meet you here.”

In truth, there had been intent behind Geom Mugeuk’s coming here.

Earlier, while fighting Hwa Mugi, Geom Mugeuk had felt as though his soul kept slipping out of his body.

He had sensed something strange then, and he could have retreated with Flash Step. Throughout the fight, he had kept in mind the possibility that Hwa Mugi might use some hidden trump card.

Just as he was about to avoid it, the mysterious energy within his body reacted. It was the first time since meeting Hwa Mugi that such a thing had happened.

In the end, being trapped here had been his own instinctive choice.

When he met the Martial Emperor Divine Sword in this place, Geom Mugeuk felt that his choice had been correct. The energy of the Six Paths Origin Qi was clearly related to these souls.

“I believe my coming here is fate as well.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword nodded. Yes, how could meeting as souls inside the body of the Heavenly Slaughter Star not be fated?

“Can you see or hear what is happening outside from here?”

When they had come out by borrowing Hwa Mugi’s body, they had known the situation outside.

“There is a separate place where that can be done.”

“Would you take me there?”

“Follow me.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword took the lead, pushing through the fog.

Following behind him, Geom Mugeuk asked,

“Why are you helping me? I am the successor of the Divine Cult.”

Without looking back, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword answered,

“Think of it as repayment for the heart you showed in wanting to treat me to a meal.”

As if that could be the reason.

—Alliance Leader, your ambition saved the murim.

Geom Mugeuk had said the words he had wanted to hear from someone’s mouth.

—Please forgive yourself now.

At those words from Geom Mugeuk, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword forgave himself. Even if he died now—though he was already no different from dead—he would have no lingering regrets.

As they left the fog-filled place, the scenery around them began to reveal itself.

The first thing that caught his eye was the sky.

When he raised his head, the sun and moon were floating there at the same time, and mountains hung upside down. Waterfalls within the mountains were flowing upward from below. It was truly a sight one could only see in a dream.

To the left was a swamp. If the Poison King had come in with him, he would have run straight toward it. All sorts of poisonous creatures were swarming in the swamp.

Beyond that swamp, on a hill, stood a huge castle. It was a castle with an atmosphere that truly seemed as though ghosts might appear at any moment.

On the plain to the opposite side, corpses were strewn everywhere, and smoke was rising from a small village beyond them. The stench of blood that had filled the air came from that place.

There was also a space where walls stood like a maze, and a place where blades grew so densely like a forest that one could not enter. White bones hung from spearheads set up along the path.

“A strange place, isn’t it?”

“Yes. I never imagined it would be a place like this.”

He had thought the souls would be trapped somewhere like a cramped room, but they had been living in a world of their own.

“This place is constantly changing. It is completely different now from when I first came. Now that you have entered, it will change again.”

It was clear that the inside of this place changed according to Hwa Mugi’s thoughts or emotions.

After walking farther, they came to a sheer cliff. When he looked down, he could not see the bottom.

As they crossed a shabby wooden bridge hanging between cliff and cliff, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword said,

“At first, it was hard to believe. That such a place existed within a person’s heart. Sometimes I find myself wondering. What sort of places would exist within my own heart?”

Though much of his heart had been eased through Geom Mugeuk, he was still afraid. People would come and see what lay in the heart of the Murim Alliance Leader.

“Would there not be things even more hideous and harsh than this?”

At that, Geom Mugeuk spoke as if declaring it.

“There would not.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword stopped in the middle of the bridge and turned back. The wind that blew in shook the bridge, but Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly to him.

“If you were someone who had such scenery within you, you would not have come to help me like this. And even if it was a little more desolate than expected, so what? ‘You brats, it became that way because I wandered battlefields for the sake of the murim. It all ended up that way because I was doing it for you.’ That is what you should say.”

How wonderfully simple. A smile formed on the lips of the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.

“You have a talent for making people feel good.”

“Isn’t it thanks to that talent that I am receiving your help like this, Divine Sword?”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword once again took the lead and crossed the bridge.

“Your heart must be filled with warm scenery.”

The gaze of Geom Mugeuk, who walked behind him, deepened.

“Would it truly be?”

After crossing the cliff, they arrived at a field where a large white circle had been drawn on the ground. Around that circle, unknown diagrams and letters were drawn densely.

A wondrous energy flowed around it. This place felt like a space separate from the places they had passed through before.

“This place is called the Heart Circle. From here, one can see and hear the situation outside. However, he has completely cut off the outside from this place. It seems he has no intention of opening it until he has fully trapped you.”

Though he did not know for certain, the outside must be in chaos right now. After all, he would have suddenly collapsed in the middle of the fight.

Still, he was not worried. It was filled with people he could truly trust, so he believed they would handle it well on their own. Right now, he had to focus only on matters here.

“How do I speak with him?”

“He can hear whatever we say from anywhere.”

“Then he must be hearing the conversation we are having now.”

When the Martial Emperor Divine Sword nodded, Geom Mugeuk looked up into the air.

“I should say something to him.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword expected something like, You cowardly bastard, trapping me like this—what a waste of the name Heavenly Slaughter Star! But instead, Geom Mugeuk shouted in a cheerful voice.

“Heavenly Slaughter Star, I have safely entered your heart! Thank you for showing me around this place! This is the first time in my life I’ve experienced something like this. It’s probably an experience I wouldn’t have even until the day I died. And thanks to you, I’m very happy to meet the Martial Emperor Divine Sword again here.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword looked at Geom Mugeuk. He was not being sarcastic toward the Heavenly Slaughter Star. Geom Mugeuk was genuinely pleased.

“When the fight is over, you promised we would talk to our hearts’ content. Make sure you keep that promise!”

After shouting that into the air, he asked the Martial Emperor Divine Sword,

“Does he answer too?”

“Sometimes.”

It was then. From behind, someone walked toward them and said,

“What kind of dogshit trick is this?”

Among the souls here, there was only one person who would spit out such words as his first sentence.

The person who appeared was indeed the Demon Cult Leader, Hyeok Docheon. He too looked exactly as he had in the past before being sucked into the secret coffer. His anger was not directed at Geom Mugeuk, but at the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.

“Were you planning to escape alone?”

When Hyeok Docheon questioned him, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword looked dumbfounded.

“You are once again trying to fault someone with utter nonsense.”

From that brief exchange, one could tell how the two had been getting along.

Geom Mugeuk greeted Hyeok Docheon.

“I pay my respects to the Cult Leader.”

Hyeok Docheon’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk had not said a single kind word to him. He had called him cruel and infamous, and had even said that if he had not been drawn into the secret coffer, the situation of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would have become even worse.

He had also offended him by saying that a true Heavenly Demon would not have done something like breaking a wine cup.

Even so, the reason Hyeok Docheon welcomed Geom Mugeuk was this.

“You said you might be able to get me out of this place, didn’t you?”

“I did.”

“Did you come in here to take me out?”

At the expectant look in his eyes, Geom Mugeuk answered,

“I wish that were the case, but I was captured and brought here too.”

“You fool!”

Hyeok Docheon’s expression twisted at once. If you cannot get me out, there is no need to bother looking good to you. Just as Hyeok Docheon was about to shout,

“If luck is with us, I may be able to take you out of this place. Perhaps it might even be easier from the inside.”

The anger rising in Hyeok Docheon subsided. If he could get out of this place, he could do anything.

Having experienced what kind of person the Young Cult Leader was, he could not easily discard the hope that he might be able to take him out.

“You said you do not desire unification of the murim, did you not?”

He had said that if Geom Mugeuk let him out, he would achieve the unification of the murim for him, but Geom Mugeuk had refused.

“I do not.”

“Why? You are a man who will one day become Demon Cult Leader, yet you do not even have the ambition to seize the murim in your grasp?”

“I do not.”

He could endure other things, but that was difficult to tolerate.

“How did someone as soft as you become the Young Cult Leader of our cult? If it is the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, then all people under heaven should tremble merely at hearing its name! That is the status and dignity of our cult!”

At that, Geom Mugeuk asked calmly,

“What are you afraid of?”

“What?”

“Isn’t trying to become an object of fear to others done in order to protect yourself? ‘I am a frightening person, so do not challenge me.’”

Thinking he was being treated like a coward, Hyeok Docheon shouted,

“It is not because I am afraid! It has always been that way. We are such beings!”

“In the long history of the murim, wouldn’t it be fine for there to be at least one Demon Cult Leader who is loved by the people of the murim?”

“You lunatic!”

Rage boiled up inside Hyeok Docheon, but he forced himself to endure it. For now, he still had to place his hope in this Young Cult Leader.

“Fine, enough nonsense. Then what is it that you want?”

What Geom Mugeuk wanted was nothing grand.

“Please help me for just one shichen. Only if I get out can the Cult Leader get out as well.”

“Say anything. I will do it.”

“First, please call everyone else here.”

At that moment, another voice came from behind.

“There is no need. We have all come.”

The Cheonui Palace Lord and the Evil Dao Alliance Leader, Ju Gasin, walked toward them. However, the final soul, Hyeok Docheon’s Heavenly Demon Soul, did not appear.

Perhaps he had almost no interaction with them, or perhaps he did not appear when Hyeok Docheon was present.

The Cheonui Palace Lord said to Geom Mugeuk,

“You acted so high and mighty all by yourself, but it seems you are no different after all.”

Though he spoke that way, there was no great hostility in his eyes as he looked at Geom Mugeuk. It was because he had heard that Geom Mugeuk had personally closed the eyes of the Guardian Palace Lord.

Geom Mugeuk readily acknowledged the Cheonui Palace Lord’s words.

“Isn’t it said that an empty cart makes the most noise? I will need all of you to fill that cart.”

Everyone looked at Geom Mugeuk with expressions that asked what he meant.

“As all of you know, any sorcery has a method of undoing it.”

Geom Mugeuk knew well that the stronger the sorcery, the easier it could sometimes be to find its weakness.

“If all of you help me, I will try to find a way to take you out with me.”

Everyone looked at Geom Mugeuk in surprise. Even when he had told Hyeok Docheon earlier that they could get out, they had not believed him. They had thought he said it merely to provoke Hyeok Docheon. In truth, they still did not believe him now.

“How do you intend to get us out? Are you going to put us into someone else’s body?”

To the Cheonui Palace Lord’s question, Geom Mugeuk could have given them false hope by saying he would. But he did not.

“I do not know.”

“You do not know?”

“It is only a premonition for me as well. If fate guided me to this place, then perhaps it meant for me to take all of you out.
