If that succeeds, whether you will enter other people’s bodies after leaving this place, be reborn, or go to paradise or hell—I do not know. Only the heavens would know.”

At those words, Hyeok Docheon’s expression hardened.

“I will certainly go to hell. If that is the case, it would be better to remain in this body.”

It was the same for the Cheonui Palace Lord and Ju Gasin.

At that, Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“I believe all of you will at least not go to hell.”

“Why do you think that?”

“Because you have already been trapped in hell for three hundred years.”

“!”

A heavy silence flowed through that place.

After giving them a moment to think, Geom Mugeuk spoke again.

“I would not dare claim to know what kind of lives all of you have lived over the past three hundred years, nor what thoughts you hold now. I will not forcibly take anyone who does not wish to go. If you remain…”

Depending on the result of this battle, their fate would be decided as well.

“You will either spend this life together with the Heavenly Slaughter Star, or die together with him today.”

The Cheonui Palace Lord asked with a suspicious gaze,

“How can we believe you will be able to do it?”

“There is no need for you to doubt and worry in advance. In any case, if I fail to get out…”

Geom Mugeuk let out a light sigh and added,

“You will be able to nag me for the rest of my life.”

Everyone nodded. After all, the person in the greatest hurry right now was Geom Mugeuk himself.

The one still most actively helping Geom Mugeuk was the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.

“How should we help?”

Geom Mugeuk could feel it. The heart with which the Martial Emperor Divine Sword helped him was not because he wanted to leave this place. He was helping out of a pure wish to allow Geom Mugeuk to get out.

At first, Geom Mugeuk had wondered whether he should run all over this place with Rapid Step to search for the method of undoing it. Finding such a method in this vast place within one shichen would not be easy.

But there had been a reason he had asked for everyone to be called here.

If fate had truly led him here, did that not mean he was meant to receive the help of these souls? Yes, do not try to do it alone. Receive their help.

“Is there perhaps any strange place inside here? Somewhere that stands out, or some special thing whose nature you do not understand.”

“As you saw on the way here, this place is full of strange things.”

At the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s answer, Geom Mugeuk spoke as though he had realized something.

“Then I should ask the opposite. Is there perhaps a place that is not special?”

Everyone tried to recall whether such a place existed. Because they had been here for far too long, no place easily came to mind.

At that moment, someone suddenly answered. It was a person who had not said a single word since meeting Geom Mugeuk here.

“There is such a place.”

It was none other than Ju Gasin.

“Since we came here, this place has continued to change, but…”

Even ordinarily, he had almost never revealed his thoughts or emotions, so it was impossible to know whether he truly wanted to leave this place.

That man looked at Geom Mugeuk and calmly added,

“There is exactly one place that has remained the same from the beginning until now.”

Chapter 838: You Will Die in One Shichen
----------------------------------------
Geom Ujin was fighting alongside him as well.

Though he had only been watching, in his heart he had shared his son’s battle.

He had thrust his sword with him, and blocked the swords that came flying in.

Because they had moved as one in that way, he was able to react faster than anyone.

The instant he felt every last bit of strength drain from his son’s body.

Geom Ujin’s figure was already cutting through the air like a bolt of lightning.

Before Geom Mugeuk could collapse, Geom Ujin had already caught his son in his arms.

With one hand he held his son, and with the Heavenly Demon Sword in his other hand, he sent it flying toward Hwa Mugi’s throat.

Shiiik!

Hwa Mugi did not dodge, standing rooted to the spot.

It was a situation in which Geom Ujin, swept up in emotion, might have pierced his throat with the sword, but Hwa Mugi was certain he would not kill him. He trusted in his opponent’s skill. Trusted that he would respond calmly no matter the situation.

Sure enough, the Heavenly Demon Sword stopped at a point that seemed just shy of touching his throat.

Gazing at Geom Ujin, who stood beyond the cold blade aimed at him, Hwa Mugi spoke calmly.

“If you kill me, your son dies.”

Geom Ujin could tell at a glance that there was almost no internal energy left in Hwa Mugi’s body.

‘That one move took everything he had.’

And as a result, his son lay unconscious in his arms.

“Even if you lay a hand on my body, your son dies.”

Geom Ujin merely stared at him with indifferent eyes and showed no reaction. Rather, now that things had come to this, he had grown even colder.

In the meantime, the Fist Demon approached and took Geom Mugeuk into his arms.

After carefully examining Geom Mugeuk’s condition, the Fist Demon spoke calmly.

“There are no external injuries, but he has completely lost consciousness.”

Jin Paecheon, Baek Jagang, the Pungcheon Cult Leader, the Poison King, and the Sword King also approached Geom Mugeuk. The sight of the ever-talkative Geom Mugeuk with his eyes closed and his mouth shut truly did not suit him. To varying degrees, each of them was worried about Geom Mugeuk.

Next, the Poison King stepped forward and examined Geom Mugeuk.

“He has not been poisoned.”

Unlike usual, the Poison King’s voice was slightly raised. Open irritation showed in his gaze toward Hwa Mugi. It was almost the first time he had ever revealed his emotions like this with Geom Ujin beside him.

Hwa Mugi said to Geom Ujin,

“Your son is inside my body.”

At those words, the Pungcheon Cult Leader guessed what Hwa Mugi’s secret art was.

“The Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art! A secret art of the Dark Palace that traps a person’s soul inside the body.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader added in a slightly tense voice,

“If it truly is the Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art, he must break it and come out within one double-hour. If he cannot come out… he will be trapped inside that man’s body forever.”

As if to say he had guessed correctly, Hwa Mugi provoked Geom Ujin.

“I wonder. Can the Demon Cult Leader of this era truly kill his enemy when his child’s soul is inside that enemy’s body?”

Hwa Mugi spread both arms wide, as if daring him to kill him if he could.

Geom Ujin silently stared at Hwa Mugi. Now that his opponent’s internal energy had hit bottom, killing him would take no more than the flick of a hand.

At that moment, when taut tension filled the air, Jin Paecheon stepped forward.

“Cult Leader Geom is not someone bound by personal sentiment, so he may well kill you.”

Of course, he had not stepped forward to lend weight to Hwa Mugi’s provocation.

“But there is no need for that. The Young Cult Leader will certainly break your sorcery and come out.”

Hwa Mugi’s gaze moved past Geom Ujin to Jin Paecheon.

“Then there is no need to worry.”

Hwa Mugi’s confident demeanor was more than enough to make everyone uneasy.

If this side had Geom Mugeuk, the other side had the Heavenly Slaughter Star, who had come alone to the main altar of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

This time, the Pungcheon Cult Leader stepped forward.

“Are you sure about this? If the Young Cult Leader gets trapped inside you, he’ll be chattering away all day, every day. Are you confident you can live with him for the rest of your life? Don’t come crying later, begging us to take him back. Hurry up and hand over the Young Cult Leader.”

No one took the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s words as a joke.

“The Young Cult Leader is not someone a person can handle alone.”

They agreed wholeheartedly with the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s words.

Putting everything else aside, if Geom Mugeuk’s soul were to endlessly pour curses out from inside one’s mind? That would truly not be easy to endure.

Geom Ujin asked Hwa Mugi,

“What do you want?”

Hwa Mugi said something unexpected.

“Release me, and I will release the Young Cult Leader as well.”

Astonishingly, Hwa Mugi was trying to use Geom Mugeuk as a hostage to escape this place.

It felt unexpected. Whether he survived alone, or whether he killed them all, he had seemed like the Heavenly Slaughter Star who would see things through to the end here. And yet he was planning for the future.

“If you do not release me at once, the Young Cult Leader dies!”

The instant those words ended.

Thud!

Geom Ujin’s fist smashed into his face. Hwa Mugi flew through the air and rolled across the ground.

No one had expected Geom Ujin to strike him without warning, and they all looked at Geom Ujin in shock.

Fortunately, it had not been a fist infused with internal energy, so his head was not smashed apart.

He had said that even if they touched his body, he would kill Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Ujin was certain he could not do so.

Striding over, Geom Ujin grabbed him by the collar as he lay on the ground and hauled him up.

Though it had been a fist without internal energy behind it, Hwa Mugi had also taken the blow without protective qi, so he looked at Geom Ujin with his face wounded.

Geom Ujin sensed that this Heavenly Slaughter Star had not used this method simply to escape this place. He did not know what it was, but the bastard was plotting something. Some dangerous plan that stirred his instinct for crisis.

Without showing any of that, Geom Ujin said coldly,

“Whether Mugeuk breaks it and comes out, or fails to come out…”

After a brief pause, Geom Ujin spoke coldly.

“One double-hour from now, you die.”

The Fist Demon walked over and sealed Hwa Mugi’s acupoints. It was possible he might recover his internal energy even while standing there.

The Fist Demon knew that what Geom Ujin had just said was sincere. It was an action and words made possible because he trusted his son more than anyone. Because he believed Geom Mugeuk would certainly break it and come out.

But he also knew the opposite case was sincere as well.

Even if Geom Mugeuk failed to break it and come out, the Cult Leader truly would cut off Hwa Mugi’s head. Because he was a Cult Leader who would not allow his son to live inside the body of the Heavenly Slaughter Star.

Your son would not want that either.

Geom Ujin would surely think so.

That was why the Fist Demon sincerely wished it.

‘So, Mugeuk, you must get out.’

***

No one had expected Ju Gasin to step forward, so everyone’s eyes focused on him.

Geom Mugeuk asked him for a favor. The place in this world within Hwa Mugi’s mind that had changed the least.

“Can you guide us there?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s request, Ju Gasin nodded.

He had spoken up in the first place because he had intended to tell him the location.

“I can.”

When Ju Gasin readily agreed, Geom Mugeuk asked with an apologetic expression,

“But won’t there be any disadvantage to you if you help me like this? Like being locked up in some dungeon somewhere.”

Ju Gasin said nothing. The one who answered instead was the Martial Emperor Divine Sword.

“He may even receive a reward instead.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword did not trust Ju Gasin.

“Because we cannot be certain whether he is truly helping you.”

He could lead them to some absurd place and waste their time. The Martial Emperor Divine Sword was inwardly uneasy, thinking that might be the case. Even though they had spent such a long time together, he still did not really know what kind of person Ju Gasin was, or what he was thinking.

This matter was the same. He could not tell what his intent was in trying to help Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk, on the other hand, was not uneasy.

“That will not happen.”

“Why do you think so?”

“Because if that happens, it won’t be me, but Cult Leader Hyeok who torments him for years to come.”

Because Hyeok Docheon was desperate to get out of this place. Sure enough, Hyeok Docheon was glaring at Ju Gasin with eyes that said he would not let him off if he lied.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the others and said,

“Will you come with us?”

Aside from Hyeok Docheon, who had clearly demanded to be let outside, the others had yet to express any intention.

Naturally, Hyeok Docheon stepped forward and said he would go, and the Martial Emperor Divine Sword also intended to help Geom Mugeuk to the very end. The Cheonui Palace Lord likewise decided to go with them.

“We have no time, so let us go with qinggong.”

Geom Mugeuk held out his hands to both sides. Everyone was startled at the sight.

“Surely not? You want us to go holding hands?”

“You have no internal energy, do you? I can’t very well tuck you under my arms and carry you.”

In this place, the souls behaved exactly like living people, but they had no internal energy.

Geom Mugeuk, however, could still use not only internal energy but martial arts as well. If he put his mind to it, he could destroy this entire world within the mind.

Of course, since it was within the mind, all sorts of things would form again, but it would become a world different from the one before them now.

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword, who stood to Geom Mugeuk’s right, took Geom Mugeuk’s hand without hesitation.

By contrast, Hyeok Docheon, who stood on the other side, hesitated.

“Are you doing this deliberately to make things awkward for us? Use Void Grasping to take us along. With your martial arts, that should be more than enough.”

Geom Mugeuk rejected him at once.

“We do not know what might happen, so I cannot waste internal energy like that for no reason. If you do not like it, you may stay here. I will go with these two Alliance Leaders.”

In the end, Hyeok Docheon took Geom Mugeuk’s hand. Rather, taking this strange Young Cult Leader’s hand was comparatively easy. The problem was Ju Gasin’s hand, which he had to hold with his other hand.

But Ju Gasin took his hand without much deliberation. He could not have been pleased about holding Hyeok Docheon’s hand, yet he did not hesitate.

‘An incomprehensible bastard!’

Hyeok Docheon likewise did not know Ju Gasin’s true thoughts.

The final hurdle was the Martial Emperor Divine Sword and the Cheonui Palace Lord.

Under normal circumstances, she was someone he would not have held hands with even in death, but the Martial Emperor Divine Sword willingly extended his hand.

“Take it.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword placed helping Geom Mugeuk above his own feelings. He felt it. That fate had given him one final mission: to help this Young Cult Leader.

Had she sensed the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s heart, so different from usual? Or perhaps she simply had no desire to be left out of this matter. The Cheonui Palace Lord also took the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s hand.

“Now then, here we go!”

When Geom Mugeuk flew upward, all of them rose into the air with him. At first they thought it was madness, but the cool wind blew all such thoughts away.

How long had it been since they had flown?

How much had they wanted to fly freely like this?

Earlier, they had borrowed Hwa Mugi’s body and fought, but this was their first time flying through the air and soaring across their own world like this.

For the first time, they were holding hands with those they had spent three hundred years with, and for the first time, they were flying through the sky together. It was truly a strange feeling.

Just then, the weather suddenly changed, and dark clouds gathered in an instant.

As if they would never permit their flight.

With a flash, lightning struck.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

When Geom Mugeuk twisted his body in midair, the lightning brushed past them.

“What happens if we die here?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s urgent question, the Martial Emperor Divine Sword answered.

“We come back to life after some time. Sometimes immediately, and sometimes after several days.”

Though they had no internal energy, it seemed they had killed one another before. Perhaps those fights had been even more desperate.

“We absolutely cannot die within one double-hour!”

Geom Mugeuk made clear his determination to take them all out.

Flash! Flash!

Rumble! Kwa-kwang!

Whether it was Hwa Mugi interfering, or the Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art itself interfering, lightning continued to strike down at them.

But the person flying while carrying them was Geom Mugeuk. Moreover, though they were flying by using Geom Mugeuk’s internal energy, they were all people who had been Cult Leaders, Alliance Leaders, and Palace Lords.

Lightning could not break their will to go forward. They became one and wove between the bolts of lightning. If someone had seen it, it would have been a sight impossible to believe.

“It is there.”

Geom Mugeuk descended with everyone to the place Ju Gasin pointed to.

They had literally flown through the sky and arrived there in an instant.

“I thought we would be going to that castle.”

He had expected to be taken to the castle on the hill far beyond the swamp. It was an ancient castle that looked as though it had been there for a very long time.

But the place Ju Gasin guided them to was not a mountain floating in the sky, nor a complex maze, nor that grotesque castle.

It was a village. The village beyond the field where corpses lay strewn about, where smoke had been rising. Corpses were strewn about there as well.

Hyeok Docheon looked around and said,

“Yes, come to think of it, this village was here from the beginning.”

The stench of rotting corpses had been so terrible that they had not gone near it. Furthermore, there was nothing special here. It was simply a small rural village, a place where corpses lay scattered about as though it had endured a war.

Then Ju Gasin suddenly said,

“This place has changed a great deal too.”

Everyone’s eyes turned toward Ju Gasin.

“The roads changed, and the houses and these walls changed little by little as well. At times the roads narrowed, and the walls grew higher or lower. But the basic terrain of this village remained the same.”

Geom Mugeuk could tell. Ju Gasin had come here many times.

Perhaps Ju Gasin was the one who had wandered through this mental world the most, searching for a way to escape.

“Alliance Leader, did you also want to get out of this place?”

Ju Gasin said to Geom Mugeuk,

“Who would not?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned back toward the village.

“If it has been here from the beginning, then this must be a place the Heavenly Slaughter Star cannot forget.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, everyone nodded.

“Could it be his hometown?”

At that, Hyeok Docheon spoke as if he had finally understood.

“The Dark Palace Lord must have massacred his family and the villagers when he took him away. In order to further stoke the killing intent of the Heavenly Slaughter.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s expression hardened. He was in pain. If that were true, then they could not say they had nothing to do with the massacre of this innocent village.

But words that would relieve his anguish flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“I think this place may not be his hometown.”

Everyone’s eyes focused on Geom Mugeuk at his unexpected words. He had a reason for thinking so.

“The Dark Palace Lord I experienced was a man who used everyone except himself. If the Heavenly Slaughter Star had a family, he would have used them instead of killing them.”

Meaning he would not have slaughtered them so indiscriminately like this. The Martial Emperor Divine Sword asked with a puzzled look,

“If it is not his hometown, then what is this place?”

If it was a place that had occupied his heart for so long?

“I believe it is the place where he committed his first murder.”

Chapter 839: You’ve Never Been the Wind, Have You?
--------------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk and four experts entered the village.

“Do you think there is a breaking method here?”

At Muhwang Singeom’s question, Geom Mugeuk answered.

“Since this is the place Alliance Leader Ju spoke of. He must have prepared another gift for me.”

Perhaps because that was somewhat burdensome, Ju Gasin spoke in a calm tone.

“Do not put too much faith in my words. I merely told you that such a place exists.”

Geom Mugeuk said with a smile.

“Please say that to the person beside me, not me. Even if we cannot find it, I will not resent you.”

Ju Gasin looked at Hyeok Docheon, who stood beside him.

“If a man opens his mouth, he must take responsibility.”

Ju Gasin turned his head back without a word.

They looked around the village. Muhwang Singeom sighed as he looked at the corpses strewn throughout the village.

“He truly killed many.”

An entire village had been annihilated. Down to the children and women. It was a truly miserable sight.

Muhwang Singeom had come once or twice at first, but hardly ever came here after that. It was a sight he did not wish to see.

“Until now, I have never once thought deeply about this place. I simply thought it was natural that there would be places strewn with corpses, since this is the heart of the Cheonsal Star, and so I regarded it as a matter of course. I also disliked the stench of rotting corpses.”

However, Geom Mugeuk predicted that this was the place where Cheonsal Seong had committed his first murder.

“You figured it out as soon as you arrived.”

“It may not be. I am merely sharing a guess.”

That was not the only thing Geom Mugeuk noticed. When they arrived at a house on the edge of the village.

“I believe this is the house we must find.”

Because he spoke as if he had known beforehand, the four experts including Muhwang Singeom felt puzzled. To them, it looked like an ordinary house no different from the others.

“Why do you think that?”

In response to Muhwang Singeom’s question, Geom Mugeuk spoke while looking around the yard.

“Look there.”

What Geom Mugeuk pointed to was the swallow’s nest under the eaves.

“And look there as well.”

Jars were neatly arranged in the yard—something they had not seen at other houses. There were also farming tools neatly lined against the wall. Flowers were blooming in a corner of the yard as well.

“If this village was created from his memories, this place has far more intricate details than the other houses.”

Muhwang Singeom understood what Geom Mugeuk was trying to say.

“It is a house he remembers in more detail.”

“He likely came here often.”

Muhwang Singeom had observed carefully as he came, but he had not realized such a difference existed.

Even Ju Gasin, who had come here often, had not noticed that difference. They were once again amazed by Geom Mugeuk’s keen eye.

Geom Mugeuk passed through the yard and entered the house.

And just as Geom Mugeuk had said, this was indeed the house they needed to find.

When the door opened, a truly unexpected scene unfolded.

Right inside, Hwa Mugi was waiting.

“How many years have we been together? Truly, what a bunch of heartless people.”

Since he had heard everything they said, this entrance was not surprising at all.

“If your companion has made such a choice despite so many years together, shouldn’t you look at yourself rather than mock others?”

From Geom Mugeuk’s attitude, Hwa Mugi could tell.

“You knew I would be waiting.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Because you must have instinctively known this is the last chance to speak with me.”

“The last? You won’t be able to escape, and we will be together for the rest of our lives.”

“If I am trapped inside you….”

Geom Mugeuk spoke as if making a declaration.

“Until the day you die, I will not speak a single word to you.”

“!”

Hwa Mugi stared at Geom Mugeuk in puzzlement.

This talkative person, saying he won’t say a word until death?

Was he saying this because it was hard to believe? No. Hwa Mugi could instinctively feel it. That this young sect leader would truly not say a single word.

“The reason I won’t speak is not because you trapped me here. If martial artists fight and fail to break a sorcery technique and get trapped, they must accept the result.”

“Then why do you say you will not speak?”

“If I end up trapped inside you and must live on, it would be a situation where you have killed my father. That is why.”

Geom Mugeuk had accurately assessed his father.

“If I cannot get out, Father will kill you. He won’t let you live even inside the Cheonsal Star. That isn’t the kind of father he is. Didn’t he already tell you he would kill you in one hour?”

Hwa Mugi listened to Geom Mugeuk’s words in silence.

“And yet, if we are in a situation where you and I converse for a lifetime, it means you have killed my father and everyone else. That is why. The reason I will not speak. Because you are the enemy who killed my father and the people I love.”

Hwa Mugi raised one hand into the air and moved it lightly.

“Then we will all die.”

Black smoke rose from Hwa Mugi’s fingertips, forming a large eye resembling a human’s in the air. And through it, the outside world that Hwa Mugi was gazing at became visible.

Beyond it, his father could be seen standing with Gwonma. His father wore the same expression as usual.

But now Geom Mugeuk knew. Beneath that stoic face, how great a worry was hidden.

‘I’m sorry for worrying you, Father.’

Geom Mugeuk said loudly.

“Father, please save your son!”

Of course, even though he shouted, it could not be heard outside.

Yet at that moment, Geom Ujin glanced this way.

It must have been a coincidence, but it truly felt as though he had turned because he heard his son’s voice.

Geom Ujin, who had been looking at Hwa Mugi for a moment, turned his head back toward Gwonma.

When Hwa Mugi turned his head to the side, he saw himself lying down a short distance from Father.

The person sitting beside him was the Sword King.

The Pungcheon Sect Leader approached them.

“Do not worry. Don’t you know? Even if you throw the young sect leader into hell, he’ll infuriate even the King of Hell and escape.”

“I am not worried.”

“You aren’t?”

“I was simply watching because he looks so peaceful when he sleeps. He sleeps like a baby.”

“Isn’t it peaceful because the young sect leader isn’t talking?”

The Sword King smiled at the Pungcheon Sect Leader’s jest.

In truth, the one most worried was the Pungcheon Sect Leader. The Pungcheon Sect Leader came close and peered into Hwa Mugi’s eyes. His face looked huge.

“Young Sect Leader, can you hear me? I say. Startling old people constantly is bad for the heart. Stop startling the people inside there too. And stop startling us too. Hurry and come out. Didn’t you say you would travel to Gowol!”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze as he looked at the Pungcheon Sect Leader deepened.

“Yes, I will come out soon.”

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang were nowhere to be seen; perhaps they were in a different direction.

“Show the Alliance Lords too!”

But with Hwa Mugi’s gesture, the eye floating in the air disappeared.

“As you can see, I am suppressed. But how can I kill your father? Right now I don’t have a handful of internal energy, and even my acupoints are suppressed.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“Wasn’t that the situation you wanted?”

Hwa Mugi’s expression faltered for a moment.

“You have sufficient internal energy, don’t you?”

Geom Mugeuk understood why Hwa Mugi had used this technique to the point of exhausting all his internal energy.

“I heard that if I am trapped here and an hour passes, I can never escape. And my internal energy, innate true qi, and even my martial arts will be absorbed by you.”

The Pungcheon Sect Leader probably knows up to here. But would he know this as well?

Geom Mugeuk turned to the Cheonui Palace Lord and asked.

“What happens to the suppressed demonic blood points then?”

It was the Cheonui Palace Lord, who knew more about the Amcheon Sokhonsul than anyone.

“The moment all your internal energy and innate true qi become the Cheonsal Star’s, all your blood vessels will open, rendering the suppression of the demonic blood points useless.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward Hwa Mugi.

“You intend to use that internal energy to launch a surprise attack with the Cheonma Myeolse, don’t you?”

Hwa Mugi had indeed been aiming for that very move.

Provoking the Demonic Sect Leader into landing a blow was also part of that hidden plan. To make the opponent lower his guard as much as possible.

If successful, he could kill everyone in an instant. Especially since his own internal energy was being suppressed, the opponent had let his guard down.

Hwa Mugi had truly prepared a perfect move.

Hwa Mugi looked at Geom Mugeuk with a surprised expression.

“How did you know?”

“I’m inside your heart right now.”

Even at Geom Mugeuk’s jest, Hwa Mugi did not laugh. He merely thought it was truly astonishing.

“Young Sect Leader, I truly acknowledge you.”

Hwa Mugi acknowledged him sincerely.

Then Hyeok Docheon abruptly spoke.

“You’re so caught up in praise that you’ve forgotten what needs to be done.”

It was a remark that now was not the time to leisurely exchange conversation with the Cheonsal Star.

Those watching were anxious. Muhwang Singeom had been looking around the room for a while.

‘If the breaking method is in this room, I must find it quickly.’

Time was continuously passing, but Geom Mugeuk was comfortably chatting away like that, so he would have to search himself.

He wanted to properly fulfill the role of the Divine Sword at a time like this. But no matter how much he looked, there was nothing that appeared to be a breaking method.

“That person is deliberately stalling for time.”

In response to Ju Gasin’s remark that Hwa Mugi was stalling for time, Geom Mugeuk answered.

“It is the opposite. I am the one holding him here. Because I want to hear his story.”

He asked a question that proved it was truly so.

“Who in this house did you kill?”

Hyeok Docheon could not hold back and shouted fiercely.

“Is now the time for such a question?”

He could understand the conversation up to that point. Because it was about grasping the opponent’s plan.

But asking who killed whom? Why is that important right now?

Since stalling for time was advantageous, it was a question Hwa Mugi would welcome with open arms.

“I don’t know who lived in this house. Because I killed everyone in the village.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“No, you killed exactly one person. Only the person who lived in this house.”

In that instant, Hwa Mugi’s eyes flashed sharply.

Muhwang Singeom asked with a surprised expression.

“This village wasn’t annihilated by his hands?”

“Yes, I believe he killed only one person in this village.”

“Why do you think that?”

“Would a person cruel enough to annihilate an entire village have kept this village in his heart until now?”

Muhwang Singeom nodded. Now that he heard it, that was true. What pangs of conscience would such a cruel person have to keep this village cherished in his heart for so long?

“Then what of the corpses we saw on the way?”

“They must be illusions created by guilt. ‘I am not someone who killed that person, but a murderous star who kills everyone.’ He must have convinced himself of that in order to endure.”

Without refuting it, Hwa Mugi stared at Geom Mugeuk as if piercing through him.

“That first murder—by the time you came to your senses, it had already happened, hadn’t it?”

Just as Hwa Mugi’s face twitched, astonishing words flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“Tell me about that day.”

“!”

Hyeok Docheon reacted before Hwa Mugi.

“You want to hear such nonsense at this precious time?”

Hyeok Docheon grabbed Geom Mugeuk by the collar.

“Stop talking nonsense and hurry up and find the breaking method! Use that clever head of yours, not that talkative mouth!”

Muhwang Singeom did not stop Hyeok Docheon. He too could not understand Geom Mugeuk. Because he had wondered if Geom Mugeuk had perhaps given up in despair.

Even with his collar grabbed, Geom Mugeuk did not yield.

“I want to hear your story.”

Hyeok Docheon eventually released the collar and pushed him back nervously. Without internal energy, he was helpless against this opponent. If not for that, he would have beaten him half to death to make him find the breaking method.

“We will end up trapped here for life.”

Even in the midst of this, Hwa Mugi felt puzzled as he looked at Geom Mugeuk gazing at him.

Now that he thought about it, Geom Mugeuk had wanted to talk since the very first moment they met.

“Why do you want to hear my words so badly?”

No matter how much he thought, he could not understand.

To him, Geom Mugeuk conveyed his honest feelings.

“Cheonsal Seong, you are a powerful light that could astonish the world, yet you have never once shone by your own will, have you? You have only spun like a pinwheel; you have never once become the wind, have you?”

“!”

After committing a murder that would remain an unforgettable wound for so long.

“After that, you must have been dragged off by the Dark Palace Lord, who planted these people’s souls into your body. Learning martial arts, and later being sealed away, none of that could have been your own will. What about when you broke the seal and came here? Was that your will? The Dark Palace Lord probably told you to wipe out the Demon Cult first and then unite the murim.”

Geom Mugeuk looked back.

“On the other hand, everyone here had a will of their own. For the peace of the murim, for the unification of the murim, for the Heavenly Will, for a world under the heretical path. Because of each of their wills, this result came about today. But what about you?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Hwa Mugi again.

“I hope the words that carry your will amount to more than just ‘I’ll kill you.’”

Because all these years, neither the Dark Palace Lord nor the four masters here would ever have truly listened to you.

“I’ll listen.”

Because thanks to you, I was able to come this far. Because thanks to you, I became happy.

So unburden that deep resentment you could never tell anyone about, and leave it with me.

For a long while, Hwa Mugi stared silently at Geom Mugeuk. At last, he opened his mouth.

“……What the hell do you take me for, you crazy bastard!”

With those words, Hwa Mugi began spewing curses. He spat them out wildly, without restraint.

Geom Mugeuk simply listened to every curse he hurled.

After pouring out every insult he knew, Hwa Mugi said,

“Let’s see if you can still say that after all your people are dead.”

In an instant, Hwa Mugi vanished from before his eyes.

Until the very last moment, Geom Mugeuk watched him. And after Hwa Mugi disappeared, Geom Mugeuk turned around.

“Well, at least he got to curse to his heart’s content before leaving.”

The four of them were looking at Geom Mugeuk with different expressions.

Hyeok Docheon, whose face was still cursing at him; the Martial Emperor Divine Sword, who was smiling as if he had given up; Ju Gasin, still showing no emotion; and the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace, who was sneering.

“So your grand Heavenly Will was a conversation with the enemy.”

It was mockery from the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace, but Geom Mugeuk nodded with a serious expression.

“Perhaps it was.”

At the sight of him readily admitting it, Hyeok Docheon shook his head.

“Then you should have found a way to break it with that precious conversation of yours! It’s all over now anyway. You wasted all our time spouting nonsense.”

It was not a matter of how much time remained. From the beginning, he had had no intention of finding a way to break it.

Ju Gasin interpreted Geom Mugeuk’s choice this way.

“You judged from the start that there was no way to break it.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword, sensing some special feeling in Geom Mugeuk that he himself did not understand, said,

“I hope this choice held value for you.”

Just as everyone was giving up, Geom Mugeuk released his internal energy and cast a barrier around them. He made it so that the sounds of their conversation would not travel outside.

In that state, Geom Mugeuk revealed an astonishing fact.

“I’ve already found the way to break it.”

It was a single statement that explained at once why he had been acting so leisurely.

As everyone stared at him in shock, an even more astonishing truth followed.

“You and I are the way to break it.”

Chapter 840: I’m the Only One Guarding It
-----------------------------------------
“What do you mean, we are the way to break it?”

At the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s question, Geom Mugeuk answered calmly.

“I mean exactly that. The way to break it is me and the four of you.”

They still wore expressions of incomprehension.

When Hwa Mugi had unleashed the Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art, that mysterious energy in his dantian had reacted. That was why he had accepted it instead of avoiding it.

When he met Hwa Mugi in the room earlier, as if to say his choice had been right, his body had begun circulating qi on its own according to the formula he had learned from the Divine Maiden Palace Lord.

Then the energy of the Six Paths Primordial Qi, which had merged into one and permeated his internal energy, began to course through the blood vessels of his entire body.

And that was not all. The energy containing that mysterious power was emitted outward.

Saaaaaaa.

The moment the energy emerged into the world, its mystery reached its peak. It shone brilliantly, like light bestowed upon the chosen.

Amazingly, no one there could see or sense that energy. Even Hwa Mugi, the master of this space, did not notice what was happening.

Only Geom Mugeuk saw and felt that energy.

The light pouring from Geom Mugeuk reached out to the four who had come with him. They did not feel the energy of the Six Paths Primordial Qi wrapping around them.

The moment he saw that sight, Geom Mugeuk understood.

‘Ah! This heart method is the way to break it.’

He could tell that the energy of the Six Paths Primordial Qi completely overwhelmed the Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art.

And instinctively, he felt it. This sacred energy would free them from this place.

He also understood why it had been said that Hwa Mugi could be controlled with the energy of the Six Paths Primordial Qi.

When they were freed from this place, they would probably recover all of their innate true qi, internal energy, and even martial arts that had gone to Hwa Mugi. In other words, they would become his power.

—If you can unite these six energies into one, you will be able to possess the greatest power of all time.

Now he knew for certain why the Secret Coffer had said those words.

Hyeok Docheon asked urgently.

“How do you intend to break it?”

He could not hide his excitement. Just as he had been about to give up on leaving this place, a new situation had unfolded.

“The heart method I learned a short while ago began circulating qi on its own. I believe this heart method is the one that will send all of you out of this place.”

“What kind of heart method is it?”

“It is a heart method I learned from the Divine Maiden Palace Lord.”

At those words, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will thought of the divine maiden who had tried to stop him until the very end. It was a heart method taught by that divine maiden’s descendant. Had she truly seen this very moment?

“What happens if you circulate that heart method?”

At Hyeok Docheon’s question, Geom Mugeuk answered.

“As I told you before, I don’t know either. Whether your souls will vanish and you will fall into eternal sleep, whether you will be born anew as someone’s child, whether you will return to the past, go to paradise, go to hell. Or whether you will enter someone else’s body. Heaven will decide your fates.”

Geom Mugeuk did not forget to add a joke.

“If, by any chance, you enter someone else’s body later, come find me and give me a little signal. Wouldn’t that be enough of a connection to share a drink?”

He said it so lightly that it made them think such a thing might truly happen.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected to Hyeok Docheon.

“And please call the Heavenly Demon Soul as well.”

Hyeok Docheon was surprised by the unexpected request.

“Are you serious?”

“Yes.”

Perhaps because he was connected to Hyeok Docheon by the soul, the Heavenly Demon Soul immediately appeared there.

“You spent all those years together too, so I should give you a chance to choose.”

As if he had never imagined he would be included as well, the Heavenly Demon Soul looked somewhat surprised.

“I tried to kill you.”

There was another reason Geom Mugeuk had called him.

“This is courtesy toward the Heavenly Demon Soul. You Heavenly Demon Souls aren’t beings that should be discarded so carelessly, are you?”

The Heavenly Demon Soul’s gaze trembled slightly. When Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Soul had forgiven Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Soul, he had once again felt what kind of existence he himself was. And now, through that man’s son, he felt the same emotion.

Just as he had spoken to the other souls, Geom Mugeuk spoke to him as well.

“I don’t know what will happen to you.”

Geom Mugeuk looked up at the sky through the window. It was a sky where a mountain floated upside down, and the sun and moon hung together.

“But one thing I do believe is that the sky you go to won’t be as strange as this one. I’ll ask you last, so think it over slowly.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Hyeok Docheon.

“You have the most straightforward personality, so I will ask you first, Cult Leader. Will you trust my method of breaking it?”

What Hyeok Docheon still feared most was this.

“Are you certain I won’t go to hell?”

Geom Mugeuk had said earlier that since spending three hundred years trapped here had been like hell itself, he would not go to hell.

But now, he spoke honestly.

“To be honest, I don’t know either. How could I know?”

Rather, that strengthened Hyeok Docheon’s decision.

“True. How could you know?”

He had always lived according to his own will. And he no longer wanted to remain here.

“I’m going! Before it’s too late, hurry and carry out the method!”

“I should ask the others too.”

Hyeok Docheon shouted at the rest.

“Don’t dawdle and decide quickly! If you don’t want to leave, then get lost!”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword did not hesitate in his decision. It was not because of Hyeok Docheon’s urging, but because he had already made up his mind.

“Yes, let us go. It is time to see the end.”

He had no desire to go to paradise. Whether he ended up in hell or simply fell into an eternal sleep like this, it was time to end this life.

“Thank you.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword cupped his fist politely and expressed his gratitude. Thanks to Geom Mugeuk, the anguish and torment that had plagued him for so many long years had disappeared.

“If it is a place you send me to, I will gladly go anywhere.”

Geom Mugeuk also cupped his fist politely and received his greeting.

“I truly hope to meet you again and properly treat you to a meal.”

Next was Ju Gasin. He found it fascinating that Hyeok Docheon and the Martial Emperor Divine Sword had made the same decision.

“In all those long years, this is the first time those two have become of one mind.”

Ju Gasin conveyed to the two of them the feelings he had never once revealed.

“You considered me a sinister man whose thoughts could not be read. I simply disliked you and found you irritating. So much so that I didn’t even want to exchange words with you.”

The Martial Emperor Divine Sword was surprised that he would say such a thing to him.

“Then you should have at least shown that irritation sooner.”

Hyeok Docheon also added a word.

“That is exactly why you’re sinister. Saying something like this at a moment like this.”

Ju Gasin accepted the Martial Emperor Divine Sword’s words for the last time.

“You irritating people!”

Finally, Ju Gasin had one request for Geom Mugeuk.

“Tell the leader of the Evil Path Alliance this. He must accomplish what he desires. If you say it like that, he will understand.”

Geom Mugeuk could guess what that meant.

“That desire will threaten us and the martial world, but I will still be sure to tell him.”

What Ju Gasin wanted to see one last time was not a person, but the Red Wolf he had taken around with him.

If he were to be born as a beast, he hoped he would be born as a wolf like the Red Wolf.

This time, Geom Mugeuk looked at the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will. Though the man had mocked and ridiculed him earlier, the look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes as he looked at him was no different from when he looked at the others.

“Everyone else is going, so you should go as well, Palace Lord.”

From Geom Mugeuk’s attitude toward him, the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will could tell. This was not something that could be feigned.

‘He was a man of a different vessel from the very beginning.’

Why on earth was this man like this?

Having such a thought itself, he realized, was like a small bird watching a great roc flying through the heavens and wondering, Why does it fly so high with such effort?

“Perhaps the Heavenly Will from three hundred years later was with you.”

He was a man enthralled by Heavenly Will until the very end.

“To be honest, I have no interest in Heavenly Will. It is difficult enough to protect even one will of my own as I please, so how could I live while concerning myself with the will of heaven?”

Geom Mugeuk conveyed his honest feelings to the Palace Lord of Heavenly Will.

“Of course, there are times when I too lean on fate. Times when I simply want to think that all of this must be destiny. Looking back, those were always times when I was suffering. Times so painful that I wanted to escape in any way I could.”

The Palace Lord of Heavenly Will’s expression hardened. Geom Mugeuk was saying that perhaps the Heavenly Will he spoke of now had been nothing more than an escape from reality.

Was he saying this to mock him? But for that to be true, Geom Mugeuk’s eyes were far too clear and deep.

“Palace Lord, you regard Heavenly Will as something immensely important, but did they truly feel the same?”

“Who are you talking about?”

“I mean the Guardian Palace Lord who tried to protect you, and the countless people who died in that war. Did they truly give their lives for Heavenly Will?”

“!”

“They likely did not. They simply trusted and followed your will, Palace Lord. Not some prophecy that suddenly dropped out of nowhere one day, but the judgment of the Palace Lord who had cared for them all their lives.”

Looking into his eyes, Geom Mugeuk asked.

“Before you spoke of Heavenly Will to those around you, do you remember what kind of heart you had, Palace Lord?”

The Palace Lord of Heavenly Will could not answer.

Instead, he asked in a sunken voice.

“If I leave this place, will I be able to meet them?”

Geom Mugeuk quietly gazed at him, then said,

“There are no prophecies now, so do not ask. From now on, act according to your own will, Palace Lord.”

Without waiting for his reply, Geom Mugeuk looked at the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“Will you go with us?”

Amazingly, the Heavenly Demon Soul shook his head.

It was a decision he made because he had remembered his own great nature.

Though he had not lived honorably for the past three hundred years, at least at the very end.

“My mind has become too tainted. I have become a being who must not be born again.”

At the very end, he would be not a vulgar Heavenly Demon Soul, but a dignified one.

The last person the Heavenly Demon Soul looked at was Hyeok Docheon. He left no words for him. He gazed at him silently for a moment, then disappeared without a sound.

It was regrettable, but now was not the time to sink into emotion over his disappearance.

“There is not much time left now.”

Geom Mugeuk held out both hands and said,

“Form a circle and hold the hand of the person beside you.”

Hyeok Docheon frowned and shouted,

“You want us to hold hands again?”

* * *

Hwa Mugi was recalling what Geom Mugeuk had said.

—I will listen.

The more he thought about it, the angrier he became.

‘Insolent bastard, pitying me!’

But Hwa Mugi knew. When Geom Mugeuk had said those words, he had been sincere. He had truly wanted to listen to his story.

It was something that enraged him just to think about, something he had tried desperately to forget.

But he could not forget it. The more he tried to forget, the more clearly it remained engraved in him.

‘What could I possibly have to say?’

Hwa Mugi clenched his fist tightly.

Then the Fist Demon’s voice was heard.

“There is one quarter-hour left.”

Tension heightened in that place. Already, two hours had passed, and now only one quarter-hour remained.

Geom Ujin stood with his hands behind his back, looking up at the sky.

The Fist Demon knew the Cult Leader better than anyone, but at least in this moment, he dared not even imagine what the man might be thinking.

Then Cult Leader Pungcheon approached Geom Ujin and asked in a whisper,

“You don’t truly intend to kill him, do you?”

Geom Ujin did not answer that question.

Cult Leader Pungcheon felt a slight unease. Even if he did not answer, he should have at least shown a look that said, How could I kill him? But from Geom Ujin’s tightly shut lips, he could sense a certain will.

Cult Leader Pungcheon spoke as if this were nothing serious.

“Even if he is trapped in that body, there will be some way to pull him out. Don’t you know what kind of person your son is? He’ll wake up saying, ‘I’m sorry I’m late. There were many people to chat with inside there too, so I was delayed.’”

Geom Ujin turned his head and looked at him.

The moment their eyes met, Cult Leader Pungcheon knew. He had truly made up his mind to kill him.

“Are you insane?”

It was the first time Cult Leader Pungcheon had ever spoken rudely to Geom Ujin.

But neither Geom Ujin, nor the Fist Demon beside him, nor anyone else said anything.

“Being cold-headed is all well and good, and keeping the words you have spoken is all well and good, but what exactly are you trying to do right now?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon shouted at Geom Ujin, and at everyone.

“Do not even dream of it.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon strode forward and stood in front of Hwa Mugi, revealing his own aura.

“If you mean to kill this man, you’ll have to kill me first.”

Everyone stared at him in silence.

Cult Leader Pungcheon sought help from the Sword King, with whom he had grown rather close.

“You’ll help me, won’t you?”

The Sword King did not immediately answer that he would. If Geom Ujin had made up his mind to kill his son, would he truly have done so lightly? There had to be some plan or thought behind it that he himself did not know.

Of course, if Geom Ujin truly tried to kill Hwa Mugi, he would have stepped in without standing by.

“You call yourself my friend! You accepted my sword and even my shoes! If you act like this, I won’t name your tavern for you!”

For now, he did not step forward. For Cult Leader Pungcheon’s sake.

The Murim Alliance Leader, Jin Paecheon, felt similarly. He believed Geom Ujin would not kill Hwa Mugi.

Then Baek Jagang, the Sado Alliance Leader, who had been silent until now, stepped forward.

“That man must not be protected.”

Everyone’s gaze turned toward him. He could clearly sense what thoughts the Heavenly Slaughter Star was harboring.

“That man has laid a plan to kill us all. You know that better than anyone, don’t you?”

After all, the one deeply versed in the Dark Demonic Art was Cult Leader Pungcheon.

Cult Leader Pungcheon looked back at Hwa Mugi behind him.

“Of course there’s a conspiracy. I’m sure it’s a grand and impressive conspiracy. Otherwise, would this man be in such a state? He’d be trying to beat me to death first, the very one protecting him.”

“Then why are you doing this?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon spoke to everyone.

“Even so, we cannot kill the Young Cult Leader.”

They all stared at Cult Leader Pungcheon in silence. If it had been the Extreme Evil Little Demon, this would have been only natural, but from Cult Leader Pungcheon, it was somewhat unexpected.

Only then did they all realize anew: just as much as he cursed and grumbled about Geom Mugeuk when he was not present, he was also someone who protected him when he was not there.

Then Hwa Mugi’s voice came from behind him.

“What exactly is the Young Cult Leader to you, that you would go this far?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon turned to Hwa Mugi.

“Did you fall for those enticing words of the Young Cult Leader’s too, meant only to curry your favor?”

Looking at him, Cult Leader Pungcheon asked,

“Have you ever said even one such enticing word for someone else’s sake?”

Hwa Mugi could not answer.

It was truly a relationship he could not understand. No, he was irritated because he was beginning to understand it.

‘That damned Young Cult Leader!’

Hwa Mugi looked into his own mind. For a moment, while listening to their conversation, he had failed to pay attention to Geom Mugeuk.

When Hwa Mugi looked inside his mind, he was startled. They were standing in a circle, holding hands.

A sense of foreboding swept over him, and before he knew it, he blurted out,

“No.”

It was a low murmur, but Cult Leader Pungcheon, who was right in front of him, heard it.

“Yes!”

Cult Leader Pungcheon turned back to him and shouted.

“It’s the Young Cult Leader, isn’t it? The Young Cult Leader is finally making his move, isn’t he? Young Cult Leader, come out at once and see!”

Cult Leader Pungcheon looked into Hwa Mugi’s eyes and shouted even louder.

“I’m the only one protecting you!”

Chapter 841: Why Don't You Resent Me?
-------------------------------------
The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword held out his hand to Hyeok Docheon.

“Let me clasp hands with you once as well.”

Since he had held hands with the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace while flying earlier, he meant to hold Hyeok Docheon’s hand last.

“You and I fought quite a lot.”

Hyeok Docheon was in a hurry, so he did not refuse and seized the hand at once. Right now, all he wanted was to get out of this place as quickly as possible.

Of course, even as he took his hand, he did not forget to say one thing.

“You were the one who acted so frustratingly.”

In the past, he would not have let that pass, but the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword merely smiled.

“You two, hurry up and take hold too. If we’re too late and can’t get out, you’ll all die by my hand!”

At Hyeok Docheon’s urging, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace and Ju Gashin also took hands. They all knew there was no time, so they moved swiftly.

As the four of them formed a circle and clasped hands, Geom Mugeuk began to circulate the mnemonic chant.

As his true qi moved according to the chant, the energy of the Six Paths Primordial Qi traveled through Geom Mugeuk’s hands and was transmitted to them.

Hyeok Docheon’s eyes flew wide at the energy entering his body. Never in his life had he felt such power.

It was the same for the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword beside him and the other two. They instinctively knew that this was not an energy of the human world.

As the true qi flowing out from Geom Mugeuk passed through their bodies, a mysterious radiance began to emanate from them, united as one through the chant.

It was right then.

Hwa Mugi appeared there once more.

“What kind of trickery is this!”

While his attention had been briefly drawn to the Cult Leader of Pungcheon, something completely unexpected had been taking place inside him.

Hwa Mugi knew instinctively. This was something he absolutely could not allow. With almost one shichen having passed, what they were doing was surely an attempt to break the Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art.

Hwa Mugi rushed at them and tried to tear apart their clasped hands.

Judging from their appearance, they were undoubtedly using some mental cultivation method, so he had to separate those hands and sever the flow of true qi.

But the radiance pouring from their bodies blocked any approach from outside.

Because his original body was in a state where its internal energy had been suppressed, his soul likewise could not use internal energy.

Even if he could use martial arts, it did not seem he would be able to pierce through this light, but—

“Stop!”

Hwa Mugi did not give up and tried to force his way into them, but it was useless.

The only hope left to Hwa Mugi now was time.

Nearly one shichen had already passed. There was not even enough time left for a single stick of incense to burn down.

‘One shichen must pass before their circulation ends!’

The four of them felt changes arising within their bodies. The sensation of holding hands grew faint, and they even forgot the existence of the mysterious energy that had entered them.

Feeling as if they stood alone in an empty space, the days they had lived flashed by in an instant. All the emotions they had experienced brushed past each of their hearts.

The energy of the Six Paths Primordial Qi, which had moved as one, split apart and scattered into different lights.

The pure white energy of the White Essence wrapped around the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword’s body. He looked as though he were vanishing into a thick fog.

“Thank you, Young Cult Leader.”

The Martial Sovereign Divine Sword smiled brightly and disappeared together with the white light.

What wrapped around Hyeok Docheon was the pitch-black energy of the Black Essence. He seemed to walk down a dark night street and vanish into the darkness.

“You’re too soft! The Demon Cult must be rough and strong!”

Somehow, from the final shape made by the darkness enveloping him, the image of the Heavenly Demon Soul seemed to flash past. If that was true? Even now, may it remain with him to the very end.

The energy that wrapped around Ju Gashin was the energy of the Red Essence. The crimson energy blazing like fire took the shape of a red wolf, then disappeared with him.

“Still, for a Demon Cult bastard, you weren’t bad.”

That was the final thing he left for Geom Mugeuk.

The light that wrapped around the last one, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace, was golden. The sight of him turning away looked as though he were walking lonely into the sunset. A final lingering attachment he had never been able to cast off.

“Even so, there is heavenly will. I hope the heavenly will granted to you can change the world.”

And so the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace disappeared as well.

Even Geom Mugeuk could not know where they had gone.

“May you have gone to the place you wished!”

Geom Mugeuk bowed respectfully toward the place where they had vanished. It was the moment an affinity that had continued for over three hundred years came to an end.

At that very instant, exactly one shichen passed.

Swaaaaaaak.

In that moment, Hwa Mugi’s expression brightened radiantly.

Geom Mugeuk’s innate true qi, internal energy, and martial arts had begun to enter his body. At the fact that the Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art had not been broken, Hwa Mugi rejoiced greatly.

“Yes, I don’t need them. You can take their place!”

Hwa Mugi laughed loudly. What delighted him even more was the nature of the internal energy entering his body.

‘To think such pure internal energy exists?’

Even the internal energy of the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword, an absolute master of the orthodox faction, had not been this pure.

Geom Mugeuk’s innate true qi, internal energy, and even his martial arts were all absorbed into Hwa Mugi’s body.

It was right then that something even more astonishing occurred.

Swaaaaaaaa.

The internal energy of Hyeok Docheon, the Martial Sovereign Divine Sword, the Lord of the Heavenly Will Palace, and Ju Gashin, which he had obtained earlier, also began to fill him again. In the reality outside, that internal energy had already been completely exhausted.

Perhaps, as their souls left his body forever, all that power was being restored once more.

‘The heavens are helping me!’

When that innate true qi and internal energy combined with Geom Mugeuk’s, he felt a truly tremendous power. With this strength, he felt confident he could fight the entire murim alone.

“I am the greatest of all ages!”

With this power, even without using the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World that he had originally planned, he felt as if he could go out and kill them all.

Hwa Mugi said to Geom Mugeuk,

“If there was a way to break it, you should have gone out alone instead of trying to save them. That half-baked desire to be a good person to others has trapped you here for the rest of your life.”

Geom Mugeuk readily admitted it.

“Yes, I know that the desire to be a good person can become a great weakness.”

Had he given up on everything? Geom Mugeuk was calm.

“But what can I do? I’m greedy, and I want to be that kind of person. I’m someone who cares terribly about how others see me and wants them to tell me I’m impressive.”

Geom Mugeuk’s calm did not come from despair. Though all his innate true qi and internal energy had been drained away as if by a lie, the energy of the Blue Essence and the energy of the Violet Essence still remained within his body. He could feel them vividly, as though this was not yet the end.

Wooooong.

Sure enough, the energy of the Blue Essence within Geom Mugeuk’s body began to circulate on its own.

Then a blue radiance poured from Geom Mugeuk’s body.

It was not just light.

The light began to absorb all the innate true qi and internal energy in Hwa Mugi’s body, as well as every martial art he had learned.

“No!”

Hwa Mugi gritted his teeth and resisted, but it was a force he could not refuse. The moment he was exposed to the light, he could not move so much as a finger.

Only now did he realize that the Dark Heaven Soul-Binding Art had already been broken. The reason Geom Mugeuk had obediently given up his internal energy earlier was to gather all that power in one place and absorb it all at once.

“Young Cult Leader! You son of a bitch! Stop! I said stop!”

He tried to leave the world of the mind, but that too was impossible. From the beginning, he could not resist the power of the Six Paths Primordial Qi. It had been destined the moment he accepted the souls of the past into his body.

Hwa Mugi did not give up. He drew up all the murderous intent he possessed.

“I’ll kill you!”

Hwa Mugi’s two eyes burned crimson, and the most powerful killing intent burst forth. He had revealed astonishing killing intent many times before, but none of it could compare to the killing intent now.

All of Geom Mugeuk’s innate true qi, internal energy, and martial arts had returned, and the innate true qi and internal energy of the other four masters had been absorbed.

When that power combined with his own internal energy, Geom Mugeuk could understand why Hwa Mugi had shouted that he was the greatest of all ages.

Hwa Mugi held on as if in a desperate struggle. He did not give up.

‘I can’t let it end like this.’

The house they had been in disappeared, and the village disappeared as well. The mountain that had been floating upside down in midair crashed to the ground, and the swamp overturned, scattering all manner of poisonous creatures in every direction.

As the sun and moon that had floated in the sky at the same time vanished, the sky grew dark. The world sank into pitch-black darkness.

At that very moment!

Twinkle.

In the midst of it, a single star began to shine.

The Heavenly Slaughter Star.

The light of Heavenly Slaughter poured down upon Hwa Mugi.

Now, from Hwa Mugi’s eyes, there poured a murderous intent that was no longer human.

The final power arranged by fate was granted to Hwa Mugi in this moment.

‘Please! Just once! Leave me only enough internal energy to use that!’

At the very moment that will reached the Heavenly Slaughter Star, the light of the Heavenly Slaughter Star severed Geom Mugeuk’s blue light.

In that instant, Hwa Mugi disappeared.

***

“Neither of them has opened their eyes yet.”

Even though one shichen had passed, both Hwa Mugi and Geom Mugeuk remained with their eyes closed.

Everyone could feel it. The two of them were fighting within Hwa Mugi’s mind.

“Young Cult Leader, come out quickly.”

The Cult Leader of Pungcheon was beside Geom Ujin together with the Sword King. The Sword King had forcibly brought him there, saying it was dangerous to stand right in front of Hwa Mugi.

The Cult Leader of Pungcheon stood beside Geom Ujin, his nerves on edge, ready to act the moment Geom Ujin did.

Geom Ujin had seemed as though he would kill Hwa Mugi the instant one shichen passed.

In the end, however, Geom Ujin did not kill Hwa Mugi.

The Fist Demon could tell how difficult a decision that had been.

‘This is the first moment the Cult Leader has failed to keep words he himself spoke.’

Jin Paecheon, Baek Jagang, and the Poison King had also come near Geom Ujin.

Jin Paecheon was of the mind that they should trust Geom Mugeuk and wait, while Baek Jagang believed it was dangerous to keep dragging things out like this.

The Poison King silently stared at Geom Mugeuk, who lay there unmoving.

As such suffocating tension flowed through the air—

Hwa Mugi opened his eyes first.

He did not hesitate. He had no leisure to think of what came after. If he did not kill them first, he would die. Before Geom Mugeuk woke up, he had to sweep them all away.

The moment Hwa Mugi raised his hand and thrust it forward—

Flash!

It was the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World.

The whole world turned white. As if the sun had exploded, no sound could be heard in that enormous detonation.

Though he had not killed Hwa Mugi, Geom Ujin had not let down his guard.

The instant Hwa Mugi awoke and unleashed the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World, he erected the Great Demonic Wall and then also used the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World.

Flash.

The situation everyone had feared most unfolded.

Blocking with the Great Demonic Wall, then the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World.

That very situation in which both sides would perish together.

The two men’s Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World were different.

Unlike Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World, which annihilated everything visible to the eye, Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World became a single pure-white sword and flew forth.

Far behind Hwa Mugi, the main altar, where demonic flames were rising, could be seen. He could not blow away that place too.

It was the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World he had once shown Geom Mugeuk before, proving that if one achieved great completion of the Twelfth Star, such control was possible.

A sword of light flying without a sound.

Hwa Mugi’s Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World was erasing everything, but the sword created by Geom Ujin’s Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World continued to fly. The sword flew on without being annihilated even by the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World.

Puaaaaaak!

It pierced straight through Hwa Mugi’s chest.

Now the problem was on this side.

Swaaaaaaaa.

The Great Demonic Wall was melting. It was a Great Demonic Wall of the Twelfth Star’s great completion, but from the start, the Great Demonic Wall could not block the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World.

Geom Ujin and the Fist Demon stood at the very front.

The Cult Leader of Pungcheon prepared to use even the Space-Time Transposition Art.

But he could feel that even the Space-Time Transposition Art could not block the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World. Any demonic art or sorcery in this space would all be annihilated.

When the Great Demonic Wall had almost completely melted away, the Cult Leader of Pungcheon could no longer hold back and revealed his true feelings.

“Even if we all died, we should not have killed that Hwa Mugi.”

Without even thinking of the fact that Geom Mugeuk’s body lying there would also be annihilated, he protected the Second Young Master, the Young Cult Leader, until the very end.

“The Young Cult Leader would somehow have survived and escaped that body, and he would have created a murim better than ours.”

Then came a welcome voice from behind him.

“It feels awkward to make my entrance when you aren’t cursing at me.”

The Cult Leader of Pungcheon turned around in shock and saw Geom Mugeuk standing there.

“I’ll greet you properly in a moment!”

Geom Mugeuk immediately erected a new Great Demonic Wall behind his father’s.

Swaaaaaaak.

His father’s Great Demonic Wall melted away, and now Geom Mugeuk’s Great Demonic Wall endured the Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World.

Those standing behind watched the astonishing sight of a second Great Demon Wall holding back Heavenly Demon World-Annihilation. It was a scene that had never existed in the history of the martial world before, and would never appear again.

Geom Mugeuk looked at his father.

“Father.”

Geom Ujin merely stared in silence at the Great Demon Wall his son had unfolded. If his eyes met his son’s, it would reveal just how worried he had been, so he kept looking straight ahead.

The Fist Demon was certain. The reason Geom Ujin had unleashed Heavenly Demon World-Annihilation at the Heavenly Slaughter Star was because he had sensed that Geom Mugeuk had awakened. Surely he had been the first to realize his son was awake.

Ssssss.

The pure white gangqi that had tried to pierce through the Great Demon Wall slowly vanished.

Geom Mugeuk’s Great Demon Wall had endured Heavenly Demon World-Annihilation.

Because his father’s Great Demon Wall had already blocked it once, and because he had absorbed all of the innate true qi and internal energy dwelling in Hwa Mugi’s body, he had been able to stop Heavenly Demon World-Annihilation.

As the light that had illuminated the world so brilliantly disappeared, Hwa Mugi’s figure came into view.

He lay on the ground with a gaping hole through his chest.

“In the end, Father killed him for us.”

Geom Ujin had kept his word that they would kill the Heavenly Slaughter Star together.

Geom Mugeuk slowly walked over to Hwa Mugi. He was still breathing.

Geom Mugeuk looked down at him. When villains died, he had never tried to attach any meaning to their deaths, had not even looked at them—but he could not do that with Hwa Mugi.

“If you have anything to say, say it now.”

Hwa Mugi spoke, blood pouring from his mouth in great mouthfuls.

“......You madman.”

When he had seen him walking over, he had thought he would hurl curses and maledictions at him, but thinking about it now, he had already cursed him to his heart’s content.

“......I resent you.”

Geom Mugeuk, meanwhile, looked down at him with deep eyes.

Hwa Mugi could feel it. There was no ill will contained in those eyes.

I tried to kill you, and I tried to kill your father and everyone you hold dear.

“......Why don’t you resent me?”

Because I resented you enough in my life before regression.

“They say resentment always has two edges. That’s why. So I get hurt less. So don’t resent me too much either.”

“You, to the very end............”

Hwa Mugi could not continue. Just as his breath was about to cease.

Something astonishing happened.

Swaaaaaak.

The last remaining energy of amethyst within Geom Mugeuk’s body flowed out and wrapped around Hwa Mugi’s body.

Geom Mugeuk could tell that this energy of the Six Paths Origin Qi—no, those heavens—intended to take him away as well.

Geom Ujin and the others watching looked on in astonishment. Perhaps Geom Mugeuk had been fighting a far more difficult battle than any of them had imagined.

Hwa Mugi, whose breath had been fading, opened his eyes again.

He had been granted a moment to share a final farewell.

When Geom Mugeuk had first set out on this path, he had imagined a final moment where he would ask why Hwa Mugi had killed even children who knew no martial arts, then shatter him to pieces in cathartic vengeance. But his farewell with him had arrived before him in this form.

“May you live an ordinary and happy life with her in your next life.”

From the way Hwa Mugi’s eyes widened in surprise, it was clear she had been the woman the person he killed had loved.

“How did you know?”

“I saw belongings in your room that belonged to a young woman.”

Even in the midst of all that, he had seen that—thinking this, Hwa Mugi let out a hollow laugh before he could stop himself.

But in his final moment, he did not want to show Geom Mugeuk a smiling face.

“In my next life............ I’ll definitely kill them all.”

But that vicious killing intent no longer remained in Hwa Mugi’s eyes.

Geom Mugeuk looked at him and smiled brightly.

“That’s fine. The person who’ll suffer to stop you then won’t be me.”

As violet light scattered radiantly, Hwa Mugi disappeared along with it. Like a star vanishing from the night sky, leaving behind countless fragments.

After meeting Hwa Mugi, he had called him the Heavenly Slaughter Star and had not once called him by his name.

For the first and last time, Geom Mugeuk called his name.

“............Farewell, Hwa Mugi.”

Chapter 842: You Survived Too
-----------------------------
Geom Mugeuk stood there and looked up at the sky.

He felt as though he had finished the greatest task life had given him. He felt light. Truly, as if he might fly away with joy.

Because this was the moment when all the time he had spent wandering in search of the materials for the Great Art before his regression, and all the effort he had put in since his regression, bore fruit.

This moment was worthwhile not because he had killed Hwa Mugi. It was because no one had died.

Geom Mugeuk slowly turned around.

Toward the place where his true life was waiting.

Geom Mugeuk greeted his father first.

“It was thanks to you, Father, that I was able to end this safely. Thank you.”

He meant it sincerely. Because his father had been there, he had been able to act freely without worry. He had been able to do everything he had wanted with an easy heart.

Geom Ujin, who had been gazing at his son, spoke calmly.

“Come closer.”

When Geom Mugeuk approached his father, something truly unexpected happened.

Geom Ujin silently embraced his son.

Everyone watching stared in astonishment. They had never imagined Geom Ujin would embrace his son in front of everyone like this.

The most surprised person was Geom Mugeuk. He was so shocked that not even a silly joke came to mind.

Then came Geom Ujin’s single remark.

“Your internal energy has increased again.”

Geom Mugeuk wore an expression of disbelief.

“Don’t tell me you hugged me just to check exactly how much my internal energy increased?”

“Then did you think I hugged you because I wanted to?”

At the sight of his father stepping back as if his business were done, Geom Mugeuk raised his voice.

“That is too much!”

But Geom Mugeuk had felt it. The feeling contained in his father’s hands when he embraced him.

I’m sorry for making you worry, Father.

Next, Geom Mugeuk greeted Kwonma.

“Master.”

Kwonma answered with a single nod.

“You worked hard.”

As always, his reaction was blunt, but in truth, if the Cult Leader had not embraced him, he would have done it himself. To tell him he had worked hard. At least today, he had wanted to do that.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to the two Alliance Leaders. First, he relayed Ju Gasin’s words to Baek Jagang.

“Alliance Leader Ju asked me to tell you something. He said you must achieve what you desire, Alliance Leader.”

“I understand.”

Geom Mugeuk knew exactly what that dream was, but he glibly changed the subject.

“It won’t be easy. Traveling around with my father and Alliance Leader Jin to enjoy the rest of your lives, I mean.”

At that, Jin Paecheon, who had been listening, casually interjected.

“I would not mind, but I do not know whether Cult Leader Geom would wish for it.”

He said it like a joke, but there was meaning beneath his words.

Having clearly witnessed Geom Ujin’s strength over the course of this battle, he was worried that the end of this fight might be the beginning of a new one.

He had come to help for the sake of his grandson, for the sake of Geom Mugeuk. But apart from those good intentions, he could not help worrying about what was to come.

Geom Mugeuk could not confidently tell Jin Paecheon, My father will come with us too!

Because he still did not know what his father was thinking.

If he had not given up on his will, then perhaps stopping his father from unifying the murim would be far more difficult than stopping Hwa Mugi.

Indeed, his father did not answer, even as empty words, Let us do that. Perhaps in this moment, he was sending a signal to the two Alliance Leaders.

My mind has not changed, so prepare yourselves.

As an inexplicable tension flowed through the air, Cult Leader Pungcheon stepped forward.

“Young Cult Leader, since it seems you do not know, I will tell you. While you were trapped in there, do you know who stepped forward to protect you? I mean the one person who came forth like a hero among those heartless people who tried to kill the Heavenly Slaughter Star before you had even come out.”

His expression said, It was me, me! but Geom Mugeuk looked at the Sword King.

“Of course it must have been my friend. He would have rushed out barefoot.”

The Sword King’s face was serene. In the process of watching his own fight and the fights of others this time, he had gained a tremendous realization in swordsmanship. And Geom Mugeuk was safe on top of that? There could be no better result.

Cult Leader Pungcheon waved his hands.

“Unfortunate as it is, your friend was thinking about the tavern he would open and paid no attention to your danger.”

Seeing the smile deepen at the corners of the Sword King’s mouth, Geom Mugeuk looked at the Poison King this time.

“Despite how he looks, the Poison King is secretly a man of much affection.”

This time, Cult Leader Pungcheon lowered his voice and whispered into Geom Mugeuk’s ear.

“The truth is, the person who had the easiest time in this battle was the Poison King. He did not lift a finger.”

“Wouldn’t that have made it harder for him?”

They spoke in whispers, but everyone heard their conversation.

“I’m telling you, it was someone else!”

“The one who would do that for me would be Extreme Evil Soma.”

“Don’t look for someone who isn’t here!”

“Could it have been one of the two Alliance Leaders...”

When he kept joking until the end, Cult Leader Pungcheon’s lips jutted out in a pout.

“So in the end, I am treated as that Cult Leader from back then.”

Only then did Geom Mugeuk look at him and speak calmly.

“That Cult Leader from back then protected me now.”

The look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes toward him held sincere gratitude.

“I mean that Cult Leader from back then, who gave me everything without holding anything back.”

Yes, though he had complained every time about having his divine treasures taken, Geom Mugeuk had never once shown dislike. Yes, nothing he had given him had been a waste.

“When are the three of us going traveling with Gowol?”

“We should go. We will go soon! But for the time being, I have a mountain of work piled up, so pack your bags first and wait!”

Just then, something entered Geom Mugeuk’s sight.

“Ah. Look over there.”

Everyone’s gaze turned behind them.

Geom Mugeuk’s delighted voice flew toward what was there.

“You survived too.”

***

Geom Muyang stood atop the palm of the giant demon statue holding up the Demon Flower and looked in the direction of Maga Village.

Several days had passed, but those who had gone out with his father had not returned.

As the Demon Flower bloomed, Tongcheongak’s information network had been moving even more swiftly.

He had heard that the Sado Alliance Leader and the Murim Alliance Leader had come to help, and at the end, he had heard that Geom Mugeuk had arrived as well.

“Is there still no news?”

At Geom Muyang’s words, Hwi’s answer came from midair.

“No, not yet.”

And yet the battle had not ended.
