"Would you like a drink? It so happens that the housekeeper made savory pancakes today."

The rascal had planned everything! Yes, there should be a day to slack off once in a while.

"Let us have a drink, for the first time in a while."

The moment the words left his mouth, Geommugeuk instantly flew off and returned with alcohol and savory pancakes.

The two drank in the pavilion, the rain and savory pancakes serving as their drinking snacks.

In fact, even counting his life before the regression, this was the first time Geommugeuk had spent so long alone with someone. His father was likely the same. Geommugeuk did not find this time awkward or burdensome in the slightest.

It was not solely thanks to his efforts until now. His efforts to make his father feel comfortable here, as if he were outside, had erased any such awkwardness.

How could there be any room for awkwardness? Each time, he had been busy creating new spaces and striving to make them a little more tasteful.

Whoosh.

Simply sitting still and watching the rain was enough to lift his spirits.

Then Geommugeuk cautiously spoke up.

"I may lose all the points I've worked so hard to build up, but there is a question I dearly wish to ask."

It was a question he had never once brought up until now.

"What was my mother like?"

Had he expected his son to ask someday? Even at the sudden question, his father was not surprised.

"When I was very young, I once asked my older brother. About Mother."

It was a time when they were both so young that they did not even think about succession struggles.

"That's when my brother told me. That Father hated it, so I should never bring it up."

Brother had been young then too.

"I never brought it up again after that day."

He had lived as if she were someone who must never be mentioned.

Having no memory of his mother, he felt no tender longing. Moreover, with Hwamugi's appearance, his entire life until now had been focused on stopping him.

Now that Hwamugi was dead, he thought the time had come to ask his father at least once.

His father said nothing. He merely gazed silently at the falling rain.

Geommugeuk did not press the question further. His father would tell him when the time was right.

Whoosh.

* * *

"Another bottle, if you would."

At the words of Martial Alliance Lord Jinpaecheon, the waiter quickly ran over, delivered the liquor, and left.

"Here you are! Please enjoy!"

Jinpaecheon was in an inn bustling with people.

On the road back to the Martial Alliance, he had stopped by a tavern once more.

Even when he had gone to aid the Heavenly Demon Cult, he had stopped by a tavern and enjoyed a brief escapade where no one recognized him.

He had not been at leisure back then, but now he calmly savored the moment.

This time, too, no one recognized him. Who could imagine the Martial Alliance Lord drinking alone in such an inn? Even if someone who knew him passed by, they would only tilt their heads in confusion and walk on.

In that raucous place filled with so many people—those eating, those chatting over drinks, families sharing a meal, escort warriors on a delivery mission—Jinpaecheon was watching the crowd.

Then Jinpaecheon caught sight of a boy standing by the roadside, staring into the inn. The boy, dressed in beggar's rags, was tightly gripping his younger brother's hand.

Without hesitation, Jinpaecheon beckoned the child over.

The boy gave a wary look but soon brought his brother inside.

When the innkeeper tried to chase the children out at the entrance,

"I called for these children. Let them in."

Seeing that he was clearly no ordinary martial artist, the innkeeper promptly sent the children over to him.

"This old man ordered too much food and cannot finish it all. It would be a waste to throw the dishes away, so would you boys help me?"

The younger child might not have understood, but the older boy had a look that said he knew full well what kind of heart lay behind those words.

"Thank you."

The boy fed his brother first. He stopped his brother, who was devouring the food.

"Slowly! Eat slowly."

Only after seeing to his brother did the boy eat.

"How old are you?"

"Twelve, sir."

"Your parents?"

"They passed away."

He explained that they had been left with relatives, subjected to abuse, and had fled with his brother in the night.

Could this child really survive for even a few days in this harsh world, dragging his young brother along?

Jinpaecheon told them to eat comfortably and did not bother the boy with any more words.

When the children had finished their meal, Jinpaecheon wrote something on a piece of paper and handed it to the boy.

"Do you know where the Martial Alliance branch office is in this town?"

"Yes, I saw it on the way here."

"Then go straight to the Martial Alliance branch with this. Hand over this letter, and you will be able to find work that will keep your brother from starving."

The boy was clever and quick-witted. Had he been told to go somewhere else, he would not have gone, but a Martial Alliance branch would at least not sell him off somewhere.

"Don't be scared and make sure you go. Trust this old man. Do you understand?"

"Thank you."

The boy gave a deep bow and left with his brother.

Once the two children were gone, a man walked over and sat down.

It was Jinhageun.

"My Lord, it is a true relief that you have returned safely."

Since Jinpaecheon had left, Jinhageun had not had a single day of restful sleep. He had regretted, time and again, whether he had asked something unnecessary of his grandfather.

So when he received his grandfather's urgent message telling him to meet here, he could not fathom how happy he felt. He was far more delighted that his grandfather was safe than by the repelling of Cheonsalseong.

"Send a subordinate to make sure those children find their way to the branch safely. And speak well of them to the branch members."

"Yes, sir."

Jinhageun relayed the order directly to a subordinate of the Demon Slayer Corps waiting outside.

When Jinhageun sat back down, Jinpaecheon calmly relayed the results of the battle.

"There were many battles, but in the end, the one who repelled Cheonsalseong was the Demon Cult Leader, and the one who blocked his final counterattack was the Young Cult Leader."

Jinpaecheon conveyed his honest heart to his grandson.

"Even during the battle, I worried about their strength, and after it ended, I worried still. I wondered if the Demon Cult Leader might be trying to realize the futile dream of the Righteous-Demonic Great War. That was my only concern on the entire way here."

But while feeding the children just now, he had recalled one thing he had forgotten.

Jinpaecheon asked his grandson.

"If you had been sitting here, would you have called those children in and fed them?"

Jinhageun did not hesitate. He would have done so without fail.

"Yes, I would have."

"Yes. That is who we are."

In the process of making Jinhageun the Young Alliance Lord, Jinpaecheon's trust in his grandson had deepened boundlessly. Now he no longer deceived his own heart for the sake of pride.

"This old man was overwhelmed by the Demon Cult Leader's powerful martial arts and momentarily forgot what kind of people we are. I was only worrying about the war."

The worry and anguish that had lingered on Jinpaecheon's face had already vanished.

"What has protected the righteous martial world for so long was not a technique that could overcome the Demon Cult Leader's Guhwamagong, but that very heart. Shamefully, this old man had momentarily forgotten that."

Firm resolve filled Jinpaecheon's voice.

"Remember this. Those we must protect are not only those children, but all those who seek to protect those children. We will protect them all. That is the reason we exist. Do you understand?"

"Yes, I will take it to heart."

"This old man intends to work even harder from now on."

Looking into Jinpaecheon's brightly shining eyes, Jinhageun had this thought.

Did people grow cooler as they aged? No. Cool people grew old in a cool way, and uncool people simply became lame old men.

"I will definitely become a cool Lord like you, Grandfather."

Smiling, Jinpaecheon unfastened the Gentleman's Sword at his waist and held it out to his grandson.

"Take your sword."

* * *

Today, a Guhwamagong duel was unfolding.

Grand Annihilation Form against Grand Annihilation Form.

Squelch, squelch.

Demons split apart simultaneously from both sides.

Geommugeuk's eyes went wide as he looked at the demons arrayed before Geomujin. Though he had executed the Grand Annihilation Form countless times, the one his father was unleashing now was unlike anything he had ever seen.

"So this technique could be transformed like this?"

Surprisingly, his father's Grand Annihilation Form was in two rows.

The demons had originally stretched out in a single file, but upon achieving the Great Perfection of the Twelve Stars, it became possible to form them into two rows.

"Were these guys always so obedient?"

The number of splitting demons had increased as well, so it truly felt like an army of demons lined up in formation.

"This is cheating, Father!"

But before his words could even finish, his father's demons surged forward.

Boom, boom, boom, boom, boom.

Geommugeuk's demons charged forward without fear.

Kaboooom!

Geommugeuk's demons collided with the first row of demons and annihilated one another.

But immediately after, the demons in the second row pressed forward as if to overwhelm him.

Bang! Crash!

The Great Demon Wall erected before Geommugeuk blocked the fearsome onslaught.

Was his father resolved to do something? Today, he was pushing with more powerful internal energy than ever before.

"This time I really almost died..."

In that instant, Geommugeuk could not finish his sentence. A shadow began to fall over the place where he and his father stood.

"No way? Right?"

Behind his father, the Heavenly Demon Soul rose, lifting its body.

There was not even a warning. Immediately upon manifesting, dozens of sword-shaped astral energies began to appear in the empty air behind the Heavenly Demon Soul like stars.

Guhwamagong, Seventh Form: Demon Soul Genesis.

Without looking back, Geommugeuk unleashed the Extinction Flash Step to escape from that spot.

Swoosh, swoosh, swoosh, swoosh!

Slam, slam, slam, slam, slam!

Astral energies poured down and struck the ground just barely behind the fleeing Geommugeuk.

"No matter how great Demon Soul Genesis is, it can't catch me!"

Right at that moment.

Swhoooooosh.

The massive Heavenly Demon Soul charged at Geommugeuk with terrifying speed. It was the Eighth Form: Demon Soul Dash.

Having caught up to Geommugeuk with tremendous speed, the Heavenly Demon Soul's eyes turned white. It was preparing to unleash the Ninth Form: Heavenly Demon Supreme.

Father, why are you doing this? I am going to die!

"Save me, Heavenly Demon Soul!"

Of course, it was a shout calling forth his own Heavenly Demon Soul.

Geommugeuk erected the Great Demon Wall.

Screeeeeech!

The Great Demon Wall was being pierced by the Demon Soul Supreme. If it broke through, this was not an attack that could be blocked by a protective astral barrier.

Neither his father nor the Heavenly Demon Soul stopped. From his father's cold eyes, Geommugeuk could sense this thought:

If you can't figure a way out, you die!

In the next instant!

Flash.

The astral energy that had pierced through the Great Demon Wall collided with a pure white light.

Kra-boooom!

An enormous explosion roared, and the surroundings shook as if an earthquake had struck. Dirt and dust from the overturned ground rained down in heaps.

When the dust cleared, Geommugeuk stood there. Surprisingly, he remained unharmed even by the Demon Soul Supreme.

"I imitated what you did, Father, and it worked."

Geommugeuk had unleashed the Heavenly Demon World Annihilation. Not the Heavenly Demon World Annihilation that annihilates all things, but the very one his father had used to create a sword of light and kill Hwamugi. That technique which had been manifested through a blade! He had countered the ultimate move with the ultimate move.

"With my life on the line, I was able to imitate it."

His father gave a peculiar smile.

"Did you think you could imitate that merely by trying?"

Astonishing words flowed from his father's mouth.

"You have achieved the Great Perfection of the Twelve Stars."

Because he had achieved the Great Perfection of the Twelve Stars, he could transform and use the Heavenly Demon World Annihilation.

"You achieved it several days ago. You knew, didn't you?"

Geommugeuk hung his head low.

"Yes, I knew."

"Why didn't you say anything?"

"Because I wanted to stay with Father a few days longer, I did not tell you. I am sorry."

How could he scold such a son for hiding it? While everyone in the world feared him, only his son wished to remain by his side a little longer.

In truth, their hearts were the same. Geomujin, too, had realized his son achieved the Great Perfection of the Twelve Stars several days ago.

"You've finally done it."

"Yes, I've finally done it."

"Congratulations."

"Thanks to Father, I have easily accomplished a grand feat that would have taken a lifetime. I am truly grateful."

Geommugeuk bowed his head to convey his gratitude.

Though he did not show it outwardly, Geomujin was every bit as pleased as his son.

"It is thanks to your own efforts. You've worked hard."

The two stood side by side, gazing at the distant horizon of the plains. Just then, as the sun was setting, a red sunset was beautifully dyeing the world.

"Now, this place is the last one, too."

After standing like that for a moment, watching the sunset, Geomujin suddenly spoke.

"She is the only person I ever loved."

"!"

Geommugeuk looked at his father in shock.

A yearning swept through his father's eyes as he gazed at the sunset. It was truly the first time he had seen such a look.

A sigh of relief escaped Geommugeuk before he knew it. How did his father think of his mother? Did he hate her? Did he regret meeting her? Was there some unspeakable secret? He had worried about all manner of things.

But now, none of that mattered.

If his father had truly loved her.

"I believe she must have been the most wonderful person in the world."

The gaze Geomujin fixed on his son deepened.

"If she had seen you, she would have adored you."

As long as Father carried Mother in his heart.

"I'm sure she is watching me even now."

An uncontrollable surge of emotion flashed across Geomujin's face.

As if trying to hide that emotion, Geomujin turned his head and gazed at the distant sunset.

"Let us leave now."

"Yes, Father."

Geommugeuk snapped his fingers. It was the crispest, most cheerful, and most pleasant snap since he had first executed the Spacetime Transference Technique—snap!

Chapter 848: Do Not Overturn the Cult Leader's Drinking Table
-------------------------------------------------------------
When Geom Mugeuk and Geom Ujin came outside, Hwi happened to have just brought food and left it there.

At their emergence, Hwi greeted them with a glad expression, cupping his hands.

"We greet the Cult Leader and the Young Cult Leader."

As he did so, he examined Geom Ujin first. Because time flowed differently inside and outside, he had been worried if there might be any changes to the Cult Leader. Fortunately, Geom Ujin was no different now than before he had entered.

"Uncle, you must have had a hard time?"

"Not at all. I merely carried the food."

"Please tell the chefs! That their hard work is finally over!"

Thanks to the chefs and Hwi, his father and he had been able to eat well and train.

"If we had only eaten jerky and Wall-Breaking Pellets, we wouldn't have come out yet."

Hwi spoke respectfully to Geom Mugeuk.

"Congratulations on achieving a great accomplishment."

Geom Mugeuk asked with a surprised face.

"Is it showing?"

"No. Your realm is now beyond even my ability to fathom."

It was not something said out of courtesy, but the truth. Having already surpassed the realm of Banbak Gwijin, Geom Mugeuk now stood at the final realm of martial arts.

"But how did you know?"

"Had it not been a crucial moment, you would not have entered joint closed-door seclusion, and having entered such seclusion, you would not have emerged empty-handed."

Hwi knew the two's personalities accurately. He was the one who knew the Cult Leader better than anyone. For the Cult Leader, there was no such thing as giving up halfway.

Geom Ujin spoke as if proud.

"Mugeuk has achieved the Twelve-Star Grand Completion of the Guhwamagong at the youngest age in the history of our cult."

At the words "Twelve-Star Grand Completion," Hwi congratulated Geom Mugeuk again with a surprised face.

"Young Cult Leader, I sincerely congratulate you."

He had truly become the second-strongest expert under heaven. Because he was still young, becoming the number-one expert under heaven was only a matter of time. Considering the outstanding achievements he had shown until now, it would not take very long.

The fact that he achieved the Twelve-Star Grand Completion was indeed amazing, but to Hwi, there was something even more surprising.

'Cult Leader, do you realize you just boasted about your son to me for the first time?'

Geom Mugeuk attributed that achievement to his father.

"I don't fall behind anyone when it comes to the fortune of having parents, do I? Who in the world is there who could guide someone as young as me to the Twelve-Star Grand Completion?"

It wasn't just this closed-door training session. Even in the previous process of achieving the Ten-Star Grand Completion, his father's help had been the greatest.

"That's enough."

Truthfully, it had also been a time of great help to Geom Ujin. It was an experience where he realized that the completion of martial arts was far, far away, and that even after achieving the Twelve-Star Grand Completion of the Guhwamagong, there was still a road remaining.

Hwi delivered the news he needed to convey while the two were present.

"The Demon Lords, Magun, and the Ghost Shadow Corps have all returned. However, during that process, an incident occurred."

After the battle with the Dark Palace Lord, the large coffer that had remained there was being moved to the main headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

"It is said that the coffer disappeared during transport. Not looted or lost, but vanished without a trace. The Yellow Heaven Pavilion has launched an investigation regarding that matter."

Geom Ujin looked at Geom Mugeuk. Because when it came to the coffer, his son knew best.

"I don't think we need to investigate."

Geom Mugeuk could tell. That the old man who had allowed his regression had retrieved the coffer.

Considering the mysterious efficacy of the coffer, perhaps from the very beginning it had not been an object of the human world.

"It seems the coffer has returned to where it originally belonged."

At Geom Mugeuk's words, Geom Ujin nodded. He knew that the multiple souls entering the body of the Thousand-Killing Star had also been because of that coffer. Therefore, it was something that absolutely had to be retrieved, but if his son spoke thus, there must surely be something he suspected.

"Have the investigation halted."

"I will relay that."

Geom Mugeuk spoke to his father.

"Oh, and Father. Why don't we open the Heavenly Demon Treasury wide open this time?"

At the questioning look, Geom Mugeuk made an unexpected suggestion.

"The great battle is over, so why don't you gather all the Demon Lords and hold a tournament soon? With some ten-thousand-year snow ginseng roots as prizes."

"What kind of tournament?"

"Since the Demon Lords can't brawl and fight, how about a fishing tournament? A contest of pride! You, me, and Hyung will attend too."

At the mention of fishing, competitive spirit flashed across Geom Ujin's face.

"Excellent!"

Geom Ujin accepted in one breath and strode toward the Heavenly Demon Hall, and Hwi followed behind him.

A faint smile formed at the corners of Geom Ujin's mouth as he walked.

How could he not know? His son was setting up an opportunity for him to look after the Demon Lords. Using the fishing tournament as an excuse to comfort the Demon Lords who had suffered.

Even after Geom Ujin and Hwi walked toward the Heavenly Demon Hall, Geom Mugeuk stood there for a moment, looking up at the sky.

His original plan had been to play to his heart's content, but unexpectedly he had emerged from the most difficult training of his entire life.

And so he shouted toward the real sky he was seeing after a long time.

"Don't try to stop me now! I'm really going to play!"

In the sky that offered no reply, only indifferent fluffy clouds slowly drifted by.

* * *

Because he had achieved the Twelve-Star Grand Completion of the Guhwamagong, there was something he absolutely had to do.

Returning to his quarters, Geom Mugeuk immediately unleashed the Space-Time Transposition Technique. A blue plain unfolded. Geom Mugeuk summoned a being well-suited to this vast space.

A massive Heavenly Demon Soul descended.

It was the first time seeing his own Heavenly Demon Soul after achieving the Twelve-Star Grand Completion. Just as his father's Heavenly Demon Soul had changed, his own Heavenly Demon Soul's appearance had also changed.

The flame pattern on its chest had grown larger and clearer, and the symbol of the Heavenly Demon Soul was engraved on the backs of both hands. His father's Heavenly Demon Soul had been engraved only on the back of his right hand, but his own was on both hands.

Various parts of its body were different from before, as if wearing armor. It was a far cooler and more refined appearance, but its eyes were only fiercer and more intense.

"Congratulations. On becoming a Twelve-Star Heavenly Demon Soul."

The Heavenly Demon Soul slowly brought its hands together and then cupped them slightly like a human, lowering its head. Geom Mugeuk could tell. That the Heavenly Demon Soul was celebrating his achievement of the Twelve-Star Grand Completion.

"Thank you, my friend. We have somewhere to go right now, so descend at human size."

At that, the Heavenly Demon Soul disappeared and then descended again at a small size.

The small form of the Heavenly Demon Soul definitely gave off a much more intense feeling.

Snap.

When Geom Mugeuk snapped his fingers, the space the two were in changed.

It was that very small island that Geom Mugeuk liked the most.

There was a tree with broad leaves on a white sandy beach that stretched out under the blazing sun. Under its shade, two comfortable chairs were placed.

"This is your seat."

At those words, the Heavenly Demon Soul's pupils shook slightly. It is said that a Heavenly Demon Soul's personality takes after its master; indeed, the reaction of Geom Mugeuk's Heavenly Demon Soul was extraordinary.

Geom Mugeuk leaned back into the chair first, half-reclining.

"You have no idea how much I missed this place throughout my training."

Geom Mugeuk continued speaking while still looking at the sea.

"Sitting here like this and looking at the sea, you can forget all your worries for a while. Though I don't know if you have any worries."

The Heavenly Demon Soul glanced at Geom Mugeuk, then looked again at the sea that Geom Mugeuk was watching.

"Now that you've become Twelve-Star, you'll be able to speak too, right? Honestly, your mouth is itching to talk too, isn't it? Father said so. There were Heavenly Demon Souls in the past that conversed with their masters. Since you're not the first, you don't need to feel burdened about speaking. And how can it be possible that the Heavenly Demon Soul of a master who loves to talk so much can't speak?"

The Heavenly Demon Soul remained silent.

In truth, it didn't matter if it didn't speak. Just having a being that listened to his words like this was something to be grateful enough for.

It was right at that moment. The Heavenly Demon Soul slowly sat in the chair beside him.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Heavenly Demon Soul with an emotional face. It was not simply obeying an order to sit. This action was clearly an emotional exchange with him. An indication that they had grown one step closer.

With a stoic face, the Heavenly Demon Soul gazed far out at the sea.

He wondered if perhaps this Heavenly Demon Soul resembled not his talkative self after the regression, but rather the self from before the regression. One that resembled his true personality from deep within his heart.

"Yeah, if both of us talked a lot, it'd be too noisy, right?"

And so Geom Mugeuk and the Heavenly Demon Soul sat silently on the beach, watching the sea.

* * *

"You already came out of closed-door training?"

Chwima was startled to see Geom Mugeuk. After resting on the beach with his Heavenly Demon Soul, the first person he had gone to find was Chwima.

"I heard you entered joint closed-door training with the Cult Leader, so I thought you'd come out after quite a while?"

He seemed surprised that he had come out after only a few days.

"That's how it turned out. Any injuries?"

"I'm fine now. Don't tell me you came because you were worried about me?"

Geom Mugeuk nodded. Chwima had fought fiercely, even drinking all the alcohol he had. This time, he had wanted to look after him first above all.

"Because I wanted to share a celebratory drink with you."

"Good."

About to fetch the alcohol tucked away in a corner of the Drunken Dream Pavilion, Chwima suddenly spoke as if something had come to mind.

"No, there's somewhere I need to take you."

Chwima took Geom Mugeuk and headed somewhere.

The place they arrived at together was the Absolute Forbidden Zone deep within the Great Drunken Forest.

The very place where the Jujeong was kept.

Geom Mugeuk could understand why Chwima had brought him here. Having killed the Thousand-Killing Star, he wanted to check if the Jujeong had been damaged.

The two entered with tense hearts.

Having checked the Jujeong there, Chwima shouted with a joyful face.

"The Jujeong is unharmed!"

Chwima cheered loudly.

"It has finally returned to normal!"

Chwima was truly happy like a child. Geom Mugeuk could feel anew how important this Jujeong was to him.

"Are you that happy?"

"I am."

Others might think it superstition, but to Chwima, it was the most important thing.

"Congratulations, hyung."

"Thank you. Let's have our celebratory drink here."

"Good."

Chwima brought out the alcohol and cups stored in the forbidden zone.

The two sat beside the Jujeong and drank. Chwima smiled incessantly, seemingly in a good mood just from seeing the clear Jujeong.

"The taste of this liquor is incredible. Is this a liquor I've never had before?"

"It's alcohol I'd been saving. I was going to drink it when the Jujeong returned to normal."

"Your saved alcohol is endless."

"I also have alcohol I'd set aside for the day you become Cult Leader. That's the one I treasure most."

"Isn't the alcohol you were going to drink when you retire the best?"

Geom Mugeuk burst into laughter at the sight of Chwima flinching.

Drinking in the Absolute Forbidden Zone of the Great Drunken Forest had its own unique charm.

After they had drunk a few cups, Chwima spoke to Geom Mugeuk.

"Don't worry about the future beforehand."

"Is it showing?"

"Your face is full of worry."

"You know me. I'm the type to worry unnecessarily about useless things. I worry about things that might not even happen. Because of the gravity of the situation, it's not easy to put it off as something for later."

Geom Mugeuk emptied his cup and conveyed his honest feelings.

"Hyung, I even had a terrible thought just now. That I wished this Jujeong would be damaged again when Father starts trying to unify the martial arts world in earnest. Then I could use it to stop him, or so I thought."

"The Cult Leader wouldn't even care about something like the Jujeong being damaged."

Chwima refilled Geom Mugeuk's empty cup.

He had nothing to say. Because he was someone who would follow the Cult Leader's orders exactly as given. Even if an order came down to kill Jinhagun or Bisain, he would carry it out gladly.

Chwima looked down at the cup in his hand for a moment, then suddenly spat out words he hadn't expected.

"Don't overturn the Cult Leader's drinking table."

Geom Mugeuk looked at Chwima. Chwima continued speaking while keeping his eyes on the cup.

"The Cult Leader has drunk the liquor called Unification of the Martial Arts World for a long, long time, and is intoxicated by it. But if someone suddenly appeared, overturned the table, and doused him with cold water to sober him up, how would he feel? Even if it's his son."

Geom Mugeuk asked Chwima.

"Then what should I do?"

Chwima emptied his own cup and answered.

"You sit at that drinking table too."

It was something Geom Mugeuk had never considered.

"Just sit down and think first. Don't plan to recklessly overturn the table."

Sit at Father's drinking table? And then what?

"What if I get drunk too?"

"Even I don't know that."

Chwima smiled self-deprecatingly and poured alcohol into Geom Mugeuk's empty cup.

"Well, that's the advice of a drunkard for you."

"No. It was helpful advice."

"Really?"

Geom Mugeuk nodded. Until now, he had only been thinking of overturning the drinking table, just as Chwima said. He had only thought about how to end the drinking session.

But Chwima was right. From Father's standpoint, it was sudden and unfair.

"I felt it when we went to the Ice Palace before too, but hyung, you're really smart."

Chwima waved his hands saying no, but he couldn't hide the glint in his eyes that said 'tell me more'.

"You're the smartest among the Demon Lords."

"If only I hadn't been so caught up in alcohol, I might have served as a strategist at the Tongcheon Pavilion."

Gowol had told him to become Cult Leader, the Wind-Heaven Cult Leader had told him to give him a grandchild, and Chwima told him to get drunk with his father. They were all giving him advice in their own ways.

"Yeah, I should sit at that drinking table first, as you said."

At least one thing was clear.

"Father must know too. How terrible the hangover from this drinking session will be."

Because it would involve countless lives being lost.

'If I become his drinking companion instead, yet never bring the drinking session to an end?'

He didn't know if that was possible, but he had at least clearly grasped one direction.

He shouldn't try to stop his father recklessly.

Chwima brought out a box of alcohol he had kept in a corner of the forbidden zone.

"Take it with you when you go. It's the liquor we just drank."

"Why are you giving me something this precious? And a whole box?"

It wasn't because he had praised him for being smart.

"Didn't you say you're going to meet people and have fun now? Good alcohol makes a good atmosphere, and a good atmosphere..."

Chwima held out the box of alcohol and added,

"It will help you find the answer."

Chapter 849: Have You Asked the Cult Leader?
--------------------------------------------
When Cha Iran arrived at Geom Mugeuk’s residence, she ran into someone unexpected at the entrance.

“Huh?”

She was a woman who would not so much as blink no matter what vicious demons she faced, but there was only one person who could fluster her—and that was the Sword King.

The two of them bowed to each other in greeting.

“The Young Cult Leader invited me for a meal.”

“As he did me.”

It was clear that Geom Mugeuk had called them both without telling either of them.

Since the Sword King already knew that Cha Iran had feelings for him, meeting like this was terribly awkward.

“Let’s go in.”

“Yes.”

The two of them stepped into the place from which a delicious smell was wafting.

“Brother Ak, you’re here? Welcome, Squad Leader Cha.”

Geom Mugeuk was cooking in the courtyard. A large table was already set with liquor and dishes, and meat was grilling beside it.

“Please wait just a moment and you’ll be able to eat.”

Just then, Ian came out of the building carrying wine cups, bowls, and chopsticks.

Cha Iran greeted her first.

“Unit Leader.”

“Squad Leader Cha, you’re here?”

Looking at the dishes laid out on the table, Cha Iran asked Ian,

“Is today a special day?”

Ian turned and asked Geom Mugeuk the exact same thing.

“Is today a special day?”

“It’s the day the Drunken Demon gifted me some fine liquor, so I’m having a drink with friends. Also, it’s the day our Brother Ak announces the design for his new life.”

When Cha Iran looked at him as if asking what that meant, the Sword King appeared flustered, as though this was the first he had heard of it.

Cha Iran grasped the situation.

“Now that even the Heavenly Killing Star is gone, you needed a new prey.”

At her joke, the Sword King nodded.

“We do come from that side, don’t we?”

This time, Cha Iran nodded in agreement.

“Don’t misunderstand! Today’s prey is this meat I cooked.”

Geom Mugeuk cut the meat he had been grilling and brought it to the table.

“This is the first time the four of us have eaten together, isn’t it?”

It was a gathering Geom Mugeuk had planned. He had wanted to drink with the four of them like this, and as it happened, the Sword King had provided the pretext, while the Drunken Demon had provided good liquor.

After tasting the Drunken Demon’s liquor, Cha Iran marveled at its flavor.

“It’s truly delicious.”

It was a taste she had never experienced before, one that naturally lifted her spirits.

Ian sent a sound transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

—You prepared this gathering to bring those two together, didn’t you?

—No.

—Then why?

—Because I wanted to spend time with you. Those two were just a bonus while I was at it.

Knowing full well that it was not true, Ian still smiled happily at the pleasing words.

Seeing them exchange sound transmissions and smile, the Sword King shook his head. How could he not know what that Geom Mugeuk was scheming? He was probably going to bring up the inn to Cha Iran.

Just then, Cha Iran asked the Sword King,

“Do you still wish to leave?”

By now, the Sword King had completely abandoned the thought of leaving.

“It feels as though the place I reached after leaving was here.”

At those words, Cha Iran smiled. The same was true for her.

“Oh, right. You agreed to take charge of our squad’s swordsmanship training, didn’t you?”

Geom Mugeuk cut into their conversation.

“That may be difficult. Brother Ak is planning a new business.”

“What kind of business?”

“He intends to build the largest inn in the Central Plains.”

Geom Mugeuk explained what kind of inn it would be. Luxurious and splendid, with the rule that absolutely no fighting would be allowed inside.

“He’s looking for a business partner right now. Someone sharp and decisive would be ideal. Our Brother Ak is surprisingly softhearted.”

Geom Mugeuk openly revealed his true intentions.

“Are you not interested in such a business, Squad Leader Cha? You must have earned plenty of money to invest, too.”

When Cha Iran hesitated to answer, the Sword King stepped in.

“Stop talking nonsense.”

He was stopping him because he worried Cha Iran might feel pressured. Of course, Geom Mugeuk was not the sort to stop just because of that.

“I’m not telling the two of you to date. I’m giving Squad Leader Cha a chance to choose.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned to Cha Iran. She could feel it. He truly was giving her an opportunity, exactly as he said.

Not an opportunity to go into the inn business, but an opportunity to hold on to this man named Ak Gunhak.

Her relationship with Ak Gunhak right now was ambiguous. If only he had not known that she liked him, perhaps it would not have been this awkward.

If she could not create some kind of change, their relationship would simply drift along and come to an end. Moreover, if he truly proceeded with that inn business, there would be no occasion for them to meet in the Divine Cult.

Geom Mugeuk’s eyes seemed to be saying this to her.

Aren’t you an assassin? Your target is right in front of you. Don’t let him get away!

At last, her answer came.

“I’m interested.”

Since he had not expected Cha Iran to say she was interested, the Sword King was slightly flustered.

“I’d like to take part in that business too.”

The Sword King asked her,

“Were you interested in running an inn?”

Cha Iran answered honestly.

“No. Not once in my life have I ever thought of running an inn.”

“Then why?”

Cha Iran gazed at the Sword King and mustered her final bit of courage.

“Because I am still holding on to your right foot.”

The Sword King understood what she meant.

On the day he had first reunited with her in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, Geom Mugeuk had said jokingly that he would hold on to Ak Gunhak’s left leg so he could not run away, and told her to hold tightly on to his right leg.

That was why he knew how much courage she had gathered to say this now.

This was her confession.

The Sword King did not dislike her either.

It would be a lie to say he disliked a woman so beautiful she could contend for the title of the greatest beauty under heaven, but because she was such a woman, he also felt burdened. Most importantly, he still did not know what kind of person she was.

“We do not know each other well, do we?”

Looking quietly at the Sword King, Cha Iran replied,

“Does knowing someone for a long time mean you will know them well?”

She looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“Did you decide to change your life only after knowing exactly what kind of person the Young Cult Leader was?”

At least, she had not.

And neither had the Sword King. Even now, he still did not know everything about what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was.

And yet he had become his friend. He had seen one good side of a person, become intoxicated by its intensity, and changed his life.

When the Sword King made no reply, Cha Iran emptied the cup before her and said,

“Don’t feel burdened. I only said it because I thought if I didn’t today, I never would. I hope your business prospers.”

She also greeted Geom Mugeuk and Ian.

“Thank you for inviting me today. The liquor and food were truly delicious.”

She had no regrets. If she had let him go without even saying this properly, she would have regretted it all along.

Just as she rose from her seat, the Sword King spoke in a strong voice.

“Let’s do it.”

Cha Iran looked at the Sword King in surprise.

“Let’s open an inn together.”

The Sword King lifted the liquor bottle, telling her to receive a drink.

“They say we can’t fight inside the inn, but if there are bastards who refuse to listen to the very end, let’s beat them up together as we run it. If it fails, let’s curse that friend of mine to our hearts’ content. We’ll say he lured us into opening some strange inn and made us ruin ourselves splendidly.”

Cha Iran sat back down, accepted the Sword King’s drink, and said,

“If I don’t start, then so be it, but if I do, I will absolutely make it the number one inn in the Central Plains.”

At that momentum, the Sword King flinched inwardly. So this was why she was needed?

Only belatedly did Cha Iran look at Ian and say,

“Ah, I should have received your permission first, Unit Leader.”

Ian said playfully,

“Indeed. Unauthorized departure is a serious crime, so what should we do?”

“How about this? We’ll allow you and the Young Cult Leader to use our inn for free for the rest of your lives.”

“I think that should be enough.”

Meanwhile, Geom Mugeuk and the Sword King were exchanging sound transmissions.

—This is an unexpected variable, Brother Ak! I feel like Squad Leader Cha has already taken your inn from you! Maybe even you too.

—Don’t make me walk this path and think you alone can slip away.

—I’ll have to watch and judge whether Brother Ak lives well or not.

The two of them looked at each other and smiled.

It had only been a thought of trying his hand at running an inn, but Geom Mugeuk had set the stage for that inn to grow into the number one inn in the Central Plains, and for him to do it together with the greatest beauty under heaven.

Both the Martial Alliance Leader and the Evil Dao Alliance Leader had watched his every move and struggled to take him in, yet this Young Cult Leader was trying to send him out like this. Telling him to live his own life.

‘Thank you, my friend.’

Geom Mugeuk raised his cup high and said,

“Now then, to our lifelong free inn!”

* * *

The next day, when Geom Mugeuk went to the Thousand Poison Forest with a bottle of liquor in hand, the Poison King’s right-hand man, Sangseon, came to greet him.

“It has been a long time, Young Cult Leader.”

“Have you been well?”

“Thanks to you, I have been well. Young Cult Leader, congratulations on repelling the great enemy.”

They walked together, exchanging various words, when Sangseon said something unexpected.

“Ah, please do not be surprised today.”

“What do you mean?”

“After the battle at Maga Village, the Poison King changed a little.”

“Don’t tell me he has shut himself off from the outside entirely and sunk into a world of his own?”

At that, Sangseon gave an unexpected answer.

“No. It is the opposite.”

“Pardon?”

“You will understand when you see him.”

Sangseon gave an unreadable smile.

In that way, Geom Mugeuk arrived at the Poison King’s workshop. The Poison King was alone in his workshop. Sitting on the edge of a large worktable, the Poison King lifted his head and greeted Geom Mugeuk.

“You’re here?”

Geom Mugeuk flinched in surprise at the unfamiliar sight of the Poison King greeting him first. He had always been deeply absorbed in something.

“Have you eaten?”

When he even asked whether he had eaten, Geom Mugeuk shouted,

“Who are you? Who are you, and why are you inside the Poison King’s body?”

The Poison King laughed and looked at the bottle Geom Mugeuk was holding.

“Is that liquor? Bring it here.”

He felt like a completely different person. Geom Mugeuk understood why Sangseon had told him not to be surprised.

With a bewildered look, Geom Mugeuk approached and handed him the liquor he had brought.

The Poison King drank straight from the bottle. He wiped the liquor running down the corner of his mouth with his sleeve.

“Unfortunately, I just failed to compound the Blood Serpent Deadly Poison. I thought I would succeed this time. But what kind of liquor is this, for it to be so good?”

“The liquor is good because the Drunken Demon gave it to me, but…”

Geom Mugeuk stared fixedly at the Poison King’s face.

“Were you originally this kind of person?”

“What was I like?”

“You didn’t speak much, and you were always lost in some other thought.”

“That would not do. A person who handles poison must be colder and more rational than anyone.”

Now that he thought about it, that was true. How could someone who blankly stared at poisonous insects handle poisons capable of killing hundreds or thousands of people in an instant?

Just as there was a lonely side of himself, the Poison King had another side hidden as well.

“Demon Buddha! Look at this. You’ve been completely fooled until now! You were used as an herb gatherer by such a coldhearted man!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s cry, the Poison King smiled faintly and held out the bottle. Geom Mugeuk also drank deeply and refreshingly before asking again,

“Why did you put on that act?”

“It was not an act.”

“Then?”

“With only a cold and rational self, one cannot comprehend the ultimate essence of poison arts. Only by going mad over poison, watching poisonous insects all day, and thinking only of poison can one complete poison arts.”

When he needed to immerse himself in his own world, he had to do so, and when he needed to be rational like this, he had to be this way.

He was saying that only by possessing both sides could one reach the true final realm of poison arts.

“If poison is used incorrectly, it is not only you who dies. Your family dies, your comrades die, and even passersby die. That is why, if you are not prepared to go mad over poison, you should not learn poison arts in the first place.”

Perhaps for the Poison King, of those two contradictory sides, the more comfortable one was when he was deeply immersed in his own world.

But now, he had closed the door to that world and come out to this side. Geom Mugeuk had the feeling that even if he came tomorrow, or the day after, the Poison King would remain exactly like this.

“It is because of my father, isn’t it?”

The Poison King, who had been drinking, flinched.

Until now, the Poison King’s existence had been the strongest deterrent against war. But once war broke out, the Poison King would become the cult’s most powerful weapon.

This intelligent Poison King knew. With the death of the Heavenly Killing Star, a true battle would now begin.

Not a battle to protect the Young Cult Leader, but his father’s war to unify murim. In the end, this was what the Poison King’s change meant.

The Poison King had begun preparing for war.

Geom Mugeuk handed him the liquor bottle and said,

“Please teach me how to make one antidote.”

“What need does someone immune to all poisons have for an antidote?”

Then unexpected words flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“An antidote that can stop the unification of murim.”

The Poison King looked at Geom Mugeuk. No matter how much his words and actions had changed, the person inside was still the same Poison King who had once barked like a dog with him.

“To make an antidote, you must first understand the poison properly.”

The Poison King had said before that the flower of poison arts was not poisoning, but detoxification.

“Have you ever asked the Cult Leader about the unification of murim he dreams of?”

“!”

He had not yet asked his father. What kind of world do you dream of?

“If you want to make an antidote, you must know exactly what kind of poison it is.”

The Poison King’s advice told him what he had to do first when he sat at that drinking table, just as the Drunken Demon had advised.

With a bright smile, Geom Mugeuk expressed his gratitude to the Poison King.

“Thank you for your words. Thanks to you, I have a sense of how I should make the antidote.”

But the Poison King did not give him only a clue to the antidote. He also left him with the question of whether that poison truly needed to be detoxified.

“On that day, the Cult Leader was prepared to die.”

The Poison King’s gaze deepened as he recalled that time.

“He was determined to save you, no matter what. He was sincere.”

Could he dare imagine his father’s feelings as he entrusted his safety to the Poison King? Or the feelings of the Poison King as he received that order?

“Considering that the person he entrusted his son to at the very end was you, Poison King, it seems my father trusted you the most.”

The Poison King shook his head.

“That cannot be.”

Perhaps the relationship between the Poison King and his father was like the relationship between him and his father before his regression. Misunderstanding, rigidity, and prejudice had formed a wall between them.

“Sometime, please speak openly with my father about poison arts. Show him this side of you as well. It may not be as bad as you think.”

Have a conversation with the Cult Leader? That was not an easy thing for the Poison King.

“There are days like today, when no matter how hard you try and how much care you take, you fail to compound a poison.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk said casually, for the sake of improving the relationship between his father and the Poison King.

“Shall I teach you one way to make an antidote too?”

With an expression that said this was truly a secret, and that he was telling only him in particular, Geom Mugeuk whispered,

“Start practicing fishing from today.”

Chapter 850: It Is Time to Remove the Mask
------------------------------------------
When Geom Mugeuk returned to his quarters after meeting the Poison King, Seo Daeryong was waiting in front of the door.

“Young Cult Leader!”

“My right arm!”

Geom Mugeuk greeted Seo Daeryong warmly. Seeing him welcome him with such a bright face dispelled all the worry he had felt while waiting, wondering if he had come for no reason when the man was so busy.

“I know you are busy, but I still wanted to see your face, so I came.”

“You did well to come. Let’s go inside.”

“No, it’s all right. I’ve seen your face, so that’s enough.”

“It’s not enough for me. Come in.”

When Geom Mugeuk went in first, Seo Daeryong had no choice but to follow him inside.

“Yeongha is growing well, isn’t he?”

Seo Daeryong was deeply grateful that Geom Mugeuk remembered his son’s name like this.

“He’s growing bigger by the day. If you saw him, you would be shocked.”

“I can still see that little fellow’s eyes. You should have brought him.”

“It’s good that I love my son, but I need a little freedom too. You wouldn’t know this, would you? Young Cult Leader, you may be someone who doesn’t have a single thing in the world you don’t know, but you won’t know this.”

Seo Daeryong exaggeratedly drooped his shoulders as he spoke.

“Raising a child is harder than training in martial arts.”

At those words, Geom Mugeuk burst out laughing.

“At first, he kept waking up at night and wouldn’t let us sleep. Now, if I look away for even a moment, he gets into such trouble. Bumping into things, falling over, swallowing things. Young Cult Leader, was this perhaps how you felt watching us?”

Then Seo Daeryong gave an awkward look.

“I said something unnecessary in front of someone who isn’t even married, didn’t I?”

“No. In this field, you’re my senior, aren’t you? Pass on all your information to me. Now, sit down first.”

After seating Seo Daeryong at the table in the courtyard, Geom Mugeuk brought out wine from a box placed in the corner. Each time a bottle of wine disappeared from the box, the person sitting before Geom Mugeuk changed.

“This is special wine given to me by the Drunken Demon. The taste is incredible.”

Seo Daeryong glanced at the bottles left in the box. There were only a few remaining. He thought that even if Geom Mugeuk only took care of the Demon Supremes, there would not be enough wine.

“I’m fine without wine.”

But how could Geom Mugeuk fail to notice Seo Daeryong being considerate?

Without hesitation, Geom Mugeuk opened the stopper and poured him a cup.

“Drink.”

“Is it really all right to give such precious wine to me?”

“It’s because it’s precious that I’m giving it to you.”

Seo Daeryong spoke in heartfelt emotion.

“When I think of the grace you have shown me, you may curse at me every day until I die.”

“The moment you actually hear even one harsh word, the first thought that’ll come to you is: How could you do this to me!”

Seo Daeryong laughed and emptied his cup.

“The taste of this wine is truly intoxicating.”

As the Drunken Demon had said, good wine had its own power. It made one feel even better, making him say two things where he would have said one.

In that way, Geom Mugeuk heard news of Seo Daeryong’s wife Dana and her younger sisters, and also heard that, thanks to the victory in this battle, his father’s and his own popularity within the cult were soaring to the heavens.

As they spoke of this and that, Geom Mugeuk asked casually.

“Daeryong, if Yeongha grows up and strongly opposes something you want to do, how do you think you would feel?”

“My son?”

It was not any other time, but these days, when he was suffering from the hardships of raising a child.

“My son, whom I’ve raised like gold and jade? My son, whom I’ve raised without being able to sleep properly or even eat properly? I would gladly give my life for that boy. After raising him like that, if he says, Father, I hate it. That isn’t right! If he does that, I’ll drag him to the sparring stage and just—!”

Seo Daeryong, who had been raising his voice, soon let out a light sigh. What he truly wanted to say began now.

“I would be hurt, but in the end, I would understand my son and follow what he says.”

“Why?”

“Because if my son opposes it, there must be a reason worthy of it.”

“Your son could be wrong, couldn’t he?”

“No, he won’t be wrong. I’ll raise him well enough that I can trust him like that. Like you, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk asked with a slightly self-deprecating smile.

“Do I look like a good son?”

“Of course. Do you know how much I hope my son grows up to be a son like you? It’s my wife’s and my wish.”

Soon, Seo Daeryong let out a light sigh and said.

“Even if I tell my child a hundred times, You must grow up like the Young Cult Leader! he is still my child, so he’ll probably grow up like Seo Daeryong. That’s why I’ll try to become more impressive myself.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled.

Certainly, Seo Daeryong, now a father, had become more mature than before. He could feel Seo Daeryong’s change as clearly as he had felt the Poison King’s.

Geom Mugeuk patted Seo Daeryong on the shoulder.

“You’re impressive, my right arm. Today, drink all that wine with me. For today, you’re completely escaping from childcare! What can they do? Daddy is going to get a little drunk. Miss Dan, your husband belongs to me today!”

Seo Daeryong looked at Geom Mugeuk. He was saying they should drink all that precious wine together. There must still be Demon Supremes and other important people left who should be drinking it.

If someone were to look inside his emotions, they would probably grimace in boredom.

—Are you moved by the Young Cult Leader again? Is being moved the only emotion you can feel toward him?

But what could he do? The emotion Geom Mugeuk gave him was this unchanging.

If it had been him, he would have chosen and chosen among people, weighing who would be of the greatest help to him before attaching them as his right arm.

Yet this precious man had taken him, who had been shabby and nothing at all, as his right arm until the very end.

When Geom Mugeuk went to the wine box, saying he would bring more wine,

Seo Daeryong sprang up from his seat and hurriedly ran to the entrance.

“Ah! I completely forgot that my wife told me to buy something. I’ll be going first, Young Cult Leader!”

* * *

The next day, the person Geom Mugeuk visited with a bottle of wine was the Fist Demon.

“Master!”

When Geom Mugeuk arrived, the Fist Demon was training in fist arts in the courtyard with his upper body bare.

“Master, do you still have more training left to do?”

“If I didn’t, the Heavenly Killing Star would have died by my fist, wouldn’t he?”

Seeing the Fist Demon still absorbed in training, Geom Mugeuk set down the bottle he had brought beside him.

“This wine was a gift from the Drunken Demon. Put it aside and later drink it with Ian. It tastes incredible.”

He stood at the side and watched the Fist Demon train for a while.

“When you swing your fists, Master, I simply can’t resist. I’ll join you.”

Geom Mugeuk took off his upper garment and leaped into the training.

Two Thunderclap Asura Fists that had reached the realm of mastery created art in the air.

Throwing his fist in the same motion, Geom Mugeuk suddenly said,

“Father told me about Mother.”

In that instant, he felt the Fist Demon’s fist flinch.

“Did he?”

“Yes.”

He could have asked the Fist Demon about his mother. But Geom Mugeuk did not. The Fist Demon’s fists did not stop and continued on.

When the training ended, for whatever reason, the Fist Demon took Geom Mugeuk to that cliff.

Looking up at the cliff, Geom Mugeuk said,

“This cliff hasn’t changed.”

“What do you think? Do you think you can now bring it down with one punch?”

Strength entered Geom Mugeuk’s fist. If he poured all his internal energy into this fist, could he not bring down that cliff with a single blow?

The Fist Demon asked in a calm tone,

“What meaning does this cliff hold for you?”

How should he answer? Geom Mugeuk could not answer easily.

Rather, the Fist Demon knew that better than he did.

“Even if you could bring this cliff down with one punch, you probably wouldn’t. You’re not the type to boast of your skill, and more than anything, you’d think that if this cliff collapsed, the trees and animals living here would all die. You’d think that if it were simply left alone, they would go on living in harmony.”

Geom Mugeuk readily admitted it. Even if he could bring it down, he would not.

“Now, let’s go up.”

“Didn’t you say this cliff wasn’t meant to be climbed, but meant to be destroyed?”

The Fist Demon did not answer and launched himself forward. His massive body lightly rode up the cliff and began to soar.

Tap.

The two stepped on protrusions in the cliff to leap upward, flying up in an instant. Now Geom Mugeuk could fly all the way to the top without any midair steps, but he followed exactly the places the Fist Demon stepped on and ascended.

In that way, the two stood atop the cliff.

Looking down from there, not only the Eastern Fist Gate but the scenery of the main headquarters could be seen clearly. Even the Heavenly Demon Hall could be seen in the distance.

“How long has it been since you came up here?”

“Since I resolved to bring this cliff down, I have never climbed it.”

Geom Mugeuk could once again confirm that the Fist Demon had now completely abandoned that resolve. This change was also proof that the Fist Demon had grown stronger. It meant his goal had changed from the cliff to something within his heart.

Whoooosh.

The wind that blew in fluttered the hems of their clothes.

As they stood there for a while, unexpected words flowed from the Fist Demon.

“To the Cult Leader, the unification of murim must have held the same meaning as this cliff did for me.”

“!”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Fist Demon standing beside him. The Fist Demon continued speaking with a blunt face.

“It must have been something he always thought about and set as his goal. He must have thought every day that he would surely accomplish it someday. As much as my longing to bring down this cliff.”

Geom Mugeuk had no intention of persuading the Fist Demon. No, even if he could persuade him, he would not.

Even if he succeeded in persuading all the Demon Supremes, he wanted the Fist Demon, at least, to stand beside his father. Of course, he was someone who would not be moved by any persuasion to begin with.

Suddenly, Geom Mugeuk recalled something.

“Long ago, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon once told me this. Back in the old days, when Father roamed the Central Plains fighting alongside you, Master, one day Father returned to the cult and said this to the old Saber Demon.”

Geom Mugeuk repeated the words of his father that the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had conveyed to him.

“I think I shall take this murim for myself.”

Perhaps that day had been the first time his father had conceived the dream of unifying murim.

“Do you know why Father said that?”

The Fist Demon, who had been standing silently, slowly nodded. As expected, the Fist Demon knew.

Soon after, an astonishing fact was revealed from his mouth.

“It was because of your mother.”

“!”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Fist Demon with a startled face. As if telling him to ask the Cult Leader himself if he wanted to know more, the Fist Demon said nothing further.

After silently gazing for a long while at the distant Heavenly Demon Hall, Geom Mugeuk broke the silence.

“Master, leaving aside all other reasons, there is one reason you should not bring down this cliff.”

Geom Mugeuk looked around and spoke pleasantly.

“The view here is too beautiful.”

* * *

Among the subordinates of the Eight Demon Supremes, the ones who welcomed Geom Mugeuk most fervently were those strange people who were perfectly normal when alone but became odd the moment they gathered together—the Faceless Guests of the Valley of Villains.

Since they had once carried out an operation together, they liked Geom Mugeuk more than anyone.

The Faceless Guests crouching in the trees, the Faceless Guests passing along the road, and the Faceless Guests gathered with their heads together doing who knew what, all welcomed Geom Mugeuk with bright smiles.

Receiving such hospitality from the Faceless Guests, Geom Mugeuk arrived at the residence of the Extreme Evil Soma.

“Lord Soma! I’m here!”

The Extreme Evil Soma welcomed Geom Mugeuk gladly.

“Welcome, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk set down the wine bottle.

“This is wine I received from the Drunken Demon. It truly tastes excellent.”

Now, in the Extreme Evil Soma’s room, there was even a table at which they could sit together and drink. Of course, the only person with whom he could drink or eat anything was Geom Mugeuk.

“How was your closed-door cultivation?”

Geom Mugeuk told the Extreme Evil Soma about the Shicheon Secret Art. He also did not hide his martial achievement.

“So this time, I spent quite a long time together with Father. In the end, I also achieved great completion of the twelfth level of the Nine Flowers Demonic Art.”

The Extreme Evil Soma was delighted as if it were his own affair and offered a courteous congratulation.

“Achieving great completion of the twelfth level at your age, Young Cult Leader, will remain a legend in the history of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Truly, congratulations!”

“Thank you.”

“We must drink a celebratory cup today.”

“Of course.”

The two sat facing each other at the table.

“How was it with the Cult Leader?”

“It was good.”

Such an opportunity would not easily come again.

“The longer I spent with Father, the weaker my heart became. I had this thought. When Father says, Let us go to war, can I really say, I cannot go?”

To the Extreme Evil Soma, he conveyed honest feelings he had not shared with the other Demon Supremes.

“To be honest, I began to feel that I did not want to go against Father. So I kept rationalizing it like this. If he unifies murim, might murim not become happier than if it remains divided into the Demonic, Orthodox, and Evil factions and continues fighting? Could one great sacrifice prevent even greater sacrifices in the future? I also had this thought. Is this murim truly a place worth protecting even if it means standing against Father?”

The Extreme Evil Soma nodded as if he understood.

“To be honest, I still don’t know what I should do.”

He had heard various pieces of advice and made plans for what he should do, but his worry came first: could he truly change his father’s mind?

The Extreme Evil Soma, who had been quietly gazing at Geom Mugeuk, removed the mask he was wearing. It was the Extreme Evil Soma’s face, one Geom Mugeuk had not seen in a long time.

“How do I look with my mask removed?”

“What is there to say? It makes me feel ashamed for insisting that I’m first among the Four Handsome Men.”

As if he had not removed it simply to drink, the Extreme Evil Soma asked again,

“Am I, with my mask removed, still the Extreme Evil Soma?”

“Though it feels awkward and unfamiliar, you are still Lord Soma.”

After a brief pause, the Extreme Evil Soma said something astonishing.

“It is time for you to remove your mask as well, Young Cult Leader.”

“!”

“The mask of being a good son.”

The Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes were more serious than ever.

“The reason this matter feels so difficult is because you are trying to fight without removing that mask.”

The Extreme Evil Soma looked at Geom Mugeuk and asked,

“Are you afraid to remove the mask?”

Geom Mugeuk let out a sigh and said,

“I can remove it.”

The Extreme Evil Soma immediately understood Geom Mugeuk’s heart.

“You are afraid of seeing the Cult Leader after he removes his mask.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded, honestly admitting it. Yes, perhaps he did not want to see that face.

“It is unfortunate, but this fight was never one that could be fought with masks on from the beginning. Nor should it be.”

The Extreme Evil Soma was usually a man of few words, but at this moment, he did not spare his advice.

“Rather, you must make the Cult Leader remove his mask as well. If even one of you fights without removing your mask, then it will become a fight where one side yielded, and that fight will leave behind a lifetime of regret and resentment. Far in the future, at the moment of death, you will recall that matter. So do not fear the Cult Leader’s unfamiliar face.”

As if to say that the celebratory wine should be drunk when the true battle was over, the Extreme Evil Soma put back on the mask he had set down on the table.

“And do not forget that I am behind you, Young Cult Leader.”
